kṣemendraviracitā

bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatā |

namaḥ sarvajñāya |

1. prabhāsāvadānam |

cittaṃ yasya sphaṭikavimalaṃ naiva gṛhṇāti rāgaṃ
kāruṇyārdre manasi nikhilāḥ śoṣitā yena doṣāḥ |
akrodhena svayamabhihato yena saṃsāraśatruḥ
sarvajño'sau bhavatu bhavatāṃ śreyase niścalāya || k1.1 ||
sacchāyaḥ sthiradharmamūlavalayaḥ puṇyālavālasthitir
dhīvidyākaruṇāmbhasā hi vilasadvistīrṇaśākhānvitaḥ |
saṃtoṣojjvalapallavaḥ śuciyaśaḥpuṣpaḥ sadāsatphalaḥ
sarvāśāparipūrako vijayate śrībuddhakalpadrumaḥ || k1.2 ||
jāyate jagaduddhartuṃ saṃsāramakarākarāt |
matirmahānubhāvānāmatrānuśrūyate yathā || k1.3 ||
asti prabhāvatī nāma hemaharmyagṛhairvṛtā |
purī prabhāvatīva dyaurvimānaiḥ puṇyakarmaṇām || k1.4 ||
vidyādharavatī siddhagandharvagaṇasevitā |
gāṃ śritā śakranagarī sukṛtena satāmiva || k1.5 ||
sevitā satataṃ satyavratadānadayāmayaiḥ |
rājadhānīva dharmasya puṇyāvasathaśālinī || k1.6 ||
abhūdbhūtilakastasyāṃ prabhāso nāma bhūpatiḥ |
saprabhā sādarairyasya kīrtirabhyarcyate suraiḥ || k1.7 ||
guṇasaurabhasaṃbhārāḥ sarvāsāṃ hariṇīdṛśām |
yadyaśaḥpuṣpamañjaryo yātāḥ karṇavataṃsatām || k1.8 ||
upāyajñasya yasyājñāṃ suvaṇakusumojjvalām |
mālāmiva mahīpālā maulicakreṣu cakrire || k1.9 ||
taṃ kadācitsamāsīnamabhyetya bhuvaneśvaram |
uvāca kṣitivinyastajānurnāgavanādhipaḥ || k1.10 ||
deva divyadyutirdantī gṛhīto'smābhiradbhutaḥ |
tvatkīrtiśravaṇādbhūmimairāvaṇa ivāgataḥ || k1.11 ||
dvāri sthito'sau dviradastridaśārhaḥ pradṛśyatām |
bhṛtyānāṃ prabhunāṃ dṛṣṭaḥ saphalo hi pariśramaḥ || k1.12 ||
etadākarṇya nṛpatirnirgatyāmātyasaṃmataḥ |
dadarśa dviradaṃ dvāri kailāsamiva jaṅgamam || k1.13 ||
uddāmasaurabhāhūtairbhramarairgaṇḍaḍiṇḍimaiḥ |
śṛṅgārābharaṇodāraṃ vasantamiva sevitam || k1.14 ||
dantaparyantaviśrāntakaraṃ mīlitalocanam |
smarantaṃ vindhyakadalīsallakīkānanaśriyaḥ || k1.15 ||
agastyaśāsanādyātaṃ bhuvi kuñjararājatām |
sphuratsaptacchadāmodaṃ vindhyācalamivonnatam || k1.16 ||
taṃ vilokya kṣitipatirdantastambhavibhūṣitam |
lakṣmīvilāsabhavanaṃ vismayādityacintayat || k1.17 ||
aho navanavotkarṣā nirmāṇāścaryaśālinām |
karmaṇāmanavacchinnā saṃsārasargasaṃtatiḥ || k1.18 ||
amanthena sudhāmbhodheranāyāsena vāsukeḥ |
anākarṣeṇa śailasya kenāyaṃ janito gajaḥ || k1.19 ||
atha hastimahāmātraṃ saṃyātaṃ nāma bhūpatiḥ |
ādideśārcitādeśaṃ gajo'yaṃ damyatāmiti || k1.20 ||
tadādiśya mahīpāle yāte'ntaḥpuramandiram |
nāgaṃ jagrāha saṃyātaḥ sarvaśikṣābharakṣamam || k1.21 ||
sa sacchiṣya iva prājñaḥ prāgjanmābhyāsayantritaḥ |
nītastena prayatnena sarvaśikṣāvinītatām || k1.22 ||
bahudānanirudvegaḥ śaktyutsāhayutaḥ kṣamī |
ripupraghātasugatiḥ sa rājñastulyatāṃ yayau || k1.23 ||
damyakriyāsamuttīrṇaṃ tatastaṃ kuñjareśvaram |
nareśvarāya saṃyātaḥ kṛtakṛtyo nyavedayat || k1.24 ||
dṛṣṭvā tamaṅkuśāyattaṃ nirvikārabalodayam |
utsāhaśikharārūḍhaṃ mene rājā jayaśriyam || k1.25 ||
sa saṃjātapraharṣotthadākṣyaśikṣādidṛkṣayā |
tamāruroha sotsāhaḥ sahasrāṃśurivodayam || k1.26 ||
saṃyāto'tha gajendrasya mantrīva vaśavartinaḥ |
sarvamaṇḍalasaṃcāracāturthaṃ samadarśayat || k1.27 ||
gajaprekṣāprasaṅgena mṛgayākelilālasaḥ |
rājā nijotsāhamiva vyagāhata vanaṃ mahat || k1.28 ||
sa yayau ratnakeyūrakiraṇairdūrasarpībhiḥ |
sallakīpallavavarairdignāgānāhvayanniva || k1.29 ||
vrajantaṃ tatra dadṛśustaṃ vane vanadevatāḥ |
praharṣavismayākīrṇakarṇapūrīkṛtekṣaṇāḥ || k1.30 ||
śabarīkabarīpāśapuṣpasaurabhanirbharāḥ |
vaindhyā vasuṃdharādhīśaṃ marutastaṃ siṣevire || k1.31 ||
atha vindhyopakaṇṭheṣu svacchandasukhaśākhiṣu |
smṛtvā vilāsavṛttāntaṃ gajaḥ sotkaṇṭhatāṃ yayau || k1.32 ||
kariṇyāḥ premabaddhāyā gandhamāghrāya sa dvipaḥ |
nītiṃ nṛpa ivotsiktastatyājāṅkuśayantraṇām || k1.33 ||
savegaṃ dhāvatastasya rāgākṛṣṭasya daṇḍinaḥ |
vimūḍhasyeva saṃsāre nābhavadviratiḥ kkacit || k1.34 ||
dṛṣṭvā prabhañjanajavaṃ kuñjaraṃ rājakuñjaraḥ |
vrajantaṃ jātasaṃdehaḥ saṃyātamidamabravīt || k1.35 ||
aho batāyaṃ bhavatā vinayaṃ grāhito gajaḥ |
dṛṣṭaḥ prayāto vaimukhyaṃ gurorasyāṅkuśasya yaḥ || k1.36 ||
bhramatīva diśāṃ cakramanuyāntīva pādapāḥ |
pādanyāsabhareṇāsya kṣībeṇādhūrṇate kṣitiḥ || k1.37 ||
asmin deva ivākāle prayāte pratikūlatām |
sarvāḥ puruṣakārasya niṣphalā yatnavṛttayaḥ || k1.38 ||
vacaḥ śrutveti saṃyātaḥ prabhorāyātasādhvasaḥ |
śikṣāpavādavailakṣyāduvāca racitāñjaliḥ || k1.39 ||
deva sarvakriyāyattaḥ kuñjaro'yaṃ mayā kṛtaḥ |
kariṇīgandhamāghrāya yātaḥ kiṃ tvadya vikriyām || k1.40 ||
nopadeśaṃ na niyamaṃ na dākṣiṇyaṃ na sādhutām |
smaranti jantavaḥ kāmaṃ kāmasya vaśamāgatāḥ || k1.41 ||
kena ratirasotsiktā viṣayābhimukhī matiḥ |
adabhraśvabhravibhraṣṭaśaolakulyeva vāryate || k1.42 ||
śarīraśramaśikṣāyāṃ damakāḥ kuśalā vayam |
manoniyamaśikṣāyāṃ munayo'pi na paṇḍitāḥ || k1.43 ||
rāgādagaṇitāyāsaḥ skhalitākhilasaṃyamaḥ |
eṣa dhāvatyamāreṇa mūrkhaḥ khala iva dvipaḥ || k1.44 ||
vṛkṣaśākhāṃ samālambya tyajemaṃ pṛthivīpate |
vyasanī patitaḥ satyaṃ pātayatyeva durjanaḥ || k1.45 ||
saṃyātasya vacaḥ śrutvā tatkālasadṛśaṃ nṛpaḥ |
tenaiva sahitaḥ śākhāmālalambe mahātaroḥ || k1.46 ||
avatīrya taroraśvamāruhya nṛpatau gate |
prāpyāliliṅga kariṇīṃ vigāhya gahanaṃ gajaḥ || k1.47 ||
tataḥ śāntasmaro hastī dinairabhyetya saptabhiḥ |
svayamālānasaṃbaddhastasthau bhuktvā yathāsukham || k1.48 ||
śikṣāsaṃyamayantritaṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā svayamāgatam |
saṃyātaḥ kauśalotkarṣaharṣādrājñe nyavedayat || k1.49 ||
rāgavāgurayākṛṣṭaḥ prayayau yaḥ smarāturaḥ |
śikṣāyāmavisaṃvādī so'yaṃ prāptaḥ svayaṃ gajaḥ || k1.50 ||
saṃketayantrito vaśyo rasajñaḥ sallakībhuvām |
saṃtaptalohakavalaṃ gṛhṇāti vinaye sthitaḥ || k1.51 ||
eṣa kāmarasākṛṣṭaḥ kaṣṭāṃ vikṛtimāyayau |
punaḥ prakṛtimāpannaḥ praśāntamadanajvaraḥ || k1.52 ||
śakyā damayituṃ deva siṃhavyāghragajādayaḥ |
na tu rāgāsavakṣībaviṣayābhimukhaṃ manaḥ || k1.53 ||
etadākarṇya bhūpālastattatheti vicintayan |
uvāca satyamucitaṃ saṃyāta kathitaṃ tvayā || k1.54 ||
apyasti kaścilloke'smin yena cittamadadvipaḥ |
nītaḥ praśamaśīlena saṃyamālānalīnatām || k1.55 ||
ityukte de vatāviṣṭaḥ saṃyātastamabhāṣata |
deva santi jagatkleśaniḥśeṣonmūlanodyatāḥ || k1.56 ||
vivekālokitā loke vairāgyajanitāgrahāḥ |
śamasaṃtoṣaviśadā buddhā eva prabodhinaḥ || k1.57 ||
iti buddhābhidhāṃ śrutvā samyaksaṃbodhicetasaḥ |
rājñaḥ prāgjanmajābhyāsapraṇidhānamajāyata || k1.58 ||
vinimajjajjagadidaṃ saṃsāre makarākare |
saṃtārayeyaṃ saṃbodhimuktaḥ kuśalasetunā || k1.59 ||
athocurdevatā vyonmastaṃ śuddhāvāsakāyikāḥ |
samyaksaṃbodhisaṃbuddho bhaviṣyasi mahāmate || k1.60 ||
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā rājā virajasāṃ varaḥ |
jātismaro divyacakṣuḥ prayayau bodhisattvatām || k1.61 ||
atha sa vipulasattvastattvanikṣiptacakṣur
bhavajalanidhimajjatsarvabhūtānukampī |
abhavadabhinavodyatsaṃvuditsāhayogād
dalitakuśalasetuḥ sattvasaṃtāraṇāya || k1.62 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ prabhāsāvadānaṃ nāma prathamaḥ pallavaḥ samāptaḥ ||


2. śrīsenāvadānam |

te jayanti jagatyasjin puṇyacandanapādapāḥ |
chedanirgharṣadāhe'pi ye parārtheṣu nirvyathāḥ || k2.1 ||
gaṇanīyā guṇagaṇairastyariṣṭābhidhā purī |
spardhayā śakranagarī yasyā na syādgarīyasī || k2.2 ||
tasyāṃ babhūva bhūpālaḥ śrīsena iti viśrutaḥ |
samagraguṇaratnānāṃ ratnākara ivākaraḥ || k2.3 ||
paropakāraśaktasya caturasya prabhāvataḥ |
anuraktā diśaḥ sarvāḥ sūryasyeva prabhāvataḥ || k2.4 ||
yaśobhi śobhitaṃ yena dhanadānasugandhibhiḥ |
gajaiśca bhūtidhavalairjagatkalpadrumairiva || k2.5 ||
kalālayo'pi saralaḥ saralo'pi mahāmatiḥ |
yo babhūva prajāpuṇyairmatimānapyavañcakaḥ || k2.6 ||
yāvattapati tigmāṃśuryāvadvahati mārutaḥ |
tāvadājñā ca kīrtiśca yasyāpratihatābhavat || k2.7 ||
śamavyāyāmaviduṣāṃ ṣasṅguṇajñānacakṣuṣām |
yaṃ dvādaśasahasrāṇi mantriṇāṃ paryupāsate || k2.8 ||
tasmindharmapare rājñi babhūva sukṛtī janaḥ |
bhartṛtulyā bhavantyeva guṇaiḥ striya iva prajāḥ || k2.9 ||
tasya puṇyādhivāsena janāstridivagāminah |
vimānaiḥ śakranagarīṃ niḥsaṃcārāḥ pravakrire || k2.10 ||
dṛṣṭvā manujalokena suralokasamāvṛtim |
jātavairaḥ kṣitiopatau śatakraturacintayat || k2.11 ||
seneva lakṣmī vasudheṣu cārasyāścaryakartavya ca dattanityam |
kalyāṇaśīlena ca sa rvacetanyapravyahāsāmana asmakāśca (?) || k2.12 ||
tasyākhaṇḍitacetasaḥ daddhidvalyānubhāvapām (?) |
kartavyā dhairyājijñāsā mayā māyāvidhāyinā || k2.13 ||
iti saṃcintya suciraṃ sarvairanugataḥ suraiḥ |
rūpaṃ śakraḥ parāvartya martyalokamavātarat || k2.14 ||
atrāntare prajākāryaparyālocanatatparaḥ |
rājyarakṣāgururmantrī nṛpamūce mahāmatiḥ || k2.15 ||
rājan virajasā rājyarājamānena nirjitaḥ |
nirvyājadānairbhavatā lajjate tridaśeśvaraḥ || k2.16 ||
parasya pūrṇaguṇatāmātmanastadvihīnatām |
dṛṣṭvā ko nāma nāyāti mātsaryasya vidheyatām || k2.17 ||
īrṣyālavaḥ parotkarṣasaṃgharṣasya juṣo janāḥ |
prāyeṇodvegamāyānti mahatāṃ sukṛteṣvapi || k2.18 ||
sarvasvadānamaryādādānavyasanino'stu te |
putradārātmadāne tu saṃkalpo hyatisāhasaḥ || k2.19 ||
dṛśyante dāruṇāste te svapnāḥ sādhvasahetavaḥ |
jagataḥ sūcyate tīvraṃ yaiścūḍāmaṇikhaṇḍanam || k2.20 ||
daivajñānāṃ pravādaśca śrūyate tattvavādinām |
śarīraṃ pṛthivīpālo dāsyatīti suduḥsahaḥ || k2.21 ||
śarīradānaṃ sarvārthisārthanaiṣphalyakāraṇam |
sarvaprado bhavatyeva tiṣṭhan kalpamahīruhaḥ || k2.22 ||
tasmādasmānmahīpāla virama tyāgasāhasāt |
rakṣāratnaṃ hi jagataḥ prajāyattaṃ vapustava || k2.23 ||
iti mantrireṇoktamākarṇya vasudhādhipaḥ |
tamūce sattvadhavalasmitaghautādharadyutiḥ || k2.24 ||
uktaṃ hitaṃ mhāmātya bhavatā sacivocitam |
kiṃ tvarthinavaimukhyasaṃtāpaṃ nāhamutsahe || k2.25 ||
dehīti vādiṣu giro niṣedhaparuṣākṣarāḥ |
sphuranti vadane yeṣāṃ sajīvāste gatāsavaḥ || k2.26 ||
idamasmādavāpsyāmītyādhāya hṛdi yācakaḥ |
prāpyaḥ prayāti vaimukhyaṃ yasmin kiṃ tena jīvatā || k2.27 ||
dhigjanma puṇyahīnasya tasya niṣkaruṇātmanaḥ |
yasyārtajanasaṃtāpaśravaṇe śītalaṃ manaḥ || k2.28 ||
etadarthamayaṃ kāyaḥ sāpāyo'pi satāṃ priyaḥ |
yatkasyacitkkacidyāti kadācidupakāritām || k2.29 ||
śrutveti nṛpatervākyamamātyaḥ sattvaśālinaḥ |
novāca kiṃcidacalāṃ vicintya bhavitasyatām || k2.30 ||
tataḥ kadācidbhūbhartustasya līlāvihāriṇaḥ |
jāyāṃ jayaprabhāṃ nāma ratiṃ ratipateriva || k2.31 ||
dūrādyadṛcchayāyātāṃ cittasāsaṅgavāgurām |
muniradhyāpakaḥ kāntāṃ dadarśa vinimeṣadṛk || k2.32 ||
prāgjanmābhyāsasaṃbandhasnehātparicitāmiva |
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā sa dhṛteḥ prāpa dhairyarāśiranīśatām || k2.33 ||
tasya vītaspṛhasyāpi vāsanollasitaṃ manaḥ |
utsṛjya bhavavaimukhyamabhilāṣabhuvaṃ yayau || k2.34 ||
iyaṃ hi satatasyūtā saṃtataprītitantubhiḥ |
nāpaiti sarvajantūnāṃ prāgjanmābhyāsavāsanā || k2.35 ||
tadāśramapadaṃ prāptaḥ samāptādhyayanavrataḥ |
śiṣyo māṇavakaḥ prāha dakṣiṇā gṛhyatāmiti || k2.36 ||
sa tamūce na me vatsa vane vṛttiḥ prayojanam |
tathāpi yadi nirbandhaḥ śrūyatāṃ yadabhīpsitam || k2.37 ||
śrīsenasya kṣitipateyadi devī jayaprabhā |
labhyate bhavatā dātuṃ tadasau mama dakṣiṇā || k2.38 ||
ityuktaṃ guruṇā śrutvā śiṣyaḥ kampitamānasaḥ |
aśakyaprārthanālābhe saṃśayākulito'bhavat || k2.39 ||
sa gatvā satatasvecchāvivṛtadvāramarthinām |
viveśa svairaviśrambhabhavanaṃ bhūbhṛtāṃ prabhoḥ || k2.40 ||
alabhyārthārthanādainyacintātikliṣṭamānasaḥ |
namrānano'tivailakṣyādvīkṣamāṇa iva kṣitim || k2.41 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvārthinamāyātaṃ prahṛṣṭo'bhūnmahīpatiḥ |
sudhāpradānasannaddhasamudbhūtirivāmbudhiḥ || k2.42 ||
kiṃ tavepsitamityuktvā pūjitaḥ sa mahībhujā |
uvācānucitākhyānavailajjaskhalitākṣaraḥ || k2.43 ||
anarthitaparaḥ pūrvamarthikalpatarostava |
garvarthamarthitāṃ yātaḥ sudurlabhapade'pyaham || k2.44 ||
mama vidyāvrate pūrṇe dakṣiṇābhimatā guroḥ |
rājan jayaprabhā devī dīyatā yadi śakyate || k2.45 ||
ityukre muniśiṣyeṇa sahasaiva mahīpateḥ |
snehadānasāviddhaṃ dvidhābhūtamabhūnmanaḥ || k2.46 ||
sa jagādvijaṃ dantajyotiṣāgravisāriṇā |
gṛhyatāṃ dayitā svacchavāsasācchādayanniva || k2.47 ||
avicārya mayā deyamīpsitaṃ tava yadguroḥ |
viyogacakitaṃ cetaḥ satyaṃ na gaṇayāmyaham || k2.48 ||
ityuktvāhūya dayitāṃ rājā rājīvalocanām |
sadā hṛdayasaṃsaktāṃ jīvavṛttimivāparām || k2.49 ||
nivārito'pi guruṇā viyogavyasanāgninā |
niṣiddho'pyativṛddhena snehena smarabandhunā || k2.50 ||
pradadau muniśiṣyāya sahasā hariṇīmiva |
kimetaditi sāyāsasaṃtrāsataralekṣaṇām || k2.51 ||
dattāyāṃ tyāgaśīlena priyāyāṃ pṛthivībhujā |
cakampe tyāgabhīteva bhūmirlolābdhimekhalā || k2.52 ||
yatkṛte durdaśāṃ dehe sehire duḥsahāmapi |
indracandrādayo devāstāhpriyāḥ kasya na priyāḥ || k2.53 ||
śīlaṃ keciddhanaṃ keciddharmaṃ kecittapaḥ pare |
lajjāṃ kecittanuṃ kecittyajanti yoṣitāṃ kṛte || k2.54 ||
yadeva rāgasarvasvaṃ puṃsāṃ jīvitajīvitam |
tadeva sphīṭasattvānāṃ dāne tṛṇalavāyate || k2.55 ||
tāmādāya gate tasmin virahākulito nṛpaḥ |
viraheṇa sukhadveṣī manobhava ivābhavat || k2.56 ||
śiṣyeṇa munirānītāṃ dṛṣṭvā bhūpativallabhām |
rahitāṃ jīviteneva paralokabhuvaṃ gatām || k2.57 ||
gāḍhānuśayasaṃtaptaḥ paraṃ lajjānimīlitaḥ |
acintayadanaucityamātmanaḥ karmavuplavāt || k2.58 ||
aho nu bālakeneva mayā kevalacāpalāt |
niḥśankamayaśaḥpaṅke svayamātmā nipatitaḥ || k2.59 ||
iyaṃ prajānāṃ jananī bharmyāṇāṃ dharmakāriṇā |
varṇāśramagurorjāyāṃ mayā duḥkhānale'rpitā || k2.60 ||
kiṃ tu nākalitaṃ śīlaṃ na smṛtaḥ saṃyamo mayā |
gaṇitaṃ naiva vairāgyaṃ viveko nāvalokitaḥ || k2.61 ||
aho'tra nirvicārāṇāṃ sanmārgavimukhaṃ manaḥ |
asaṃyamāsavakṣībamapatheṣveva dhāvati || k2.62 ||
iti saṃcitya sa munirvailakṣyakṣapitadyutiḥ |
abhyetya rājadayitāmuvāca vinatānanaḥ || k2.63 ||
samāśvasihi he mātarna śokaṃ kartukarhasi |
bhavitavyatayaivāyaṃ kleśaste durnayaśca me || k2.64 ||
tyaktvā hi śramasaṃtāpamasya tīrataroradhaḥ |
adhunaiva nijaṃ dhāma sahāsmābhirgamiṣyasi || k2.65 ||
ityukte muninā devī sīktevāmṛtavṛṣṭibhiḥ |
avāptajīvitadhṛtistatyāja bhayasaṃbhramam || k2.66 ||
śrutvaitattridivavyāpi dātuścaritamadbhutam |
rājñaḥ sattvadayāṃ jñātuṃ vāsavaḥ samupāyayau || k2.67 ||
bhakṣitādhaḥśarīrārdho vyāghreṇa vijane vane |
putraiścaturbhirākrandairgṛhīto brāḥmaṇākṛtiḥ || k2.68 ||
prasravadbhūrirūdhiro lambamānāntramaṇḍalah |
kṛccheṣvapagataprāṇaḥ pāpairiva dṛḍhīkṛtaḥ || k2.69 ||
pratyagrāmiṣyāgandhena kravyādairbhṛśamanvitaḥ |
lubdhapārthivacaurotthairanarthairarthavāniva || k2.70 ||
nagarāntaramāsādya sa yayau puravāsinām |
kāruṇyadainyaduḥkhārto smayāvismayahetutām || k2.71 ||
sa śoka iva sākāraḥ sa trāsa iva duḥsahaḥ |
vidadhe sādhvasāyāsaṃ sahasā paurayoṣitām || k2.72 ||
so'rthisaṃdarśanasthānasthitasyātha mahīpateḥ |
putrarūpaiścaturbhistairnyasto'gre mañcikārpitah || k2.73 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā vaiśasāveśaviṣamakleśavihvalam |
niṣkūṇitānanavano jano'bhūnmīlitekṣaṇah || k2.74 ||
sa kampavihvalaṃ vakṣo muktamudyamya dakṣiṇam |
bhujaṃ jagāda bhūpālaṃ vyathāśithilitākṣaraḥ || k2.75 ||
svasti tubhyaṃ mahīpate brāhmaṇo'hamimāṃ daśām |
tīvrapāpa iva prātaḥ paśya māṃ karuṇānidhe || k2.76 ||
saṃsāreghoragahane vane vyāghreṇa bhakṣitaḥ |
jīvāmyavaśyabhogyatvādduḥkhasyāsya garīyasaḥ || k2.77 ||
asminnapi vipattāpe tīvrakleśasahiṣṇavaḥ |
vimuñcanti na māṃ prāṇāḥ sahṛdaḥ sajjanā iva || k2.78 ||
dadāti yadi te kaścitchittvā dehārdhamātmanaḥ |
tatte jīvitamastīti māmūce vyomadevatā || k2.| 79 ||
ko dadāti jagatyasmin jīvitaṃ karuṇānidhe |
prāyeṇa svasukhānveṣī parārthavimukho janaḥ || k2.80 ||
sarvadā sarvadātāraṃ dīnavyasanabāndhavam |
dehadāne'pyavimukhaṃ tvāmasmi śaraṇaṃ gataḥ || k2.81 ||
ekastvameva loke'smin jātaḥ sukṛtapādapaḥ |
nirvyājamādarodāraṃ dānaṃ yasya phalodgatiḥ || k2.82 ||
kimanyairvā vadānyasya kīrtitairbhavato guṇaiḥ |
dānamevāhato yasya loke sukṛtaḍiṇḍimaḥ || k2.83 ||
āpannārtiparitrāṇapavitracaritavratāḥ |
prāyante puṇyapaṇyena vipatkāle bhavadvidhāḥ || k2.84 ||
amandānandasuhṛdo haricandanaśītalāḥ |
haranti santaḥ saṃtāpaṃ dakṣiṇāḥ pavanā iva || k2.85 ||
pūrṇendusundarādasmāduditā vadanāttava |
jyoptsneva jīvayatyeva vāṇī pīyūṣavarṣiṇī || k2.86 ||
ityuktastena sahasā hṛdi saṃkrāntatadvyathaḥ |
saṃmohamūrcchitaṃ rājā tamūce vācamākulam || k2.87 ||
samāśvasihi muñca tvaṃ bahyaṃ prāṇaviyogajam |
prayacchāmi śarīrārdhamavicāryaiva te dvija || k2.88 ||
dhanyasyayātyayaṃ kāyaḥ paropakṛtaye kṣatim |
kṣaṇakṣayī hi deho'yaṃ rakṣyamāṇo'pi nākṣayaḥ || k2.89 ||
ityuktavati bhūpāle samutkampitamānasaḥ |
vajrāhata ivovāca mahāmātyo mahāmatiḥ || k2.90 ||
aho nu sāhasābhyāsādāyāsavyasanī prabhuḥ |
hitaṃ na gaṇayatyeva prajāpuṇyaparikṣayāt || k2.91 ||
prajānāṃ bhūtaye śaktaḥ ko'nyastvatsadṛśo guṇī |
yadbhaktimukharo bhṛtyaḥ śrotā kartā ca bhūpatiḥ || k2.92 ||
na karoti hitaṃ svāmī gajalīlānimīlitaḥ |
gaṇanīyāḥ subhṛtyānāmiyatyo bhogasaṃpadaḥ || k2.93 ||
bhānti te suciraṃ karṇe yaiḥ kṛtā madhumañjarī |
kalyāṇakarṇikākīrṇā vāṇī vinayavādinām || k2.94 ||
rākṣaso'yaṃ piśāco ca chadmanā brāhmaṇākṛtiḥ |
rakṣāratnasya jagatāṃ śarīrairarthitāṃ gatah || k2.95 ||
yadi nāma na māyeyaṃ kṛtā tena mahīyasī |
tatkathaṃ kṛttadehasya kṣaṇamapyasti jīvitam || k2.96 ||
avicāryaiva sukṛtaṃ kriyate durgraheṇa yat |
tadātmapīḍāoparuṣaṃ paraloke'pi niḥsukham || k2.97 ||
śakyameva sadā dadyādaśakyaṃ dīyate katham |
sarvasvadehadānādipravāda eva śobhanaḥ || k2.98 ||
karṇāmṛtamidaṃ dūrādyanmahārthimaṇipradaḥ |
saṃprāptānāṃ punastatra pānamasyānyato'rthinām || k2.99 ||
rakṣyaḥ sarvaprayatnena pareṣāmapi jīvitaiḥ |
prajānāṃ jīvitaṃ rājannarthicintāmaṇirbhavān || k2.100 ||
prasīda dayasvāsmāsu deva mā sāhasaṃ kṛthāḥ |
nakācaśakalasyārthe kriyate cātmavikrayaḥ || k2.101 ||
ityuktvā pādayoḥ patyuḥ papātāmātyapuṃgavaḥ |
śarīradānasaṃkalpānnoccacāla ca bhūpatiḥ || k2.102 ||
so'vadatpraṇayasmeracikasaddaśanadyutiḥ |
jīvitasnehasaṃmohatamahpariharanniva || k2.103 ||
kevalaṃ bhaktisaṃyuktamuktaṃ vyaktamidaṃ tvayā |
na sahe'haṃ mahāmātya viprasya prāṇasaṃśayam || k2.104 ||
hāraistuṣāraiḥ kamalairmṛṇālairinducandanaiḥ |
nivartate'ntaḥsaṃtāpo nārthivaimukhyaduḥkhajaḥ || k2.105 ||
sarvathā sarvaduḥkhārtiharaṇodyatacetasaḥ |
na bodherantarāyaṃ me sumate kartumarhasi || k2.106 ||
janmāntare'pi dadato dehaṃ me na vyathābhavat |
smarāmyatītavṛttasya samyaksaṃbodhicetasā || k2.107 ||
purā dṛṣṭvodyatāṃ vyāghrīṃ kṣutkṣāmāṃ potabhakṣaṇe |
tadrakṣāyai mayā dattaṃ śarīramavikṣariṇā || k2.108 ||
śibijanmani cāndhāya dattaṃ netrayugaṃ mayā |
rakṣitaśca svadehena kapotaḥ śyenakādbhayāt || k2.109 ||
candraprabhāvatāre ca raudrākṣāyārpitaṃ śiraḥ |
sarvasvaputradārādi dattaṃ cānyeṣu janmasu || k2.110 ||
ityukte bodhisattvenabhūbhujāmātyapuṃgavaḥ |
na sajīvo nanirjīva ivābhūdvyathitendriyaḥ || k2.111 ||
alaṅghyaśāsanenātha rājñā krakacadhārayā |
niyuktau palagaṇḍākhyau śarīracchedakarmaṇi || k2.112 ||
tau tīvraśokavivaśau śakramāyāvimohitau |
kathaṃcidiva bhūbharturdehacchede samudyatau || k2.113 ||
nṛpaternirvikārasya krūrakrakacadhārayā |
vidāryamāṇe dehārdhe pṛthivī samakampata || k2.114 ||
bhraṣṭolkā raktavasanā virghātacyutatārakā |
dyauḥ saśabdaṃ rurodeva kīrṇāśrukaṇasaṃtatiḥ || k2.115 ||
vaiśasālokanedbhūtatīvraduḥkhāsahiṣṇunā |
tūrṇaṃ rajaḥpaṭeneva raviṇā pihitaṃ mukham || k2.116 ||
tasmin prajāhprajānāthe krakacākrāntavigrahe |
cakranduḥ pūritākrandā digvadhūmiḥ pratisvanaiḥ || k2.117 ||
akṣubdhasattvamālokya nṛpaṃ śakro dvihākṛtiḥ |
vismayānuśayākrāntacittaściramacintayat || k2.118 ||
aho mahāmaterasya karuṇākomalaṃ manaḥ |
prāptaṃ parārthapīḍāsu vajrādapi kaṭhoratām || k2.119 ||
sāgarādapi gambhīraṃ merorapi samunnatam |
tridivādapi sāścaryamaho vṛttaṃ mahātmanām || k2.120 ||
ahi prāṇapravāse'pi sattvaṃ sattvamahodadheḥ |
sādhoriva vipatpāte mahattvaṃ nāvahīyate || k2.121 ||
iti cintayati kṣipraṃ sahasrākṣe kṣitiprabhoḥ |
nābheradhaḥśarīrārdhaṃ nikṛttamapatatkṣitau || k2.122 ||
sa dvidhābhūtadehoapi harṣotsāhamayo'bhavat |
sarvabhūtaparitrāṇasattvena dhṛtajīvitaḥ || k2.123 ||
tadājñayā śarīrārdhe śleṣṭe saṃpūrṇavigrahaḥ |
svasthakṣatiḥ samutthāya brāḥmaṇastamabhāṣata || k2.124 ||
aho virājase rājan pūnjaṃ virajasastava |
nirvyājadehadānena viśeṣaṃ tu bnhavadyaśaḥ || k2.125 ||
tvanmanomaṇivaimalyatulyaṃ kiṃcidakurvataḥ |
upamānena dāridyramaho mugdhasya vedhasaḥ || k2.126 ||
kṛttaḥ suvṛttaḥ saralaḥ parārthe madhurāśayaḥ |
sahase duḥsahāṃ pīḍāmikṣukāṇḍa ivonnataḥ || k2.127 ||
ityuktvā brāhmaṇākāraḥ śakrasta smṛtijanmabhiḥ |
saṃjīvanaiṣadhijātaiḥ sudhāsyandairapūrayat || k2.128 ||
tataḥ prakaṭitākāraḥ paritoṣātpuraṃdaraḥ |
suśliṣṭanijadehārdhaṃ praśaśaṃsa mahīpatim || k2.129 ||
athābmarānnipatitaḥ sitakusumasaṃcayaḥ |
tatkālollāsitakṣoṇīharṣahāsa ivābabhau || k2.130 ||
atrāntare munistasmau priyāṃ jāyāṃ jayaprabhām |
ādāyābhyetya tadvṛttaṃ jātāścaryo nyavedayat || k2.131 ||
pūtayā saṃgataḥ patnayā svakīrtyeva viśuddhayā |
uvāca pṛṣṭaḥ śakreṇa nikāre nirvikāritah || k2.132 ||
tataḥ siṃhāsane divyae viśvakarmavinirmite |
ratnavarṣasamākīrṇe jambudvīpe sa bhūpatiḥ || k2.133 ||
abhiṣiktaḥ surendreṇa prasādya dayitāsakhaḥ |
dānapuṇyaprabhāvotthakuśalavyāpitaprajaḥ || k2.134 ||
samāptasattvasaṃsārasaṃtāraṇakṛtavratah |
samyaksaṃbodhisaṃbuddhamanāḥ pramudito'bhavat || k2.135 ||
maitraṃ cetastaruṇakaruṇaṃ sattvasiddhaṃ viśuddhamāpannārtipraśamanaphalasphūītamātmapradānam |
dṛṣṭvā rājñahpramudasalilakṣālitākṣo vilakṣaḥ
śakraḥ prāyādamaranagarīṃ pūritāṃ tadyaśobhiḥ || k2.136 ||
iti sa vibudhavṛndaiḥ siddhayakṣoragendraiḥ pulakaruciravarcairarcyamānaprabhāvaḥ |
avanimavanaśaktaḥ kalpayannākakalpāmabahvavibhavaśobhāmāptavān bodhisattvaḥ || k2.137 ||
pūrvāvatārasaṃvāde dānotkarṣamudāharan |
upadeśāya bhikṣūṇāmityāha bhagavān jinah || k2.138 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ śrīsenāvadānaṃ nāma dvitīyaḥ pallavaḥ ||


3. maṇicūḍāvadānam |

asminnadbhutasarge makarākarajāyamānamaṇīvarte |
ko'pi prakaṭitasugatiḥ puruṣamaṇirjāyate (bhavyaḥ) || k3.1 ||
asti saubhiprabhāpūrakarpūraparipāṇḍuram |
sāketaṃ nāma nagaraṃ saubhāgyatilakaṃ bhuvaḥ || k3.2 ||
sevyaiḥ prabhāsattvamayairgaṅgāvimalamānasaiḥ |
tīrthairiva sthitaṃ yatra pavitraḥ puṇyakartṛbhiḥ || k3.3 ||
yaśaḥkusumite yatra puṇyasaurabhanirbhare |
ramante sukṛtodyāne nandane puravāsinaḥ || k3.4 ||
tatrābhūdbhūpatirbhuriguṇaratnamahodadhiḥ |
bhīturyaśaḥśaśāṅasya mehacūḍa iti śrutaḥ || k3.5 ||
sadā sadāśrayārheṇa kalikālāpahāriṇā |
kṛtaḥ kṛtayugeneva yena dharmadharo janaḥ || k3.6 ||
mahīpatiḥ kṣamāśaktaḥ śrīvṛtaḥ karuṇārataḥ |
vallabho'bhūtprajānāṃ yaḥ prakhyāto vijitendriyaḥ || k3.7 ||
amarāsavasaṃpūrṇamahiṃsāsatradīkṣitaḥ |
dadau yaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ puṇyāmabhayadakṣiṇam || k3.8 ||
nirmado yaḥ prabhāve'pi vibhave'pi priyaṃvadaḥ |
kṣamāśīlaḥ prabhutve'pi yauvane'pi jitendriyaḥ || k3.9 ||
gambhīreṇonnatimatā śūreṇa śaśikāntinā |
satpakṣeṇa kṣitibhṛtā yenātanyata vismayaḥ || k3.10 ||
rājñastasyādvitīyasya babhūvābharaṇadvayam |
tyāgapūrṇaṃ ca kāruṇyaṃ tārūṇyaṃ sukṛtaśriyaḥ || k3.11 ||
padmākarasya tasyābhūddevī kāntimatī priyā |
prabhātaśrīriva sadā nirdoṣābhyudayotsavā || k3.12 ||
nītiḥ prabhuguṇevena tyāgena śrīrivojjvalā |
rarāja rājacandreṇa sā śīleneva cārutā || k3.13 ||
sadānandanavikhyātayaśaḥprasarayā tayā |
merūstridivalakṣmyeva babhau bhūmibhṛtāṃ varaḥ || k3.14 ||
kāle kalyāṇanilayaṃ bhartuḥ sā garbhamādadhe |
bhūtyai bhuvanapadmasya divākaramivāditiḥ || k3.15 ||
araṇiḥ pāvakeneva velevābdheḥ sudhāṃśunā |
brahmābjeneva govindanābhirgarbheṇa sā babhau || k3.16 ||
tasyā garbhānubhāvena dohadābhimataṃ nṛpaḥ |
dadau sarvārthisārthebhyo vāñchitābhyadhikaṃ vasu || k3.17 ||
punarbhūmibhujā pṛṣṭā dohadaṃ śubhagarbhiṇī |
sarasvatīva sā cakre svayaṃ saddharmadeśanām || k3.18 ||
pūrṇapuṇyamaṇirdharmanidhirvidhisamuddhṛtaḥ |
vupadvipuladuḥkhebhyaḥ sadā rakṣati rakṣitaḥ || k3.19 ||
kāntāradurgeṣu paricyutānāṃ tāpāturāṇāṃ paralokamārge |
snigdhaḥ pravṛddhaḥ phalapūritāśaḥ chāyātarurdharmasamo'sti nānyaḥ || k3.20 ||
ālokastimire vipadviṣamaṇiḥ pāte karālambanaṃ
yācñākalpatarurjagajjayarathaḥ pātheyamanthe pathi |
duḥkhavyādhimahauṣadhaṃ bhavabhayodbhāntāśayāśvāsanaṃ
tāpe candanakānanaṃ sthirasuhṛddharmaḥ satāṃ bāndhavaḥ || k3.21 ||
ityādi dharmadhavalaṃ śrutvā nṛpavadhūvacaḥ |
dharmaikaśaraṇaḥ śrīmān babhūva bhuvane janaḥ || k3.22 ||
tataḥ kālena saṃpūrṇaṃ dyaurivāmṛtadīdhitim |
asūta dārakaṃ devī jagattimiradārakam || k3.23 ||
ajāyatāsya sahajaścūḍālaṃkaraṇaṃ maṇiḥ |
prāgjanmāntarasaṃsakto viveka iva nirmalaḥ || k3.24 ||
sa babhau subhagastasya mūrdhni puṇyamayo maṇiḥ |
yasya prabhāprabhāveṇa yāminyo dinatāṃ yayuḥ || k3.25 ||
soṣṇīṣasya maṇestasya pīyūṣasyandibindivaḥ |
nayanti hematāṃ lohaṃ duritaṃ śamayanti ca || k3.26 ||
śiśorjātismarasyātha vacasā tasya bhūpatiḥ |
dadau hema sadārthibhyaḥ sarvaṃ maṇirasodbhavam || k3.27 ||
puṣparatnadhvajacchatrapatākāvyajanāṃśukaiḥ |
apūrayan puraṃ vyonmastasya janmani devatāḥ || k3.28 ||
suprakāśoditāśeṣavidyāvidyotitātmanaḥ |
maṇicūḍa iti khyātaṃ nāma tasyākaronnṛpaḥ || k3.29 ||
sa cakārāśayaṃ harṣapīyūṣocchalitaṃ pituḥ |
abhijātaḥ suto jātaḥ pārijāta ivodadheḥ || k3.30 ||
paulomīva jayanteṇa jananī pūjyajanmanā |
babhau tena kumāreṇa kumāreṇeva pārvatī || k3.31 ||
tataḥ kālena sukṛtasopānaiḥ pṛthivīpatau |
divyadhāmasamārūḍhe maṇicūḍo'bhavannṛpaḥ || k3.32 ||
arthicintāmaṇestasya dānena paripūrite |
loke puṇyasukhāloke nārto'bhūnna ca yācakaḥ || k3.33 ||
tasya bhadragirirnāma babhūvaṃ gajapuṃgavaḥ |
prabhorivānukāreṇa dānārdrakarapuṣkaraḥ || k3.34 ||
taṃ kadācinmahāsthānasthitaṃ bhuvananāyakam |
bhavabhūtiḥ samabhyāyādbhṛguvaṃśabhavo muniḥ || k3.35 ||
divyakānyāṃ samādāya lāvaṇyalalitānanām |
mūrtāmiva prabhālakṣmīmakṣīṇasya kṣapāpateḥ || k3.36 ||
kucayoravivekena rāgeṇa caraṇābjayoḥ |
netrayoścāpaleneva sā jagatyatilajjitā || k3.37 ||
tapaḥśriyeva sahitaṃ taṃ kanyānugataṃ munim |
aoūjayatprajānāthaḥ kṛtāsanaparigraham || k3.38 ||
kanyāpi nṛpamālokya dhīraṃ gambhīrasundaram |
parapīḍāsu kāruṇyānnyastacāpamiva smaram || k3.39 ||
cūḍāratnasya kīraṇairduritakṣayakāribhiḥ |
likhantaṃ kuṅkumeneva dikṣu rakṣākṣarāvalim || k3.40 ||
vikṣepakṣiptamarutā cāmareṇa virājitam |
socchāseneva sattvena jagatsaṃtāraṇaṃ vinā || k3.41 ||
ratnodāreṇa hāreṇa hṛdayagrahakāriṇā |
pātālavipadāṃ śāntyai śaiṣeṇeva niṣevitam || k3.42 ||
vahantaṃ mahatā doṣṇā kṣamāṃ cittena ca kṣamām |
prayayau sābhilāṣasya vismayasya vidheyatām || k3.43 ||
gṛhītvā munirutsaṅge kuraṅgataralekṣaṇām |
jīvanīṃ tāmanaṅgasya jagādjagatīpatim || k3.44 ||
uditena jagannetraśatapatravikāśinā |
bhavatā bhāti loko'yaṃ devena ca vivasvatā || k3.45 ||
aho nu tava nāstyeva vibhūtisulabhodbhavaḥ |
sādhoriva guṇadveṣaḥ saṃmohopacito madaḥ || k3.46 ||
lokanāthasya te lokakāruṇyapūrṇacetasaḥ |
rājanmaitrījuṣā kīrtiḥ sthirā pāramitā param || k3.47 ||
akhedasaralo dātā nirvyājasukṛto bhavān |
ata eva viśeṣeṇa mānanīyo manīṣiṇām || k3.48 ||
padmodarasamudbhūtā kanyā kamalalocanā |
homāvaśeṣapayasā vardhiteyaṃ mayāśrame || k3.49 ||
gṛhyatāmagramahiṣī patnītve bhavatā nṛpa |
viṣṇoḥ śrīriva yogyeyaṃ tavaiva puruṣottama || k3.50 ||
yajñapuṇyaphalaṃ pūrṇaṃ kālena mama dāsyasi |
ityuktvā vidhinā rājñe kanyāṃ datvā yayau muniḥ || k3.51 ||
priyāṃ padmāvatīṃ rājā ratiṃ prāpyeva manmathaḥ |
araṃsta rucirodyāne sukṛte puṇyavāniva || k3.52 ||
tataḥ kālena sā putraṃ vaṃśavalliva mauktikam |
asūta padmacūḍākhyaṃ guṇānāṃ darpaṇaṃ pituḥ || k3.53 ||
śakrādibhirlokapālairanullaṅghyamahodayaḥ |
saṃstūyamānacaritaḥ svayaṃ kamalajanmanā || k3.54 ||
yaśaḥsaurabhasaṃbhārasaṃpūritadigantaraḥ |
sarvārthisārthakalyāṇakalanākalpapādapaḥ || k3.55 ||
smṛtvā munervacaḥ kāle kartuṃ vipuladakṣiṇām |
ahiṃsāvasusaṃpūrṇāmājahāra mahīpatiḥ || k3.56 ||
tasmin yajñe samājagmuḥ sarvakāmairanargale |
munayo bhārgavamukhā nṛpā duṣprasahādayaḥ || k3.57 ||
vartamāne makhe tasminniḥsaṃkhyavasuvarṣiṇi |
rakṣorūpaḥ samuttasthau vahnimadhyātsureśvaraḥ || k3.58 ||
upasṛtya sa bhūpālaṃ kṛśo vikṛtavigrahaḥ |
kṣuptipāsārdito'smīti yayāce pānabhojanam || k3.59 ||
śāsanādatha bhūbhartustasmai vividhabhojanam |
upaninyuḥ paricitāḥ pānaṃ ca paricārakāḥ || k3.60 ||
tataḥ kiṃcidvihasyaiva kṣitipaṃ prāha rākṣasaḥ |
nedamasmatpriyaṃ rājan vayaṃ hi piśitāśanāḥ || k3.61 ||
sadyohatasya māṃsena rudhireṇa ca bhūyasā |
tṛptirutpadyate'smākaṃ dīyatāṃ yadabhīpsitam || k3.62 ||
sarvakāmaprado'sīti tvamahaṃ samupāgataḥ |
dadāmīti pratiśrutya na niṣedhastavocitaḥ || k3.63 ||
iti rakṣavacaḥ śrutvā karuṇakulito nṛpaḥ |
ahiṃsāniyamenābhūdarthivaimukhyaduḥkhitaḥ || k3.64 ||
so'cintayattadā daivājjāto'yaṃ dharmasaṃśayaḥ |
na sahe duḥsahāṃ hiṃsāṃ naṃ naiṣphalyamarthinaḥ || k3.65 ||
na ca māṃsaṃ śarīrebhyo labhyate vaiśasaṃ vinā |
nāhaṃ pipīlakasyāpi kāyakleśalavaṃ sahe || k3.66 ||
datvāhaṃ sarvabhūtebhyaḥ puṇyāmabhayadakṣiṇam |
kathamasmai prayacchāmi māṃsaṃ prāṇivadhodbhavam || k3.67 ||
iti saṃcitya nṝpatistamūce karuṇākulaḥ |
svaśarīrasamutkṛttamasṛṅbhāṃsaṃ dadāmi te || k3.68 ||
ityukte bhūmipatinā babhūvākulitaṃ jagat |
na ca dehavyayotsāhaṃ sacivāstasaya sehire || k3.69 ||
praṇayādvāryamāṇo'pi bhūpālairmunibhistathā |
dadau svadehamutkṛtya tasmai māṃsamasṛgvasām || k3.70 ||
ākaṇṭhaṃ pītaraktena rākṣasena kṣitiprabhoḥ |
bhakṣyamāṇeṣu māṃseṣu kṣḥṇaṃ khṣitirakampataḥ || k3.71 ||
tataḥ padmāvatī devī patiṃ dṛṣṭvā tathāgatam |
vilapantī nipatitā mohamūrcchākulābhavat || k3.72 ||
manujendrasya devendrastaddṛṣṭvā sattvamūrjitam |
rakṣorūpaṃ parityajya tamuvāca kṛtāñjaliḥ || k3.73 ||
aho nu karmaṇā rājan duṣkareṇa tavāmunā |
romāñcakañcukākīrṇaḥ kāyaḥ kasya na jāyate || k3.74 ||
aho puṇyamasāmānyamaho sattvamanuttaram |
aho dhariyamamaryādaṃ rājān virajasastava || k3.75 ||
duḥkhitāḥ paraduḥkheṣu nirlobhā durlabheṣu ca |
vipakṣeṣu kṣamāvantaḥ santaḥ sukṛtasetavaḥ || k3.76 ||
samunmiṣati ko'pyeṣa sattvotsāho mahātmanām |
trailokyaṃ karuṇārdrāṇāṃ yena yātyanukampyatām || k3.77 ||
uktveti divyauṣadhibhistaṃ kṛtvā svasthavigraham |
prasādya lajjāvanataḥ śakraḥ svanilayaṃ yayau || k3.78 ||
tataḥ samāpte vidhivadyajñe rājñāṃ mahīpatiḥ |
cakre munivarāṇāṃ ca pūjāṃ tridaśapūjitaḥ || k3.79 ||
sa ratnavarṣairyajñānte kanyāgrāmapurapradaḥ |
sahitaṃ tridaśārheṇa hariṇā hemamālinā || k3.80 ||
dadaru rājagajaṃ brahmarathākhyāya purodhase |
yojanānāṃ śataṃ tūrṇamekenāhnā prayāti yaḥ || k3.81 ||
tasmai samarpitaṃ dṛṣṭvā rājñā bnhadragiriṃ gajam |
abhūdduṣprasaho rājā tatspṛhākṛṣṭamānasaḥ || k3.82 ||
prayāteṣvatha bhūpeṣu vismiteṣu makhaśriyā |
samarpite yajñaphale bhārgavāya mahībhujā || k3.83 ||
tamuvāca samabhyetya svastivādapuraḥsaram |
marīciśiṣyo vāhīkaḥ prāptapūjāsano munoḥ || k3.84 ||
rājannadhyayanasyānte gururme gurudakṣiṇām |
īhate paricaryārthī sāmānyajanadurlabhām || k3.85 ||
ekastvameva vidhinā nirmito durlabhapradaḥ |
bahavo jātu jāyante na loke kalpapādapāḥ || k3.86 ||
devī padmāvatī putrasahitā gurave mama |
tapaḥkṛśāya vṛddhāya dīyatāṃ paricārikā || k3.87 ||
ityukte muninā rājā dayitāviprayogajām |
rujaṃ saṃstabhya manasa tamūce dhairyabhūdharaḥ || k3.88 ||
prayacchāmi mune tubhyamīpsītāṃ gurudakṣiṇām |
sahitāṃ yuvajārena jīvitābhyadhikāṃ priyām || k3.89 ||
ityuktvā sasutāṃ tasmai dadau padmāvatīṃ nṛpaḥ |
svajīvite vinisnehastyāgaḥ sattvamayātmanām || k3.90 ||
ādāya rājadayitāṃ virahakleśakātarām |
saputrāmāśramaṃ gatvā pradadau gurave muniḥ || k3.91 ||
atrāntare duṣprasahaḥ kururājaḥ kṣitīśvaram |
dṛpto yayāce dūtena bhūtyai bhadragiriṃ gajam || k3.92 ||
purohitārpitaṃ rājā na dadau dviradaṃ yadā |
tadā vipulasaunyena svayaṃ yoddhuṃ samāyayau || k3.93 ||
balinā kururājena ruddheṣu puravatmasu |
babhūva bhūmipālasya sainyaṃ raṇarasodbhaṭam || k3.94 ||
sa vīrakuñjarahariḥ śakto'pyarividāraṇe |
janakṣayabhayodvignaḥ kāruṇyātsamacintayat || k3.95 ||
aho'nukūlamitraṃ me rājā duṣprasahaḥ param |
mātaṅgalobhamohena sahasā śatrutāṃ gataḥ || k3.96 ||
snehāntāḥ sujanaiḥ snehā niḥsnehāntāśca madhyamaiḥ |
durjanairghoravairāntā bhavanti prāṇahāriṇaḥ || k3.97 ||
aho bibhavalibhena kṣaṇakṣayiṇi jīvite |
samudyamo'yamasmākaṃ paraprāṇanipātane || k3.98 ||
hiṃsayāpapraśāntānāṃ saktānāṃ kalikarmasu |
raṇaraktābhiṣiktānāṃ bhaktārtho'yaṃ samudyamaḥ || k3.99 ||
sevāvikrītajīvānāṃ caṇḍapiṇḍārthināmayam |
kalaho duḥsahaḥ kruryapiśunānāṃ śunāmiva || k3.100 ||
aho vibhavalubdhānāṃ parasaṃtāpaśītalāḥ |
svasukhāyaiva dhāvanti nṛśaṃsacaritā dhiyaḥ || k3.101 ||
ye yudhi siddhisaṃnaddhā raktāntāṃ bhuñjate śriyam |
kutaḥ kruratare teṣāṃ hṛdaye karuṇākaṇaḥ || k3.102 ||
eṣa duṣprahaso rājā lubdho vibahvamohitaḥ |
na vadhyaḥ sāparādho'pi kāruṇyāyatanaṃ mama || k3.103 ||
iti cintayatastasya kāruṇyāta kānanaiṣiṇaḥ |
pratyekabuddhāścatvāraḥ svayaṃ vyomnā samāyayuḥ || k3.104 ||
prātapūjāsanāḥ śrutvā sarvajñāstatsamīhitam |
rājñaḥ praśamaśīlasya prasannāstattvamūcire || k3.105 ||
saṃmohapaṭalāndheṣu saṃsāriṣu dayālutā |
śobhate tava bhūpāla sattvalokavivekinaḥ || k3.106 ||
kriyatāmīpsitaṃ rājan bodhau buddhirnidhīyatām |
saṃprati pratirodhe'smin vanameva vigāhyatām || k3.107 ||
svairanirjnarajnaṅkārakīrṇasaṃtoṣaśīkarāḥ |
viviktakānanoddeśāḥ śamināmeva vallabhāḥ || k3.108 ||
ityuktvānugrahadhiyā vidhāyāsya viyadgatim |
prabhāprasādhitadiśaste tena sahitā yayuḥ || k3.109 ||
yāteṣu svapadaṃ teṣu himavattaṭakānanam |
saṃprāpya pṛthivīpālaḥ prayatapraśamo'bhavat || k3.110 ||
vivekavimalāstasya dhiyaḥ sattvavatāmiva |
babhuḥ priyanivuḥ pūrṇanirvāṇyo (?)vanabhūmayaḥ || k3.111 ||
bhūdharāntarite tasmin sahasā bhūpabhāsvati |
śuśucurmohatimiraprāptaśokaprajāḥ prajāḥ || k3.112 ||
tatastatsacivā jagmurmarīcaṃ munimāśrame |
śaktaṃ rājyasya rakṣāyai rājaputraiṃ yayācire || k3.113 ||
muninā nirvikāreṇa dattamādāya mantriṇaḥ |
rājasūnuṃ svanagare cakruḥ sainyasamudyamam || k3.114 ||
muninā nirvikāreka dattamādāya mantriṇaḥ |
subhaṭāgresarahprāpa kururājaṃ raṇājire || k3.115 ||
sa tena hatavidhvastabhagnasyandnakuñjarah |
palāyanaparitrāṇaḥ prayayauḥ hastināpuram || k3.116 ||
balinā rājaputreṇa jite duṣprasahe yudhi |
mantribhistadbhujanyastā bhūmiḥ śeṣadhṛtiṃ yayau || k3.117 ||
rājño duṣprasahasyātha kālena kaluṣātmanah |
babhūvāvṛṣṭidurbhikṣamarakopalpavaḥ pure || k3.118 ||
sa vicintyānutāpārtastībrāṃ janapadāpadam |
na viveda paritrāṇaṃ viphalasvastikakriyaḥ || k3.119 ||
pṛṣṭhā vipatpratīkāraṃ tenāmātyāstamūcire |
duḥsaho'yaṃ mahārāja prajānāṃ vyasanodbhavaḥ || k3.120 ||
maṇicūḍasya bhūbharturyadi cūḍāmaṇiḥ prabho |
labhyate sa sudhāsyandī teneyaṃ tīryate vipat || k3.121 ||
cārebhyaḥ śrutamasmābhiḥ sa rājā himavattaṭe |
sthitaḥ saṃsāravaimukhyavivekavimalāśayaḥ || k3.122 ||
arthitaḥ sa dadātyeva viśvacintāmaṇirmaṇim |
putradāraśarīrādi nādeyaṃ tasya kiṃcana || k3.123 ||
iti mantrivacaḥ śrutvā tathetyādhārya cetasi |
sa dvijānmaṇiyācñāyau visasarja tadantikam || k3.124 ||
asminnavasare rājā maṇicūṣaścaran vane |
marīcerāśramopāntamavāpa vipulaṃ muneḥ || k3.125 ||
devī padmāvatī tatra phalamūladhṛtavratā |
vrajantī vijane bhītā vipune muniśāsanāt || k3.126 ||
śabarairmṛgayāyātairdṛṣṭvā kaṣṭadaśāṃ śritā |
jughṛkṣubhiḥ kampamānācukrośa karuṇasvaram || k3.127 ||
ākarṇya karuṇākrande kurarūīkūjitopamam |
hā rājanmaṇicūḍeti trāyasveti suduḥsaham || k3.128 ||
sahasābhidrutaḥ kāntāṃ dadarśa nṛpatirnijām |
rāhusaṃtrāsitasyendordyutiṃ nipatitāmiva || k3.129 ||
vītarāgāṅgavasanāṃ nirañjanaparigrahām |
vadantīmiva saṃbhogasaṃyogānāmanityatām || k3.130 ||
tāṃ rājahaṃsasugatāṃ vihārastanamaṇḍalām |
aśrukāṣāyanayanāṃ vilokya karuṇāvanīm || k3.131 ||
saṃsāracaritāścaryavicāreṣvapi karkaśam |
kṛpākṛpāṇīnirlūnamivāsīrbhūpatermanaḥ || k3.132 ||
ekākīnaṃ vane devī vigatacchatracāmaram |
dṛṣṭvā nāthamanāthaiva lokanāthaṃ tathāgatam || k3.133 ||
tadviyogaviṣākrāntā taddarśanarasākulā |
śokaharṣasamākīrṇā babhūva bhṛśavihvalā || k3.134 ||
sā nītā śabarā rājñā śāpabhītāḥ pradurdruvuḥ |
na nāmābhyudaye bhānordṛṣṭaṃ sapratibhaṃ tamah || k3.135 ||
atrāntare śamadveṣī sarvabhūtāśayāśayaḥ |
māraḥ puruṣarūpeṇa sametya nṝpamabravīt || k3.136 ||
rājan rājīvanayanāṃ priyāṃ praṇayiṇīmimām |
na tyakrumarhasyajane vane vanajalocana || k3.137 ||
iyaṃ hi te manovṛttiriva niḥsukhatāṃ gatā |
varjitā rājyabhogena rājarāja na rājate || k3.138 ||
etadārkarṇya nṛpatistaṃ vijñāya manobhavam |
antarāyaṃ vivekasya pratyabhāṣata sasmitaḥ || k3.139 ||
jānāmi tvāmahaṃ kāmamakāmaṃ śamasaṃyame |
saṃtoṣavatāṃ ko nāma bhavatā na vimohitaḥ || k3.140 ||
itivādini bhūpāle sahasāntarite smare |
babhūva viklavā devī taptā virahavahninā || k3.141 ||
duḥkhitāṃ tāmārtaduḥkhāṃ patibhogaviyoginīm |
uvācāśvāsayan rājā jāyāṃ jitamanobhavaḥ || k3.142 ||
devi dharmakriyāyuktā na śokaṃ kartumarhasi |
duḥkhāvasānī virasaḥ sarvo'yaṃ bhogacibhramaḥ || k3.143 ||
dehināṃ yaditāsaṅgāstaraṅgataralāyuṣām |
lolapadmapalāśāgraskhalajjalalavākulāḥ || k3.144 ||
imā muhūrtanartakyaḥ kālameghataḍillatāḥ |
saṃsārasarparasanā vilāsacapalāḥ śriyāḥ || k3.145 ||
bhogakṣaṇenaiva viyogarogo vibhūtayaḥ svapnavivāhatulyāḥ |
vātāhatā dīpaśikhā sukhaśrīrunmattanṝtyaṃ bhavavṛttametat || k3.146 ||
sarvopajīvyā karuṇā na lakṣmīḥ dharmaḥ prakāśaḥ satataṃ na dīpāḥ |
yaśāṃsi ramyāṇi na yauvanāni sthirāṇi puṇyāni na jīvitāni || k3.147 ||
satyavratastāmiti sāntvayitvā visṛjya jāyāṃ nilaye maharṣeḥ |
cacāra saṃsāraparāṅbhukhānāṃ saṃtoṣapuṇyeṣu tapovaneṣu || k3.148 ||
tadāgatāste tvarayā visṛṣṭāḥ pañca dvijā duṣprasahena rājñā |
tamarthināmakamakālabandhuṃ viśuddhasattvaṃ dadṛśurvanānte || k3.149 ||
te svastivādaṃ śanakaurvidhāya viśastadhairyā iva sādhvasena |
tamūcire sūcitatīvratapāḥ dīrghoṣṇaniśvāsasamīraṇena || k3.150 ||
rājan pure duṣprasahasya rājñaḥ krūropasargaurhataśāntavargaḥ |
janaḥ kṛtaḥ kṛttasamastakāmaḥ prakāmamārtasvanamātraśeṣaḥ || k3.151 ||
aśoṣadoṣapraśamaikahetuḥ trailokyarakṣāprathitaprabhāvaḥ |
cūḍāmaṇirdeva bhavadvitīrṇaḥ karoti tasyopanipātaśāntim || k3.152 ||
dayāyuṣaścandanapallavārdrāḥ svacchāśayāścandramaṇiprakāśāḥ |
saṃtāpakāle śaraṇaṃ janānāṃ bhavadvidhā eva bhave bhavanti || k3.153 ||
ityarthitastairaviluptasattvaḥ saṃpūryamāṇaḥ karuṇārasena |
uvāca saṃcintya janopatātaṃ saṃkrāntamantaḥ śrutivartmaneva || k3.154 ||
aho sa rājā sahate kathaṃ nu devopaghātena nipīḍitānām |
vidāritāntaḥkaraṇaṃ prajānāṃ viyogaḥduḥkhodbhavamārtanādam || k3.155 ||
ayaṃ maṇirmastakamūlajanmā niṣkṛttya tūrṇaṃ pratigṛhṛtāṃ me |
dhanyo'smi yadyarthijanasya duḥkhakṣaye kṣaṇaṃ kāraṇatāṃ vrajāmi || k3.156 ||
ityuktamātre vasudhādhipena dharādharāmbhodhimahīdharitrī |
ciraṃ cakampe cakiteva tasya śirastaṭotpāṭanatīvraduḥkhāt || k3.157 ||
tataḥ kṛpākomalacittavṛtteḥ sutīkṣṇaśastrairvacasā nṛpasya |
sutīkṣṇaśastrādapi tīkṣṇacittaḥ svayaṃ śiraḥ pāṭayituṃ pravṛttaḥ || k3.158 ||
tadduṣkaraṃ karma nareśvarasya vyomni vimānairnalināsanādyāḥ |
surāḥ savidyādharasiddhasākhyāḥ samāyayudraṣṭumaluptasattvam || k3.159 ||
vipāṭyamāne śirasi prasahya ratnaprabhāvibhramamādadhānaiḥ |
sa raktapūrairabhikṣiktakāyaḥ sehe vyathāmarthisukhe pravṛttaḥ || k3.160 ||
vilpkya taṃ sattva nibaddhadhairyaṃ tīvravyathāveganimīlitākṣam |
yayurvirāmaṃ na nṛśaṃsavṛtterviprāḥ kṣaṇaṃ rākṣasatāmavāptāḥ || k3.161 ||
vicārya rājā śvaśarīraduḥkhaṃ saṃsāriṇāṃ kleśamayaṃ śarīram |
evaṃvidhairduḥkhasahasralakṣairākrāntamityārtataro babhūva || k3.162 ||
so'cintayaddehanibaddharatnadānena yatpuṇyphalaṃ mayāptam |
tenograduḥkhaṃ kalayāmi mā bhūdapuṇyapāke narake narāṇam || k3.163 ||
samuddhṛte rakravasavasikte tasminmaṇau niścalatālumūlāt |
mūrcchākulo'pi prayayau saharṣaṃ saṃpūraṇenārthimanorathasya || k3.164 ||
sa kampamānāṅgulipallavena datvā svahastena maṇiṃ dvijebhyaḥ |
nimīlayan samtamasena lokaṃ papāta tigmāṃśurivātiraktaḥ || k3.165 ||
aluptasattve patite pṛthivyāṃ tasmin surāṇāṃ saha puṣpavarṣaiḥ |
maṇiṃ samādāya yayurdvijāste tūrṇaṃ puraṃ duṣprasahasya rājñaḥ || k3.166 ||
sa tenasadyaḥ śamitopasargaḥ svargocitāsāditabhogavargaḥ |
tadbodhisattvasya samastasattvasaṃtāraṇārhaṃ praśaśaṃsa sattvam || k3.167 ||
atrāntare kiṃcidavāptasaṃjñaṃ nareśvaraṃ viśrutaratnadānam |
samāyayurbhārgavagautamādyā marīcimukhyā munayo vanebhyaḥ || k3.168 ||
marīcimevānugatā ca devī padmāvatī vīkṣya parikṣataṃ tam |
saṃmohavegābhihatā papāta kṣaṇaṃ kṣitau bālalateva lūnā || k3.169 ||
digantasaṃcāriṇī cāraṇānāṃ nabhaścarāṇāṃ nṛpasādhuvāde |
sarājaputrāḥ saha mantrimukhyaiḥ prajāḥ prajānāthamathopajagmuḥ || k3.170 ||
vīkṣyaḥ kṣitīśaṃ kṣatajokṣitāṅgamakṣīṇasattvaṃ patitaṃ pṛthivyām |
pṛthavyathākleśajuṣaṃ janānāmabhūdabhūtārthavikalpajalpaḥ || k3.171 ||
kuṭhārikaiḥ kaiścidaho dayārdraḥ sarvārthisevyaḥ saralaḥ suvṛttaḥ |
durātmabhiḥ svārthalavābhiyuktaiḥ chāyātaruḥ kaṣṭamayaḥ nikṛttaḥ || k3.172 ||
aho parārthojjhitajīvito'yaṃ parāṃ camatkāradaśāṃ prayātaḥ |
sasaurabhacchinnatanurgatātmā bhavatyudāraḥ sahakāra eva || k3.173 ||
lubdhasya na svaḥ svajano'pi jantoḥ na kāmakāmasya dhane'nurodhaḥ |
sarvātmanā sattvahitodyatasya deho'pi na snehapadaṃ dayāloḥ || k3.174 ||
yeṣāṃ kṛte dainyamayaṃ prayāti sarvātmanā cārthijano'rthibhāvam |
ta eva dīnoddharaṇavratānāṃ prāṇāḥ paritrāṇapaṇe tṛṇāni || k3.175 ||
iti pravāde vividhānubhāve vijṛmbhamāṇe munimaṇḍalasya |
bhūpālamabhyetya sabāṣpacakṣurmunirmarīciḥ praṇayāduvāca || k3.176 ||
aho nu niṣkāraṇabandhubhāvamālambya rājan dayayā janasya |
prajāparitrāṇavidhānabhūmistanustaveyaṃ tṛṇavadvitīrṇā || k3.177 ||
kṣayaḥ pravṛtto nirapekṣavṛttestavārthibandhornijajīvite'pi |
yadeṣa kāmaṃ kamalānikāyaḥ kāyastvayāpāyapade niyuktaḥ || k3.178 ||
apyasti rājan sukṛtavrate'smin phalaspṛhā prāṇapaṇe'pi kācit |
asthārthihetostava pālubhedakhedādvikāraṃ bhajate na cetaḥ || k3.179 ||
ityadbhutāviṣkṛtamānasena munīndramadhye muninā sa pṛṣṭaḥ |
uvāca saṃstabhya rujaṃ prayatnādraktābhiṣiktaṃ vadanaṃ pramṛjya || k3.180 ||
phalaspṛhā nāsti mune mamānyā kiṃ tveka eva pracuro'bhilāṣaḥ |
yaddhorasaṃsāranimagnajantusaṃtāraṇāyaiva bhave bhaveyam || k3.181 ||
arthipriye dehavidāraṇe'sminnaivāsti me ko'pi vikāraleśaḥ |
yadyeṣa satyaḥ samayo mayoktastadastu me svasthamidaṃ śarīram || k3.182 ||
ityuktamātre sahajānubhāve sattvocite sa tyadhanena rājñā |
abhūdvapuḥ satyabalena tasya rūḍhavraṇaṃ tatkṣaṇajātaratnam || k3.183 ||
tataḥ suraiḥ śakraviriñcimukhyairnātapraharṣairmunibhiśca sarvaiḥ |
abhyarthito'pi kṣitipālanāya bhogābhilāṣī na babhūva bhūpaḥ || k3.184 ||
avāptasaṃjñā muninā prayuktā padmāvatī rājasutena sārdham |
patiṃ yayāve virahopaśāntyai siṃhāsanākrāntisukhaṃ prajānām || k3.185 ||
tatastamabhyetya kṛpākulāste pratyekabuddhā jagato hitāya |
dehaprabhāpūritadigvibhāgā babhāṣire harṣamivodgirantaḥ || k3.186 ||
cirādavāpte virahāvasāne punaḥ parityāgadaśāmasahyām |
na rājaputraḥ sahate na devī duḥkhānubandho hyasamṛnnipātaḥ || k3.187 ||
svamarthine yaḥ radadāti dehamāpannaduḥkhapraśamaikahetuḥ |
kathaṃ sa kuryātsvajane'pyupekṣāṃ dharmo'pyayaṃ yasya parārtha eva || k3.188 ||
ityuktamākarṇya nareśvarastaistatheti niścitya dhiyā kathaṃcit |
vyomnā vimānaiḥ svapurīmavāpya bheje nijaṃ rājyapadaṃ saputraḥ || k3.189 ||
iti sa vipulasattvaḥ satyavān bodhisattvaḥ suciravihitarājyaḥ saugataṃ dhāma bheje |
jinapuramaṇicaityacchatraratnapradīpaprakaṭitavividhaśrīrlakṣaṇābhyastabodhiḥ || k3.190 ||
ityāha bhagavān buddhaḥ svavṝttāntanidarśane |
dānopadeśe bhikṣūṇāṃ samyaksaṃbodhisiddhaye || k3.191 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ maṇicūḍāvadānaṃ nāma tṛtīyaḥ pallavaḥ ||


4. māndhātravadānam |

śobhante bhuvaneṣu bhavyamanasāṃ yannākakāntākara- prauḍhodañcitacārucāmarasitacchatrasmitāḥ saṃpadaḥ |
yaccotsarpati tarpitaśruti yaśaḥ karpūrapūrojjvalaṃ
svalpaṃ dānakaṇasya tatphalamaho dānaṃ nidānaṃ śriyaḥ || k4.1 ||
abhūdupoṣadho nāma bhūbhṛdhyasya vibhāvataḥ |
vibudhābhimatā kīrtiḥ sudhā dugdhodadheriva || k4.2 ||
vasudhāmavato yasya vasudhāmavataḥ puraḥ |
nanāma praṇatau kasya na nāma nṛpateḥ śiraḥ || k4.3 ||
śuddhā dhīriva dharmeṇa dāneneva dayālutā |
vibhūtirvinayeneva bhūṣitā yena bhūrabhūt || k4.4 ||
guṇinaḥ prāṃśuvaṃśasya babhūvendudyuteḥ sthitiḥ |
yasya sarvātapatrasya mūrdhni sarvamahībhṛtām || k4.5 ||
yasyeśvaraśiraḥsthāyi śubhaṃ gaṅgājalojjvalam |
bhramatyadabhraṃ lokeṣu bhuvanābharaṇaṃ yaśaḥ || k4.6 ||
kartu kratusahasrāṇāṃ sahasrākṣādhikaśriyaḥ |
yasya ṣaṣṭisahasrāṇi kalatraṃ sudṛśāmabhūt || k4.7 ||
kadācinmunirakṣāyai rakṣaḥkṣayakṛtakṣaṇaḥ |
vicacārāśvamāruhya sa taponanabhūmiṣu || k4.8 ||
tatra rājarṣibhiḥ kaiścitputreṣṭikalaśaṃ dhṛtam |
dūrādhvaśramasaṃtaptaḥ sa payaḥpūrṇamāpapau || k4.9 ||
vijanāsāditaṃ pītvā sa mantrakalaśātpayaḥ |
rājadhānīṃ samāsādya garbhaṃ lebhe vibhurbhuvaḥ || k4.10 ||
svapnamāyendrajālādi yasyāḥ kautukavipraṣuḥ |
jayatyadbhutasaṃbhārabhūmiḥ sā bhavitavyatā || k4.11 ||
vidhervividhavaicitryacitrakarmavidhāyinaḥ |
āścaryarekhāvinyāsaṃ kaḥ paricchettumīśvaraḥ || k4.12 ||
kālena tasya mūrdhānaṃ bhittvā bāloṃ'śumāniva |
rūḍhavraṇasya sahasā divyadyutirajāyata || k4.13 ||
taṃ rājajāyā jagṛhurjagatsāmrājyalakṣaṇam |
vātsalyaprasrutakṣīrāḥ puṇyaṃmūrtimivāśritam || k4.14 ||
māṃ dhārayiṣyati śiśuḥ ślāghyo'yaṃ jananīpade |
iti tāsāṃ mitho vākyairmāndhātābhūnnṛpātmajaḥ || k4.15 ||
tasya pravardhamānasya bālakrīḍāvilāsinaḥ |
ṣaḍindraḥ prayayau kālaḥ puṇyakrīḍakṣayāyuṣaḥ || k4.16 ||
navayauvanamārūḍhaḥ sarvavidyāsu pāragaḥ |
sa yāte pitari svargaṃ bheje rājyaṃ kramāgatam || k4.17 ||
yakṣo divaukaso nāma sukṛterdāsatāṃ gataḥ |
abhiṣekopakaraṇaṃ divyaṃ tasyopanītavān || k4.18 ||
sa svarṇairbaddhamukuṭaḥ kalpitoṣṇīṣaśekharaḥ |
śradabhrāvataṃsasya meroḥ śobhāmavāptavān || k4.19 ||
sapta ratnāni tasyātha prādurbhūtāni takṣaṇe |
cakrāśvamaṇihastiśrīgṛhasenāgragāṇyapi || k4.20 ||
babhuva cāsya putrāṇāṃ tulyarūpabalaujasām |
sahasraṃ vijitārāterbhujānāmiva bhūbhujaḥ || k4.21 ||
vasuṃdharāṃ samastābdhuivelākalitamekhalām |
nikhilāṃ vidadhe doṣṇi śeṣaviśrāntirnirvṛtām || k4.22 ||
bhuvanatrāṇasaṃsaddhahpratyagrakamalāśrayaḥ |
cakravartī sa sukṛterviṣṇoḥ kara ivābabhau || k4.23 ||
trijagajjāhnavī kīrtiḥ prabhāvābharaṇāḥ śriyaḥ |
so'yaṃ sukṝtavallīnāṃ prathamaṃ kusumodgamaḥ || k4.24 ||
sa kadāciddvanānteṣu vikāśikusumaśriyaḥ |
ruciraṃ sacivaiḥ sārdhaṃ vicacāra vilokayan || k4.25 ||
dadarśa tatra niṣpakṣān vihagān pādacāriṇaḥ |
vyomamārgagatiṃ smṛtvā prayātān kṛśatāmiva || k4.26 ||
pakṣahīnānagatikān vṛttikṣīṇānnirambarān |
daridrāniva tān vīkṣya provāca kṛpayā nṛpaḥ || k4.27 ||
aho varākaurvihagaiḥ kimetaiḥ kukṛtaṃ kṛtam |
yadete pakṣavikalāḥ kṛcchracaraṇacāriṇaḥ || k4.28 ||
ityukte bhūmipatinā karuṇākulitātmanā |
puraḥsthito mahāmātyaḥ satyasenastamabravīt || k4.29 ||
śrutametanmayā deva kathyamānaṃ vanecaraiḥ |
kāraṇaṃ pakṣapatane yadabhūtpakṣiṇāmiha || k4.30 ||
santi pañca śatānyatra puṇyadhāmni tapovane |
tapaḥsvādhyāyasaktānāṃ munīnāṃ dīptatejasām || k4.31 ||
teṣāmadyayanadhyānajapavighnavidhāyinaḥ |
ete lokāhalaṃ cakruḥ khagāstarūvane sadā || k4.32 ||
tasmai vihagasaṃghāya karṇāpāyakṛte param |
atisaṃvardhamānāya cukopa munimaṇḍalam || k4.33 ||
tadurbhūtamahāśāpatāpalpoṣeṇa sarvataḥ |
kṣaṇena pakṣiṇāṃ pakṣā vyaśīryanta kṛtāgasām || k4.34 ||
ta ete vihagāḥ pakṣarahitāḥ kṛcchravartinaḥ |
tvadvipakṣā iva vane śrāntāśracaraṇacāriṇaḥ || k4.35 ||
mahāmātyena kathitaṃ niśmaitanmahīpatiḥ |
uvāca karuṇākrāntastaptaḥ śāpena pakṣiṇām || k4.36 ||
aho tejaḥ pariṇataṃ śāntānāmapi kānane |
aṅgārāṇāṃ munīnāṃ ca dahatyevānivāritam || k4.37 ||
mithyātapasvibhiḥ kiṃ taiḥ svasukhāya na yaiḥ kṛtaḥ |
manasaḥ kopataptasya pariṣekaḥ kṣamāmbubhiḥ || k4.38 ||
prasannā dhīrmaṇo maitraṃ dayā dānaṃ damahkṣamā |
yeṣāṃ teṣāṃ tapaḥ ślādhyaṃ śeṣāṇāṃ kāyaśoṣaṇam || k4.39 ||
kiṃ tapobhiḥ sakopānāṃ vilputānāṃ vanena kim |
vibhavaiḥ kiṃ salobhānāṃ durvṛttānāṃ śrutena kim || k4.40 ||
evaṃ kaluṣacittāste tībramanyuparāyaṇāḥ |
duḥsahā eva munayaḥ prayāntu viṣayānmama || k4.41 ||
ityuktvā prāḥiṇottebhyaḥ saṃdeśaṃ puruṣairnṛpaḥ |
yāvatī madvaśā bhūmistāvatī tyajyatāmiti || k4.42 ||
vihaṃgapakṣapātena kupitasya mahīpateḥ |
saṃdeśaṃ munayaḥ śrutvā vilakṣāḥ samacintayan || k4.43 ||
catuḥsamudraparikhāmekhalāyāḥ kṣiteḥ patiḥ |
narendro'yaṃ kkva gacchāmaḥ ko deśo'sya vaśe na yaḥ || k4.44 ||
iti saṃcintyaṃ munayaḥ pārśvaṃ kanakabhūbhṛtaḥ |
surasiddhasamākīrṇaṃ jambūkhaṇḍāntikaṃ yayuḥ || k4.45 ||
atha tasya mahībhartuḥ prabḥāveṇa mahīyasā |
abhūdadṛṣṭaśasyā bhūdyāśca ratnāmbaraprasūḥ || k4.46 ||
pākaśāsanavailakṣyakaraṇāstasya śāsanāt |
saptāhaṃ hema vavṛṣurmeghāḥ saṃgḥātavarṣiṇaḥ || k4.47 ||
sa prabhāveṇa mahatā saha sainyairnabhogatiḥ |
cakre pūrvavidehākhyaṃ dvīpaṃ divyajanaṃ vaśe || k4.48 ||
babhūvuragre saunyāni sphīṭaśauryabalaujasām |
bhaṭānāṃ vyomagamane tasyāṣṭādaśakoṭayaḥ || k4.49 ||
godānīyaṃ tato dvīpamathottarakurūnapi |
pārśvāni sa sumerośca śaśāsāsataśāsanaḥ || k4.50 ||
sukhaṃ viharatastasya meroḥ kanakasānuṣu |
bahuśakro yayau kālaścaturdvīpamahīpateḥ || k4.51 ||
sa kadācitsurān draṣṭuṃ vyāmnā gaccan suropamaḥ |
cakāra nīlajaladairvyāptā iva gajairdiśaḥ || k4.52 ||
atha teṣāṃ nirastānāṃ merupārśve tapasyatām |
munīnāmapatanmūrdhni tadgajāśvaśakṛddivaḥ || k4.53 ||
tataste krodhasaṃtaptadṛśā vyomāvalokinaḥ |
cakruḥ piṅgaparbhāvallikalāpakapilā diśaḥ || k4.54 ||
kopātkimetadityuktvā śāpāgnivisisṛkṣatā |
abhyetya devadūtastān praharṣākulito'vadat || k4.55 ||
eṣā niḥśeṣabhūpālamauliviśrāntaśāsanaḥ |
pākaśāsanatulyaśrīrmāndhātā pṛthivīpatiḥ || k4.56 ||
nabhasā naradevi'yaṃ saha sainyaiḥ prasarpati |
yasya kīrtanadhanyeyaṃ vāṇī puṇyābhimāninī || k4.57 ||
na dṛṣṭo yasya nirdiṣṭasarvalokasukhaśriyaḥ |
mohaḥ saṃbinmayasyeva vibhavaprabhavo madaḥ || k4.58 ||
kauberaṃ dhanadavyaktyā kaumāraṃ śaktimattayā |
aiśvaraṃ vṛṣasaṃyogādvaiṣṇavaṃ śrīsamāgamāt || k4.59 ||
pratāpaprasarātsauramaindavaṃ jananandanāt |
aindraṃ dṛptabalacchadāddivyaṃ rūpaṃ bibhartyayam || k4.60 ||
baliḥ prayātaḥ pātālaṃ dadhīco'pyasthiśeṣatām |
asya tyāgena jaladhiḥ kṣobhamadyāpi nojjhati || k4.61 ||
śrutveti devadūtasya vacanaṃ munimadhyagaḥ |
sasarja durmukho nāma muniḥ śāpajalaṃ divi || k4.62 ||
prahasannatha taṃ prāha senānāṃ pariṇāyakaḥ |
maharṣe saṃhara rūṣaṃ mā kṛthāstapasaḥ kṣayam || k4.63 ||
vaiphalyalajjāṃ śāpo'thaṃ yāsyatyagre mahīpateḥ |
naite bata khagā yeṣāṃ yūyaṃ pakṣakṣayakṣamāḥ || k4.64 ||
ityukte sauntyapatinā śāpastabdhāmanīkinīm |
dṛṣṭvāgre vismayādūce kimetaditi bhūpatiḥ || k4.65 ||
saṃrabdho'tha samabhyetya senāpatiruvāca tam |
teṣāṃ deva maharṣīṇāṃ śāpātsaimyaṃ na sarpati || k4.66 ||
idaṃ ca cakraratnaṃ te vyomni śāpavighūrṇitam |
dhatte jaladasaṃruddhatigmadīdhititulyatām || k4.67 ||
etadākarṇya nṛpatirdṛṣṭā cāgre tathaiva tat |
dṛśaiva divadhe śāpaṃ viphaloccaṇḍavilpavam || k4.68 ||
dehakṣayaṃ maharṣīṇāṃ parirakṣan kṛpākulaḥ |
jaṭā nyapātayadbhūmau sa līlālasaśāsanaḥ || k4.69 ||
ajitakrodhamohānāṃ bhārabhūtā vṛthā vayam |
itīva lajjayā teṣāṃ līnāḥ kṣititale jaṭāh || k4.70 ||
atha meruśiraḥ prāpya nṛpaḥ suraniketanam |
puraṃ sudarśanaṃ nāma dardarśa priyadarśanam || k4.71 ||
nāgāstatra kṛtārakṣāḥ prakhyātodakaniḥsṛtāḥ |
karoṭapāṇayo yakṣāḥ surā mālādharābhidhāḥ || k4.72 ||
sadāmattāstathā devāḥ krodhottambhitasainikāḥ |
mahārājakāyikākhyāstridaśā balavattarāḥ || k4.73 ||
mahārājāśca catvāraḥ saṃnaddhakavacāyudhāḥ |
jitvā rājñā prabhāveṇa nijasenāgragāhkṛtāḥ || k4.74 ||
tataḥ kalpadrumodārakocidāramanoharam |
dadarśa pārijātākhyaṃ saṃśrayaṃ tridivaukasām || k4.75 ||
merormūrdhni tataḥ śubhraprabhāṃ mālāmivāmalām |
sudharmākhyāṃ sabhāṃ prāpa svabhāsodbhāsitāmbarām || k4.76 ||
hemavidramavaidūryastambhasaṃbḥārabhāsvaraḥ |
prāsādo vaijayāntākhyaḥ prakhyāto yatra rājate || k4.77 ||
yatrābjervadanairbhṛṅgairalakaistulyatāṃ gatāḥ |
padminyaḥ suranārīṇāṃ padminīnāṃ surāṅganāḥ || k4.78 ||
bimbataistridaśairyatra maṇibhūstambhabhittiṣu |
suraloko bibhartyeko'pyanekasuralokatām || k4.79 ||
ratnatoraṇaharmyāṃnivahairyatra citritāḥ |
vyāptā vibhānti kakubhaḥ śakrāyudhaśatairiva || k4.80 ||
yatra bālānilālolakalpapādapapallavaiḥ |
nṛtyaddhastā ivābhānti nandinyo nandanaśriyaḥ || k4.81 ||
yatra caitrarathaṃ nāma devodyānaṃ manoramam |
dhatte nityotsavaṃ premikāmaṃ kāmavasantayoḥ || k4.82 ||
sarvakāmaṃ sarvasukhaṃ sarvartukusumojjvalam |
sarvātiśayitaṃ dṛṣṭvā devānāṃ sadanaṃ nṛpaḥ || k4.83 ||
muhūrtavisamyāspandasānandasnigdhalocanaḥ |
acnitayatsukṛtināmimāstāhphalabhūmayaḥ || k4.84 ||
airāvaṇaṃ surapaterlolāliovalayākulam |
dadarśa tatra sāmodaṃ sākāramiva nandanam || k4.85 ||
puraṃdarastato jñātvā prāptaṃ bhūmipuraṃdaram |
pratyudyayau pramuditaḥ saha sarvairmarudgaṇaiḥ || k4.86 ||
pūjitaḥ surarājena ratnarājivirājitām |
rājarājahsabhābhūmiṃ bheje virajasāṃ varah || k4.87 ||
tridaśeṣūpaviṣṭeṣu ratnaparyaṅkapaṅktiṣu |
upāviśannṛpaḥ śrīmānāsanārdhe śatakratoḥ || k4.88 ||
ekāsanajuṣostatra surendramanujendrayoḥ |
rūpaṃ guṇagaṇodāraṃ nirviśeṣamadṛśyata || k4.89 ||
tatahsarvasurotsṛṣṭaspaṣṭalocanaṣaṭpadaiḥ |
pīyamānamukhāmbhojaṃ vyājahāra harirnṛpam || k4.90 ||
aho udayaḥ ślādhyaste tejasā tejasāṃ nidhe |
bhavatā bhūṣitā bhūmirdyauśca devena bhāsvatā || k4.91 ||
abhyunnataprabhāvo'yaṃ lasatsitayaśoṃśukaḥ |
bhrājate te tribhuvane sāmrājyavijayadhvajaḥ || k4.92 ||
tvatkathāmṛtapānasya tvaddarśanarasasya ca |
preryate śrotranetreṇa sukhākhyāne sarasvatī || k4.93 ||
sthirīkṛtastvayaivāyaṃ sukṛtāptavibhūtinā |
karmaṇāṃ phalavādasya niścaraśchinnasaṃśayaḥ || k4.94 ||
ata evendriyagrāme cakṣureva spṛhāspadam |
puṇyaiḥ puṇyocitācārā dṛśyante yadbhavadvidhāḥ || k4.95 ||
ityukte tridaśendreṇa māndhātā yaśasāṃ nidhiḥ |
tvatprasādaprabhāvo'yamityuvāca natānanaḥ || k4.96 ||
ityevaṃ pūjyamānasya tasya nityādaraiḥ suraiḥ |
ṣaḍindraḥ praṇayau kālastridive vasataḥ sataḥ || k4.97 ||
tatparākramavidhvastasamastāsuramaṇḍalaḥ |
babhūva surarājasya nirapāyodayo jayaḥ || k4.98 ||
dīptadānavasaṃgrāme tasya śauryamahātaroḥ |
viśrāntiṃ bhejire devā bhujacchāyopajīvinaḥ || k4.99 ||
tasya puṇyapaṇakrītaṃ bhiñjānasyākṣayaṃ sukham |
kālapravāhe mahati prayayuḥ ṣaṭpuraṃdarāḥ || k4.100 ||
satkarmaphalabhogasya lāñchanaṃ vimalaṃ manaḥ |
kāluṣyājjāyate tasya pratyāsannaḥ parikṣayaḥ || k4.101 ||
atha kālena kāluṣyakalitasya manorathaḥ |
abhūllobhābhibhūtasya bhūtaperabhimāninaḥ || k4.102 ||
tridaśānāmiyaṃ lakṣmīrmadbāhubalapālitā |
tadimāṃ na sahe tāvadardhāsanaviḍambanām || k4.103 ||
ahamekaḥ surapatiḥ prabhāvānna bhavāmi kim |
ayaṃ mama bhujaḥ sarvajagadbhārabharakṣamaḥ || k4.104 ||
cyāvayitvā surāśīśaṃ svargasāmrājyasaṃpadam |
ekātapatratilakāṃ svayaṃgrāhocitāṃ bahje || k4.105 ||
iti cintayatastasya śakradrohābhilāṣiṇaḥ |
śubhraprabhā prabhāvaśrīrmāleva mlānatāṃ yayau || k4.106 ||
ghanodayasamutsiktā saujanyataṭapātinī |
lolaṃ kaluṣayatyeva mānasaṃ śrītaraṅgiṇī || k4.107 ||
pramādo vipadāṃ dūto duḥsaho mahatāmapi |
kuśalonmūlanāyaiva kilbiṣākulitā matiḥ || k4.108 ||
pāpasaṃkalpamātreṇa kṣitau kṣitipatiḥ kṣaṇāt |
papāta visrastaphalaśchinnamūla iva drumaḥ || k4.109 ||
hanti vidyāmanabhyāsaḥ śriyaṃ hanti madodayaḥ |
vidveṣaḥ sādhutāṃ hanti lobhaḥ samunnatim || k4.110 ||
aho bata mahotkarṣaśṛṅgāroho mahodayaḥ |
vibhavodbhavamattānāṃ sahasaiva patatyadhaḥ || k4.111 ||
tena sarvavibhurnāma pūjitaḥ pūrvajanmani |
tatphalādāptavān rājyaṃ spṛhaṇīyaṃ marūtpateḥ || k4.112 ||
surādhipādhikaḥ ko'pi prabhāvo vismayāvahaḥ |
analpapiṇḍastasyābhutpātradānāṃśasaṃbhavah || k4.113 ||
dbandhumatyabhidhānāyāṃ nagaryāmuṣitaḥ śuciḥ |
vaṇigutkariko nāma so'bhavatpūrvajanmani || k4.114 ||
vipaśyī nāma bhikṣāyai samyaksaṃbuddhāṃ gataḥ |
viveśa ta dgṛhaṃ sarvasattvasaṃtāraṇodyataḥ || k4.115 ||
pātre tasya ca cikṣepa mudgamuṣṭiṃ prasannadhīḥ |
phalāni tatra catvāri petuḥ śeṣāṇī bhūtale || k4.116 ||
tena dānaprabhāveṇa māndhātā pṛthivīpatiḥ |
sarvadvīpapatirbhūtvā śakrārdhāsanamāptavān || k4.117 ||
mudgaśeṣaścyuto yasmādbhūtau tasyānyacetasaḥ |
tadasau phalaparyante patitastridaśālayāt || k4.118 ||
luṭhati vikalakalpā yatra saṃkalpamālā
sphuranti na ca kadācitsvapnamāyāntare yā |
bhavati vibhavabhogābhoginī bhāgyabhājā-
matulaphalatatiḥ sā dānakalpadrumāṇām || k4.119 ||
ityāha bhagavān buddhaḥ prītyā dānaphalaśriyam |
nijajanmāntarākhyāne bhikṣūṇāmanuśāsane || k4.120 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ māndhātravadānaṃ nāma caturthaḥ pallavaḥ ||


5. candraprabhāvadānam |

dugdhābdhirvibudhārthanātividhuraḥ kṣubdhaścakampe ciraṃ
kampante ca nisargataḥ kila phalotsargeṣu kalpadrumāḥ |
ekaḥ ko'pi sa jāyate tanuśatairabhyastadānasthitir
niṣkampaḥ pulalotkaraṃ vahati yaḥ kāyaṃ pradāneṣvati || k5.1 ||
asti kaulāsahāsinyāmuttarasyāmanuttarā |
diśi bhadraśilā nāma bhuvanābharaṇaṃ purī || k5.2 ||
yasyāṃ sitayaśaḥpuṣpāḥ saphalāḥ sarvasaṃpadaḥ |
dānodyānalatāḥ prītyai babhūvuḥ puravāsinām || k5.3 ||
yatra tripurajinnetraśikhitrasto manobhavaḥ |
abalābhiścalakrīḍabhrūbhaṅgaireva rakṣyate || k5.4 ||
muktājālojjvalā yatrabhāti hemagṛhāvalī |
meroḥ śikharamāleva visphutatsphītatārakā || k5.5 ||
tasyāṃ candraprabhaḥ śrīmānabhūdbhūmibhṛtāṃ varaḥ |
kaulāsa iva yaḥ kāntyā cakāra dinacandrikām || k5.6 ||
yasya dehaprabhāpūraiḥ pūrṇindudyutihāribhiḥ |
niśāsu dīpakābhāsu nābhūtu snehaguṇakṣayaḥ || k5.7 ||
smarajvaraṃ bhajante'sya darśanenaiva tārakāḥ |
iti cchatrachalādasya chāditaṃ khamivendunā || k5.8 ||
pradiśatyeṣa satataṃ śriyaṃ satkośasaṃśrayām |
iti taddarśanenaiva saṃkocaṃ prāpa padminī || k5.9 ||
senāhaṃkāramutsṛjya tyāgaśubhraśriyā śriyaḥ |
nirdiṣṭāśchatramukuṭaprakaṭāḥ puravṣinām || k5.10 ||
śuśubhe vibhavastasya puṇyālaṃkāraṇonnateḥ |
ārohati parāṃ koṭīṃ namrasya dhanuṣo guṇaḥ || k5.11 ||
catvāriṃśatsahasrāṇi vatsarāṇāṃ śatāṇi ca |
babhūva dehināmāyustasya kāle kalidviṣaḥ || k5.12 ||
tasya ṣaṣṭisahasrāṇi purīṇāṃ pūrṇasaṃpadām |
babhūvurlokapālasya lokapālādhkāśriyaḥ || k5.13 ||
yajvānaḥ kīrtitilakāstasya puṇyavibhūṣaṇāḥ |
yajñadhūmalatābhaṅgairbabhurlolālakāḥ śriyaḥ || k5.14 ||
tasya saṃpatkumudinīvikāsena sadoditaḥ |
abhūnmantrī mahācandraścadraloka ivojjvalaḥ || k5.15 ||
yena niścalalakṣyeṇa prabhoḥ prajñāpatākayā |
rājyābdhikarṇadhāreṇa pāramuttaritaṃ yaśaḥ || k5.16 ||
mahīdharābhidhaścāsau babhūvāmātyakuñjaraḥ |
bhūmibhārasahastasya diṅgāga iva pañcamah || k5.17 ||
mantraṇābhinnamantrasya yasya nītibṛhaspateḥ |
tyājitāḥ pratisāmantāḥ śauryaṃ viṣamivāhayaḥ || k5.18 ||
tenāmātyena sa nṛpaḥ sa ca rājñā vibhūṣitaḥ |
guṇaḥ satpuruṣeṇeva guṇeneva ca sajjanaḥ || k5.19 ||
kṛtajñaḥ saralaḥ svāmī sadbhṛtyo bhaktinirbharaḥ |
sukṛtaprabhaveṇaiva bhāgyayogena labhyate || k5.20 ||
iyameva cirabhrāntiviśrāntiḥ sarvasaṃpadām |
yadguṇajñatayā vetti svāmisatpuruṣāntaram || k5.21 ||
tau kadaciddadṛśatuḥ svapnamanye ca mantriṇah |
kṣayo yasya phalaṃ dānavyasanena mahīpateḥ || k5.22 ||
tau dṛṣṭvā durnimittāni prādurbhūtāni śaṅkitau |
vyagrao babhūvaturnityaṃ śāntisvastikakarmasu || k5.23 ||
nimittadarśanodvignāstapivanagatā api |
viśvāmitraprabhṛtayaḥ svāmītyūcurmaharṣayaḥ || k5.24 ||
atrāṇtare brahmabandhuḥ prāgjanmabrahmarākṣasaḥ |
raudrākṣo nāma mātsaryakrauryadaurjanyaduḥsahaḥ || k5.25 ||
śrutvā dānodbhavāṃ kīrtiṃ rājñaḥ sarvaguṇojjvalām |
nirguṇaḥ sa guṇadveṣi saṃtaptaḥ samacintayat || k5.26 ||
aho batāsya nṛpatergīyate gagane yaśaḥ |
aniśaṃ siddhagandharvagīrvāṇalalanāgaṇaiḥ || k5.27 ||
sadā viśanti me karṇe tadguṇastutisūcayaḥ |
kiṃ karomi prakṛtyaiva sahe nānyaguṇonnatim || k5.28 ||
tadgatvā dānaśīlasya tasya dānārjitaṃ yaśaḥ |
karomyeṣa śiroyācñāpratiṣedhena khaṇḍitam || k5.29 ||
yaśastyājyate dānotthaṃ śiraścenna pradāsyati |
atha dāsyati vidveṣapraśāntirme bhaviṣyati || k5.30 ||
iti saṃcintya suciraṃ sa kauryakaṭhinaḥ śaṭhaḥ |
gandhamādanapādāṇtavāsī bhadraśilāṃ yayau || k5.31 ||
indrajālaprayogajñaḥ sa kṛtvā praśamocitam |
veṣaṃ kaluṣasaṃkalpaḥ purīṃ prāpa mahīpateḥ || k5.32 ||
asmin bhavavane nityaṃ guṇadoṣasamākule |
kalpavṛkṣāḥ prajāyante jāyante ca viṣadrumāḥ || k5.33 ||
aśeṣanāśapiśunairghorasaṃtrāsakāribhiḥ |
durnimittairiva khalaiḥ khedaḥ kasya na dīyate || k5.34 ||
guṇidveṣaḥ prakṛtyaiva prakāśaparipanthinaḥ |
doṣaāśrayasya ko bhedaḥ khalasya timirasya ca || k5.35 ||
svacchandaghātī sādhūnāṃ vidveṣaviṣaduḥsahaḥ |
dīrghapakṣaḥ khalavyālakarālaḥ kena nirmitaḥ || k5.36 ||
tasmin praviṣṭe nagaraṃ rūpiṇī puradevatā |
uvācābhyetya bhūpālaṃ saṃtrāsataralekṣaṇā || k5.37 ||
śiroyācaka eṣa tvāṃ brahmabandhurupāgataḥ |
vadhyo'sau jīvitocchedī jagato jīvitasya te || k5.38 ||
niruddho nagaradvāri sa mayā malināśayaḥ |
mama taddarśanatrastaṃ dhṛtiṃ na labhate manaḥ || k5.39 ||
iti bruvāṇāṃ bhūpālaḥ provāca puradevatām |
arthisaṃrodhasaṃjātalajjayā namitānanaḥ || k5.40 ||
devi yācñābhiyāto'sau praviśatvanivāritaḥ |
dirghocchvāsaṃ sahe nāhamāśāvaiphalyamarthinaḥ || k5.41 ||
yācñā praṇayināmarthe puṇyaprāptastanuvyayaḥ |
yugasaṃkhyāmapi sthitvā vipadyante hi dehinaḥ || k5.42 ||
etadeva sujātānāṃ pūjyaṃ jagati jīvitam |
yadeṣāmagrato yāti nārthī bhagnamanorathah || k5.43 ||
kriyatāmānukūlyaṃ me bhavatyā kuśalocitam |
āśāvighāte saṃtāpastasya tūrṇaṃ nivāratām || k5.44 ||
iti bhūmibhṛtaḥ śrutvā vaco niścalaniścayam |
jagāmādarśanaṃ devī cintāsaṃtāpamānasā || k5.45 ||
athāyayau sa kuṭilaḥ khalaḥ krakacaceṣṭitaḥ |
dāruṇaḥ saralasyaiva cchedāya svayamudyataḥ || k5.46 ||
tasminnṛpagṛhaṃ prāpte vivṛtadvāramarthinām |
bhūrbhūpatikṣayabhayāccakampe sadharādharā || k5.47 ||
narendracandramāsādya sa rāhurica durmukhaḥ |
samabhyadhādvidhāya prāgaśivārthāmivāśiṣam || k5.48 ||
svasti rājan dvijanmā hi vijane siddhisādhakaḥ |
prātastvāmīpsitaprāptyai sarvārthisurapādapam || k5.49 ||
dṛṣṭirvṛṣṭirivāmṛtasya mahatī saujanyamitraṃ manaḥ
kṣāntiḥ krodharajaḥpramārjanadī duḥkhārtamātā matiḥ |
lakṣmīrdānajalābhiṣekavimalā satyopayujktaṃ vacaḥ
nityaṃ yasya sa eka eva hi bhavān jāto jagadbāndhavaḥ || k5.50 ||
siddhaye kathitaṃ kaiściccakravartiśiro mama |
dīyatāṃ ta tvadanyo vā dātuṃ śaknoti kaḥ parah || k5.51 ||
santi spaṣṭārthadāścintāmaṇikalpadrumādayaḥ |
durlabhārthapradātāro viralāstu bhavadvidhāḥ || k5.52 ||
ityukte tena nṛpatirniṣkampavipulāśayaḥ |
arthisaṃdarśanānandanirbharastamabhāṣata || k5.53 ||
dhanyo'haṃ yasya me brahmannarthināmarthasiddhaye |
nirvikalpopakaraṇaṃ vyayaṃ yāti sujīvitam || k5.54 ||
kadā prāṇāḥ parārthe me prayāntīti manorathaḥ |
kimetāni na puṇyāni prārthyante te yadi tvayā || k5.55 ||
āhopakaraṇasiddhyai ślāghyaṃ me gṛhyatāṃ śiraḥ |
tattadeva sthiraṃ loke yadyadarthisamarpitam || k5.56 ||
ityukte harṣayuktena bhūbhujā sattvaśālinā |
tamūcaturmahāmātyau mahācandramahīdharau || k5.57 ||
nijajīvitarakṣaiva dharmaste prathamaḥ prabho |
tvayi jīvati jīvanti sarve jagati jantavaḥ || k5.58 ||
na dātumarhasi śiraḥ sarvādhāraṃ hi te vapuḥ |
dīyatāṃ brāhmaṇāyāsmai hemaratnamayaṃ śiraḥ || k5.59 ||
sarvārthairarthisārthānāṃ pūryante yairmanorathāḥ |
teṣāṃ saṃrakṣaṇenaiva sarvaṃ bhavati rakṣitam || k5.60 ||
saṃkalpo'yaṃ dvijasyāsya krūraḥ kaluṣacetasaḥ |
mūlacchedopajīvyo hi na kalpatarurarthinām || k5.61 ||
hemaratnaśiraḥ prāpya yātveṣa śirasāsya kim |
cintāmaṇirviniṣprekṣyo bhujyate na bubhukṣitaiḥ || k5.62 ||
ityukte mantrimukhyābhyāṃ hemaratnamayaṃ śiraḥ |
siddho naivopayogyaṃ tanmameti brāhmaṇo'bravīt || k5.63 ||
athonmumoca nṛpatirmukuṭaṃ mauktikāṃṣubhiḥ |
śrirovirahaduḥkhena sāśrudhāramivābhitaḥ || k5.64 ||
mukuṭāni kṣaṇe tasminnipetuḥ puravāsinām |
digdāhonmukhatulyābhirulkābhiḥ saha bhūtale || k5.65 ||
rājñā pradāne śirasaḥ sarvathā parikalpite |
tau cakratustanutyāgaṃ mantriṇau draṣṭumakṣamau || k5.66 ||
ratnagarbhamathodyānaṃ praviśya pṛthivīpatiḥ |
utphullacampakasyādhaḥ śiraśchettuṃ samudyayau || k5.67 ||
udyānadevatā dṛṣṭvā taṃ śiraśchettumudyatam |
mā kṛthāḥ sāhasaṃ rājannityuvāca śucākulā || k5.68 ||
kampamānāḥ pralāpinyastaṃ mattālukulasvanaiḥ |
nyavārayannavalatā lolapallavapāṇibhiḥ || k5.69 ||
so'pi niścalasaṃkalpaḥ prasādyodyānadevatām |
vimalāṃ bodhimālambya babhūva praṇidhānavān || k5.70 ||
asmin ratnamayodyāne puṇyarāśisamunnatam |
stūpamastu praśāstustu sattvasaṃtāraṇocitam || k5.71 ||
yatkiṃcidarjitaṃ puṇyaṃ saṃkalpena mayāmunā |
bhavantu tena saṃsāre niḥsaṃsārāḥ śarīriṇaḥ || k5.72 ||
dhyātveti campakataroḥ śākhāyāṃ nṛpatiḥ śiraḥ |
baddhvā kacakalāpena chittvā prādāddvijanmane || k5.73 ||
atha narapate sattvotsāhasphuṭapraṇidhānataḥ
kimapi vimalaiḥ puṇyālokairdigantavisāribhiḥ |
vigalitamahāmohaughāntaḥ śritaḥ parinirvṛtiṃ
praviratabhavābhyāsāyāsaḥ kṣaṇādabahvajjanah || k5.74 ||
iti prāgjanmavṛttāntakathayā bhagavān jinaḥ |
bhikṣūṇāṃ vidadhe śuddhadānasaddharmadeśanām || k5.75 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ candraprabhāvadānaṃ nāma pañcamaḥ pallavaḥ ||


6. badaradvīpayātrāvadānam |

dānodyatānāṃ pṛthuvīryabhājāṃ śuddhātmanāṃ sattvamahodadhīnām |
aho mahotsāhavatāṃ parārthe bhavantyacintyāni samāhitāni || k6.1 ||
harmyārohaṇahelayā yadacalāḥ svabhraiḥ sahābhraṃlihā
yadvā goṣpadalīlayā jalabharakṣobhoddhatāhsindhavaḥ |
laṅghyante bhavanasthalīkalanayā ye cāṭavīnāṃ taṭās
tadvīryasya mahātmanāṃ vilasataḥ sattvorjitaṃ sphūrjitam || k6.2 ||
purā hi bhagavān buddhaḥ śrāvastyāṃ puravāsinām |
upadeśaprakāśena jahārājñānajaṃ tamaḥ || k6.3 ||
bhikṣusaṃghaiḥ parivṝtaḥ sa kadācidvaṇigjanaiḥ |
kṛtānuyātro magadhātsvayaṃ cārikayā yayau || k6.4 ||
mahārthasārthānugataṃ vrajantaṃ vanavartmanā |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā taskaragaṇaḥ sālāṭavyāmacintayat || k6.5 ||
eṣa prayātu bahgavān puraḥ sattvahite rataḥ |
paścātsārthaṃ grahīṣyāmaḥ pūrṇaṃ draviṇarāśibhiḥ || k6.6 ||
bhagavānatha sarvajñasteṣāṃ jñātvā samīhitam |
kimetaditi tānūce nirvikārasmitānanaḥ || k6.7 ||
te tamūcuḥ parityajya krauryaṃ madhurayā girā |
tatprasādasmitālokairvinaṣṭatimirā iva || k6.8 ||
bhagavan jīvikāsmākaṃ nindyeyaṃ karmanirmitā |
na bhṛtirna kṛṣirnānyarakṣaṇaṃ na pratigrahah || k6.9 ||
sahajaṃ krauryamasmākaṃ nisargakaluṣātmanām |
kriyate kiṃ svabhāvasya deva tīkṣṇā hi karṇikā || k6.10 ||
tasmānna vṛttilopo naḥ kartumarhasi gamyatām |
yāte tu tvayi sārthasya vayaṃ sarvārthahāriṇah || k6.11 ||
iti teṣāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā karuṇāpūrṇamānasaḥ |
dolālolāyitamatirbabhūva bahgavān kṣaṇam || k6.12 ||
tataḥ sārthadhanaṃ sarvaṃ parisaṃkhyāya tatsamam |
sa dadau cauracakrāya tatkṣaṇāptanidhānataḥ || k6.13 ||
tadvidhena krameṇaiva punaḥ pathi gatāgataiḥ |
ṣaṭkṛtvaḥ pradadau tebhyaḥ so'rthaṃ sārthasya muktaye || k6.14 ||
punaścopagate tasmin vartmanā tena sānuge |
babhūva buddhiścaorāṇāṃ tadbhojananimantraṇe || k6.15 ||
dṛśā diśanti vaimalyaṃ śubhaṃ saṃbhāṣaṇena ca |
vrajanti saṃgamābhyāsaiḥ santaḥ sanmārgasetutām || k6.16 ||
tatrātiryagdṛśā sarvaṃ sarvākuśalasaṃkṣayāt |
teṣāṃ samāhitaṃ śuddhaṃ vidadhe bhagavān jinah || k6.17 ||
yeṣāṃ saṃgrahavastūni vatvāri niyatātmanām |
arthacaryā samānārthabḥāvastyāgaḥ priyaṃ vacaḥ || k6.18 ||
yeṣāṃ brahmavihārāśca catvāraḥ sattvaśālinām |
karuṇā muditopekṣā maitrī ceti parigrahaḥ || k6.19 ||
yeṣāṃ kuśalamūlāni saktāni trīṇi cetasi |
alobhaścāparidveṣo'pyamohaśca mahātmanām || k6.20 ||
dānaśīlakṣamāvīryadhyānaprajñājuṣāṃ sadā |
upāyapraṇidhijñānabalairāśritacetasām || k6.21 ||
paritrāṇaikavīrāṇāṃ sadaivādvayavādinām |
vidyātrayapradīptānāṃ caturvaimalyaśālinām || k6.22 ||
pañcaskandhavumiktānāṃ ṣaḍayatanabhedinām |
saptabodhyaṅgayuktānāmaryāṣṭāṅgopadeśinām || k6.23 ||
navasaṃyogahīnānāṃ teṣāṃ daśabalātmanām |
kiṃ vastvaviditaṃ loke jinānāṃ janaceṣṭitam || k6.24 ||
tatasteṣvatikāruṇyāccaraṇālīnamūrdhasu |
tathetyuvāca bhagavāṃstadbhojyopanimantraṇe || k6.25 ||
taistatsaṃdarśanakṣīṇakilbiṣaiḥ samamarpitam |
bhikṣusṃghairvṛto bhojyaṃ vidhivatsarvamādade || k6.26 ||
tatastatpraṇidhānena jñānālokaśalākayā |
te samunmīlitadṛśaḥ prakāśaṃ dadṛśuḥ padam || k6.27 ||
te sadyastīvravairāgyaparipakkāḥ prasādinaḥ |
pravrajyāyogamāsajya jagmurjagati pūjyatām || k6.28 ||
tatteṣāṃ kuśalaṃ dṛṣṭvā sahasopanataṃ puraḥ |
babhāṣe bhagavān pṛṣṭaḥ kimetaditi bhikṣubhiḥ || k6.29 ||
etairyamāyaṃ saṃbandhaḥ sārtharakṣaṇaniṣkrayaiḥ |
dvīpayātrāgatasyāsīdanyasminnapi janmani || k6.30 ||
asti vistīrṇamārgasya svargavargāvadhirvidheḥ |
purī vārāṇasī nāma kauśalotkarṣaharṣabhūḥ || k6.31 ||
yasyāmamalakallolavāhinī suravāhinī |
sadā dayeva hṛdayaṃ prasādayati dehinām || k6.32 ||
ahiṃseva satāṃ sevyā vidyeva viduṣāṃ matā |
kṣameva sarvabhūtānāṃ yā viśrambhasukhasthitiḥ || k6.33 ||
brahmakalpe nṛpe tasyā vikasatkamalāśraye |
brahmadattābhidhe lokaṃ trailokyamiva rakṣati || k6.34 ||
priyasenābhidhāno'bhūttatra vaiśravaṇopamaḥ |
sārthavāho'rthasārthānāṃ sthānamabdhirivāmbhasām || k6.35 ||
tasyāsītsupriyo nāma saujanyanilayaḥ sutaḥ |
prayayau yaṃ samāśritya guṇasārthaḥ kṛtārthatām || k6.36 ||
dānaśīlakṣamāvīryadhyānaprajñāsamanvitah |
dhātrā vilobhavāyaiva yaḥ kṛtaḥ sukṛtaśriyaḥ || k6.37 ||
taṃ sarvavidyāḥ viśadāḥ kalāśca vipulāśayam |
viviśuḥ sarasodārā mahodadhimivāpagāḥ || k6.38 ||
guṇālaṃkṛtacāritraṃ lakṣaṇālaṃkṛtākṛtim |
puruṣottamalubdheva yaṃ ślāghyaṃ śrīraśiśriyat || k6.39 ||
kālena sukṛtakrītaṃ pitari tridivaṃ gate |
cakre skandhataṭe tasya vyavahārabharaḥ sthitim || k6.40 ||
so'cintayadiyaṃ lakṣmīrvipulātmakramāgatā |
tathāpi manye paryāptā na sarvārthimanorathe || k6.41 ||
kiṃ tayā sumahatyāpi śriyā satpuruṣasthayā |
pūrvāgatārthiubhukteva yā śeṣārthiṣu niṣphalā || k6.42 ||
ratnākarasya vaipulyaṃ niṣphalaṃ vedhasā kṛtam |
adyāpi pūrito yena naiko'pyarthī sa vāḍavaḥ || k6.43 ||
athavā pṛthusaṃkalpaḥ kenārthī paripūryate |
jagāmābdhiragastyasya culukācamanīyatām || k6.44 ||
kiṃ karomyatitāpo'yaṃ śrīrekā bahavi'rthinaḥ |
na tadāsādyate vittaṃ yatsarvārthibharakṣamam || k6.45 ||
pañca ṣaṭpūritā evaṃ nānye śrīkaustukādibhiḥ |
itivādyāpi tapto'ntarjvaladaurvānalo'mbudhiḥ || k6.46 ||
tasmātkaromi yatnena niṃsaṃkhyadraviṇārjanam |
na sahe duḥkhaniḥśvāsaṃ vimukhasya mukhe'rthinaḥ || k6.47 ||
iti saṃcintya sa ciraṃ sārthena mahatā vṛtaḥ |
ratnadvīpapuraṃ gatvā vidadhe ratnasaṃgraham || k6.48 ||
tataḥ pratīpamāyāntaṃ kṛtārthaṃ taṃ vanecarāḥ |
sārthārtharaṇonmuktā dadṛśurdasyavaḥ pathi || k6.49 ||
sārthārthaharaṇe dṛṣṭvā sa teṣāṃ sāhasodyamam |
nijasaravsvadānena saṃrarakṣānuyānim || k6.50 ||
punaḥ krameṇa tenaiva ratnadvīpagatāgatiḥ |
sārthatrāṇāya caurāṇāṃ ṣaṭkṛtvaḥ pradadau dhanam || k6.51 ||
tathaiva tvāṃ puṇyavipanvānisaṃprāptastathaiva tān (?) |
dadarśa caurān sārthārthaharaṇe adhikādarān || k6.52 ||
so'cintayadaho vittairmahadbhiḥ paripūritāḥ |
mayaite na nivartante parārthaharaṇodyamāt || k6.53 ||
jagatsaṃpūrayāmyarthairiyuktvāpi mayāsakṛt |
aho nu dasyavo naite varākāḥ paripūritāḥ || k6.54 ||
acitotsāhahīnasya vyāhatottaravādinaḥ |
vikatthanapratijñasya dhiṅme janma kujanmanaḥ || k6.55 ||
iti cintayatastasya taptasyānuśayāgninā |
vijane prayayau rātriḥ saṃvatsaraśatopamā || k6.56 ||
taṃ śokapaṅkasaṃmagnaṃ gajendramiva niścalam |
dīrghocchvāsaṃ maheśākhyā svapne provāca devatā || k6.57 ||
sumate mā kṛthāḥ śokaṃ śarīrocchoṣaṇaṃ vṛthā |
satsaṃkalpābhirūḍhasya bhaviṣyati tavepsitam || k6.58 ||
na tadasti jagatyasmin svapnasaṃkalpadurlabham |
yanna sidhyati yatnena dhīrāṇāṃ vyavasāyinām || k6.59 ||
sā kāpyanupamā śaktirekasyāpi dvijanmanaḥ |
yadājñāspanditenaiva vindhyaḥ kṣmāsamatāṃ yayau || k6.60 ||
viṣamaṃ samatāṃ yāti dūramāyāti cāntikam |
salilaṃ sthalatāmeti kāryakāle mahātmanām || k6.61 ||
parārtho'yaṃ tavārambhaḥ phalatyeva na saṃśayam |
na bhavanti visaṃvādasaṃdigdhāḥ sattvavṛttayaḥ || k6.62 ||
ratnāni badaradvīpe santi tridaśasevite |
yeṣāmekaprabhāvo'pi trijagatpūraṇakṣamaḥ || k6.63 ||
martyabhūmimatikramya sā hi bhūmirmahīyasī |
āsādyate puṇyamayī nāsattvairnākṛtātmabhiḥ || k6.64 ||
viṣādastyajyatāṃ putra sthirā buddhirvidhīyatām |
badaradvīpayātrāyāmutsāhaḥ parigṛhyatām || k6.65 ||
śrūyatāmeṣa tatprāptyau diṅbhātrānukramakramaḥ |
sphītasattvaprabhāvastvaṃ saṃsārottaraṇakṣamah || k6.66 ||
asti paścimadigbhāge samullaṅghya mahīyasām |
śatāni sapta dvīpānāṃ tathā sapta mahācalān || k6.67 ||
saptāpatāścānulomapratilomābhidho'mbudhiḥ |
anukūlānilairyasmin pāramāpnoti puṇyavān || k6.68 ||
tatastattulyanāmādrirvātaistimiramihakṛt |
yatrākṣṇordiśati svāsthyamamoghākhyā mahauṣadhiḥ || k6.69 ||
athāvartābhidho'mbhodhirvairambhairyatra vāyubhiḥ |
majjanonmajjanairjantuḥ saptāvarteṣu tāryate || k6.70 ||
āvartākhyastataḥ śailaḥ śaṅkhanābho niśācarah |
ghoraḥ prāṇaharo yatra tridaśatrāsakṛtsthitaḥ || k6.71 ||
kṛṣṇasarpāvṛtā yatra śankhanābhirmahauṣadhiḥ |
trāyate puṇyasaṃpannaṃ netre śirasi cārpitā || k6.72 ||
atha nīlodanāmābdhī raktākṣo yatra rākṣasaḥ |
makaryābhibhūtāṃ buddhavidyāvidvān vaśe (?) || k6.73 ||
atha nīlodanāmādrirnīlagrīvaḥ kṣapācaraḥ |
pratīptanetro yatrāste rakṣasāṃ pañcabhiḥ śataiḥ || k6.74 ||
tatraoṣadhimamoghākhyāṃ rakṣatyāśīviṣaḥ sadā |
dṛṣṭiniḥ śvāsasaṃsparśadaṃṣṭrotsṛjadviṣānalaḥ || k6.75 ||
upoṣadhavratavatā maitreṇa karuṇātmanā |
labhyate sā samutsārya kṛṣṇasarpaṃ mahauṣadhiḥ || k6.76 ||
taṃ rakṣaḥśakaṭaṃ śailaṃ niṣphalaślakṣṇakandaram |
tāmajjane śikhāyāṃ ca kṛtvā tarati puṇyavān || k6.77 ||
atha vairambhanāmādriḥ pāre yasyottarā taṭe |
ghorā tāmrāṭavī nāma mahāśālavanāntarā || k6.78 ||
mahānajagarastatra tāmrākṣo nāma duḥsahaḥ |
āsta yasyogragandhena vāyunaiva na jīvyate || k6.79 ||
ṣaṇmāsān svapato yasya lālā vyāpnoti yojanam |
kṣutsaṃtaptasya ṣaṇmāsānalpībhavati jāgrataḥ || k6.80 ||
veṇugulmaśilābaddhāṃ guhāmutpāṭya medinīm |
prāyauṣadhīṃ divārātraṃ jvalantīmañjanocitām || k6.81 ||
tasmādajagarādghorādanyato vā mahaujasaḥ |
avairākhyāṃ buddhavidyāṃ japato na bhavedbhayam || k6.82 ||
tataḥ sapta mahāśailā veṇukaṇṭakasaṃkaṭāḥ |
tāmrapaṭāṅkapādena tīryante vīryaśālinā || k6.83 ||
tataśca śālmalīvanaṃ sapta kṣārataraṅgiṇīḥ |
uttīryāsādyate prāṅgustriśaṅkurnāma parvataḥ || k6.84 ||
tatra triśaṅkavo nāma kaṇṭakā vajrabhedinaḥ |
pādayorna viśantyeva tāmrapaṭṭāvanaddhayoḥ || k6.85 ||
triśankurnāma taṭinī tatrāyaḥśaṅkuparvataḥ |
upaskīlanadī tatra tato dvidhā dvidhā sarit || k6.86 ||
athāṣṭādaśavakrākhyaḥ parvato niravagrahaḥ |
tattulyasaṃjñātha nadī ślakṣṇi nāma giristataḥ || k6.87 ||
athādridhūmanetrākhyo dhūmanirdigdhadiktaṭaḥ |
dṛṣṭisparśaviṣairvyāptaḥ krūrāśīviṣamaṇḍalaiḥ || k6.88 ||
tanmūrdhni palvalasyāntaḥ śilābaddhā mahāguhā |
jyotīraso maṇiryasyāṃ jīvanī ca mahauṣadhiḥ || k6.89 ||
bhittvā guhāṃ tadabhyakraśiraḥpādakarodaraḥ |
vajra-mantrabalopetaḥ krūrasarpairna bādhyate || k6.90 ||
athograsattvasaṃkīrṇāḥ saptāśīviṣaparvatāḥ |
nadyaśca tadvidhā yāsāmapāravārisaṃpadaḥ || k6.91 ||
etaduttīrya nikhilaṃ puṇyaiḥ parahitodyataḥ |
ārohati sudhāśailaṃ śṛṅgairāliṅgitāmbaram || k6.92 ||
tatastasyāpare pārśve kalpavṛkṣopaśobhitam |
puraṃ rohitakaṃ nāma dṛśyate svargasaṃnibham || k6.93 ||
asti tatra magho nāma maghavāniva viśrutaḥ |
sārthavāho mahāsattvaḥ sarvasattvahite rataḥ || k6.94 ||
badaradvīpayātrāyāmudyatasyānavadyadhīḥ |
mārgopadeśaṃ deśajñaḥ sa te sarvaṃ kariṣyati || k6.95 ||
ityuktvotsāhya bahuśaḥ śubhadrairiva supriyam |
vacobhiricitairdevī sahasāntaradhīyata || k6.96 ||
prabuddhaḥ supriyaḥ sarvaṃ tattatheti vicintayan |
pratasthe sattvamāruhya nijotsāhapuraḥsaraḥ || k6.97 ||
sa vrajan vinitāyāsastena nirdiṣṭavartmanā |
puṇyairdvādaśabhirvarṣaiḥ prāpa rphitakaṃ puram || k6.98 ||
atrāntare karmayogāttatra sārthapatirmaghaḥ |
vyādhinā duścikitsyena babhūvāsvasthavigrahaḥ || k6.99 ||
alabdhāntaḥpraveśo'tha gṛhe rājagṛhopame |
tasya vyalambata dvāraṃ supriyaḥ kāryasiddhaye || k6.100 ||
tato vaidyāpadeśena sa praveśamavāptavān |
upayogakathāprajñā na kasyādarabhūmayaḥ || k6.101 ||
āyurvedavidhānajñaḥ sa tasyāriṣṭalakṣaṇaiḥ |
ṣaṇmāsaśeṣamevāyurjñātvā cintāntaro'bhavat || k6.102 ||
tasya priyahitaprāyo bhaiṣajyaparicaryayā |
atyalpenaiva kālena supriyaḥ priyatāṃ yayau || k6.103 ||
bhaiṣajyayuktistatprītyā tasya vallabhatāṃ yayau |
priyopanītaṃ yatkiṃ cittatsarvaṃ manasaḥ priyam || k6.104 ||
priyopacāraistasyātha vyādhirādrāvamāyayau |
ādhiḥ śāmyati satsaṅgāttao vyādhirviśīryate || k6.105 ||
tataḥ saṃjātaviśrambhaḥ supriyaḥ praṇayānmagham |
cakre viditavṛttāntaṃ paścānnijakathākṣaṇe || k6.106 ||
badaradvīpayātrāyāṃ tasyotsāhaṃ mahātmanah |
parārthe niścalaṃ jñātvā tamūce vismayānmaghaḥ || k6.107 ||
aho batāsmin saṃsāre viḥsāre sārarūpiṇaḥ |
jāyante maṇayaḥ kecitparacintāparāyaṇāḥ || k6.108 ||
navaṃ vayaḥ priyā mūrtiḥ parārthapravaṇaṃ manaḥ |
puṇyocitastathaivāyaṃ sthāne guṇasamāgamaḥ || k6.109 ||
iyatīṃ bhūmimullaṅghya parārthe tvamupāgataḥ |
karomi tava sāhāyyaṃ kiṃ tvahaṃ bhṛśamāturaḥ || k6.110 ||
nibaddhāvadhayaḥ prāṇāḥ prayāntyeva śarīriṇām |
te vrajantu mamānte'pi tvatsamāhitahetutām || k6.111 ||
evameva vyayo yastu vyayaḥ sa parigaṇyate |
parārthe jīvitasyāpi vyayo lābhaśataiḥ samaḥ || k6.112 ||
na mayā badaradvīpaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ kiṃ tu śrutaṃ mayā |
mahābdhau diskamuddeśaṃ taistairjānāmi lakṣaṇaiḥ || k6.113 ||
ityuktvā bhūpatiṃ suhṛdbandhuvākye'pyanādaraḥ |
sa maṅgalapravahaṇaṃ supriyeṇa sahādadhe || k6.114 ||
tataḥ pravahaṇārūḍhau tau yojanśatānyapi |
pavanasyānulomyena jagmaturvipulāśayau || k6.115 ||
sthāne sthāne jalaṃ dṛṣṭvā nānāvarṇaṃ mahodadheḥ |
kimetaditi prapaccha supriyaḥ kautukānmagham || k6.116 ||
jale lohācalāḥ pañca santyasya payasāṃ nidheḥ |
tāmrarūpyamayāścānye hemaratnamayāḥ pare || k6.117 ||
teṣāṃ chāyāviśeṣeṇa nānāvarṇaḥ pade pade |
dṛśyate'bdhirayaṃ dīptaḥ prāptāntarodgatauṣadhiḥ || k6.118 ||
ityuktvā vyādhinākrāntaḥ prāptakālāvadhirmaghaḥ |
prāṇānmumoca satkītivinyastasthirajīvitaḥ || k6.119 ||
vajralepādapi dṛḍhaṃ yathā sattvaṃ mahātmanām |
tathā yadi bhadedāyuḥ kimasādhyaṃ bhave bahvet || k6.120 ||
kūlāvāptapravahaṇahsupriyastadviyogajam |
śucaṃ saṃstabhya vidadhe suhṝdastanusatkriyām || k6.121 ||
etadevonnataṃ lakṣma sattvotsāhamahātmanām |
vicchinnālambane kāle yatkartavyadṛḍhaṃ manaḥ || k6.122 ||
punaḥ pravahaṇārūḍhaḥ sa samuttīrya vāridhim |
ratnaparvatapārśvena viveśa vikaṭāṭavīm || k6.123 ||
na viyogairna codvegairnābhiyogairdviṣāmapi |
na rogaiḥ kleśabhogairvā hīyate mahatāṃ matiḥ || k6.124 ||
sa tatrākrāntagaganaṃ niruddhāśeṣadiktaṭam |
durārohaṃ dadarśāgre mūrtaṃ vighnamivācalam || k6.125 ||
upāyahīnastaṃ dṛṣṭvā giriṃ mūrkhamivoddhatam |
adhaḥ pallavaśayyāyāṃ suptaḥ so'cintayatkṣasṇam || k6.126 ||
aho bata kiyān kālaḥ prayātaḥ prasthitasya me |
badaradvīpanāmāpi na nāma śrūyate kkacit || k6.127 ||
vyavasāyasahāyo me yo'bhūtpuṇyapaṇaiḥ param |
bhagnaplava ivākāle so'pi karmormiviplavaiḥ || k6.128 ||
naṣṭopāye'pyupāye'smin vyavasāyānmahīyasaḥ |
na nāma vinivarte'haṃ siddhirnidhanamastu vā || k6.129 ||
tadekaṃ janmayātrāsu pūjyaṃ janma jagatrtraye |
yasmin paropakrārāya jāyate jīvitavyayaḥ || k6.130 ||
iti cintākulaṃ tatra taṃ jñātvā satyasāgaram |
nīlo nāma samabhyetya yakṣaḥ prāhācalāśrayaḥ || k6.131 ||
pūrveṇa yojanaṃ gatvā trīṇi śṛṅgāṇi bhūbhṛtaḥ |
vetrasopānaniśreṇyā samāruhyātha gamyatām || k6.132 ||
iti yakṣopadeśena sa vilaṅghya mahācalam |
dadarśāgre samuttuṅgaśrṛṅgaṃ sphaṭīkabhūdharam || k6.133 ||
tasminnekaśilāślakṣṇe durgame pakṣiṇāmapi |
muhūrtamabhavattasya nirvyāpāro manorathaḥ || k6.134 ||
abhyunnataṃ nirālambaṃ svasaṃkalpamivācalam |
sa taṃ vicārya suciraṃ citranyasta ivābhavat || k6.135 ||
atha candraprabho nāma yakṣaḥ śailaguhāśayaḥ |
abhyetyaḥ sattvasaṃpannaṃ tamabhāṣaṭa vusmitaḥ || k6.136 ||
krośamātramito gatvā pūrveṇāpūrvavibhramam |
dṛśyate candanavanaṃ bālānilacalallatam || k6.137 ||
tatrāste prasarā nāma guhālīnā mahauṣadhiḥ |
labhyate deharakṣāyai samuttolya mahāśilām || k6.138 ||
tatprabhāvakṛtālokaṃ sopānaiḥ sphaṭikācalam |
sahasaiva samāruhya gamyatāmīpsitāptaye || k6.139 ||
tatkṣaṇātkṛtakārtheva sā prayāti mahāuṣadhiḥ |
na khedastatkṛte kāryastaḍillolāḥ priyāptayaḥ || k6.140 ||
iti yakṣopadiṣṭena vidhānena sa bhūdharam |
samutkramya dadarśāgre nagaraṃ hemamandiram || k6.141 ||
merūkūṭairivākīrṇaṃ prakāśairiva nirmitam |
savāścaryairiva kṛtaṃ taddṛṣṭvā vismito'bhavat || k6.142 ||
mahāhemakapāṭābhyāṃ ruddhaddhāraṃ vilokya tat |
nirjanaṃ vārasaṃcāraṃ vanānte niṣasāda saḥ || k6.143 ||
atrāntare dinasyānte vyomānantapathādhvagaḥ |
avāpāstācalopāntaṃ pariśrānta ivāṃśumān || k6.144 ||
astaṃ gate sahasrāṃśau rajanīramaṇī śanaiḥ |
tārāpatimivānveṣṭuṃ prasasārābhisārikā || k6.145 ||
atha prakāśavibahvaiḥ sarvāśāpūraṇonmukhaḥ |
bodhisattva iva svacchaḥ sudhādīdhitirudyayau || k6.146 ||
sphīṭā tamaḥ samūhasya niḥśeṣapraśamocitā |
mānasollāsinī jyotsnā sattvavṛttirivābabhau || k6.147 ||
tamomohaṃ jahārendurdiśāṃ dinaviyogajam |
paropakāre hi paro dūrāroho mahātmanām || k6.148 ||
supriyaścandrakiraṇaiḥ pūryamāṇatanuḥ kṣaṇam |
nidrāṃ kāryasamudrormikṣobhamudrāmavāptavān || k6.149 ||
kṣapāyāṃ kṣīyamāṇāyāṃ guṇadākṣiṇyasādarā |
jagāda devatā svapne maheśākhyā sametya tam || k6.150 ||
aho bata mahāsattva sattattvābhiniveśinā |
parārthe vipulaḥ kleśaḥ kṛtaḥ sukṛtinā tvayā || k6.151 ||
alpaśeṣe prayāse'sminnodvegaṃ kartumarhasi |
aparyuṣitasattvānāṃ svādhīnāḥ sarvasiddhayaḥ || k6.152 ||
haimaṃ yadetannagaraṃ trīṇi cānyānyataḥ param |
santi ratnapurāṇyatra vicitrāṇyuttarottaram || k6.153 ||
tebhyo niryānti kinnaryaścataśro'ṣṭau ca ṣoḍaśa |
dvātriṃśacca krameṇaiva tvayā dvāri vighaṭṭite || k6.154 ||
jitendriyasya bhavatastatpramādamavedinaḥ |
kimanyadacireṇaivvāñchitāptirbhaviṣyati || k6.155 ||
ityuktaḥ sādaraṃ devyā pratibuddho'tha supriyaḥ |
jaghāna nagaradvāraṃ triḥ samabhyetya pāṇinā || k6.156 ||
tataścatasraḥ kinnaryo niryayustaralekṣaṇāḥ |
āścaryatarumañjarya iva līlānilākulāḥ || k6.157 ||
mānasillāsakāriṇyo nayanāmṛtavṛṣṭayaḥ |
vadanendusamudyotairdivāpi kṛtacandrikāḥ || k6.158 ||
tāḥ saṃpūjya smarodāraṃ supriyaṃ priyadarśanāḥ |
tasyābhilāṣapraṇayairātithyamiva cakrire || k6.159 ||
candrakāntasamāsīnaṃ kṛtāsanaparigrahāḥ |
jīvanauṣadhayo jātāḥ smarasyeva savigrahāḥ || k6.160 ||
tāstamūcuḥ samunmīladvilāsahasitatviṣaḥ |
dadatya iva karpūraṃ premopāyanatāṃ puraḥ || k6.161 ||
aho dhanyā vayaṃ yāsāṃ sadguṇālakṛtākṛtiḥ |
svayaemvābhigamyo'pi bhavānadyāgato gṛham || k6.162 ||
vidheṣaḥ kasya pīyūṣe candane kasya vāruciḥ |
indau mandādaraḥ ko vā sādhuḥ kasya na saṃmataḥ || k6.163 ||
strīṇāṃ yadyapi saubhāgyabhaṅgāya praṇayaḥ svayam |
kramastvaddarśanenaiva tathāpi mukharīkṛtāḥ || k6.164 ||
idaṃ ca kinnarapuraṃ vayaṃ ca praṇayārpitāḥ |
ratnaṃ ca saubhāṣaṇikaṃ sādho svādhīnameva te || k6.165 ||
iti tāsāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā supriyaḥ praṇayocitam |
uvāca sattvadhavalāṃ diśan daśanacandrikām || k6.166 ||
bahumānāspadaṃ kasya nedaṃ saṃbhāṣaṇāmṛtam |
ātmano'pyādarasthānaṃ bahvatībhiḥ kṛtādaraḥ || k6.167 ||
ślāghyaṃ darśanamevedaṃ tatrāpyayamanugrahaḥ |
muktālatāstāpaharāḥ kiṃ punaścandanokṣitāḥ || k6.168 ||
evaṃ vidhānāṃ svacchānāmaindavīnāmiva tviṣām |
ākṛtīnāṃ samucitā rucirā lokavṛttayaḥ || k6.169 ||
aucityacārucaritaṃ prasādaviśadaṃ manah |
vātsalyapeśalā vāṇī na kasyādarabhūmayaḥ || k6.170 ||
gṛhīto'smābhirācāraḥ pūjāparikarocitah |
ātmārpaṇaṃ kulāntaṃ vaḥ parāyattā hi yoṣitaḥ || k6.171 ||
kanyābhāvādapakrāntā yūyaṃ paraparigrahāḥ |
