viśrambheṇa bhagiṇyo me jananyaḥ snehagauravāt || k6.172 ||
paravittaṃ viṣaṃ yeṣāṃ jananyaścānyayoṣitaḥ |
parahiṃsātmahiṃsauva pakṣāsteṣāṃ niratyayāḥ || k6.173 ||
pauśunyāsatyapāruṣyabhinnavādojjhitaṃ vacaḥ |
sadaiva vadane yeṣāṃ teṣāṃ sarvāśiṣā diśaḥ || k6.174 ||
abhidhyārahitaṃ ceto vyāpāraparivarjitam |
mithyādṛṣṭivihīnaṃ ca yeṣāṃ te satpathaṃ śritāḥ || k6.175 ||
daśākuśalamārgebhyo nirgatānāṃ nisargataḥ |
ete kuśalavargasya mārgāḥ svarge nirargalāḥ || k6.176 ||
dhīreva dhanyaṃ dhanamunnatānāṃ vidyaiva cakṣurvijitendriyāṇām |
dayaiva puṇyaṃ puruṣottamānāmātmaiva tīrthaṃ śucimānasānām || k6.177 ||
evaṃvidho'yaṃ guṇasaṃniveśaḥ śīlena vaimalyamupaiti puṃsām |
sadratnamuktānikarātiritaṃ śīlaṃ satāmābharaṇaṃ vadanti || k6.178 ||
ityuktamākarṇya guṇānurūpaṃ sattvārthinā tena jitendriyeṇa |
tuṣṭāstamūcurbhuvi candralokaṃ tāḥ kautukāyaivamukhaiḥ sṛjantyaḥ || k6.179 ||
maṇerivānarghaguṇojjvalasya dṛṣṭaiva sādhorucitā ruciste |
yayaiva maulau hṛdaye śrutau ca sadbhiḥ sadaivābharaṇīkṛto'si || k6.180 ||
maṇirmahārhaḥ prathitaprabhāvaḥ pragṛhyatāmātmasamastvayāyam |
dhvajārpito varṣati yojanānāṃ sahasramevārthisamīhitaṃ yaḥ || k6.181 ||
uktveti ratnapravaraṃ taruṇyastasmei dadurmūrtamiva prasādam |
ādāya taṃ ca praṇayopacāraṃ so'pi dvitīyaṃ puramāpa raupyam || k6.182 ||
tatrādarāttaddviguṇābhireva sa pūjitahkinnarakāminībhiḥ |
krameṇa tenaiva viśuddhabuddhirlebhe maṇiṃ taddviguṇaprabhāvam || k6.183 ||
* * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * |
* * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * || k6.184 ||
prāptaśca taṃ ratnamayaṃ caturthaṃ puraṃ tataḥ sarvapurādhikaści |
so'bhyarthitastaddviguṇābhiragre guṇādhikaḥ kinnarasundarībhiḥ || k6.185 ||
tathaiva saddharmakathāprasaṅgaistātoṣitāstena susaṃyatena |
utphullanītpaladāmadīrghakaṭākṣavikṣiptakarāstamūcuḥ || k6.186 ||
bhrātāsti naḥ kinnararājavaṃśaratnākarendurbadarābhidhānaḥ |
tasyāspadaṃ dvīpamidaṃ mahārhaṃ svanāmacihnaṃ prathitaṃ samṛddhyā || k6.187 ||
ratnaṃ cedaṃ niyamavidhinā poṣadhākhyavratena nyastaṃ bhāsvatkiraṇanikaraṃ puṇyabhājāṃ prayatnātvarṣatyeva sthiraparahitavyāptaye gṛhyatāṃ tat || k6.188 ||
ityutpāṭyāmarataruphalaṃ sādaraṃ sundarībhiḥ
premoddāmapraṇayasubhagaṃ dattamāsādya ratnam |
bālāhākhyaṃ vijitapavanaṃ taṃ prakṛṣṭaṃ turaṅgaṃ
so'pyāruhya svanagaramagāllabdhamārgopadeśaḥ || k6.189 ||
tasmin kāle vipulakuśalaiḥ svargamārgaṃ prayāte
vārāṇasyāṃ viśadayaśasi kṣmāpatau brahmadatte |
śrīmān sarvapraṇayiphaladaḥ supriyaḥ pauramukhyair
lokatrāṇe vinihitamatirdharmarājye'bhiṣiktaḥ || k6.190 ||
tataḥ śiraḥsnānavidhikrameṇa tatpañcadaśyāṃ dhvajamūrdhni ratnam |
sa poṣadhopoṣita eva dhṛtvā cakāra viśvaṃ paripūrṇakāmam || k6.191 ||
kṛtvā yātrāṃ parahitaphalāṃ vatsarāṇāṃ śatena
sthitvā rājye mahati nikhilaṃ pūrayitvā ca lokam |
putraṃ dhṛtvā narapatipade prāpya sarvopaśāntiṃ
tattvajño'sau kimapi paramaṃ brahmabhāvaṃ jagāma || k6.192 ||
syurete dasyavaḥ sarve pūrvaṃ ye pūritā mayā |
ratnadrīpābhigamane tasmin supriyajanmani || k6.193 ||
iti śāstā svavṛttāntakathayā vidadhe vibhuḥ |
dānavīryopadeśena bhikṣūṇāmanuśāsanam || k6.194 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ badaradvīpayātrāvadānaṃ nāma ṣaṣṭhaḥ pallavaḥ ||


7. muktālatāvadānam |

kuślapraṇidhānaśuddhadhānmāṃ vimalālokavivekabodhakānām |
parikīrtanamātrameva yeṣāṃ bhavamohāpahatesta eva dhanyāḥ || k7.1 ||
purā parṣatsahasrāṇāṃ nyagrodhopavanasthitiḥ |
kapilākhye pure cakre bhagavān dharmadeśanām || k7.2 ||
amandānandasaṃdohasyandi candanaśītalam |
tasya vāgamṛtaṃ dhanyāḥ kṛtāṅjalipuṭāḥ papuḥ || k7.3 ||
rājā śuddhodanastatra dharmaśravaṇasaṃgame |
puṇyopadeśasalilairlebhe vaimalyanirvṛtim || k7.4 ||
atha tatra mahānāmā śākyarājakulodbhavaḥ |
dharmopadeśamākarṇya prātaḥ svagṛhamabravīt || k7.5 ||
aho nu bhagavān buddho dharmaḥ saṃghaśca siddhaye |
buddhotpādo'yamasmākaṃ nirvāṇāya mahāphalaḥ || k7.6 ||
upadeśaviśeṣāptānirvṛtestasya tadvacaḥ |
śrutvā śaśiprabhā patnī praṇayāttamabhāṣataḥ || k7.7 ||
anugrāhyā bhagavataḥ puruṣāḥ puṇyabhāginah |
nindyāstadupadeśānāmanarhā yoṣito vayam || k7.8 ||
iti jāyāvacaḥ śrutvā sa jagāda jagadguroḥ |
bhadre bhagavato nāsti bhedaḥ kāruṇyadarśane || k7.9 ||
samā sarvatra bhā bhānoḥ samā vṛṣṭiḥ payomucaḥ |
samā bhagavato dṛṣṭiḥ sarvasattvānukampinaḥ || k7.10 ||
mahāprajāpatervākyādaparāhṇakṣaṇaṃ tapaḥ |
śuddhodanaḥ karotyeva rājā bahgavato'ntike || k7.11 ||
iti patyuḥ priyagirā vṛtā śākyāṅganāgaṇaiḥ |
yācane tadbhagavatahsā puṇyopavanaṃ yayau || k7.12 ||
sā satvakusumaṃ tatra taṃ dadarśa mahāphalam |
praśamāmṛtasaṃsiktaṃ karuṇākalpapādapam || k7.13 ||
lateva pavanānamrā taṃ dūrātpraṇanāma sā |
lobheneva parityakrā cyutakarṇotpalacchalāt || k7.14 ||
vilokya kāñcanaruciṃ ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitām |
ānandanāmā bhikṣustāmuvāca prahitodyataḥ || k7.15 ||
veṣaḥ praśamaśūnyo'yaṃ mātarmunitapovane |
darpotsikto na yuktaste viraktānāmidaṃ padam || k7.16 ||
guṇasaṃyamasiktāni mukharābhraṇānyaho |
nehāstadgrahaṇaṃ yuktamitīvopadiśanti te || k7.17 ||
ityuktā tena sā tanvī vailajyavinatānanā |
unmucyābharaṇaṃ sarvaṃ prāḥiṇonnijamadniram || k7.18 ||
upaviṣṭeṣu sarveṣu nirdiṣṭakuśalastataḥ |
anityataivaṃ bhagavānupadeśaṃ pravakrame || k7.19 ||
mahāmohaprabhāvo'yaṃ yena nityamanityatām |
nityatāmiva manyante mūḍhā jagati jantavaḥ || k7.20 ||
asatye ramate lokaḥ satyapratyayamohitaḥ |
na vetti sarvabhāvānāmabhāvānubhavāṃ sthitim || k7.21 ||
kecidvyākaraṇaiḥ pare śrutipathaistarkapravādaiḥ pare kecittantraparigrahairbahuvidhairanyaiḥ kalākauśalaiḥ |
saṃsaktāḥ punaruktajanmaśaraṇau yātā sahaiva kṣayaṃ
tatrāpyakṣyayalīlayā kṣaṇapadaṃ mugdhaurnibaddhā dhṛtiḥ || k7.22 ||
viṣayaviṣamāpāyaḥ kāyaḥ prapañca(ma)yāśayā
kharataramarusphārākāro mohabhāvo bhavaḥ |
hitabhūmi (?) tathākāryaṃ kāryaṃ vivekināṃ tathā
niravadhirayaṃ dṛṣṭvā vyādhiryathā (hi) nivartate || k7.23 ||
ityādyanityasaṃskārasaṃyuktaṃ yuktamudyate |
dharmopadeśakuśalaṃ vaktuṃ bhagavati svayam || k7.24 ||
ekā śākyavadhūstatra rūpasaubhāgyagarvitā |
sthitā śauśavatāruṇyasaṃghau vayasi duḥsahe || k7.25 ||
muktāhāraṃ stanataṭe lolāpāṅgairmuhurmuhuḥ |
āliloke yaśaḥsphārasāraṃ ratipateriva || k7.26 ||
hārāvalokinīṃ dṛṣṭvā tāmanekāgrahādasau |
acintayādviraktena magatpatnī śaśiprabhā || k7.27 ||
iyaṃ dharmopadeśe'pi capalā hāramīkṣate |
bhāvānāṃ na śṛṇotyeva mūḍhā kṣaṇikatāmimām || k7.28 ||
svaṃ hāraṃ darśayitvāsyā hārotsāhaṃ harāmyaham |
adhikālokanenaiva darpaḥ śāmyati dehinām || k7.29 ||
iti saṃcintya sā dāsīṃ rohikākhyāmabhāṣata |
rohike gaccha me hāraṃ gṛhātsatvaramāhara || k7.30 ||
ityuktā sā tayā tatra pravṛtte dharmasaṃśrave |
akālagamanodvignā niḥśvasyācintayatkṣaṇam || k7.31 ||
aho batāntarāyo'yaṃ saṃjātaḥ kuśale mama |
nāsmin śrotuṃ labhe dharmaṃ yatparāyattajīvitā || k7.32 ||
puṇyasaurabhasaṃbhārātkīrṇakāruṇyakesarāt |
mukhapadmādbhagavato dhanyaḥ prāpnoti vāṅbhadhuḥ || k7.33 ||
aho svācchandyavicchedastanubhaṅgaḥ sukhakṣayaḥ |
sevā jagati jantūnāṃ duḥkhe duḥkhaparaṃparā || k7.34 ||
sevāprayāsasaṃprāptaṃ dhanamānakaṇodayam |
tatpamatyuṣṇaniḥśvāsairaho kṛcchreṇa pīyate || k7.35 ||
mānaglānirguṇaglānirojaḥpunaśamaḥ śramah |
prathamaṃ sevakasyaitatphalamīśvaradarśane || k7.36 ||
lauhī bandhanaśṛṅkhalā caraṇayorhelāvamānāvanī
svavyāpāraniṣedhanityaniyatī nidrāsukhadrohiṇī |
āśāsyasya viśālajālasaraṇiḥ satsaṅgabhogāśaniḥ
mugdhānāṃ mṛgatṛṣṭikāmarumahī sevā śarīrakṣayaḥ || k7.37 ||
iti saṃcintya suciraṃ sā jagāma tadājñayā |
sevāvikrītakāyānāṃ svecchāviharaṇaṃ kutaḥ || k7.38 ||
vrajantīṃ tāṃ parapreṣyāṃ kṛpaṇāṃ karuṇākulaḥ |
nirīkṣya bahgavān divyacakṣuṣācintayatkṣasṇam || k7.39 ||
asmin janmani śeṣo'syā saṃpūrṇo jīvitāvadhiḥ |
iyaṃ varākī saṃsārāduddhartavyā svayaṃ mayā || k7.40 ||
atha tāṃ karmayogena vrahantīṃ sahasā pathi ||
vatsavātsalyavivaśā śṛṅgābhyāmājaghāna gauḥ || k7.41 ||
sā pradaghyau bhagavataḥ prasādāttanmayasmṛtiḥ |
janmāntarādhivāsena buddhālambanamānasā || k7.42 ||
aho karmormibhiḥ śīrṇe saṃsāramakarākare |
janmāvarteṣu jantūnāṃ majjanonmajjanakramaḥ || k7.43 ||
puṃso lalāṭavipulopalapaṭṭikāsu niḥśarmakarmaghaṭitaprakaṭāṅkaṭaṅkaiḥ |
nayastāni janmamaraṇaprasarākṣarāṇi naitāni pāṇiparimārjanayā calanti || k7.44 ||
iyaṃ karmāyattā pracuracitravaicitraracanā
narāṇāṃ māyūracchadapaṭalatulyā pariṇatiḥ |
yayā garbhārambhe kramanipatane vṛddhisamaye
kṣaye vā nānyatprābhajata tanulekhācchavirati || k7.45 ||
saṃcintyātha puraḥ pravṛttasudaśāsannāvasannasthitiṃ prāptevāsamadāsabhāvakalanāvailakṣyaniḥ spandatām |
ādhāya praṇidhānadhāmni dhavale saddharmaśuddhāṃ dhiyaṃ
saṃsāre vicikitsya eva malinaṃ tatyāja sā jīvitam || k7.46 ||
tataḥ sā siṃhaladvīpe samīpe svargasaṃpadām |
candralekheva dugdhābdhau divyadyutirajāyata || k7.47 ||
ādhānajanmanastasya muktāvarṣeṃ divaścyute |
sābhūnmuktālatā nāma siṃhalādhipateḥ sutā || k7.48 ||
sā puṇyamiva lāvaṇyaṃ vahantī vṛddhimāgatā |
lebhe vivekenāṅgānāṃ saṃtoṣamiva yaovanam || k7.49 ||
tataḥ kadācidvaṇijaḥ śrāvastīpuravāsinaḥ |
makarākaramuttīrya siṃhaladvīpamāyayuḥ || k7.50 ||
te tatra rātriparyante viśrāntisukhamājaguḥ |
dharmārthagāthāsaṃnaddhaprabuddhabuddhabhāṣitam || k7.51 ||
tdantaḥpuraharmyasthā śrutvaiva śravaṇāmṛtam |
kimetaditi papraccha tānānāyya nṛpātmajā || k7.52 ||
te tāmūcuḥ pramuditāmidaṃ buddhasya bḥāṣitam |
svabhāvārhaṃ bhagavataḥ sarvasattvānukampinah || k7.53 ||
buddhāmidhānaṃ śrutviva pulakālaṃkṛtākṛtiḥ |
sā babhūva samudbhūtasaṃvidbhavyānubhāvabhūḥ || k7.54 ||
unmubhī sāmayūriva śabdaireva payomucaḥ |
ka eṣa bhagavān buddha iti paparcchaḥ tān punaḥ || k7.55 ||
tataste nikhilaṃ tasyai śraddhāsaṃvardhitādarāḥ |
nyavedayan puṇyamayīṃ bhagavaccaritasthitim || k7.56 ||
atha sā tatkathāvāptaprāgjanmakuśalodayā |
vijñaptilekhaṃ pradadau teṣāṃ bhagavataḥ kṛte || k7.57 ||
kālena sindhumuttīrya saṃrpāptāste nijāṃ pirīm |
praṇamyāvedya tadvṛttaṃ dadurlekhaṃ mahātmane || k7.58 ||
bhagavānapi sarvajñaḥ pūrvameva vibhāvya tat |
muktālatāyāḥ kṛpayā svayaṃ lekhamavācayat || k7.59 ||
aho smaraṇameva te kimapi puṇyapaṇyaṃ satāṃ
bhavātibahvabhaiṣajaṃ vyasanatāpatṛṣṇāpahat |
bhavanmayakathākramopanatasaṃvidāsvādabhūḥ
sa eṣa bhagavanmahānamṛtasaṃvibhāgo mama || k7.60 ||
iti saṃkṣiptalekhārthaṃ vibhāvya bhagavān svayam |
īṣatsmitviṣā sattvaprakāśamadiśaddiśām || k7.61 ||
tataścitrakarāśakyāṃ prabhāvairanupūritām |
bhagavān prāhiṇottasyai nyastāṃ svapratimāṃ paṭe || k7.62 ||
punaḥ pravahaṇārūḍhā vaṇijaste tadājñayā |
avāpya siṃhaladvīpaṃ tasyai paṭamadarśayan || k7.63 ||
hemasiṃhāsananyaste paṭe dṛṣṭvā tathāgatam |
janastanmayatādhyānādekībhāvamivāyayau || k7.64 ||
adhastāllikhitaṃ tasmin puṇyaprāptamadṛśyata |
tisraḥ śaraṇyā gatayaḥ pañca śikṣāpadāṇi ca || k7.65 ||
sapratītyasamutpādaḥ sānulomaviparyayaḥ |
āryāṣṭāṅgastathā mārgaḥ paramāmṛtanirbharaḥ || k7.66 ||
svayaṃ bhagavatā vyastaṃ tasyopari subhāṣitam |
śobhate bhāvanālīnaṃ bhrājiṣṇu kanakākṣaram || k7.67 ||
viṣamaviṣayālolavyālāvalīvalayākulāt
taruṇatimirānniṣkramyāsmātpramohamayādgṛhāt |
jananamaraṇakleśāveśapravṛttapṛthivyathā
vrajata śaraṇaṃ bauddhaṃ dharmaṃ na cātra bhavādbhayam || k7.68 ||
jinapratikṛtiṃ puṇyāṃ paśyantī pārthivātmajā |
anādikālopacitāṃ mumocājñānavāsanām || k7.69 ||
prāṃśuṃ kavata(?) kāñcanakāntakāyaṃ suskandhamājānubhujābhirāmam |
dhyānāvadhānārthanimīlitākṣaṃ lāvaṇyadhārāyitatuṅganāsam || k7.70 ||
svabhāvabhavyaṃ pravibhāsamānaṃ pralambanirbhūṣaṇakarṇapāśam |
bālapravālāruṇavalkalāṅkaṃ saṃsaktasaṃdhyābhramivāmarādrim || k7.71 ||
tviṣā diṣāṃ śīlamivādiśantamānandadānodyatavaktracandram |
kṣamāguṇaṃ kṣāmiva śikṣayantaṃ puṇyocitā sā subhagaṃ vilokya || k7.72 ||
praṇāmaparyantakapolacumbikarṇotpalānāṃ parivartanena |
nirasya niḥsāraśarīratṛptiṃ satyānubhāvaṃ paramaṃ prapede || k7.73 ||
srotaḥsamāpattiphalaprakāśaṃ sāsādya tatra kṣaṇalabdhabodhiḥ |
vicintayantī sugataprabhāvaṃ samabhyadhādvismayaharṣabhūmiḥ || k7.74 ||
aho mahāmohatamohareṇa dūrasthitenāmi tathāgatena |
prasahya bḥāsvadvapuṣārpiteyaṃ vikāsalakṣmīḥ kuśalāmbujasya || k7.75 ||
tīrṇo bahvaḥ satpraṇidhānamāptaṃ prasannamantaḥkaraṇaṃ kṣaṇena |
aho nu tṛṣṇāparitāpaśāntyai samucchalantīva samāmṛtaughāḥ || k7.76 ||
ityuktvā sā bahgavate muktāratnānyupāyanam |
vitīrya saṃghapūjāyāṃ visasarja vaṇigjanam || k7.77 ||
te mahodadhimuttīrya prāptā bahgavato'ntikam |
tanmuktāratnanikaraṃ praṇamyāsmai nyavedayan || k7.78 ||
vaṇigbhiḥ kathitāṃ śrutvā tatkathāṃ tatra bhikṣuṇā |
ānandanāmnā pṛṣṭho'tha babhāṣe bhagavān jinaḥ || k7.79 ||
yāsau purā rohitākhyā dāsī śākyagṛhe'bahvat |
saiva muktālatā jātā satkarmapraṇidhānataḥ || k7.80 ||
abhūnmahādhano nāma vārāṇasyāṃ vaṇikpurā |
tasya ratnavatī nāma patnī puṇyocitābhavat || k7.81 ||
sā patyau pañcatāṃ yāte niḥputrā stūpaśekhare |
pūjāṃ kṛtvā mahāhārāṃ bhaktiyuktā nyavedayat || k7.82 ||
tena puṇyavipākena siṃhalādhipateḥ sutā |
jātā muktālatā saiva prātā ca parinirvṛtim || k7.83 ||
saiva janmani cānyasminnairvaryamadamohitā |
pūjādhikṣepadakṣābhūddhāsī tenātivatsaram || k7.84 ||
janmabhūmau janenātpaṃ yadyatkarma śubhāśubham |
tasya tasya sa tadrūpaṃ bhuṅkte pariṇataṃ phalam || k7.85 ||
nikhilakuśalamūlā kīrtipuṣpojjvalaśrīḥ
śubhaphalabharasūrirdharmavallī narāṇām |
bhavati ca viṣavallī kilbiṣakleśamūla-
bhramanipatanamohānantasaṃtāpahetuḥ || k7.86 ||
saṃtapte'smin kharataramaruṣphārasaṃsāramārge
pāpaṃ punyaṃ tyajata janatāḥ saktatīvrānutāpam |
puṇyaṃ puṇyaṃ kuruta satataṃ puṇyapīyūṣasiktāḥ
puṇyacchāyātarutalabhuvaḥ śītalāḥ puṇyabhājām || k7.87 ||
iti satpraṇidhānasya phalaṃ kathayatā svayam |
bhikṣūṇāmupadeśo'yaṃ bhaktyai bhagavatā kṛtaḥ || k7.88 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpatāyāṃ muktālatāvadānaṃ nāma saptamaḥ pallavaḥ ||


8. śrīguptāvadānam |

kṛtāpakāre'pi kṛpākulāni krūre'pyalaṃ pallavakomalāni |
dveṣoṣmatapte'pyatiśītalāni bhavanti cittāni sadāśayānām || k8.1 ||
purā surapurodāre pure rājagṛhābhidhe |
śrīguptākhyo gṝhapatirbabhūva dhandopamaḥ || k8.2 ||
dṛptaḥ sujanavidveṣī guṇeṣu viratādaraḥ |
sadā dhanamadādhmātaḥ sā jahāsa matiṃ satām || k8.3 ||
kaṭhine'ṣvativakreṣu śūnyeṣu mukhareṣu ca |
śankheṣviva khaleṣveva lakṣmīrdākṣiṇyamāśritā || k8.4 ||
taṃ kadācitgururjñātiputraḥ kṣapaṇakaḥ khalah |
mithaḥ svairakathāsaktaḥ puṇyadveṣādabhāṣata || k8.5 ||
ya eṣa gṛdhrakūṭākhye girau bhikṣuśatairvṛtaḥ |
sarvajñakīrtiḥ sugatāstrijagatpūjyatāṃ gataḥ || k8.6 ||
naivāsya pratibhāṃ kāṃcidbhavyāmupalabḥāmahe |
nītaḥ kiṃ tūnnatiṃ mūrkhairbahgavān bhagavāniti || k8.7 ||
avicāyava satataṃ paroktamanubhāṣate |
gatānugatikaḥ prāyaḥ prasiddhasaraṇau janaḥ || k8.8 ||
vratādiniyamastasya dambha eva vibhāvyate |
aśnāti maunakṛtmatsyānekapādavrato bakaḥ || k8.9 ||
tasmaāttasyopahāsāya kriyatāṃ kāpi vajṇcanā |
māyāmohito dhūrtānāṃ paro'pi parituṣyati || k8.10 ||
iti tenoktamākarṇya śrīguptaḥ karkmamohitaḥ |
patituṃ pāpaśvabhreṣu yuktyā tadupadiṣṭayā || k8.11 ||
pradīptakhadirāṅgārapūrṇāṃ gūḍhāṃ khadāṃ gṛhe |
kṛtvā saviṣamannaṃ ca yayau bhagavato'ntikam || k8.12 ||
tena mithyāvihitayā bhaktyā bhoktuṃ nimantritaḥ |
vijñāya sarvaṃ sarvajñastatheti prāha sasmitaḥ || k8.13 ||
viṣagniyogakupitāṃ patnīṃ saddharmavādinīm |
gṛhe babandha śrīguptaḥ śaṅkito mantraviśravāt || k8.14 ||
atha vijñātavṛtto'pi bhagavān svayamāyayau |
vandyamāno jagadvandyaiścaturmukhamukhaiḥ suraiḥ || k8.15 ||
śrīguptasya tamārambhaṃ vivedaṃ nagare janaḥ |
dikṣu dhāvati pāpānāṃ suguptamapi pātamak || k8.16 ||
tataḥ kaścitsamabhyetya bhagavantamupāsakaḥ |
uvāca caraṇālīnāścintayan dahanaṃ viṣam || k8.17 ||
mithyānamrahpriyālāpī guḍhavahniviṣānnadaḥ |
parihāryaḥ prayatnena bhagavanneṣa durjanah || k8.18 ||
kuryādanārye nāśvāsi kāryaṃ mādhuryamāśriye |
antracchedī vigīrṇo hi madhudigdhamukhaḥ kṣuraḥ || k8.19 ||
nānyastutiṃ guṇadveṣī sahate guṇināṃ khalaḥ |
santastuṣyanti yenaiva tenakupyanti durjanāḥ || k8.20 ||
tvayi lokatraye netraśatapatravikāśini |
asya rāhoḥ padaṃ prāpte nāndhībhavati kiṃ jagat || k8.21 ||
tacchrutvā bhagavānūce kiṃcitsmitasitāṃśubhiḥ |
tannikārogratimiraṃ dūrātpariharanniva || k8.22 ||
na mamāṅgaṃ spṛśatyagniḥ prabahvatyapi vā viṣam |
paradveṣadaridrāṇāṃ doṣo'pi nirupadravaḥ || k8.23 ||
aroṣaśītalaṃ cetaḥ siktaṃ yasya śamāmṛtaiḥ |
kiṃ karotyanalastasya viṣaṃ vā viṣayadviṣaḥ || k8.24 ||
viṣāyate tu pīyūṣaṃ kusumaṃ kuliśāyate |
dveṣadoṣottarasyaiva candanaṃ dahanāyate || k8.25 ||
tiryagyonigatasyāpi bodhisattvapadāsthiteḥ |
kāruṇyamaitrīyuktasya nāgnirdahati vigraham || k8.26 ||
purā kaliṅganṛpatiḥ khaṇḍadvīpābhidhāvanīm |
dadāha mṛgasaṃghānāṃ saṃkṣepe sa samudyataḥ || k8.27 ||
kānane jvalite tasminnekastittiriśāvakaḥ |
maitryā bodhiṃ samālambya dahanapraśamaṃ vyaghāt || k8.28 ||
tasmādadrohamanasāṃ na bhayaṃ vidyate kkacit |
śrūyatāṃ sattvasaṃpatteridamanyacca kautukam || k8.29 ||
avṛṣṭiviṣame kāle muneḥ kasyacidāśrame |
manuṣyasadṛśālāpaḥ śaśakaḥ suciraṃ sthitaḥ || k8.30 ||
kṣutkṣāmaṃ munimālokya phalamūlaparikṣayāt |
uvācācalasaṃkalpastadvyathāvyathitāśayaḥ || k8.31 ||
bhagavanmaṃ māṃsānāṃ saṃprati prāṇavartanam |
kriyatāṃ rakṣaṇīyaṃ tatśarīraṃ dharmasādhanam || k8.32 ||
ityuktvā dāvaśeṣāgnau vikṣepa śahskastanum |
vāryamāṇo'pi yatnena praṇayānmuninā muhyuḥ || k8.33 ||
tasya sattvaprabhāveṇa jvalajjvālākulo'nalaḥ |
prayayau majṇjuśiñjānabhramarāmbhojakhaṇḍatām || k8.34 ||
so'pi divyavapustatra kamale mahati sthitaḥ |
praṇamyamāno munirbhirvidadhe dharmadeśanām || k8.35 ||
iti bodhipravṛttānāṃ na vahnerna viṣādbhayam |
bhagavān kathayitveti śrīguptabhavanaṃ yayau || k8.36 ||
tatra tena praviśyaiva nikṣipte dakṣiṇe pade |
babhūvāgnikhadā mañjuguñjadbhṛṅgasarojinī || k8.37 ||
dṛṣṭvopaviṣṭaṃ śrīguptastaṃ sarorihavistare |
taddṛṣṭinaṣṭakāluṣyaḥ provācaḥ caraṇānataḥ || k8.38 ||
bhagavanmama pāpasya kṣantavyo'yaṃ vyatikramaḥ |
mohāndhapatite rucyaṃ kāruṇyamadhikaṃ satām || k8.39 ||
mamākalyāṇamitreṇa yo'yaṃ pāpapathe kṛtaḥ |
upadeśaḥ pramoheṇa tatra trāṇaṃ bhavatsmṛtiḥ || k8.40 ||
viṣadigdharasaṃ sarvaṃ bhojyaṃ te kalpitaṃ mayā |
aho mamaiva saṃkrāntaṃ paścāttapamayaṃ viṣam || k8.41 ||
iti bruvāṇaṃ śrīguptaṃ sāśrunetraṃ kṛpānidhiḥ |
dṛṣṭvā babhāṣe bhagavān bhikṣusaṃghasya śṛṇyavataḥ || k8.42 ||
viṣādaṃ mā kṛthāḥ sādho na vayaṃ vimukhāstvayi |
ghoravairaviṣatyāgānnaivāsmāstapate viṣam || k8.43 ||
vārāṇasyāṃ purā śrīmān brahmadatto'bhavannṛpaḥ |
abhūdanupamā nāmatasya prāṇasamāśrayā || k8.44 ||
suvarṇabhāsasaṃjñasya tatpurāntavanasthiteḥ |
ravaṃ mayūrarājasya sā kadācidathāśṛṇot || k8.45 ||
sā tasya śadamākarṇya veṇuvīṇāsvanopamam |
kimetaditi papraccha naranāthaṃ sakautukā || k8.46 ||
rājovāca vanānte'sminnasti ratnacchadaḥ śikhī |
madhuraṃyojanavyāpi yasyaitatkaṇṭhakūjitam || k8.47 ||
iti bruvāṇo nṛpatistatsaṃdarśanamarthitaḥ |
premaprayatnaiḥ preyasyā prahasan punarabravīt || k8.48 ||
darśanaṃ durlabhaṃ mugdhe tadvidhādbhutarūpiṇaḥ |
tathāpi yadi nirbandhaḥ kriyate tatpariśramaḥ || k8.49 ||
ityuktvā nṛpatistasya grahaṇe jālajīvinaḥ |
vyasṛjattasya saṃrpāptyaiḥ vidhāya vadhasaṃvidam || k8.50 ||
vaśīkṛto na vettyeva mohādakṣiparīkṣayā |
anurāgāhataḥ strībhirakarmāṇyapi kāryate || k8.51 ||
prāptānāṃ praṇayātpatnyāḥ prauḍhāyāḥ pādapīṭhatām |
īrṣyayaiva vinaśyanti dhīdhṛtismṛtikīrtayaḥ || k8.52 ||
tataḥ śākunikairnyastāḥ pāśabandhāḥ pade pade |
prabhāvādbarhirājasyvyaśīryantaiva saṃtatam || k8.53 ||
duḥkhitān yatnavaiphalyādbhītānnṛpatiśāsanāt |
mayūrarājastān dṛṣṭvā karuṇākulatāṃ yayau || k8.54 ||
so'cintayadaho bhītāḥ kṣmāpateḥ śrūraśāsanāt |
madbandhane visaṃvādādvarākā jālajīvinah || k8.55 ||
saṃcintya kṛpayā spaṣṭagbhirvisṛjya tān |
nṛpamānāyya tadveśma tenaiva sahito yayau || k8.56 ||
sa tatrāntaḥpure nityaṃ sabhāryeṇa mahībhujā |
pūjyamānaḥ paricayāduvāsa vihitādaraḥ || k8.57 ||
snigdhaśyāmāmbudatviṣā sunīlamaṇiveśmasu |
citrapatrarucā cakre saṃsaktendrāyudhabhamam || k8.58 ||
atha digjayayātrāyāṃ kadācidvasudhādhipaḥ |
yayau tadupacārāya devīmādiśya sādaraḥ || k8.59 ||
tataścānupamā devī patyau yāte pramādinī |
rūpayauvanadarpāndhā nāluloke kulasthitim || k8.60 ||
taruṇaṃ prekṣya rāgiṇyāstasyāḥ kandarpaviplave |
bhūyaḥ pralambhabhīteva lajjā dūrataraṃ yayau || k8.61 ||
malinaḥ kuṭilastīkṣṇaḥ karṇasaṃsparśanocitaḥ |
capalaścapalākṣīṇāṃ sudṛśāṃ sadṛśaḥ kramaḥ || k8.62 ||
vividhonmādakāriṇyaḥ saṃsāramakarākare |
caranti prāṇahāriṇyaḥ kālakūṭacchadāḥ striyaḥ || k8.63 ||
kusumātsukumārasya krūrasya krakacādapi |
ko jānāti paricchedaṃ strīṇāṃ citrasya cetasaḥ || k8.64 ||
pracarantīṃ priyāṃ kaṇṭhe kṛtvā ye yānti nirvṛtim |
śītalāṃ vimalāṃ snigdhāṃ khaṅgadhārāṃ pibanti te || k8.65 ||
sācintayatsthitaḥ śalyamayamantaḥpure mama |
mayūrarājaḥ śīlajñaḥ puruṣālāpaveṣṭitaḥ || k8.66 ||
kathayiṣyatyavaśyaṃ me vṛttameṣa mahīpateḥ |
nindyaṃ karma kṛtaṃ tāvadadhunā kiṃ karomyaham || k8.67 ||
āstāṃ parijñātatattvo marmajño'sau vidagdhadhīḥ |
jātā me kṛtapāpāyāḥ śaṅkā niścetaneṣvapi || k8.68 ||
iti saṃcintya sā tasya saviṣaṃ bhojanaṃ dadau |
rāgamattāḥ khalāyattāḥ kiṃ kiṃ kurvanti na striyaḥ || k8.69 ||
tayopācaryamāṇasya saviṣaiḥ pānabhojanaiḥ |
vivṛddhā barhirājasya ruruce rucirā ruciḥ || k8.70 ||
svasthamālokya taṃ devī rahasyodbhedaśaṅkitā |
śanaiḥ śokāmayagrasyā trasyā tatyāja jīvitam || k8.71 ||
evaṃ tasya viṣeṇāpi naiva glānirajāyata |
mahatāṃ cittavaimalyaṃ nirviṣaṃ kurute viṣam || k8.72 ||
rāgo viṣaṃ viṣaṃ moho dveṣaśca viṣamaṃ viṣam |
buddho dharmastathā saṃghaḥ satyaṃ ca paramāmṛtam || k8.73 ||
ghoraṃ viṣaṃ sṛjati mohamahāmburāśiḥ ghoraṃ viṣaṃ sṛjati rāgamahoragaśca |
ghoraṃ viṣaṃ sṛjati vairavanāvaniśca janmakramo'sti viṣamasya viṣasya nānyaḥ || k8.74 ||
adharmakāmaḥ kṛtavānevamevānyajanmani |
śrīgupto'gnikhadāṃ sāpi tasyābhūtsahadharmiṇī || k8.75 ||
ityuktvā bhagavān samyakkaruṇālokanāmbubhiḥ |
cakāra vītarajasaṃ śrīguptaṃ śāsanonmukham || k8.76 ||
kalitakuśalaḥ śrīgupto'tha prakāśapadāptaye
śaraṇagamanānyeva trīṇi smaran vimalasmṛtiḥ |
jinaparicayātpuṇyaṃ lebhe satāṃ hi vilokanaṃ
bhavati mahate kalyāṇāya pramodasukhāya ca || k8.77 ||
śrīguptasya nikārakilbiṣajuṣo'pyajñānamohāpahaḥ
kṛtvāvaśyamanugraheṇa bhagavān kāruṇyapuṇyodyataḥ |
bhikṣūṇāṃ bhavasaṃkṣayāya vidadhe nirvairatāśāsanaṃ
yenaite na bhavanti bandhabhavane bhūyo bhavagranthaye || k8.78 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ śrīguptāvadānaṃ nāma aṣṭamaḥ pallavaḥ ||


9. jyotiṣkāvadānam |

dhanyānāmaśivaṃ bibharti śubhatāṃ bhavyasvabhāvodbhavaṃ
mūrkhāṇāṃ kuśalaṃ prayātyahitatāmityeṣa lakṣyaḥ kramaḥ |
niśīthatimirāndhyamauṣadhivanasyātyantakāntipradaṃ
etaccaulukakūladṛṣṭahataye sarvatra maitraṃ mahaḥ || k9.1 ||
purā rājagṛhābhikhye bimbisārasya bhūpateḥ |
abhūtpauraḥ subhadrākhyaḥ paripūrṇagṛhasthiteḥ || k9.2 ||
maurkhyānmohaprapannasya sarvadarśanavidviṣaḥ |
tasya kṣapaṇakeṣveva babhūvābhyadhikādaraḥ || k9.3 ||
tasya satyavatī nāma jāyābhijanaśālinī |
garbhamādhatta pūrṇendubimbaṃ pauradarīva dik || k9.4 ||
kalandakanivāsākhyo veṇukānanasaṃśrayaḥ |
kadācitbhagavātbuddhaḥ prāptaḥ piṇḍāya targṛham || k9.5 ||
pūjāṃ sabhāryaḥ kṛtvātmai taṃ sa papraccha sādaraḥ |
garbhasthitamapatyaṃ yatkiṃrūpaṃ tadbhaviṣyati || k9.6 ||
so'vadatsaṃpadaṃ bhuktvā putraste divyamānuṣīm |
pravrajyayā śāsane me saṃyukto muktimeṣyati || k9.7 ||
yāte bhagavati spaṣṭamityādiśya nijāśrayam |
abhyāyayau gṛhapaterbhūrikaḥ kṣapaṇo gṛham || k9.8 ||
bhagavadbhāṣitaṃ tattu subhadreṇa niveditam |
śrutvā kṣapaṇakahkṣipramabhūddeṣaviṣākulaḥ || k9.9 ||
gaṇayitvā sa suciraṃ grahajñānakṛtaśramaḥ |
yadevoktaṃ bhagavatā praśne'paśyattathāvidham || k9.10 ||
so'cintayadayo satyamuktaṃ tena na saṃśayaḥ |
tatprabhāvopamādāya kiṃ svasatyaṃ vadāmyaham || k9.11 ||
tasya sarvajñatāṃ vetti subhadro yadi madgirā |
tadeṣa kṣapaṇaśraddhāṃ tyakṣyati śramaṇādarāt || k9.12 ||
iti saṃcintya sāmarṣaḥ sa subhadramabhāṣata |
asatyemetatkathitaṃ tena sarvajñamāninā || k9.13 ||
manuṣyaḥ kathamāpnoti devārhāṃ divyasaṃpadam |
pravrajyā kiṃ tu satyeva kathaṃ tenāsya cintitā || k9.14 ||
kṣīṇaḥ kṣudupasaṃtapto yasya nāstyanyato gatiḥ |
tasya tasya subhikṣārhaṃ śaraṇaṃ śramaṇavratam || k9.15 ||
paśyāmyahaṃ gṛhapate pramāṇaṃ yadi madvacaḥ |
pratyutāyaṃ śiśurjātaḥ kulaṃ saṃtāpayiṣyati || k9.16 ||
ityudīrya kṣapaṇake yāte gṛhapatiściram |
vicārya vidadhe tāṃ tāṃ yuktiṃ garbhanipātane || k9.17 ||
yadā dravyaprayoge'pi naiva garbhahparicyutaḥ |
tadāsya patnīmavadhīdekānte haṭhamardanaiḥ || k9.18 ||
tataḥ śītavanaṃ tasyāṃ śmaśānaṃ tena pāpinā |
prāpitāyāṃ kṣapaṇakāstadvārtānanditā jaguḥ || k9.19 ||
aho batāho sarvajñaḥ śiśoḥ satyaṃ taduktavān |
seyaṃ sūnāvajāte'sya jananī pañcatāṃ gatā || k9.20 ||
iyaṃ sā śrīḥ śiśordivyā soktā divyamanuṣyatā |
iyaṃ ca sāsya pravrajyā yatkukṣau nidhanaṃ gataḥ || k9.21 ||
iti teṣāṃ pravādena sopahāsena sarvataḥ |
śmaśānadarśanāyaiva babhūva janasaṃgamaḥ || k9.22 ||
atrāntare divyadṛśā bhagavān bhūtabhāvanaḥ |
sarvaṃ vijñāya tadbuddhaḥ pradadhyau sasmitaḥ kṣaṇam || k9.23 ||
aho mohānubandhena dūrasthairapi dehinām |
ālokaśchādyate mūrkhairmeghairiva vikāribhiḥ || k9.24 ||
śubhaṃ kṣapayatā tena kṣapaṇena sa mugdhadhīḥ |
aho gṛhapatiḥ pāpādakāryamapi kāritaḥ || k9.25 ||
iti saṃcintya bahgavān svayaṃ bhikṣugaṇairvṛtaḥ |
yayau śītavanaṃ kṣipraṃ śmaśānaṃ karuṇākulaḥ || k9.26 ||
śmaśānacārikāṃ jñātvā rājā bhagavataḥ svayam |
bimbisāraḥ sahāmātyaistāmeva bhivamāyayau || k9.27 ||
tataḥ subhadrajāyāyāṃ prakṣiptāyāṃ citānale |
kukṣiṃ bhittvāmbujāsīnaḥ śiśuḥ sūrya ivodyayau || k9.28 ||
jvālitāṇalamadhyasthaṃ taṃ kaścinnāgrahīdyadā |
tadā janasamūhasya hāhākāro mahānabhūt || k9.29 ||
tatastaṃ saṃbhramāvṛddhagatiḥ sugataśāsanāt |
kumārabhṛtyo jagrāha jīvakākhyaḥ kumārakam || k9.30 ||
jināvalokanenaiva bālakagrahaṇakṣaṇe |
abhūccitānalastasya haricandanaśītalaḥ || k9.31 ||
jīvantaṃ jvalanānmuktaṃ ruciraṃ vīkṣya dārakam |
vailakṣyeṇa kṣapaṇakāḥ kṣaṇaṃ tasthurmṛtā iva || k9.32 ||
tataḥ subhadraṃ bhagavān sarvabhūtahite rataḥ |
babhāṣe vismayodbhāntaṃ putro'yaṃ gṛhyatāmiti || k9.33 ||
sa tu dolākulamatiḥ kiṃ karomīti saṃśayāt |
kṣapanānāṃ mukhānyeva śikṣāyai kṣaṇamaikṣata || k9.34 ||
te tamūcurna bālo'yaṃ grāhyaḥ śmāśānavahnijaḥ |
yatrāyaṃ tiṣṭhati vyaktaṃ na bhavatyeva tadgṛham || k9.35 ||
iti teṣāṃ girā mūrkhaḥ sa jagrāha na taṃ yadā |
tadā kṣitipatirbālamādade jinaśāsanāt || k9.36 ||
jyotirmadhyādavāptasya jyotiṣkasadṛśatviṣaḥ |
jyotiṣka iti nāmāsya cakāra bhagavān svayam || k9.37 ||
tasya pravardhamānasya bhūpālabahvane śiśoḥ |
deśāntaragataḥ kāle mātulaḥ samupāyayau || k9.38 ||
sa viditvā svasurvṛttaṃ nidhanaṃ putrajanmani |
kopātsubhadramabhyetya kampamānaḥ samabhyaghāt || k9.39 ||
mūrkha kṣapaṇabhaktena tadgirā hatayoṣitā |
tvayā tyaktasvaputreṇa kiṃ nāma sukṛtā kṛtam || k9.40 ||
niścetanāḥ svabhāvena paramantrasamutthitāḥ |
sahanto'pi vinighnanti vetālā iva durjanāḥ || k9.41 ||
adhunaiva na gṛhṇāsi yadi rājagṝhātsutam |
tatte strīvadhamuddhuṣya kārayāmyarthanigraham || k9.42 ||
ityuktastena tadbhītyā sa bhūpatigṛhātsutam |
ānināya cirānmuktamakāmena mahībhujā || k9.43 ||
tataḥ subhadre kālena kālasya vaśamāgate |
abhūnnirdirvibhūtīnāṃ jyotiṣko'rka iva tviṣām || k9.44 ||
arthikalpadrumaḥ prāya saṃpadaṃ divyamānuṣīm |
sa buddhadharmasaṃgheṣu śaraṇyeṣvakaronmatim || k9.45 ||
tadbhaktyupanataṃ divyaratnasaṃcayamadbhutam |
pradadau bhikṣusaṃghebhyaḥ puṇyaratnārjanodyataḥ || k9.46 ||
tasya devanikāyebhyaḥ sāścaryā vividharddhayaḥ |
svayamevāyayurveśma mahodadhimivāpagāḥ || k9.47 ||
tṛṇe ratne ca samadhīrbhavagānapi tadgṛhe |
cakre tadanurodhena ratnapātraparigraham || k9.48 ||
sa divyavastrayugalaṃ yaśasāmupamākṣamam |
prāpa puṇyapaṇakrītaṃ nijaṃ gṛhamivāmalam || k9.49 ||
kadācidatha tadvastraṃsnānārdraṃ nyastamātape |
samīraṇenāpahṛtaṃ nyapatanmūrdhi bhūpateḥ || k9.50 ||
vilokyāpūrvaruciraṃ jyotiṣkasya tadaṃśukam |
vidyaśrīvismito rājā tṛṇaṃ mene nijaśriyam || k9.51 ||
bhoktuṃ nimantritaḥ prāpya tasya ratnamayaṃ gṛham |
nṛpatiḥ svargamajñāsītjyotiṣkabhavanasthitaḥ || k9.52 ||
atha kālena bhūpālaḥ putreṇājātaśatruṇā |
chadmanā rājyalubdhena dharmaśīlo nipātitaḥ || k9.53 ||
atīte sadguṇe rājñi tasmin kṛtayugopame |
adharma iva sa prāpa rājyaṃ rājavarātmajaḥ || k9.54 ||
sa bhūbhṛddurlabhāṃ dṛṣṭvā jyotiṣkasya gṛhe śriyam |
tamuvāca samabhyetya matpitrā tvaṃ vivardhitaḥ || k9.55 ||
bhrātā tavāhaṃ dharmeṇa vibhavārdhaṃ prayaccha me |
na cedbhāgadhandrohātkalireva prajāyate || k9.56 ||
ityuktastena kauṭilyātjyotiṣkaḥ krūrakāriṇā |
ratnapūrṇaṃ gṛhaṃ tasmai datvā prāyātparaṃ gṛham || k9.57 ||
sā divyaratnarucirasphītā lokopakāriṇī |
hyotiṣkamevānuyayau śrīḥ prabheva divākaram || k9.58 ||
punastyaktāpi sā saṃpatsaptakṛtvaḥ prabhāvatī |
jyotiṣkamaspṛṣṭanṛpā sādhvī parimivāyayau || k9.59 ||
sarvasvāharaṇodyuktaṃ dasyucaurādiyuktibhiḥ |
jyotiṣkaḥ kupitaṃ jñātvā nirviṇṇaḥ samacintayat || k9.60 ||
apuṇyaparipākeṇa prajānāṃ janakopamaḥ |
saṃyātaḥ smṛtiśeṣatvaṃ rājā vātsalyapeśalaḥ || k9.61 ||
ko'nyastatsadṛśo yasminnirvyājasarale prajāḥ |
pitarīva kṛtāśvāsāḥ sukhaṃ rātriṣu śerate || k9.62 ||
dhaninastṛṇavatprāpyāḥ prāpyante ratnavadbudhāḥ |
amṛtādapi duṣprāpyaḥ saujanyasaralo janah || k9.63 ||
nirvyājavaidagdhyajuṣāmamugdhasaralātmanām |
anuddhatonnatānāṃ ca viralaṃ janma tādṛśām || k9.64 ||
adhunā dveṣadurvṛttaḥ pravṛttanikṛtirnṛpaḥ |
pāpapākena lokānāmakāle kalirāgataḥ || k9.65 ||
mitre jagati yāte'staṃ tasmin bhāsvati bhūpatau |
doṣodayaḥ pravṛddho'yamandhakārāya tatsutaḥ || k9.66 ||
nūnaṃ satāmatītānāṃ niṣkāraṇasuhṛtkhalaḥ |
yadvṛttaparabhāgeṇa yaśasteṣāṃ prakāśate || k9.67 ||
tasmādiyaṃ parityājyā nṛpatyadhiṣṭhitā mahī |
kāle kalau kṣitīśe ca janānāṃ jīvitaṃ kutaḥ || k9.68 ||
varaparicayodārā dārāhsatāṃ guṇināṃ guṇāḥ
kulamavikalaṃ bhavyā bhūtiryaśaḥ śaśisaṃnibham |
sthitisamucitaṃ vṛttaṃ vittamanimittamanāpadaṃ
guṇavati nṛpe sarvaṃ bhavatyapāṃśulaṃ prajākulam || k9.69 ||
dharmadrumasya dhanamūlasamudgatasya nirdoṣakāmakusumapravarojjvalasya |
lokaḥ sukhāni kila puṇyaphalāni bhuṅkte hato na cetkunṛpatervinipātavātaiḥ || k9.70 ||
kaliḥ kālaḥ patirbālastatpratāpaścitānalah |
akālaviplavottālakhalavetālasaṃkulaḥ || k9.71 ||
prītirviṣaṇṇā khinnā dhīḥ sukhaśrīrgatayauvanā |
adhunā vibhavābhoge bhogayoge na me ruciḥ || k9.72 ||
dhanaṃ bhūmirgṛhaḥ dārāḥ sutā bhṛtyāḥ paricchadāḥ |
aho niravadhiḥ puṃsāmādhivyādhiparigrahaḥ || k9.73 ||
yathā yathā vivardhante grīṣmoṣmaviṣamāḥ śriyaḥ |
tathā tathā jvalatyeva tṛṣṇātāpaḥ śarīriṇām || k9.74 ||
pravṛddhairapi vittaughe rājanyopārjitairnṛṇām |
lavaṇābdheriva jalairvitṛṣṇā naiva jāyate || k9.75 ||
nāsti nāstītyasaṃtoṣādya eva dhanināṃ japaḥ |
pubarbhave bhavetko vā sa eva praśamo yadi || k9.76 ||
kiṃ vittairdurnimittaiḥ kalikalahamohalobhānuvṛttaiḥ kiṃ bhogairviprayogairvyasanaśatapatanābhyāsasaṃsaktarogaiḥ |
kiṃ vā mithyābhimānairnarapatisadanaprātasevāvamānaiḥ
asmin vairāgyameva kṣayasamayabhaye bhogyamārogyayogyam || k9.77 ||
atikrānte kāle svajanasuhṛdālokavimale
samāpanne mohaprabala (tara) kāluṣyamaline |
sukhāśvāsahpuṃsāṃ praśamasalilasnātamanasāṃ
parityaktāyāse vijanavanavāse parivayaḥ || k9.78 ||
iti saṃcintya sa ciraṃ paraṃ vairāgyamāyayau |
duḥkhaṃ mohāya mūrkhāṇāṃ vivekāya ca dhīmatām || k9.79 ||
sa datvā sarvamarthibhyaḥ prayayauḥ sugatāśramam |
śrīśṛṅkhalākṛṣṭamatirna hi satyasukhonmukhaḥ || k9.80 ||
yadaiva rājhaṃsena smaryate śuci mānasam |
tadaivāsmai vasumatī sarasīva na rocate || k9.81 ||
yāte duḥsahamohadhūmamaline bhogānurāgānale
saṃtoṣāmṛtanirjhareṇa manasi prāte śanaiḥ śītatām |
naitāḥ pānamadottaraṅgavicaladvārāṅgarāṅganābhaṅgura-
bhrūbhaṅgakṣaṇasaṃgamāḥ śamavatāṃ kurvanti vighnaṃ śriyaḥ || k9.82 ||
sarvajñaśāsanavinaṣṭabhavādhvakaṣṭaḥ pravrajyayā vimalameva padaṃ praviṣṭaḥ |
saṃprāpya sarvasamatāmasamaprakāśaḥ nirlakṣyamokṣagamanāya munirbabhūva || k9.83 ||
tāṃ bodhisiddhimālokya jyotiṣkasya savismayaiḥ |
bhikṣubhirbhagavān pṛṣṭaḥ prāgvṛttāntamabhāṣataḥ || k9.84 ||
janmakṣetraśatoptānāṃ bījānāmiva karmaṇām |
bhujyate phalasaṃpattiravisaṃvādinī janaiḥ || k9.85 ||
rājño bandhumataḥ puryāṃ bandhumatyāṃ mahāyaśāḥ |
abhūdanaṅgano nāma śrīmān gṛhapatiḥ purā || k9.86 ||
śāstātha samyaksaṃbyddho viaśvī nāma tā purīm |
janacārikayā prātaḥ kadācitsukṛtaiḥ satām || k9.87 ||
dvāṣaṣṭibhiḥ sa bhikṣūṇāṃ sahasaiḥ parivāritaḥ |
śraddhayānaṅganenaitya praṇamyopanimantritaḥ || k9.88 ||
sarvopakaraṇaistena traimāsaṃ paricāritaḥ |
yathā tathaiva rājñāpi praṇipatya nimantritaḥ || k9.89 ||
bhogaiḥ spardhānubandhena sa tābhyāmadhivāsitaḥ |
anaṅganena paurārhairbhūpālārhaiśca bhūbhujā || k9.90 ||
gajadhvajamaṇicchatracāmarodārayā śriyā |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā pūjitaṃ rājña cintārto'bhūdanaṅganaḥ || k9.91 ||
tasya sattvāvadātasya pakṣapātī śatakratuḥ |
cakāra divyayā lakṣmyā sāhāyyāṃ jinapūjane || k9.92 ||
sa tayā divyayā bhūtyā bhagavantamapūjayat |
yadagre cakravartidhrīrlajjābhājanatāṃ yayau || k9.93 ||
ratnairnyakṣatacandrasūryabhānarākaṃkīraṇairāvaṇabhair(?)
amlānāmbaragandhamālyaśabalaiḥ kampadrumāṇāṃ phalaiḥ |
bhaktiprahvaścīvilāsacanāhelocchasaccāmaraṃ
tenābhyarcitamākalayya sugataṃ lajjānato'bhūnnṛpaḥ || k9.94 ||
iti bahutaraṃ bhaktyā śāstuḥ phalaṃ tadanaṅganaḥ
śubhapariṇateḥ puṇyodāraḥ purā samavāptavān |
vimalamanasastasyaivāsau kṣaṇapraṇidhānataḥ para
iva ravijyotiṣko'bhūtsa eva padāśritaḥ || k9.95 ||
ityāha vimalajñānaprakāśitagatrtrayaḥ |
praṇīdhānopadeśāya bhikṣūṇāṃ bhagavān jinaḥ || k9.96 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ jyotiṣkāvadānaṃ nāma navamaḥ pallavaḥ ||


10. sundarīnandāvadānam |

te ke'pi sattvahitasaṃnihitānukampā bhavyā bahvanti bhuvane bahvabhītibhājām |
vātsalyapeśaladhiyaḥ kuśalāya puṃsāṃ kurvanti ye varamanugrahamāgraheṇa || k10.1 ||
nyagrodhārāmanirataṃ druṣṭuṃ kapilavastuni |
bhagavantaṃ yayau nandaḥ śākyarājasutaḥ purā || k10.2 ||
naiṣkamyadeśanāmantrakathāntesa puraḥsthitam |
uvāca bhagavān prītyā pravrajyā gṛhyatāmiti || k10.3 ||
prasādyābhinivedyāsya taṃ nandaḥ pratyabhāṣataḥ |
bhagavan puṇyalābhāpi pravrajyābhimatā na me || k10.4 ||
sarvopasthāyako bhūtvā bhikṣusaṃghaṃ yathepsitaiḥ |
sarvopakaraṇaistāvatbhikṣyaṃ paricarāmyaham || k10.5 ||
ityuktvā ratnamukuṭaiḥ spṛṣṭatatpādapaṅkajaḥ |
rājaputraḥ svabhavanaṃ yayau jāyāsamutsukaḥ || k10.6 ||
sa sundarī samāsādya dayitāṃ ratisundarīm |
vijahāra varodyānaṃ muhūrtavirahāsahaḥ || k10.7 ||
tataḥ kadācidbhagavān prakṛtyaiva guṇipriyaḥ |
svayaṃ nandasya bhavanaṃ bhikṣusaṃghaiḥ sahāyayau || k10.8 ||
sānandavadanaḥ kṛtvā nandastatpādavandanām |
taṃ mahāharsanāsīnaṃ pūjayitvā vyajijñapat || k10.9 ||
keṣāṃ puṇyaprarohāṇāṃ paripāko'yamīdṛśaḥ |
kṛto'yaṃ yadbhagavatā darśanānugrahaḥ svayam || k10.10 ||
smaraṇaṃ śravaṇaṃ vāpi darśanaṃ vā mahātmanām |
seyaṃ kuśalavallīnāṃ mahatī phalasaṃtatiḥ || k10.11 ||
asya maitrasya mahatastadālokasya darśanāt |
hṛdayasya vikāsaśrīḥ kasya nāma na jāyate || k10.12 ||
dānādapi priyataraṃ puṇyādapi mahāphalam |
sadācārādapi śrlāghyaṃ mahatāṃ kilaṃ darśanam || k10.13 ||
iti tasya bruvāṇasya bhaktipraṇayapeśalam |
abhinandya puraḥ pūjāṃ bhagavān gantumudyayau || k10.14 ||
anuvavrāja taṃ nandaḥ svacche kanakabhājane |
varopacāramādāya madhuraṃ svamivāśayam || k10.15 ||
pahcādvrajantamālokya bhaktyā bhagavataḥ pathi |
niraikṣata kaṭākṣeṇa sundarī virahāsahā || k10.16 ||
gurūṇāmagre sā saralataralāṃ locanagatiṃ
vihāyaiva trāsāñcitamukutitākṣī priyatamā |
nirīkṣyālakṣyaṃ yatkṣaṇamavanatābhūttadadhikaṃ
na gantavyaṃ nāthetyavadadivaṃ maonādavacanam || k10.17 ||
nandaḥ praṇayiṇīṃ dṛṣṭvā socchvāsaṃ calitānanām |
ayamāgata evāhamacirādityabhāṣata || k10.18 ||
tataḥ svamāśramaṃ prāptaṃ bhagavantaṃ kṛtāñjaliḥ |
vrajāmi svagṛhaṃ tāvadityāha virahāsahaḥ || k10.19 ||
tatastamāsanāsīnaḥ praṇataṃ bhagavān puraḥ |
uvāca sasmitaṃ keyaṃ gamane saṃprati tvarā || k10.20 ||
viṣayāsvādasauhārdasaṃmohārditavetasām |
aho gṛhasukheṣveva nirvedavimukhā matiḥ || k10.21 ||
guṇābharaṇamevāyurvivekābharaṇo guṇaḥ |
praśamābahraṇaścāsau vairāgyābharaṇaḥ śamaḥ || k10.22 ||
tajjāḍyaṃ tadasahyaśalyaśalakaṃ nyastaṃ suhṛccetasi
prājñistadgaṇitaṃ virācasamayairvaiphalyamevāyuṣaḥ |
yadvairāgyavivekaśūnyamanasāmete paśūnāṃ yathā
yāntyāyānti ca cakranemicalanairnirlakṣaṇānāṃ kṣaṇāḥ || k10.23 ||
puṇyaṃ sattvavatāṃ śrutaṃ matimatāṃ śīlaṃ ca vidyāvatām
sarvaṃ bhāgyavatāṃ sukhaṃ śamavatāṃ naiva kkacitdurlabham |
duṣprāpastu samastavastuvasateḥ svalpāṃśako'pyāyuṣaḥ
tadyasya kṣayameti niṣphalatayā śocyāya tasmai namaḥ || k10.24 ||
vāmāvarte viṣayajaladhau pūrṇalāvaṇyasāre darpotsarpadviṣamamakarāṅkodbhavakṣobhyamāṇe |
nityāsannaprabalavirajaprajjvaladvāḍavāgnau
puṃsāṃ setustaraṇaśaraṇe tīvravairāgyameva || k10.25 ||
tasmādgṛhāṇa pravrajyāṃ rājaputra jitendriyaḥ |
etāḥ striya iva kṣipraṃ samagamasukhāḥ śriyaḥ || k10.26 ||
kriyatāṃ kuśalāyaiva brahmacaryaparigrahaḥ |
tyajyatāmeṣa niḥsāragṛhasaṃsāradurgrahaḥ || k10.27 ||
iti śrutvā bhagavataḥ karuṇābahraṇaṃ vacaḥ |
pratyabhāṣata taṃ nandaḥ pūrvapraṇayayantritaḥ || k10.28 ||
sadā bhavadupāyaiva pravrajyā bhagavanmama |
bhikṣusaṃghopakārārthe gṛha evādaraḥ param || k10.29 ||
ityuktvā bhagavadvākyamatikrāntumanīśvaraḥ |
kṛṣyamāṇaḥ priyāpremṇā so'bhūddolākulāśayaḥ || k10.30 ||
punaḥ punaścakārāsya bhagavān vratadeśanām |
upakārodyatāḥ santaścintayanti na yogyatām || k10.31 ||
yadā necchati nandastāṃ pravrajyāmajitendriyaḥ |
tadāsya bahgavadvākyamapatadvapuṣi svayam || k10.32 ||
nandaḥ kāṣāyāvaraṇaḥ pātrapāṇiḥ sa tatkṣaṇam |
babhau cābhīkararucirmahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaiḥ || k10.33 ||
sa śāsanājjinasyābhūdāraṇyapiṇḍapātrikah |
ākārādanagāratāṃ prayātaḥ pāṃśukūlikaḥ || k10.34 ||
priyāmuvāha satataṃ śyāmāṃ pravrajito'pi saḥ |
śaśāṅka iva saṃvyaktāṃ hṛdaye lāñchanacchavim || k10.35 ||
manasaḥ sphaṭikasyeva na vidmaḥ kena vartmanā |
rāgaḥ ko'pi viśatyantarnāpaiti kṣālito'pi yaḥ || k10.36 ||
virahāpāṇḍuraruciḥ saṃsaktāruṇacīvaraḥ |
sa saṃdhyābhrakalaṅkasya jahāra śaśinaḥ śriyam || k10.37 ||
vicaran virahākṣamaḥ sa vismṝtadhṛtirvane |
janmavidyāmanaṅgasya na visasmāra sundarīm || k10.38 ||
saṃpūrṇacandravadanāvadanadhyānaniścalaḥ |
acintyacciraṃ tattadupaviṣya śilātale || k10.39 ||
aho bhagavatāpyeṣa kṛto yatnādanugrahaḥ |
nāyāti mama vaimalyaṃ rāgādhiṣṭhitacetasaḥ || k10.40 ||
śrutaṃ saṃsāracaritaṃ niḥsaṅgavratamāsthitam |
tathāpi mṛgasāvākṣīṃ na vismarati me manaḥ || k10.41 ||
kāntākuṅkumalagnarāgasubhage gātre kṛtaṃ cīvaraṃ
tatpīnastanamaṇḍalapraṇayinā pātraṃ dhṛtaṃ pāṇinā |
rāgo'yaṃ mama vardhate'sya yadi vānyasyeva vṛddhiḥ paraṃ yadbodhivyavadhānabhūtamaniśaṃ dhyānaṃ tadālambanam || k10.42 ||
kṣaṇena māṃ prāptamavehi mugdhe tāmevamuktvā gamane purastāt |
aho mayā darśanavighnabhūtaṃ paścātkṛtaghnavratametadāttam || k10.43 ||
no gantavyamiti prakampataralā vākyaṃ yadūce na sā
saṃtyajya vyajanaṃ sthite gurujane jagrāha pāṇyañcalam |
yatpādena likhantyapi kṣititalaṃ māmaukṣatālakṣitā
tenātyantaniṣedhamugdhavidhinā baddhaṃ tayā me manaḥ || k10.44 ||
madviyuktā na sā nūnaṃ śete śokapralāpinī |
puline cakravākīva harmye hariṇyalocanā || k10.45 ||
hā priye taktasaktena kitaveneva kevalam |
śritaṃ taccittacaureṇa mithyāvratamidaṃ mayā || k10.46 ||
tyaktvā vratamidaṃ tāvadgacchāmidayitāntikam |
anurāgāgnitaptānāṃ tapastāpo hi duḥsahaḥ || k10.47 ||
rājaputrī cirāyātaṃ nṛśaṃsamavalokya mām |
manyunā navalagnena na jāne kiṃ kariṣyati || k10.48 ||
na sarvatra vikārāya nikāraḥ premaduḥsahaḥ |
durnivāro bhavatyeva snehalīno rajaḥkaṇaḥ || k10.49 ||
yasmin kṣaṇe bhagavatā paśyāmi rahitaṃ vanam |
mayā tadaiva gantavyaṃ gṝhamityeṣa niścayaḥ || k10.50 ||
asminneva śilāpaṭṭe rucirairgiridhātubhiḥ |
likhāmi tāṃ śaśimukhīṃ saṃrpāpyālambanaṃ dhṛteḥ || k10.51 ||
athavā kathamālekhyaviṣayaṃ yāti sā priyā |
saundaryabidnako yasyāḥ sudhākuvalayendavaḥ || k10.52 ||
dṛṣṭirmugdhakurangasaṃcaladalivyāptotpalodvātanī
lāvaṇyodadhikūlavidrumavanaṃ bimbādharāgratviṣaḥ |
nirdoṣāmṛtaraśmisārthasaraṇiḥ sā kāpi vaktradyutiḥ
saundaryaṃ kathameti citrapadavīścaryasāraṃ vapuḥ || k10.53 ||
iti saṃcintya sa śanairālilekha śilātale |
sundarīṃ mukhamuktāśrusnātakampākulāṅguliḥ || k10.54 ||
sa saṃkalpasamudgīrṇaṃ pratibimbamivāśritām |
kṛtvā puraḥ priyatamāmūce'darbāṣpagadgadaḥ || k10.55 ||
priyāmālikhyāhaṃ nikhilasukhavṛṣṭiṃ nayanayor
na paśyāmyudbāṣpaḥ kṣaṇamapi śaraccandravadanām |
ayaṃ nūnaṃ tanvīvirahanirapekṣavratavataḥ
sphurattāpaḥ śāpaḥ kimapi mama pāpādupanataḥ || k10.56 ||
phullāmbhojavanatviṣā spṛhāvaśāsaktāśrutoyaṃ vapus
tatkālopagatāntarāyajanitaḥ kopaḥ samutsṛjyatām |
haṃho sundari dehi me prativacaḥ kiṃ maunamālambase
satyaṃ tvanmayarāgavīcaramidaṃ cittavrataṃ me vratam || k10.57 ||
iti bruvāṇaṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā dūrādālikhitaṃ ca tat |
sāsūyā bhikṣavo'bhyetya bhagavantaṃ babhāṣire || k10.58 ||
bhagavan durvinītasya vātsalyādeva kevalam |
śunaḥ kusumamāleva pravrajyeyaṃ tvayārpitā || k10.59 ||
ākhilya sundarīmukhaṃ nandaḥ svairaṃ śilātale |
tatpralāpajapāsakto dhyānālambanatāṃ gataḥ || k10.60 ||
etadākarṇya bhagavānnandamāhvāyya kananāt |
kimetaditi papraccha priyāvirahamohitam || k10.61 ||
so'bravīdbhagavan satyaṃ kāntāsaktasya me param |
bhikṣūṇāṃ saṃmate'pyasmin vane na ramate matiḥ || k10.62 ||
iti dandavacaḥ śrutvā tamūce bhagavān jinah |
mīlayanneva vaktrendukāntyā rāgasaroruham || k10.63 ||
sādho tāvanna yuktā te saṃrāgānugatā matiḥ |
vighnairnākṛṣyate cetaḥ kalyāṇābhiniveśinām || k10.64 ||
kvāyaṃ yogastanutṛṇatulātyaktabhogābhiyogaḥ
kkāyaṃ nindyaḥ kṣaṇasukhalavāsvādasaṃvāda eṣaḥ |
jātyaivāyaṃ harati kuśalaṃ dustaro māramārgaḥ
premāndhānāṃ bhavati sahasā duḥsaho yoktrajātaḥ || k10.65 ||
ityasya bhagavān kṛtvā ciraṃ vairāgyadeśanām |
saṃsthātavyamihetyuktvā svakṛtyāya svayaṃ yayau || k10.66 ||
tamevāvasaraṃ nandaḥ saṃcintya gamanocitam |
pratasthe svagṛhaṃ hṛṣṭaḥ sundarīdarśanotsukaḥ || k10.67 ||
vrajan dvāri pidhānāptairvihārairbahubhiścirāt |
nagarābhimukhaṃ mārgaṃ sa kathaṃcidavāptavān || k10.68 ||
atha vijñāya sarvajñastaṃ rāgādgantumudyatam |
uvācābhyetya bhagavānnanda tūrṇaṃ kka gamyate || k10.69 ||
sa jagāda vane tāvatbhagavannāsti me ratiḥ |
na hyaviśrāntacittānāṃ kriyā kācitprasīdati || k10.70 ||
sā śrīścāmarahāsinī maṇimayī sā ramyaharmyāvalī
sā bālānilalolacārulatikā kāntā navodyānabhūḥ |
sā tanvī kusumeṣukārmukalatā kṣāmodarī sundarī
no janmāntaravāsanā iva manaḥ saktaṃ vimuñcanti me || k10.71 ||
sarāgeṇaiva manasā brahacaryaṃ carāmyaham |
vratapañjarabandhena vihaṅga iva yantritaḥ || k10.72 ||
tyaktvā vrajāmi pravrajyāmastu me narako'kṣayaḥ |
na vītarāgatāmeti mañjiṣṭhāraktamaṃśukam || k10.73 ||
iti bruvāṇamasakṛtsvapadaṃ gantumudyatam |
nivāryānugrahadhiyā tamūce bhagavān jinaḥ || k10.74 ||
mā kṛthā viplavaṃ nanda ninditaṃ hi śrutāśrutam |
vidvajjanopadiṣṭena yathā yāti pṛthagjanaḥ || k10.75 ||
vivekavyastadoṣāṇāṃ viduṣāṃ śīlaśālinām |
niḥsārasukhalābhena nākārye dhīḥ pravartate || k10.76 ||
gāḍharāgagṛhītasya jugupsāyatane param |
jadhanyakarmaṇyāsaktiḥ kiṃ lajjājanane na te || k10.77 ||
yonijayonisaṃsaktāḥ stanapastanamardinaḥ |
aho bata na lajjante janmanyeva layaṃ gatāḥ || k10.76 ||
sadāsajjanavarjitā jananījaghanāsaktiḥ |
saṃmohāhatacittānāṃ paśūnāmeva dṛśyate || k10.79 ||
rāmāramaṇamāno'yaṃ viramya tyajyatāṃ tvayā |
bhogaiḥ saha bhujaṅgānāṃ dṛṣṭo bhavabile kṣayaḥ || k10.80 ||
jaghanyā janayatyeva na kasya viratiṃ ratiḥ |
yasyāṃ bhavati paryanteṣvapi naiva parāṅmukhaḥ || k10.81 ||
gṛhajālavimuktastvaṃ kiṃ tatraivābhidhāvasi |
na hi nirgasya sāraṅgaḥ punarviśati vāgurām || k10.82 ||
iti vākyādbhagavataḥ śāsanena niyantritaḥ |
cintayan sundarīṃ nandaḥ praviveśāśramaṃ punaḥ || k10.83 ||
tataḥ kadācidādiśya nanda māśramamārjane |
āsanānugrahavyagraḥ prayayau bhagavān punah || k10.84 ||
tacchāsanātpravṛttasya nandasyāśramaśodhane |
no bhūtalādapayayau rajo rāga ivāśayat || k10.85 ||
tasyāhartuṃ gatasyātha salilaṃpārabhāgikam |
muhuḥ pūrṇasamutkṣiptahśūnya evābhavaddhaṭaḥ || k10.86 ||
tena vighnena gamane sutarāṃ khinnamānasaḥ |
tyaktvā tuprayayau nandaḥ sundarīdarśanotsukaḥ || k10.87 ||
atha vijñāya sarvajñastaṃ yāntaṃ divyacakṣuṣā |
babhāṣe sahasābhyetya stambhamānamanorathah || k10.88 ||
pātrayogena taptasya śyāmaraktaruceḥ param |
aho snehakalaṅkaste dīpasyeva na śāmyati || k10.89 ||
alaṃ vāmābhilāṣeṇa nīlīrāga ivaiṣa te |
saṃsaktaḥ ko'pi hṛdaye yannādyāpi virajyase || k10.90 ||
andhīkaroti prārambhe ratistatkālakātaram |
āliṅgati jugupseva vṛtte mukhyāṅgasaṃgame || k10.91 ||
viṣayākhvādasaṅgena pāpamitrairivendriyaiḥ |
duḥsahavyasanāvarte pātyate narake narah || k10.92 ||
adhivāsayati sparśalekhenāpi kusaṃgamaḥ |
praklinnamatsyakuṇapātpūtigandha ivodgataḥ || k10.93 ||
kalyāṇamitrasaṃparkaḥ sarvathā kuśalāvahaḥ |
śubhāmeda iva vyāpto yaḥ karoti mahārhatām || k10.94 ||
ityukte tasya bhagavān sākṣātsadasatoḥ pathi |
ghrāṇasparśena saṃdarśya cakre tatsaṅgadeśanām || k10.95 ||
atha nandaṃ samādāya bhagavān gandhamādane |
yayau viriñciramarībālavyajanavījitaḥ || k10.96 ||
tatra dāvānalapliṣṭāmaniṣṭakliṣṭavigrahām |
kāṇāṃ karmaṭīkāmasmai darśayitvāvadajjinaḥ || k10.97 ||
imāṃ paśyasi kiṃ nanda māndyanindyatarākṛtim |
kasmaiciducitā ceyaṃ rocate priyadarśanā || k10.98 ||
sattā sadasatornāsti rāgaḥ paśyati ramyatām |
sa tasya lalito loke yo yasya dayito janaḥ || k10.99 ||
pakṣapātaṃ samṛtsṛjya satyaṃ nanda tvayocyatām |
asyāstasyāśca sundaryā lāvaṇyasya kimantaram || k10.100 ||
anarthitvādvayaṃ naiva saundaryāntaravedinaḥ |
arthipriyatvamāyāti prārthitaṃ tacca cārutām || k10.101 ||
paśyāmyahaṃ viśeṣaṃ tu tasyā nāsyāśca kaṃcana |
ramyatvaṃ māṃsavarmāsthiyantre samayamātrakam || k10.102 ||
iti pṛṣṭo bahgavatā nandastaṃ pratyabhāsata |
atyantānucitaḥ praśnaḥ ko'pi gauravayantritaḥ || k10.103 ||
kimetadbhagavān vakti keyaṃ śoke biḍambanā |
kkāpi vā viśvaguravo vineyāḥ prabhaviṣṇavah || k10.104 ||
ratiḥ sādhikasundaryāḥ parabhāgeṇa rajyate |
yāṃ dṛṣṭvā jagatāṃ jetā na ratiṃ smarati smaraḥ || k10.105 ||
jyotsnayeva na tatkāntyā nodate kumudākaraḥ |
guṇāntaraṃ na jānāti prasiddhiśaraṇo janaḥ || k10.106 ||
baddhaṃ tayā vadanasaurabhasārahāramālokya puṣyanicayaṃ pṛthukeśapāśe |
manye vilāsagatilovanakānticauraiḥ bhītyeva haṃsahariṇairvanamiva yātam || k10.107 ||
analpaiḥ saṃkalpairbahuvidhavikalpairanupamā
na sā sāraṅgākṣīlikhitumapi śakyā paricitaiḥ |
tulārohe ysyā vadanaparabhāge laghutaraḥ
sa nūnaṃ tārāṇāṃ gaganamadhirūḍhaḥ parivṛḍhaḥ || k10.108 ||
puṇyaprahvaṃ lalitalalitabhrūlatālāsyalīlā
ramyaṃ tasyā yadi na vadanaṃ nandanaṃ labhyate tat |
pravrajyeyaṃ sukṛtamadhikaṃ kiṃkarī kiṃkarī me
kasmādetaṃ vrataparikaraṃ bhārabhūtaṃ vahāmi || k10.109 ||
iti nandavacaḥ śrutvā bhagavān rāganirbharam |
upakṣipya prabhāveta ta nināya surālayam || k10.110 ||
adarśayacca tatrāsya līlodyāne śatakratoḥ |
sudhāmanthasamudbhūtāḥ kāntāstridaśayoṣitaḥ || k10.111 ||
aruṇaiḥ kāntisaṃtānaiḥ pādapadmavanoditaiḥ |
anuyātā ivāmbhodhikūlavidrumakānanaiḥ || k10.112 ||
viśālalāsyasacivaiḥ pāṇibhirvijitāmbujaiḥ |
saṃsaktaiḥ sahajasyeva pārijātasya pallavaiḥ || k10.113 ||
kāntimādhuryalalitairmadanānandabāṇdhavaiḥ |
helānimīlitāmbhojavadanaiścandrasundaraiḥ || k10.114 ||
saṃmohanairjīvanaiśca kṛṣṇasārairvilokanaiḥ |
kālakūṭacchadaspṛṣṭairamṛtoghairivāvṛtāḥ || k10.115 ||
pūrṇayauvanalāvaṇyāḥ sahasaiva vilokya tāḥ |
nandaḥ sānandavadanaḥ svedasnāta ivābhavat || k10.116 ||
padmānanāsu vipulotpalalocanāsu kundastimātu nibiḍastabakastanīṣu |
nandasya tāsu hṛdayaṃ yugapannipatya dolāvilāsataralālitumavāpa || k10.117 ||
tataḥ provāca bahgavānnandaṃ tadgatamānasam |
āsāṃ saṃdarśane nanda prītyā te ramate matiḥ || k10.118 ||
āsāṃ tasyāśca sundaryā lāvaṇye kiyadantaram |
utkarṣaḥ parabhāgeṇa sphuṭamevābhibhāvyate || k10.119 ||
nirastasundarīrūpaṃ rūpamapsarasāṃ yadi |
tadetā eva kālena kariṣyāmi tvadāśrayāḥ || k10.120 ||
ārogeṇaiva manasā brahmacaryaṃ prasannadhīḥ |
cara tāvattataste'haṃ dāsyāmyapsarasāṃ gaṇam || k10.121 ||
evaṃ bhagavato vākyānnandaḥ saṃjātaniścayaḥ |
tathetyuktvā vrate cetaścakre svargāṅganāśayā || k10.122 ||
mandādaraḥ svadāreṣu so'bhūttatsaṃgamecchayā |
guṇapṇyatulāvṛtternāsti snehasya satyatā || k10.123 ||
aho cismṛtasaṃvāsapravāsapariśoṣitā |
puṃsāmābhyāsikī prītiḥ sahasānyatra dhāvati || k10.124 ||
kṣaṇayauvanaramyāṇi premāṇi praṇayavyayaiḥ |
na satyāni na nityāni na sukhāni śarīriṇām || k10.125 ||
tato bhagavatā nandaḥ kṣaṇānītaḥ svamāśramam |
tanniścayādbrahmacaryaṃ cacāra niyatavrataḥ || k10.126 ||
sa cisasmāra sundaryāḥ kāntisaṃpadamanyadhīḥ |
kṣaṇapramuṣitā prītirmalaṃ yāti guṇeṣvapi || k10.127 ||
tataḥ kadācidvicarannandaḥ kkāpi vyalokayat |
karālanarakāsaktāṃ dhīmān kumbhībhṛtāṃ bhuvam || k10.128 ||
tāṃ vilokyaiva sākampaḥ kimetaditi duḥkhitaḥ |
sa papraccha tadāsaktān ghoranarakakāraṇam || k10.129 ||
te tamūcuriyaṃ bhūmistaptakumbhīśatācitā |
kalpitā rājaputrasya nandasyānandarāgiṇaḥ || k10.130 ||
mithyāvrataḥ sa nādyāpi bhajate vītarāgatām |
brahamcaryaṃ caratyeva svargastrūīsaṃgamāśayā || k10.131 ||
mithyāvratānāṃ lubdhānāṃ rāgadveṣakaṣāyiṇām |
etāsu nityataptāsu kumbhīṣvevākṣayaḥ kṣayaḥ || k10.132 ||
iti nanda samākarṇya jātaromāñcakañcukah |
tatra cyutāmiva tanūṃ paścāttāpādamanyataḥ || k10.133 ||
samamyetya tyaktarāgasaṃvāsavāsanahsvayam |
babhūvānuttarabrahmacaryaparyāptāṃyamaḥ || k10.134 ||
ghanamohakṣayāttasya vimukte saṃśaye tataḥ |
mahaḥ prasādamāpede śaradīvedadheḥ payaḥ || k10.135 ||
niṣkāmaḥ praśamaṃ prāptaḥ parāṃ niṣṭhāmupāgataḥ |
śuddhadhīḥ sa samabhyetya bhagavantamabhāṣata || k10.136 ||
nāpsarobhirna sundaryā bhagavan kṛtyamasti me |
etāḥ paryantavicchāyāḥ sapātā viṣayaśriyaḥ || k10.137 ||
yathā yatheyaṃ bhāvānāṃ bhāvyate niḥsvabhāvatā |
tathā tathā prṝsīdanti nirāvaraṇavṛttayaḥ || k10.138 ||
iti dandasya vadataḥ prāptasyārtapadaṃ śanaiḥ |
bhagavānnirvāṇaśuddhāmasya siddhimamanyata || k10.139 ||
keṣāṃ kuśalamūlānāṃ nandenāsāditaṃ phalam |
iti bhikṣubhirabhyetya pṛṣṭhastānavadajjinaḥ || k10.140 ||
janmāntarārjitaiḥ puṇyaiḥ sukṛtābhyāsakāriṇā |
prāptāḥ kuśalamūlānāṃ nandena phalasaṃpadaḥ || k10.141 ||
vipulavimala vaṃśe janma smarapratimā tanuḥ
surajanasakhī lakṣī vṛttiḥ priyāḥ satataṃ satām |
praśamasalilasnātaṃ cetaḥ svabhāvagatirgatiḥ
kuśalakusumasyeyaṃ puṃsāṃ viśālaphalodgatiḥ || k10.142 ||
stūpe vipaśyinaḥ samyaksaṃbuddhasyādaraḥ purā |
nagaryāmaruṇāvatyāmaruṇena mahībhūhā || k10.143 ||
kriyamāṇo maṇimaye maitro nāma dvijanmajaḥ |
mahataḥ puṇyabhogasya bhāgī kārakatāṃ yayau || k10.144 ||
tatpuṇyapraṇidhānena jāto gṝhapateḥ kule |
sa eva bhikṣusaṃghasya jantukāsnānasatrakṛt || k10.145 ||
sa puṇyaśīlaḥ pratyekabuddhopasthāyakahpurā |
stūpaṃ cakre śobhamānaṃ mālabhivaraṇojjvalam || k10.146 ||
tatpuṇyapraṇidhānena kṛkeḥ kāśīpateḥ sutaḥ |
so'bhvaddyutimānnāma divyalakṣaṇalakṣitaḥ || k10.147 ||
kāśyapasyārhataḥ samyaksaṃbuddasyāntanirvṛtau |
saptaratnamaye stūpe kṛte kāśīmahībhujā || k10.148 ||
tatsūnurdhyutimān haumacchatramāropya bhāsvaram |
jātastatpraṇidhānena nandaḥ śākyamuke'dhunā || k10.149 ||
iti sukṛtasamutthaiḥ pūrvajanvakramāptaiḥ kimapi vipulapuṇyaireva nandaḥ prapede |
kulamamalamudāraṃ rūmamagryaṃ ca bhogaṃ śamaparicitamante satpadaṃ saugataṃ ca || k10.150 ||
kathayotveti bhagavānnandakalyāṇakāraṇam |
cakāra bhikṣusaṃghasya tāṃ tāṃ sukṛtadeśanām || k10.151 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ sundarīnandāvadānaṃ nāma daśamaḥ pallavaḥ ||


11. virūḍhakāvadānam |

ārohati padamunnatamamalamatirvimalakuśalasopānaiḥ |
narakakuhareṣu nipatati malinamatirghoratimireṣu || k11.1 ||
śākyānāṃ nagare pūrvaṃ sphīte kapilavāstuni |
mahataḥ śākyamukhyasya sumukhī dāsakanyākā || k11.2 ||
śāstre kṛtaśramā sarvakalākauśalaśālinī |
mālikā nāma kāmasya mālikeva guṇocitā || k11.3 ||
prabhorgirā varodyāne kusumāvacayodyatā |
bhramantaṃ taṃ samāyāntaṃ dadarśa sugataṃ puraḥ || k11.4 ||
tasyānte'syāstamālokya prasannamabhavanmanaḥ |
śaratkāla iva svacchaḥ prasādayati mānasam || k11.5 ||
sācintayattadā lokaprītyā dṛḍhīkṛtāspadā |
sukṛtaiḥ piṇḍapātaṃ me gṛhṇīyādbhagavānapi || k11.6 ||
vijñāya tasyāhsarvajñaḥ saṃkalpaṃ karuṇākulaḥ |
prasārya pātraṃ bhagavān bhadre dehītyuvāca tām || k11.7 ||
datvā praṇamya sā tasmai paripūrṇamanorathā |
praṇidhānaṃ pravidadhe dāsyaduḥkhanivṛttaye || k11.8 ||
tataḥ kadācidāyātaḥ pituastasyāḥ sakhā dvijaḥ |
naumittikastaṃ pradeśaṃ dṛṣṭvā tāṃ vismito'va dat || k11.9 ||
aho gṛhapatestasya putrī tvaṃ śrīmatahsutā |
bandhuhīnā gatā dāsyaṃ dhanabhogavivarjitā || k11.10 ||
aho mohaghanārambhakṣaṇoddyotanavidyutaḥ |
saṃsārasarparasanāvilāsacapalāḥ śriyaḥ || k11.11 ||
gamyānāṃ mā kṛthāścintāṃ jāne'haṃ hastalakṣaṇaiḥ |
acireṇaiva bhūbharturvallabhā tvaṃ bhaviṣyasi || k11.12 ||
idaṃ paśyāmi te pāṇau lakṣmīkamalakomale |
mālācakrāṅkuśākāramityuktvā prayayau dvijaḥ || k11.13 ||
atha manmathasaṃbhogasuhṛnmadhupabāndhavaḥ |
latāliṅganasaubhāgyabhavyo'dṛśyata mādhavaḥ || k11.14 ||
madhoḥ kesariṇastasya kāntāmānadvipadviṣaḥ |
vibabhau jihmamānasya jihvevāśokamañjarī || k11.15 ||
bālākapolalāvaṇyacauraścampakasaṃcayaḥ |
sudṛśāṃ keśapāśeṣu yayau bandhanayogyatām || k11.16 ||
sahakārairvirahiṇīnidhanaṃ vidadhe madhu |
nirapekṣāparavadhe vidhurāḥ prabhaviṣṇavaḥ || k11.17 ||
yayurmadhulihāṃ cūtalatā nirbharabhogyatām |
sahasauva vidgadhānāmiva mugdhavibhūtayaḥ || k11.18 ||
rūtāyudhaścūtalatācāpanyastaśilīmukhaḥ |
jayatīti jagau bandī kandarpasyeva kokilaḥ || k11.19 ||
asminnavasare śrīmān kosalendraḥ prasenajit |
mṛgayānirgato'śvena hṛtastaṃ deśamāyayau || k11.20 ||
dhanvīmanobhavākāraḥ so'vatīrya turaṃgamāt |
dadarśānanyalāvaṇyāṃ kanyāṃ ratimivāparām || k11.21 ||
tadvilokanavistīrṇaṃ manastasya mahātmanaḥ |
vismayāddṛṣṭimārgeṇa praviveśa manobhavaḥ || k11.22 ||
tāṃ lajjāvanatāṃ dṛṣṭvā sahasodbhūītasādhvasām |
acintayannarapatiḥ kāntikallilinīhṛtaḥ || k11.23 ||
keyaṃ navā śaśimukhī śyāmā taralatārakā |
yatkāntiraniśaṃ netraśatapatravikāśinī || k11.24 ||
bakulāmodavibhrāntabhramare pāṭalādhare |
kāntaṃ vasantaṃ paśyāmi mukhe'syāḥ kumudāyudham || k11.25 ||
aho lāvaṇyamamlānaṃ tāruṇyābharaṇaṃ tanoḥ |
dhīrasyāpi dhṛtiryena śaṅkhe saṃgaliteva me || k11.26 ||
aho nu madhumañjaryāḥ prārambhe'pyadbhuto guṇaḥ |
yena gantuṃ na śaknoti ṣaṭpado'pi padātpadam || k11.27 ||
iti saṃcintya bhīpālastāṃ matvā vanadevatām |
pṛṣṭvā viveda tadvṛttaṃ krameṇa kathitaṃ tayā || k11.28 ||
tatastatra kṛtātithyastayā pallavavījanaiḥ |
śuciśītaiśca salilaiḥ prāptavānnirvṛtiṃ nṛpaḥ || k11.29 ||
śrāntaḥ saṃvāhane tasya tayā caraṇapadmayoḥ |
kṛte karāptasaṃsparśe sa nidrāṃ sahasā yayau || k11.30 ||
kṣaṇena pratibyddho'tha viśrāntamṛgayāśramaḥ |
divyasparśena tāṃ mene ratiṃ rūpāntarāgatām || k11.31 ||
mahānapi tataḥ śākyaḥ saṃprāptaṃ kosaleśvaram |
śrutvā taṃ deśamabhyetya pūjārhaṃ tamapūjayat || k11.32 ||
sādareṇārthitāṃ tena svasutāmiva mālikām |
ratnārhāya dadau tasmai smaramaṅgalamālikām || k11.33 ||
tāmādāya manojanmavaijayantīṃ sitasmitām |
nijaṃ jagāma nagaraṃ gajamāruhya bhūpatiḥ || k11.34 ||
tasminnāganagotsaṅge sā lolālakaṣaṭpadā |
babhau rājavasantena saṃgatā navamālikā || k11.35 ||
rājadhānīṃ samāsādya sundaryā sahitastayā |
ratnaharmyakarodāramandire vijahāra saḥ || k11.36 ||
varṣākārābhidhā devī rājñaḥ prathamavallabhā |
abhinnavṛttiṃ tāṃ mene rājalakṣmīmiva kṣitiḥ || k11.37 ||
divyasparśena sā tasyāḥ sā cāsyā rūpasaṃpadā |
parasparaguṇotkarṣātparaṃ vismayamāpatuḥ || k11.38 ||
divyarūpavatī jyeṣṭhā divyasparśavatī parā |
iti pravādaḥ sāścaryastayuorlokeṣu paprathe || k11.39 ||
atrāntare tayordivyarūpasaṃsparśakāraṇam |
āśrame bhikṣubhiḥ pṛṣṭaḥ provāca bhagavān jinaḥ || k11.40 ||
purā śrutavarākhyasya dvijasya gṛhamedhinaḥ |
kāntā śirīṣikā ceti priye bhārye babhūvatuḥ || k11.41 ||
sa kadācidatho kāntābhrātā pravrajyayā śanaiḥ |
pratyekabuddhatāṃ yātaḥ svasurbhavanamāyayau || k11.42 ||
traimāsikopacāreṇa sa tayā patyurājñayā |
bhaktyā nimantritastasthau tatsaptnyāvapūjitaḥ || k11.43 ||
te cārumṛdubhirbhogaistamabhyarcyānyajanmani |
jāte'dhunā cārurūpadivyasaṃsparśasaṃyute || k11.44 ||
kṛṣṭeṣu prathamaṃ prayuktavinayāmādāya gosaṃpadaṃ
satkṣetreṣu tapaḥ pratatptanuṣu prāptiṣvatisvādutām |
yatkāle śubhabījamuptamucitaṃ satkarmaśakteḥ paraṃ
bhujyante phalasaṃpadaḥ sumatibhistasyaiva pākojjvalāḥ || k11.45 ||
iti sarvajñavacanaṃ tathyamākarṇya bhikṣavaḥ |
tattatheti viniścitya babhūvuḥ śāntisaṃśrayāḥ || k11.46 ||
atha kālena bhūbharturmālikāyāmabhūtsutaḥ |
virūḍhaketimukhyākhyo vidyāsu ca kṛtaśramah || k11.47 ||
priyastulyavayāstasya purohitasuto'bhavat |
māturduḥkhena jātatvādviśruto duḥkhamātṛkaḥ || k11.48 ||
kadācitsahitastena hayārūḍho virūḍhakah |
prāpa śākyavarodyānaṃ mṝgayāyāṃ vinirgataḥ || k11.49 ||
nyakkāraṃ cakrire tatra śākyāstasyodyatāyudhāḥ |
ayaṃ dāsīsuto'smākamiti darpapravādinah || k11.50 ||
gatvāsau svapuraṃ teṣāṃ vairaṃ darpyamacintayat |
kuladarpāpavādo hi śalyatodaḥ śarīriṇām || k11.51 ||
tasya nirdahyamānasya tatpratīkāracintayā |
rājyāya jātā janake jīvatyapi parā spṛhā || k11.52 ||
sa cārāyaṇamukhyānāṃ mantriṇāṃ śatapañcakam |
svavaśaṃ piturākṛṣya vidadhe bhedayuktibhiḥ || k11.53 ||
tataḥ kadācitsaṃjātavivekaḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ |
dharmopadeśaśravaṇe vardhamānādaraḥ param || k11.54 ||
cārātyaṇagṛhītāśvaṃ rathamāruhya saṃyataḥ |
draṣṭuṃ jagāma sarvajñaṃ bhagavantaṃ prasenajit || k11.55 ||
prāyāśramaṃ bhagavataḥ kṛtvā pādābhivandanam |
dharmānvayaṃ sa śuśrāva tatprasādaprasannadhīḥ || k11.56 ||
cārāyaṇo'pyāśu gatvā rathena nagaraṃ javāt |
akarodantare tasmin rājaputrābhiṣecanam || k11.57 ||
bhagavantamathāmantrya nṛpatirgantumudyataḥ |
dadarśa nānugānagre na rathaṃ na ca mantriṇam || k11.58 ||
sa padbhyāmeva śanakaiḥ prasthitāṃ pṛthivīpatiḥ |
dūrādapaśyadāyāntīṃ varṣākārāṃ samālikām || k11.59 ||
te pṛṣṭvā tadgirā jñātvā so'bhiṣiktaṃ virūḍhakam |
visasarja sutaiśvaryaparibhogāya mālokām || k11.60 ||
varṣākārāṃ samādāya sa mitrasya mahīpateḥ |
ajātaśatrornagaraṃ prāta rājagṛhābhidham || k11.61 ||
sa tatptaśchatravirahātkṣuptipāsāśramānvitaḥ |
yayau vamanniva śvāsaṃ dīrgheśvāramamārutaiḥ || k11.62 ||
sukhamaskhalitaṃ kena prāptaṃ kasyāyurāyatam |
na kasyānupadaṃ dṛṣṭaḥ kṣayaḥ sapadi saṃpadaḥ || k11.63 ||
sa jīrṇamūlakaṃ bhuktvā karmamūlamivāyatam |
kṣaṇaṃ pītvā ca pānīyaṃ papātāptaviṣūcikaḥ || k11.64 ||
anityatāmavijñāya mohāya patate janah |
sa cāpāyanikāyasya kāyasyopāyatṛṣṇayā || k11.65 ||
ajātaśatruḥ śrutvaiva kosaleśvaramāgatam |
abhyetya pāṃśupūrṇāsyaṃ vigatāsuṃ dadarśa tam || k11.66 ||
tasya jāyānuyātasya sa kṛtvā dehasatkriyām |
bhagavantaṃ yayau druṣṭuṃ sugataṃ duḥkhaśāntaye || k11.67 ||
sa taṃ praṇamya provāca bhagavan kosaleśvaraḥ |
puraṃ me suhṝdaḥ prāya nirdhano nidhanaṃ gataḥ || k11.68 ||
dhiṅbhāmasaṃpadaṃ pāpaṃ mihādayaśasaḥ padam |
vibhavo yena naivāyaṃ mitropakaraṇīkṛtaḥ || k11.69 ||
hṛdaye viniveśyāśāṃ prāptaḥ suhṝdamāpadi |
suhṛnnaiṣphalyamāyāti yasya kiṃ tena jīvatā || k11.70 ||
mitropakaraṇaṃ lakṣmīrdīnopakaraṇaṃ dhanam |
bhītopakaraṇaṃ prāṇā yeṣāṃ teṣāṃ sujīvitam || k11.71 ||
kukarma kiṃ kṛtaṃ tena bhagavanūrvajanmani |
yasya pākena paryante prapede so'tidurdaśām || k11.72 ||
iti pṛṣṭaḥ kṣitīśena bhagavān sāśrucakṣuṣā |
tamūce tāpaśamanīṃ diśan daśanacandrikām || k11.73 ||
mā śucaḥ pṛthivīpāla svabhāvo'yaṃ bhavasthitaḥ |
evaṃvidhaiva bhāvānāmasatyānāmanityatā || k11.74 ||
visārisaṃsāravanāntare'sminnisargalolaḥ kila kāmabhṛṅgaḥ |
svacchandajātajanapuṣkarajīvapuñjakiñjalkapuñjamaniśaṃ kavalīkaroti || k11.75 ||
taraṅganto bhogāścakitahariṇīlocanacalāḥ  kṣaṇe'lakṣyā lakṣmīrjanajaladavidyotanataḍit |
śarīrābje bālātapacapalarāgaṃ navavayaḥ kṣayaṃ yāti kṣipraṃ bhavamarūtaṭe jīvitakaṇaḥ || k11.76 ||
mano maitrīpātraṃ parahitaratirdharmadhanatā
madodbhedacchedakṣamaśamavicāre paricayaḥ |
ayaṃ tatvānveṣo viṣayasukhavaimukhyasukhinām
asāre saṃsāre parihṛtavikāraḥ paribhavaḥ || k11.77 ||
janahśocati duḥkheṣu kṣipraṃ hata ivāśmanā |
na karoti punastīvratadāpātapratikriyām || k11.78 ||
paśyato'pibhavāyāsaṃ nirvivekasya sarvathā |
kriyate kiṃ janasyāsya mohādakuśalaspṛśaḥ || k11.79 ||
purā vipraḥ suśarmākhyaḥ kutaścitprāpya mūlakam |
nidhāya jananīhaste yayau snātuṃ nadītaṭam || k11.80 ||
sāpi pratyekabuddhāya tāṃ pāptāya tadantare |
praṇatā pātrahastāya tadevābhimūkhī dadau || k11.81 ||
atha snātvā samāyātastatsutastvaritaṃ kṣudhā |
jananīṃ bhojanārambhe yayāce nijamūlakam || k11.82 ||
puṇyaṃ putrānumodasva tanmayātithaye'rpitam |
iti māturvacaḥ śrutvāso'bhūdviddha iveṣuṇā || k11.83 ||
sadyo viṣūcikārtasya manmūlakamanalpakam |
kukṣiṃ bhittvā viniryātu prāṇaiḥ saha ta vātitheḥ || k11.84 ||
iti tasyāptapāpasya vākyāruṣyeṇa bhūyasā |
visūcikaiva paryante babhūvaparajanmani || k11.85 ||
prākpuṇyāntarapākena sa evādya prasenajit |
vipulaṃ rājyamāsādya tayaivānte kṣayaṃ gataḥ || k11.86 ||
saṃsārapathapānthānāmevaṃ karma śubhāśubham |
pātheyamiva hastasthaṃ bhogāyaivopapadyate || k11.87 ||
iti śrutvā bhagavatastathyaṃ pathyaṃ ca tadvacaḥ |
evametaditi dhyātvā taṃ praṇamya yayau nṛpaḥ || k11.88 ||
atrāntare prāptarājyaḥ śākyavairaṃ virūḍhakaḥ |
purohitasutenaitya smāritastatkṣayodyataḥ || k11.89 ||
prayayau śākyanagaraṃ gajāśvarathareṇunā |
moheneva diśāṃ kurvannirvivekaṃ dhiyāmiva || k11.90 ||
sarvajño bhagavān jñātvā tasya tadduṣṭaceṣṭitam |
gatvā śākyapuropānte tasthau śuṣkataroradhah || k11.91 ||
dūrāttatra sthitaṃ dṛṣṭvā tamāgacchan virūḍhakaḥ |
avatīrya rathādagramabhyetya praṇato'vadat || k11.92 ||
satsu snigdhapalāśeṣu ghanacchāyeṣu śākhiṣu |
bhagavannatra viśrāntiḥ kimu śuṣkataroradhaḥ || k11.93 ||
ityuktaḥ kṣitipālena taṃ prāha bhagavān jinaḥ |
jñāticchāyā narapateḥ candanādapi śītalā || k11.94 ||
nāsti jñātisamaṃ vittaṃ nāsti jñātisamā dhṛtiḥ |
nāsti jñātisamā chāyā nāsti jñātisamahpriyaḥ || k11.95 ||
mamaite bhūpate śākyā jñātayasyatpurāntike |
jātaḥ priyo'yaṃ tatprītyā śuṣkaśākho'pi pādapaḥ || k11.96 ||
śrutvaitadviratāmarṣaḥ śākyānāṃ pakṣapātinam |
bhagavantaṃ viditvaiva nyavartataḥ virūḍhakaḥ || k11.97 ||
bhagavānapi śākyānāṃ jñātvāgāmi bhayaṃ tataḥ |
śreyase śuddhasattvānāṃ vidadhe dharmadeśanām || k11.98 ||
śrotāpattiphalaṃ kaiścitsakṛdāgāmi cāparaiḥ |
anāgāmiphalaṃ cānyaiḥ saṃprāptaṃ tasya śāsanāt || k11.99 ||
śeṣāstu mūḍhamatayaḥ śākyāḥ prāpurna tatpadam |
santi ke'pi khagā yeṣāṃ vāsare timiro'dbhavaḥ || k11.100 ||
nivṛttasyātha nṛpateḥ purohitasutastataḥ |
vairasarpasya suptasya vidadhe pratibodhanam || k11.101 ||
sa tena preritaścakre matiṃ śākyakulakṣaye |
vairānalaṃ pracalanaṃ karoti piśunānilaḥ || k11.102 ||
ghoradurjanamantreṇa sahasotthāpitāḥ khalāḥ |
vetālā kṣitipālāśca na kasya prāṇahāriṇah || k11.103 ||
sainye gajarathodagre tatastasmin prasarpati |
babhūva purasaṃkṣobhaḥ śākyānāṃ rūddhavartmanām || k11.104 ||
tasmin bhagavān rakṣārthaṃ śākyānāṃ pakṣapātinam |
samudyataṃ tatra mahāmaudgalyāyanamabravīt || k11.105 ||
śākyānāṃ karmadoṣo'yaṃ sarvathā samupasthitaḥ |
tatra rakṣavidhānaṃ te gagane setubandhanam || k11.106 ||
puṃsamavintyavibhavāni śubhāśubhāni āyānti yānti ca muhurniravagrahāṇi |
karmākṣarāṇi nijajanmapadasvahastanyastāni nāma na bhavanti nirarthakāni || k11.107 ||
iti vākyādbhagavatastasmin yāte praṇamya tam |
cakrire saṃvidaṃ śākyāhpratyāsanne virūḍhake || k11.108 ||
hiṃsāsmābhirna kartavyā prāṇimātrasya kasyacit |
śarāḥ śarīramasmākaṃ viśantvarisamīritāḥ || k11.109 ||
iti saṃvidamādhāya te viyaṣṭikapāṇayaḥ |
dhīrḥ parodyame tasthuravārayitakārmukāḥ || k11.110 ||
atrāntare karmayogānnijadeśānavasthitaḥ |
ajñātvā saṃvidaṃ śākyaḥ śaṃpākahsamupāyayau || k11.111 ||
sa dṛṣṭvā nagare baddhasaṃnāhaṃ vasudhādhipam |
kopādekaścakārāsya raṇe subhaṭasaṃkṣayam || k11.112 ||
yuddhe puruṣasiṃhena hatāste vīrakuñjarāḥ |
prayayuḥ spṛhaṇīyatvaṃ yaśobhirmaktikairiva || k11.113 ||
sa ko'pi tasya jajvāla kopitasya parairasiḥ |
sa yayau yatpratāpena vipulāṃ ripuvāhinīm || k11.114 ||
praveśaṃ na daduḥ śākyāḥ śaṃpākasya dviṣāṃ vadhāt |
svajano'pi parityaktaḥ sa tairnistriṃśakarmaṇā || k11.115 ||
nije'pi vimukhāḥ krūre sādhave dahrambandhavaḥ |
dhānādapi vadānyatvaṃ sukṛtaṃ svajanādapi || k11.116 ||
* * * * * * * * |
śatamaucityanityānāmāyuṣo'pi yaśaḥ priyam || k11.117 ||
nirvāsitaḥ sa taiḥ prāptaḥ śanairbhagavato'ntikam |
yayāce'bhyudayāyāiva taṃ kiṃcinnijalāñchanam || k11.118 ||
ṛddhaṃ bhagavatā dattaṃ nijakeśanakhāṃśakam |
sa jagāma samādāya vākuḍaṃ nāma maṇḍalam || k11.119 ||
tatra prajñāprabhāveṇa śauryotsāhaguṇena ca |
sa prāpa rājyaṃ dhīrāṇāṃ sarvatra sulabhāḥ śriyaḥ || k11.120 ||
dakṣāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ lakṣmīḥ sahajaṃ viduṣāṃ yaśaḥ |
vyavasāyasahāyānāṃ kalatraṃ sarvasiddhayaḥ || k11.121 ||
tatra sthito bhagavataḥ so'tha keśanakhāṃśake |
stūpapratiṣṭhāmakarodvarratnavirājitām || k11.122 ||
virūḍhako'pi śākyānāṃ vairapāratitīrṣayā |
punaryuktyā puradvhārabhedena sahasāviśat || k11.123 ||
hatvā tatra sahasrāṇi śākyānāṃ saptasaptati |
baddhvā kanyākumārāṇāṃ sa sahasramathāharat || k11.124 ||
śatāni pañca śākyānāṃ gajairlohaiśca mardanaiḥ |
saṃpramṛjya purīṃ cakre kṛtāntanagarīmiva || k11.125 ||
bhagavānapi śākyānāṃ śatrūṇā bhedanaṃ kṛtam |
karmānubaddhaṃ vijñāyaṃ babhūva vimanāḥ kṣaṇam || k11.126 ||
papracchustaṃ samabhyetya bhikṣavaḥ karuṇākulāḥ |
kiṃ karma vihitaṃ śākyairghoraṃ yasyedṛśaṃ phalam || k11.127 ||
bhagavānaniti taiḥ pṛṣṭaḥ sarvajñastānabhāṣata |
nijakarmavipākena śākyānāmeṣa saṃkṣayaḥ || k11.128 ||
kṛṣṭau purā mahāmatsyau dhīvaraiḥ sarito'ntarāt |
tadā nikṛttau śalyena bhūyo'pyavyathayan dhṛtau || k11.129 ||
kālena cārutāṃ yātaistaireva parajanmani |
hatau gṝhapatī dagdhvā tāveva dhanahāribhiḥ || k11.130 ||
tau matsyau tau gṛhasthau ca virūḍhakapurohitau |
dāsānāṃ taskarāṇāṃ ca śākyānāṃ mṛtyutāṃ gatau || k11.131 ||
iti śrutvā bhagavataḥ karmaṇāṃ phalasaṃtatim |
avisaṃvādinīmeva menire sarvabhikṣavaḥ || k11.132 ||
virūḍhako'tha svapuraṃ prāpya vijayadurmadaḥ |
jetānāmnā sutenoktaḥ praṇayādbālalīlayā || k11.133 ||
deva kiṃ nihatāḥ śākyānna te'smākaṃ kṛtāgasaḥ |
iti bruvāṇamavadhīnnijasūnuṃ virūḍhakaḥ || k11.134 ||
nipātamatimṛdgati nihanti na karoti kim |
madalabdhanadhāyāso mātaṃga iva darjanaḥ || k11.135 ||
sa jagāda sabhāsīnaḥ svabhūjāvavalokayan |
aho nu mama tāpāgnau dvisadbhiḥ śalabhāyitam || k11.136 ||
kṛtāntatoraṇastambhau prājyau mama bhujāvimau |
niṣḥśeṣavadhadīkṣāyāṃ śākyānāṃ gurutāṃ gatau || k11.137 ||
taṃ tasya vikramaṃ ślāghyaṃ hṛtāstāḥ śākyakanyakāḥ |
śrutvā babhāṣire tīvramudveganamitānanāḥ || k11.138 ||
karmapāśanibaddhānāṃ khagānāmiva dehinām |
nidhanollaṅghane śaktirnāsti pakṣavatāmapi || k11.139 ||
yenāgniḥ śamameti tatkila jalaṃ prāpnotyalaṃ vāḍavaḥ
tigmāṃśugrahaṇaṃ karoti samaye helāvalehyaṃ tamah |
paryālocanavartmanāmaviṣayaṃ sāścaryacaryāspadaṃ
sarvaṃ kārmikatantrayantimidaṃ kaḥ kasya kartuṃ kṣamaḥ || k11.140 ||
etadākarṇya nṛpatiḥ padāntara iveragaḥ |
karacchedaṃ dideśāsāṃ ghorāmarṣaviṣotkaṭaḥ || k11.141 ||
tīre yasyāḥ kṛtaṃ tāsāṃ pāṇicchedanavaiśasam |
sādyāpi hastagabhati khyātā puṣkariṇī bhuvi || k11.142 ||
latāsvapi hkukūlāgniṃ krakacaṃ nalīnīṣvapi |
mālāsvapi śilāvarṣaṃ pātayantyeva nirghṛṇāḥ || k11.143 ||
tāśchinnapāṇikamalāstatra tīvravyathāturāḥ |
bhagavantaṃ dhiyā dhyātvā śaraṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ yayuḥ || k11.144 ||
tāsāṃ vijñāya sarvajñastīvrāṃ marmāhativyathām |
śacīmacintayaddevīṃ tatsamāśvāsanocitām || k11.145 ||
tatsparśajātahastābjāstā divyavasanāvṛtāḥ |
yayuścittaprasādena tāhsvargaṃ tyaktavigrahā || k11.146 ||
devakalpāstamāsādya divyapadmotpalāṅkitāḥ |
dharmadeśanayā śāstustāhprāpurvipulaṃ padam || k11.147 ||
bhikṣubhirbhagavān pṛṣṭastatkarmaphalamabhyadhāt |
pāṇicāpalyametābhiḥ kṛtaṃ bhikṣuviḍambane || k11.148 ||
karmaṇastasya pākena viśase patitāhparam |
mayi cittaprasādena prāptāścaitāhśubhāṃ gatim || k11.149 ||
ityuktvā bhagavān karmaphalapākavicitratām |
bhukṣīṇāṃ tatprasaṅgena vidadhe dharmadeśanām || k11.150 ||
atrāntare gūḍhacārī rājñā praṇihitaścaraḥ |
bhagavaccaritaṃ jñātvā virūḍhakamupāyayau || k11.151 ||
so'vadaddeva bhikṣūṇāṃ tenedaṃ kathitaṃ puraḥ |
svakarmaphalamāsannaṃ tasya paśyāmi bhūpateh || k11.152 ||
saptāhenāgninā dagdhaḥ sa pāpātmā purohitaḥ |
avīcināmni narake duḥsahe nipatiṣyati || k11.153 ||
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā nṛpatiḥ sapurohitaḥ |
yatnāduvāsa saptāhaṃ jalānvitagṛhāntare || k11.154 ||
kṣaṇāvaśeṣe saptāhe tasminnantaḥpuraṃ gate |
sūryakāntārkasaṃtāpayogājjajvāla pāvakaḥ || k11.155 ||
udbhūtena pralayasavanāvartinevāśu vegān
nirdagdho'sau dhagiti śikhinā nārakaṃ prāpa vahnim |
asmiṃllike jvalanajaṭilāḥ pāpināṃ pretya rāgāḥ
sarvatraiva sthirasukhabhuvaḥ śītalāḥ puṇyabhājām || k11.156 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ virūḍhakāvadānaṃ nāma ekādaśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


12. hārītikādamanāvadānam |

duḥkhaṃ nudanti sukhasaṃpadamādiśanti saṃjīvayanti janatāṃ timiraṃ haranti |
sanmānasasya kalayanti vikāsahāsaṃ santaḥ sudhārdravadanāḥ śaśina karāśca || k12.1 ||
bimbisāraḥ kṣiteḥ sāre pure rājagṛhābhidhe |
sāraḥ samastabhūpānāmabhūdbhūmipuraṃdaraḥ || k12.2 ||
kṣamādhāre bhuje yasya kṣamādhāre ca cetasi |
bāhyaḥ samastacittānāmaśu nyastakaro janaḥ || k12.3 ||
kurvatastyāgaśauryābhyāmāśāyāḥ paripūraṇam |
pāṇau vimuktaratnaughe subaddho'bhūdasigrahaḥ || k12.4 ||
kadācidabhavattasya viplavo nagare guruḥ |
navābhyudayasaṃjātadarpakāla ivākulaḥ || k12.5 ||
tamāsthānasamāsīnaṃ janacintākṛtakṣaṇam |
vyajijñipata saurājaṃ prajānāṃ janakopamam || k12.6 ||
deva divyaprabhāvasya niyataḥ śāsanena te |
janahsadā samudro'yaṃ maryādāṃ nātivartate || k12.7 ||
yenāsya kṛtavṛttasya sanmārgeṇa prasarpataḥ |
upasargodgamaḥ kasmādakasmādayamāgataḥ || k12.8 ||
svadharmasaṃvṛttena hi karmaṇā śarmaṇā nṛṇām |
sunṛpe na ca guhyānāmāpatanti vipattayaḥ || k12.9 ||
hriyante naḥ prasūtīnāṃ gṛhiṇīnāṃgṛhe kayā |
apatyāni phalānīva satkriyāṇāmasaṃyamāt || k12.10 ||
kiṃtu bhūtānna vidmastānmāyāṃ cāpi mahīpate |
yatprabhāveṇa nīyante kulāni nirapatyatām || k12.11 ||
iti teṣāṃ girā bhūbhṛdabhūtsaṃkrāntatadvyathaḥ |
paraṃ duḥkhaṃ viśatyantaḥ satāṃ kedāravārivat || k12.12 ||
sarvāṅgavyāpinā tena janaduḥkhena bhūyasā |
viṣeṇevāvṛtaḥ so'bhūdudbhrāntahṛdayaḥ kṣaṇam || k12.13 ||
so'bravītkiṃ karomyatrābhujādhīne vipaurūṣe |
kathaṃ nāma pravartante durlakṣyeṣu pratikriyāḥ || k12.14 ||
dinamekaṃ vrajantvadya bhavanto nijamāspadam |
savrataścintayāmyeva rakṣāṃ vaḥ prasvakṣaye || k12.15 ||
iti rājavacaḥ śrutvā hṛṣṭāḥ pauramahattamāḥ |
jagadustaṃ samāvarjya pūjāvyañjanamajṇjalim || k12.16 ||
deva tvadavadhānena praṇayākarṇanena ca |
tvayi vinyastacintānāṃ nāsmākamadhunā śramaḥ || k12.17 ||
anuddhatamudāraṃ catvatprasādāvalokanam |
idameva janasyāsya jīvitānīva varṣati || k12.18 ||
kiṃ punaḥ priyametatte pīyūṣasadṛśaṃ vacaḥ |
tāpāpahaṃ mṛhu svādu kiṃ kiṃ na vidadhāti naḥ || k12.19 ||
kṛtī kṛtajñaḥ kāruṇyanidhiḥ sulabhadarśanaḥ |
labhyate bhāgyabhogyena saujanyasaralaḥ prabhu || k12.20 ||
pīyūṣādatipeśalaḥ paricayaḥ śrāvyaṃ vacaḥ pañcamam
ācāraḥ śaradinduvṛndamahaso'syānandasaṃdohadaḥ |
saccitte vasatāṃ satāṃ kimaparaṃ puṣpānmanahkomalaṃ
saujanyaṃ haricandanādapi paraṃ saṃtāpanirvāpaṇam || k12.21 ||
ityuktvā prayayuḥ paurāstaṃ praṇamya prasādinam |
kirantastadguṇodārāmāśākusumamālikām || k12.22 ||
rājāpi nagare kṛtvā bhūtapūjāvidhikramam |
śāntisvastikasaṃbhāraṃ cakāra niyatavrataḥ || k12.23 ||
yakṣī hārītikā nāma bālakān puravāsinī |
haratīti sa śuśrāva puradevatayoditam || k12.24 ||
tataḥ paurajanaiḥ sārdhaṃ sāmātyaḥ pṛthīvīpatiḥ |
kalandakanivāsākhye sthitaṃ veṇuvanāśrame || k12.25 ||
bhagavantaṃ yayau draṣṭuṃ sugataṃ doṣaśāntaye |
sarvaduḥkhajvarāyāsajuṣāmakaṭukauṣadham || k12.26 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā nṛpatirdūrātpraṇamya priyadarśanam |
upaviśyāgratastasmai pauraduḥkhaṃ nyavedayat || k12.27 ||
bhagavānapi vijñāya paurāṇāṃ saṃtatikṣayam |
cintāniścañcalaḥ kṣipramuvāca karuṇānidhiḥ || k12.28 ||
sa visṛjya jagadbandhuḥ sanṛpaṃ pauramaṇḍalam |
pātracīvaramādāya yayau yakṣīniketanam || k12.29 ||
tayā virahitaṃ prāpya tadgṛhaṃ bhagavān jinaḥ |
priyaṃkarākhyaṃ tatputraṃ nināyaikamadarśanam || k12.30 ||
yāte bhagavati kṣipraṃ yakṣī svagṛhamāgatā |
prabhūtaputrā nāpaśyatpriyaṃ putraṃ priyaṃkaram || k12.31 ||
tamīkṣamāṇāṃ vivaśā hṛtavatseva dhenukā |
babhrāma saṃbhramodbhrāntā sā janeṣu cā || k12.32 ||
hā priyaṃkara hā putra kka nu paśyāmi te mukham |
iti pralāpinī tāraṃ niḥśeṣāḥ sā yayau diśaḥ || k12.33 ||
sā vicityāśu sarvāśā nirāśā putradarśane |
krośantī parvatadvīpaṃ samudravalayaṃ yayau || k12.34 ||
martyabhūmimatikramya ghoreṣu nagareṣu sā |
svargoddeśeṣva śeṣeṣu vimānodyānaśāliṣu || k12.35 ||
śrāntā kkacinna viśrāntā yakṣiṇī praṇighātinī |
putramanviṣya nāpaśyallokapālapureṣu ca || k12.36 ||
kuberasyātha vacasā gatvā ca sugatāśramam |
bhagavantaṃ viyogārtā śaraṇyaṃ śaraṇaṃ yayau || k12.37 ||
tayā tadduḥkhavṛttāntaṃ saṃ niśamya niveditam |
tāmavocata śocantīṃ kiṃcitsmitasitādharaḥ || k12.38 ||
hārīti tava putrāṇāṃ santi pañcaśatānyaho |
iti tenoktamākarṇya yakṣī duḥkhakṣatāvadat || k12.39 ||
putralakṣe'pi bhagavan sahyā naikasutakṣatiḥ |
putrātpriyataraṃ nānyatkiṃ duḥkhaṃ tatkṣayātparam || k12.40 ||
putravāneva jānāti putrasnehaviṣavyathām |
sahajaiva sutaprītirakāraṇanibandhanā || k12.41 ||
* * * * * * * * |
malino vikalaḥ kṣīṇaḥ kasya nendusamaḥ sutaḥ || k12.42 ||
iti yakṣavadhūvākyaṃ śrutvā vātsalyavihvalam |
bhūtānukampī bhagavān sasmitastāmabhāṣata || k12.43 ||
śoko'yaṃ bahyputrāyā yadyekavirahe tava |
hṛte tvayaikavatsānāṃ putraike kīdṛśī vyathā || k12.44 ||
tvaṃ praviśya sadā gehaṃ strīṇāṃ putramalakṣitām |
aśnāsi putramātāpi vyāghrīva mṛgaśāvakān || k12.45 ||
yena yena svadehasya duḥkhaṃ yātyupabhogatām |
na tatparasya kurvīta samāno'nubhavaḥ śucām || k12.46 ||
tvaṃ buddhadharmasaṃghānāṃ trīṇi śikṣāpadāni cet |
gṛhṇāsi hiṃsāvimukhī tatprāpnoṣi priyaṃ sutam || k12.47 ||
ityuktā sā bhagavatā prāptaśikṣāpadā tataḥ |
hiṃsāvirāmāttaṃ gatvā putraṃ prāpa priyaṃkaram || k12.48 ||
tasyāḥ prāgjanmavṛttāntaṃ tasyāḥ karmaphalānvayam |
bhikṣubhirbhagavān pṛṣṭastadvṛttāntamabhāṣataḥ || k12.49 ||
purāsminneva nagare paurāhke'pyupabhoginaḥ |
parvatodyānamālāyāṃ vijahrurnartanādinā || k12.50 ||
atha tena pathā kāpi gopakāntā ghanastanī |
mathitaṃ paṇyamādāya hariṇākṣī samāyayau || k12.51 ||
garbhabhārālasagatiḥ pratyuptā gajagāminī |
sā śanairupasarpantī saspṛhaṃ tān vyalokayat || k12.52 ||
tasyā vanamṛgīmugdhairavadagdhā vilokanaiḥ |
asaṃvṛttā vilāsārdraiste'pi sotkaṇṭhatāṃ yayuḥ || k12.53 ||
sā tairnimantritā tatra madanakṣībatāṃ gatā |
hāritaṃ sahasā śīlaṃ na viveda pramādinī || k12.54 ||
tatasteṣū prayāteṣu tadā tasyā ratiśramāt |
papāta saha dhairyeṇa garbhaū kopādivāruṇaḥ || k12.55 ||
atrāntare samāyātaṃ tatpuṇyaistena vartamanā |
pratyekabuddhaṃ sādrākṣītkāyacittaprasādanam || k12.56 ||
sāsmai mathitamūlyāptamāmrāṇāṃ śatapañcakam |
dūrātpraṇāmavinatā manasaiva nyavedat || k12.57 ||
tataḥ puṇyarddhimatyasmin jātā yakṣakule'dhunā |
jātamāmrārpaṇenāsyāḥ putrāṇāṃ śatapañcakam || k12.58 ||
hiṃsāvatī pāpatyāgātśīlavismaraṇātparam |
pratyekabuddhapraṇateḥ prāptaśikṣāpadādya sā || k12.59 ||
iti vividhavipākaṃ karmatantraṃ vicitraṃ kimapisa kathayitvā tatra yakṣāṅganāyāḥ |
kalitakuśalasetuḥ saṃbhavābdhau janānāmakṛta sukṛtacittaṃ sarvalokasya śāstā || k12.60 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ hārītikādamanāvadānaṃ nāma dvādaśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


13. pratihāryāvadānam ||

yaḥ saṃkalpapathā sadaiva carati projjṛmbhamāṇādbhutaṃ
svapnairyasya na saṃgatiḥ paricayo yasminnapūrvakramaḥ |
vāṇī maunavatī ca yatra hi nṛṇāṃ yaḥ śrotranetrātithis
taṃ nirvyājajanaprabhāvavibhavaṃ manaurameyaṃ numaḥ || k13.1 ||
pure rājagṛhābhikhye bimbirāseṇa bhūbhujā |
pūjyamānaṃ jina dṛṣṭvā sthitaṃ veṇuvanāśrame || k13.2 ||
mātsaryaviṣasaṃtaptā mūrkhāḥ sarvjñamāninaḥ |
na sehire tadutkarṣaṃ prakāśamiva kauśikāḥ || k13.3 ||
malinaiḥ svavināśāya parabhāgoditaiḥ sadā |
kriyate vāsaraspardhā śārvaraistimirotkaraiḥ || k13.4 ||
maskarī saṃjayī vairairajitaḥ kakudastathā |
pūraṇajñātiputrādyā mūrkhāḥ kṣapaṇakāḥ pare || k13.5 ||
ūcurnṛpatimabhyetya māramāyāvimohitāḥ |
saṃgharṣadveṣadoṣeṇa dhūmenevāndhakāritāḥ || k13.6 ||
eṣa sarvajñatāmānī vane yaḥ śramaṇaḥ sthitaḥ |
ṛddhiprabhāvo bhavatā tasyāsmākaṃ ca dṛśyatām || k13.7 ||
ṛddhiprabhāvādyatkiṃcitjanavyāvarjanorjitam |
drśyate mahadāścaryaṃ prātihāryaṃ taducyate || k13.8 ||
śaktiḥ saṃsadi yasyāsti pratihāryasya darśane |
asmākaṃ tasya vā rājan pūjāḥ santu jagatrtraye || k13.9 ||
iti teṣāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā taddarpavimukho nṛpaḥ |
uvāca vāñchā keyaṃ vaḥ paṅgūnāṃ girilaṅghane || k13.10 ||
asamañjasamevaitatkā spardhāgneḥ pataṅgakaiḥ |
naitadvācyaṃ punarvādī mayā niṣkāsyate purāt || k13.11 ||
iti rājñā guṇajñena pratyākhyātodyamāḥ khalāḥ |
prayayuste nirālambe lambamānā ivāmbare || k13.12 ||
bimbisāro narapatirmūrkhatāpakṣapātavān |
anyaṃ vrajāmo bhūpālamiti te samacintayan || k13.13 ||
atrāṇtare bhagavati śrāvastīmabhitaḥ purīm |
prāpte tejavanārāmaṃ digantāneva te yayuḥ || k13.14 ||
te prasenajitaṃ tatra prāpya kosalabhūpatim |
prātihāryakṛtaspardhāṃ tāmevāsmai nyavedayan || k13.15 ||
guṇāntarajño nṛpatisteṣāṃ darpakṣayecchayā |
ṛddhisaṃdarśanotsāhādyayau bhagavato'ntikam || k13.16 ||
sa samabhyetya vinayātpraṇipatya tamabravīt |
bhagavan darpabalanaṃ tīrthyānāṃ kartumarhasi || k13.17 ||
ṛddhispardhānubandhena tvatprabhāvadidṛkṣayā |
svaguṇaślāghayāsmākaṃ taiḥ karṇau badhirīkṛtau || k13.18 ||
prakāśaya nijaṃ tejaḥ sajjanāvarjanaṃ vibho |
tīrthyābhidhānāmakhilaṃ prayātu pralayaṃ tamaḥ || k13.19 ||
iti rājavacaḥ śrutvā nirvikāro mahāśayaḥ |
bhagavān viratāmarṣaḥ saharṣastamabhāṣataḥ || k13.20 ||
rājannānyopamardāya vivādāya madāya vā |
vivekābharaṇārho'yaṃ kriyate guṇasaṃgrahaḥ || k13.21 ||
mātsaryamalinaiḥ kiṃ tairvicāraviguṇairguṇaiḥ |
ye haranti parotkarṣaṃ spardhābandhaprasāritāḥ || k13.22 ||
guṇācchādanamanyasya svaguṇena karoti yaḥ |
dharmastenāpraśastena svayameva nighātitaḥ || k13.23 ||
sadguṇānāṃ parikṣaiva paravailakṣyakāriṇī |
ucitā na hi śuddhānāṃ tulārohaviḍambanā || k13.24 ||
guṇavānapi nāyāti yaḥ pareṣu prasannatām |
sa dīpahastastatpātracchāyayā malinīkṛtaḥ || k13.25 ||
loka ta eva sarvajñā vidmaḥ kimadhikaṃ vayam |
parābhimānābhibhavaprāgalbhyaṃ svaparābhavaḥ || k13.26 ||
iti śrutvā bhagavataḥ praśamābhimataṃ vacaḥ |
bhṛśamabhyarthanāṃ rājā cakārāścaryadarśane || k13.27 ||
tataḥ dṛcchrādbhagavatā kṛtābhyupagamo nṛpaḥ |
rājadhānīṃ yayau hṛṣṭaḥ saptāhāvadhisaṃvidā || k13.28 ||
asminnavasare bhrātā bhūmibharturasodaraḥ |
cacārāntaḥpuropānte prāsādatalavartmanā || k13.29 ||
salīlaṃ vrajatastasya karmavātairiveritā |
kusumasrakpapātāṃse rājapatnīkarāccyutā || k13.30 ||
tasya vijñātadoṣasya doṣaṃ saṃbhāvya sākṣibhiḥ |
piśunāḥ kiṃvadantīṃ tāṃ cakrire rājagāminīm || k13.31 ||
chidramalpamapi prāpya kṣudrāḥ sarvāpakāriṇaḥ |
dvijihvāḥ praviśantyāśu prabhūnāṃ śūnyamāśayam || k13.32 ||
piśunapretiro rājā bhrāturīrṣyāviṣolbaṇaḥ |
chedamasyādideśāśu pāṇipādasya mūrcchitaḥ || k13.33 ||
nikṛttapāṇicaraṇaḥ kumāraḥ karmaviplavāt |
sa vadhyavasudhāśāyī viveśa viṣamāpadam || k13.34 ||
tīvravyathāparivṛtaṃ śocadbhirmātṛbandhubhiḥ |
dadṛśustaṃ kṣapaṇakāḥ kṣaṇaṃ nayanacālane || k13.35 ||
tān samabhyetya śokārtāste rājasutabāndhavāḥ |
jagadustatparitrāṇasaṃliptāḥ sarvaprāṇinah || k13.36 ||
doṣaṃ nigṛhīto'yaṃ kālanāmā nṛpātmajam |
sarvajñavādino yūyaṃ prasādo'sya vidhīyatām || k13.37 ||
iti tai prasaradbāṣpairarthyamānāhpralāpibhiḥ |
te maunino niṣpratibhā vailakṣyādanyato yayuḥ || k13.38 ||
atha tena yathāyāto bhikṣuḥ sugataśāsanāt |
ānando vidadhe'ṅgāni tasya satyopayācanāt || k13.39 ||
rājaputrastu saṃjātapāṇipādaḥ prasannadhīḥ |
jinaṃ śaraṇamabhyetya tadupasthāyako'bhavat || k13.40 ||
saptaratre vyatīte'tha śrāntihāryaṃ gṛhaṃ mahat |
ṛddhiṃ bhagavato draṣṭuṃ mahīpatirakārayat || k13.41 ||
upaviṣṭe nṛpe tatra saha kṣapaṇakādibhiḥ |
kalpavṛkṣīkṛtā bhūmirabhavatsugatecchayā || k13.42 ||
tataḥ prāpteṣu deveṣu draṣṭuṃ bhagavataḥ prabhām |
ratnapradīpaṃ bhagavān bheje siṃhāsanaṃ mahat || k13.43 ||
tejodhātuṃ prapannasya tasya gaṇḍasamudgataiḥ |
vyāptaṃ pāvakasaṃghātairabhūdbhuvanamaṇḍalam || k13.44 ||
śānte śanaiḥ kamalakānanasaṃnikāśe vahnau samastabhuvanasthitibhaṅgabhītyā |
dehāttato bhagavataḥ karuṇāmburāśeḥ pūrṇāmṛtormivimalā rucayaḥ prasasruḥ || k13.45 ||
lāvaṇyasāramaticandrasahasrakāntiṃ tejaḥ pratānaviphalīkṛtasūryacakram |
taṃ nāganāyakanikāyavilocanāni prītyā papuḥ sukṛtalabdhamapūrvaharṣam || k13.46 ||
vaidūryanālavipulāruṇaratnapātrakāntollasatkanakakesarakarṇikāni |
abhyudyayuḥ kṣititalādatha tatsamīpe padmāni saurabhabharāhṛtaṣaṭpadāni || k13.47 ||
teṣūpaviṣṭamatha kāñcanacārukāntiṃ snigdhekṣasṇaṃ sugatacakramadṛśyatārāt |
pīyūṣapeśalaśaśidyutiśītalena yasyodayena sahasā sukhamāpa lokaḥ || k13.48 ||
teṣāṃ prabhāvavibhavaṃ bahgavān babhāra madhye'dhikaṃ kanakaśaila ivācalānām |
suskandhabandhuraghanadyutisaṃniveśaḥ prāṃśuḥ surakṣitiruhāmiva pārijātaḥ || k13.49 ||
svargāṅganākarakuśeśayakīryamāṇairamlānamālyavalayaiḥ kalitottamāṅgāḥ |
tasyānanāmbujavilokananirnimeṣe martyā api kṣaṇamavāpuramartyabhāvam || k13.50 ||
vyomāṅgaṇeṣu suradundubhiśaṅkhatūryaghoṣāvṛtaḥ kusumavarṣamahāṭṭahāsaḥ |
gandharvakinnaramunīśvaracāraṇānāṃ sphīṭaścacāra bhagavatstutivādanādaḥ || k13.51 ||
tatrāruṇādharadalāddaśanāṃśuśubhrādvyākīrṇakeśarakulāddaśanāravindāt |
satsaurabhaṃ bhagavataḥ svarasaṃnivṛttaṃ dhanyāḥ papurmadhuravāṅbhadhu puṇyasūtam || k13.52 ||
pāpaṃ vimuñcata niṣiñcata puṇyabījaṃ vairaṃ parityajata sāmyasukhaṃ bhajadhvam |
jñānāmṛtaṃ pibataṃ mṛtyuviṣāpahāri neyaṃ tanuḥ kuśalakarmasakhī cirāya || k13.53 ||
lakṣmīścalā taruṇatā ca jarānuyātā kāyo'pyapāyanicayasya nivāsa eva |
prāṇāḥ śarīrakakuṭiṣu muhūrtapānthā nityodaye kuruta dharmamaye prayatnam || k13.54 ||
ityādibhirbhagavataḥ pravibhaktadīptajñānairvivekavimalaiḥ kuśalopadeśaiḥ |
vajrairivāśu dalanaṃ prayayau janānāṃ satkāyadṛṣṭisamaviṃśatiśṛṅgaśailaḥ || k13.55 ||
ṛddhiprabhāṃ bhagavataḥ pravibhāvya tīrthyā mantrahatā viṣadharā iva bhagnadarpāḥ |
dīpā ivārkakiraṇapratibhābhibhūtāścitrārpitā iva yayuściraniścalatvam || k13.56 ||
atrāntare bhagavataḥ satataṃ vipakṣaḥ sarvātmanā kṣapaṇako navadharmayakṣaḥ |
kṣiptaśravān sa vṛtavarṣavaraiścakāra vidravya randhraśaraṇān bhuvi vajrapāṇiḥ || k13.57 ||
addiśya tānatha kṛpārdrabhayāśaraṇyaḥ sarvopadeśaviṣayān bhagavān babhāṣe |
bhūbhṛdvanābanimaṇirvivarādi sarvaṃ tene bhayeṣu śaraṇaṃ kila kātarāṇām || k13.58 ||
buddhiṃ prabodha mama dhāmbi nidhāya buddhiṃ
dharmaṃ sasaṃghamapi ye śaraṇaṃ prapannāḥ |
teṣā jagatkṣayabhayeṣvapi nirbhayāṇāṃ
naivānyataḥ śaraṇadainyaparigraho'sti || k13.59 ||
