durvāre paralokatīvratimire dharmaḥ pravṛddho'śumān
dānaṃ duḥsahapāpatāpavipadāmabhyudgame vāridaḥ |
prajñā mohamahāprapātaviṣamaśvabhre karālambanaṃ
dainyākrāntamahīnameva śaraṇaṃ sarvatra puṇyaṃ nṛṇām || k13.60 ||
iti timiravṛtākṣṇāṃ cakṣurunmīlanārhaṃ daśanamaṇimarīcivyajyamānaprakāśam |
sadasi sugatacandraḥ śuddhadharmopadeśaṃ sthirapadamiva kṛtvā kānanaṃ svaṃ jagāma || k13.61 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ prātihāryāvadānaṃ nāma trayodaśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


14. devāvatāravadānam |

jayati mahatāṃ prabhāvaḥ paścādagre ca vartamāno yaḥ |
janakuśalakarmasaraṇiḥ prakāśaratnadīpo vaḥ || k14.1 ||
purā surapure pāṇḍukambalākhye śilātale |
samīpe pārijātasya kovidārasya suprabhe || k14.2 ||
kṛtvā tridaśasaṃghānāṃ bhagavān dharmadeśanām |
anugrahāya martyānāṃ jambūdvīpamavātarat || k14.3 ||
amarairanuyātasya tasyāvatarato bhuvam |
vimānakānanākīrṇaṃ babhūva bhuvanāṅgaṇam || k14.4 ||
tasya dantāṃśusaṃtānairupadeśamivācitam |
jagrāha candraruciraṃ cāmaraṃ caturānanaḥ || k14.5 ||
chatraṃ śataśalākāṅkamanaṅkaṃ raṅkupāṇḍuram |
prasādamiva sākāramādade'sya puraṃdaraḥ || k14.6 ||
saṃkāśyanagaropānte kānanodumbarāntike |
avatīrṇaṃ sukṛtinaḥ sānandāstaṃ vavandire || k14.7 ||
tasminnutpalavarṇākhyā bhikṣukī janasaṃgame |
alabdhāvasarā draṣṭuṃ nṛparūpamupādade || k14.8 ||
pradīptaratnamukuṭaṃ gaṇḍatāṇḍavikuṇḍalam |
dṛṣṭvaivāsya navaṃ rūpaṃ jahāsoṣṇīṣapallavaḥ || k14.9 ||
sā cintayantī ko vāyaṃ janairnirvivaraḥ puraḥ |
antaraṃ nṛparūpaṃ me dṛṣṭvā dāsyati sādaraḥ || k14.10 ||
atp'nyathā tu bhagavatpraṇatirmama durlabhā |
na guṇaṃ gauravasthānamaiśvaryapraṇayī janaḥ || k14.11 ||
aho tṛṇatṛlālolairniḥsāravirasairdhanaiḥ |
hriyate vāsanābhyāsānnirvicāratayā janaḥ || k14.12 ||
dattāntarā sā sahasā janena nṛpagauravāt |
lolahāraṃ bhagavataḥ praṇāmamakārotpuraḥ || k14.13 ||
asminnavasare bhikṣurudayī nāma saṃsadi |
tāṃ vilokya tathārūpāmavadatsasmitānanaḥ || k14.14 ||
iyamutpalavarṇākhyā bhikṣukī nṛparūpiṇī |
ṛddhyā bhagavataḥ pādau vandate janavanditā || k14.15 ||
utpalāmodavarṇābhuāṃ vijñāteyaṃ mayā purā |
ityuktvā virate tasmin bhagavānapyabhāṣata || k14.16 ||
ayuktameva bhikṣukyā darpadṛddhiprakāśanam |
karoti praśamaglānimabhimānena ca jvaraḥ || k14.17 ||
ityuktvā bhagavān kṛtvā tāṃ tāṃ śubhropadeśanām |
visṛjya devān prayayau svapadaṃ saha bhikṣubhiḥ || k14.18 ||
tatropaviṣṭaḥ pṛṣṭastaiḥ praṇatairbhikṣukīkathām |
prāgjanmakarmasaṃbaddhāṃ sarvajñastānabhāṣata || k14.19 ||
vārāṇasyāmabhūtpūrvaṃsārthavāho mahādhanaḥ |
patnī dhanavatī nāma tasya prāṇasamābhavat || k14.20 ||
pāṇipallavinī tanvīyaovanodyānamañjarī |
phalapuṣpavatī kāle sā tasmādgarbhamādadhe || k14.21 ||
atrāntare jalanidhidvīpānugamanodyatam |
pratyāsannaviyogārtā sā vallabhamabhāṣata || k14.22 ||
kiyatī dhanasaṃpattirvṛddhimadyāpi nīyate |
yatkṛte ghpragambhīrastīryate makarākaraḥ || k14.23 ||
bahvapāyaṃ dhanādānaṃ nirapāyā guṇārjanam |
svadeśātparadeśaṃ hi gacchanti draviṇārthinah || k14.24 ||
kecidduḥkhānnivartante dūraṃ gatvāpi niṣphalāḥ |
niścalā dhaninaścānye karmaṇāmeṣa niścayaḥ || k14.25 ||
iti priyāvacaḥ śrutvā sārthavāhastato'bhyadhāt |
mugdhe saṃbhāvanāpātro bhavatyevaṃ dhanodyataḥ || k14.26 ||
dhanārjanavihīnānāṃ puṅguvanmūlabhakṣaṇāt |
adya śvo vā sukhasthānāṃ bhogaiḥ saha parikṣayaḥ || k14.27 ||
svagṛhe'pi daridrāṇāṃ janahkrakacaniṣṭhuraḥ |
dhanināṃ paraloko'pi premasnigdhajanaṃ bhuvaḥ || k14.28 ||
kṣīṇamapyudyataṃ vṛddhyai na veṇuṃ bandhate janaḥ |
na tu sa pūrṇatāṃ yāti pratyāsannaparikṣayaḥ || k14.29 ||
mūrkho'pi viduṣāṃ vandyaḥ strīṇāṃ vṛddho'pi vallabhaḥ |
klibo'pi sevyaḥ śūrāṇāmāsannābhyudayo janaḥ || k14.30 ||
kenānyakaraṇaṃ bhuktvā pītvā kāvyāmṛtāni vā |
kṣaṇaṃ vicakṣaṇenāpi kṣuptipāse vivarjite || k14.31 ||
yasyārthaḥ sa guṇonnataiḥ kṛtanutiḥ kaṃ vā na dhatte guṇaṃ
dāridyrodayadoṣadūṣitarucāṃ nirmālyatulyā guṇāḥ |
vittenaiva guṇā guṇī na tu dhanī dhanyo dhanī no guṇī kāyādduṣkṛtasaṃnipātaśamanādāyurguṇānāṃ dhanam || k14.32 ||
iti prāṇādhikārthasya patyurākarṇya sā vacaḥ |
sāñjanāśrukaṇotkīrṇā latevābhṛtaṣaṭpadā || k14.33 ||
atha pravahaṇaṃ bheje sārthanāthastayā saha |
tīvratṛṣṇāgṛhītānāṃ hastapātraṃ mahodadhiḥ || k14.34 ||
tasya jāyāsakhasyāsau karmavātānuvartinaḥ |
abhajyata pravahaṇaṃ samanorathajīvitam || k14.35 ||
tataḥ phalakamāsādya bhāgaśeṣācca karmaṇaḥ |
kaśerudvīpamāsādya tatpatirvipadaṃ gataḥ || k14.36 ||
anāthāṃ tatra śocantīṃ vihaṅgaḥ puruṣākṛtiḥ |
tāmāpa pādadīrṇāśāṃ suvarṇakulasaṃbhavaḥ || k14.37 ||
sa kāntāṃ sumukho nāma tāmuvāca rucākṛtaḥ |
samāśvasihi lolākṣi nirbhayo'yaṃ tavāśrayaḥ || k14.38 ||
divyeyaṃ subhagā bhūmirvayaṃ tvatpraṇayaiṣaṇaḥ |
puṇyāyātāsi kalyāṇi ghoro'yaṃ makarākaraḥ || k14.39 ||
ityuktvā tena śanakairnītā ratnālayaṃ gṛham |
saṃpūrṇagarbhā tanayaṃ cārurūpamasūta sā || k14.40 ||
vardhamāne śiśau tasmin sā śanaiḥ priyavādinā |
mugdhā tena vidagdhena saṃbhogābhimukhīkṛtā || k14.41 ||
saralatvānmṛdutvācca samīpapraṇayī janaḥ |
svayamāliṅgyate strībhirlatābhiriva pādapaḥ || k14.42 ||
divyodyāneṣu sā tena ramamāṇāṃ ghanastanī |
kumāraṃ rucirākāraṃ suṣuve sadṛśaṃ pituḥ || k14.43 ||
tasmin padmamukhābhikhye yauvanālaṃkṛtākṛtau |
sumukhaḥ pakṣiṇāṃ rājā kāle lokāntaraṃ yayau || k14.44 ||
tataḥ padmamukhaḥ śrīmānāsasāda padaṃ pituḥ |
guṇināmavivādena svādhīnāḥ kulasaṃpadaḥ || k14.45 ||
prāptaiśvaryaṃ tamavadadvijane jananī sutam |
tatprabhāvasya saṃbhāvya sarvatra prabhaviṣṇutām || k14.46 ||
putra prāptā tvayā lakṣmīriyaṃ nijakulocitā |
ayaṃ tu sārthavāhānme jātaḥ putro niraṃśakaḥ || k14.47 ||
vārāṇasyāmayaṃ rājā śaśaktyā kriyatāṃ tvayā |
prītīsaṃvādasāsvādaḥ svadeśe seva saṃpadaḥ || k14.48 ||
iti māturgirā pakṣī pakṣapātena bhūyasā |
skandhe bhrātaramāropya vyomnā vārāṇasīṃ yayau || k14.49 ||
tatra siṃhāsanāsīnaṃ brahmadattaṃ mahīpatim |
saṃjaghānaikadainaṃ sa vajragranakharaiḥ kharaiḥ || k14.50 ||
abhiṣicyāgrajaṃ pūrṇaṃ tasminneva nṛpāsane |
amātyān so'vadadbhītān samagrānagravikramaḥ || k14.51 ||
yasya rājño'bhiṣiktasya mayā yaḥ pracalīkaraḥ (?) |
so'pyatītaḥ prabho bhaktyā tamevānugamiṣyati || k14.52 ||
ityuktvā pravarāmātyān svairaṃ vihagapuṇgavaḥ |
yayau bhrātaramāmantrya punardeśanasaṃvidā || k14.53 ||
sa eva brahmadatto'yamiti mantrita mantriṇām (?) |
sa nṛpaḥ khyātimāyātaḥ svajaneṣu pareṣu ca || k14.54 ||
atrāntare samānītā sagarbhā hastinī vanāt |
na mumocārdhaniryātagarbhaṃ ruddhamivāntare || k14.55 ||
sādhvīkarāgrasaṃsparśādiyaṃ garbhaṃ vimuñcati |
iti mauhūrtikādiṣṭaṃ rājñe mantrī nyavedayat || k14.56 ||
śāsanādatha bhūbhartuḥ spṛṣṭvā hastena hastinīm |
antaḥ purāṅganāścakrustatra satyopayāvanam || k14.57 ||
tāsāṃ satyagirā garbhaṃ nātyajatkariṇī yadā |
tadā vilakṣyaḥ sarvo'bhūtbhūpasyāntaḥpure janaḥ || k14.58 ||
atha gopāṅganābhyetya śīlasatyopayācanam |
kṛtvā pasparśa kariṇīṃ yenāsau garbhamatyajat || k14.59 ||
kṛtvā nijajāyānāṃ jñātvā śīladaridratām |
mene gopāṃ manaḥsveva tāṃ jagatrtritaye satīm || k14.60 ||
sa satījātilobhena sośumbāṃ nāma tatsutām |
pariṇīyānināyāgre devīśabdasya pātratām || k14.61 ||
tasyāḥ saṃcintya lāvaṇyaṃ capalatvaṃ ca yoṣitām |
sa sarvagāmīnīṃ nidrāmapi tatyāja śaṅkitaḥ || k14.62 ||
asminnavasare druṣṭuṃ bhrātaraṃ vihagādhiyaḥ |
yayau padmamukhastatra snehādatiśayotsukhaḥ || k14.63 ||
bhūpālo'pi tamāliṅgya prītyā vihitasatkṛtiḥ |
vijane svakathāmasmai nivedya punarabravīt || k14.64 ||
śīlasatyatulārohātdṛṣṭadoṣeṇa yoṣitām |
mamāntaḥpuravaimukhyātvivāho'bhinavaḥ kṛtaḥ || k14.65 ||
rūpayauvanagāminmyāṃ tasyāmapi na me dhṛtiḥ |
ekatra dṛṣṭadoṣāṇāṃ sarvatrāśaṅkate manaḥ || k14.66 ||
tasmāttava pure bhrātarvimānuṣye nidhīyatām |
śīlaśankāṃ parityajya bhavāmi vigatajvaraḥ || k14.67 ||
tasmātpratiniśaṃ pakṣī śāsanāttava madgṛham |
prāpayiṣyati tāṃ svairamityayaṃ me manorathaḥ || k14.68 ||
iti bhrāturvacaḥ śrutvā tamuvāca vihaṅgamaḥ |
īrṣyāśaṅkākalaṅkena rājanmithyaiva mā kṛthāḥ || k14.69 ||
na nāma ramate ramye nāsvādaṃ vetti bhojane |
na paśyati na nidrāti nityamīrṣyāvaśāturaḥ || k14.70 ||
klībaḥ kāmī sukhī vidvān dhanī namraḥ prabhuḥ kṣamī |
arthī mānyaḥ khalaḥ snigdhaḥ strī satīti kathaiva kā || k14.71 ||
saralatve'pi kuṭilāḥ sthāyinyo'pyaticañcalāḥ |
kulīnā apui pārśvasthamāliṅgantyabalā latāḥ || k14.72 ||
dṛṣṭirlolādharo rāgī bhrūrvākrā kaṭhinau stanau |
dṛśyate naiva nirdoṣaḥ strīṇāmavayaveṣvapi || k14.73 ||
bhujyante kuśalaiḥ śyāmā bhramadbhramaravibhramaiḥ |
mūlānveṣī sarojinyāḥ paṅkenaivāvalipyata || k14.74 ||
naikasmin vismayabhuvāṃ sasmitānāṃ niyantrite |
śuciśīlavirāmāṇāṃ rāmāṇāṃ ramaṇe matiḥ || k14.75 ||
tathāpi matpurodyāne nirjane sā nidhīyatām || k14.76 ||
ityuktaḥ pakṣiṇā bhrātā nṛpatirvisasarja tām |
kāntāṃ kaśerukadvīpe taṃ ca satkṛtya sādaraḥ || k14.77 ||
sāpi pratiniśaṃ vyomnaḥ khagārūḍhā samāyayau |
divyagandhamayīṃ mālāmādāya dvīpasaṃbhavām || k14.78 ||
pārijātānvayatarostāni puṣpāṇyavāpa sā |
khyātāni timirāṇīva bhramadbhṛṅgāndhakārataḥ || k14.79 ||
atha vārāṇasīvāsī kadācinmānavābhudhaḥ |
prayayau samidhāhārī dvijanmā kānanaṃ yuvā || k14.80 ||
tatra kinnarakāminyā sa dṛṣṭaḥ spaṣṭamanmathaḥ |
yasya saṃdarśanenaiva sābhavadvismṛtasmṛtiḥ || k14.81 ||
asau navābhilāṣeṇa janakeneva sārpitā |
kāntā kāntimayī nāma vijahāra guhāgṛhe || k14.82 ||
tatrābharaṇaratnāśuḥ pratītatimirotkare |
ramamāṇā ciraṃ tena kāle putramavāpa sā || k14.83 ||
balavānmarududbhavaḥ sa bālye'pi yadā śiśuḥ |
tadā mātā tasya saṃjñāṃ śīghraga ityasādhayat || k14.84 ||
sāpi nivāghnasaṃbhogā sukhātṛptā guhāntare |
priyaṃ dhṝtvā sadā yāti pidhāya śilayā gṛham || k14.85 ||
kadācidatha vṛttāntaṃ nijapitrā niveditam |
ākarṇya śīghragaścintāvismayākulito'vadat || k14.86 ||
śilānibaddhadvāre'sminnandhasyaiva gṝhāntare |
aho sneho'pyayaṃ tāta tava bandhanatāṃ gataḥ || k14.87 ||
ehi vārāṇasīmeva gacchāvaste nijāspadam |
vioulāmapyayatnena śilāmutsārayāmyaham || k14.88 ||
svadeśavirahakleśaṃ dyḥśaṃ sahase katham |
tyaktuṃ na śakyate kauściddeśo deha iva svakaḥ || k14.89 ||
bhāraṃ draviṇasaṃbhāraṃ vetti granthiguṇāguṇaḥ |
bhogaṃ nirupabhogaṃ ca svadeśavirahī janaḥ || k14.90 ||
ityuktvā sa guhāgohādutpāṭya vipulāṃ śilām |
kṛtābhyupagamenāśu janakena yayau saha || k14.91 ||
prayātayostatastūrṇaṃ samabhyetyātha kinnarī |
śūnyaṃ dṛṣṭvā guhāgehaṃ nirvedādityavintayat || k14.92 ||
aho me vismṛtasnehaḥ sa gataḥ kkāpi durjanaḥ |
dvijihvānāṃ bhujaṃgānāṃ kauṭilyaṃ vā kimadbhutam || k14.93 ||
na ramante palāyante paryante sukharāgiṇaḥ |
cirasthā api niḥsnehāḥ śukā iva dvijātayaḥ || k14.94 ||
iti saṃcintya sā patyurnikārātprītimatyajat |
puṣpopamāni premāṇi na sahante kadarthanām || k14.95 ||
vidyāguṇena kenāsau putro me bhuvi jāīvati |
iti dhyātvā sakhīhaste tasmai vīṇāṃ dideśa sā || k14.96 ||
saṃbhogasukhapaṇyaiva prītiḥ patiṣu yoṣitām |
aparyuṣitavātsalyā putraprītistu niścalā || k14.97 ||
javena vrajatostūrṇaṃ tayordaurjanyalajjayā |
śīgragāya dadau vīṇāṃ tatsakhī vegagāminī || k14.98 ||
ādyā tantririyaṃ nāsyāḥ spraṣṭavyā vighnakāriṇī |
ityābhāṣya tayā dattāṃ vīṇāṃ prāpya jagāma saḥ || k14.99 ||
tataḥ svadeśe janakaṃ svagṛhe viniveśya saḥ |
vīṇāpravīṇaḥ sarvatra lābhapūjāmavāptavān || k14.100 ||
tataḥ kadāvidvāṇijā ambudhidvīpagāminā |
āropitaḥ pravahaṇaṃ divyavīṇānurāgiṇā || k14.101 ||
vīṇāmūrcchanayā tasya śrotrapīyūṣadhārayā |
kṣaṇe kṣaṇe samudro'pi nistaraṅga ivābhavat || k14.102 ||
athādyatantrisaṃsparśādutpannopaplavoplute |
bhagne pravahaṇe sarvavaṇijāmabhavatkṣayaḥ || k14.103 ||
tato balāhakāvāptyā pavanapretitaḥ kṣaṇāt |
kaśerudvīpamāsede karmaśeṣeṇa śīghragaḥ || k14.104 ||
tatrābdhikūlasaṃlīnaṃ divyodyānaṃ praviśya saḥ |
śyāmāṃ dadarśa sośumbāṃ mūrdhanyastabakastanām || k14.105 ||
grandhantīṃ timirākhyānāṃ puṣpāṇāmujjvalasrajam |
nibandhanaṃ tanuguṇaiḥ kurvāṇāmapyacetasām || k14.106 ||
sāpi taṃ rucirākāraṃ dṛṣṭvā vismayamāyayau |
dhīraṃ śaiśavatāruṇyasaṃdhimadhyasthatāṃ gatam || k14.107 ||
māramārutasaṃcālasakampakarapallavā |
sā śīrṇaśīlakusumā lateva praṇanāma tam || k14.108 ||
cirārūḍheva sahasā prītiḥ prauḍhā tayorabhūt |
prāgjanmasnehasaṃlīnaṃ na muñcati mano manaḥ || k14.109 ||
ramamāṇāṃ divā tena niśāyāṃ ca mahībhujā |
mene vāmācaritatāṃ tāṃ priyo gūḍhakāmukaḥ || k14.110 ||
tena vārāṇasīṃ gantuṃ jñātvā vṛttaṃ samarthitā |
tannināya khagārūḍhā tadgirā mīlitekṣaṇam || k14.111 ||
vārito'pi tadā vyomni nayanonmīlane tayā |
so'bahvatsahasaivāndhaścāpalādvivṛtekṣaṇaḥ || k14.112 ||
sā tamantaḥpurodyāne nidhāya bhayakātarā |
viveśa śokasaṃtaptā śayyāveśma mahīpateḥ || k14.113 ||
dūyamānena manasā rajanīmativāhya tām |
prātarna gantuṃ na sthātuṃ cintākrāntā śaśāka sā || k14.114 ||
atrāntare samudbhūtaścyutasaurabhanirbharaḥ |
madhumāso vilāsānāṃ yauvanaṃ puṣpadhanvanaḥ || k14.115 ||
kokilālikulaiḥ kālaḥ kālaḥ kālo viyoginām |
śīrṇaśokanavāśokaduḥsahaḥ pratidṛṣyate || k14.116 ||
rājāpyaviratautsukyādudyānaṃ gantumudyataḥ |
dinamekaṃ na tatyāja sośumbāṃ kāmamohitaḥ || k14.117 ||
sa tayāsaha rāgasya madasya madanasya ca |
saṃsāramiva viśrāntipadapuṣpavanaṃ yayau || k14.118 ||
tatra bālānilālolalatāvailakṣyakāriṇīm |
paśyan pramodamāsede yaditāmeva bhūpatiḥ || k14.119 ||
anyarāgaviṣākrāntā sāpyabhūnmalinasmṛtiḥ |
sukhamapyasukhaṃ vetti cintāśalyākulaṃ manaḥ || k14.120 ||
antargatabhujaṃgābhiḥ strībhiratyantarāgiṇaḥ |
kaṇṭhe kṛtābhirnṛtyanti mālābhiriva mohitāḥ || k14.121 ||
tatraikāntalatākuñjanikuñjanihitasthitiḥ |
andhaḥ saurabhamāghrāya sośumbātimirasrajaḥ || k14.122 ||
sahasaiva vikāreṇa rāgādvismṛtasaṃvṛtiḥ |
agāyanmadanakṣībā gaṇayanti bahyaṃ kutaḥ || k14.123 ||
tanupavanavilāsaiḥ kīryamāṇaḥ priyāyāḥ
samadabadanapadmāmodasaṃbhārasāraḥ |
timirakusumagandhaḥ so'yamāyāti dūrāt
bhramarasaraṇivīṇāvibhramārāvaramyaḥ || k14.124 ||
śrutvā hṛdayasaṃvādagītaṃ tattasya bhūpatiḥ |
udyānavicayaṃ kṛtvā taṃ dadarśa latāntare || k14.125 ||
gāḍharocamadakṣībaṃ sa taṃ papraccha śaṅkitaḥ |
api jānasi sośumbāṃ tasya vā lakṣaṇaṃ tanoḥ || k14.126 ||
so'bravītkiṃ na jānāmi sośumbāṃ bimbapāṭalām |
upaviṣṭo'dhare yasyā rāgarājye manobhavaḥ || k14.127 ||
nyastaṃ smareṇeva tadūrūmūle lekhāmayaṃ svastikamasti kāntam |
āvartaśobhā stanamaṇḍale vā lāvaṇyakallolanibhāsti tasyāḥ || k14.128 ||
etadākarṇya nṛpatiḥ sadyaḥ saṃtāpaśoṣitam |
mumoca rāgakusumaṃ nirmālyaminva cetasaḥ || k14.129 ||
so'bravīnnāsti nārīṇāṃ śīlarakṣā śatairati |
khapuṣpamāleva satī sarvathā naiva jāyate || k14.130 ||
ityuktvāndhena tāṃ rājā saha śmaśānakānanam |
gardabhāropitāṃ tūrṇaṃ tatyāja nagarādbahiḥ || k14.131 ||
sā tena saha nirlajjā vrajantī dinasaṃkṣaye |
aṭavyāṃ caurapatinā prāptaiva saha saṃpadā || k14.132 ||
janairabhidrute tasmin sahasā cauramaṇḍale |
niraparāśa evāndhaścaurabhrāntyā nipātitaḥ || k14.133 ||
cauro'pi niśāṃ bhuktvā sośumbāṃ kṣasṇasaṃgataḥ |
gṛhītvābharaṇānyasyā jagāmottīrya nimnagām || k14.134 ||
kāraṇḍavāyāhsaritastasyāstīre nirambarā |
śuśova sāñjanairaśrujālaiḥ sā malinastanī || k14.135 ||
tasmin kṣaṇe mukhāsaktaṃ māṃsamutsṛjya jambuke |
yāte jalotplutaṃ matsyaṃ tajjahāraṃ vihaṅgamaḥ || k14.136 ||
matsye nimagne sahasā khagena piśite hṛte |
sa babhūvobhayabhraṃśāccintāniścalalocanaḥ || k14.137 ||
tasyāstaṃ vīkṣyaṃ duḥkhe'pi mukhe smitamadṝśyata |
hāsaḥ parasya skhalite duḥsthasyāpyupajāyate || k14.138 ||
sa tāṃ vailakṣyakupitaḥ provācānucitasmitām |
aho hasasi māṃ loke hāsyāyatanatāṃ gatām || k14.139 ||
nṛpaṃ tyaktvāgatā hyandhamtyaktavāndhaṃ cauramāśritā |
tavāhamubhayabhraṣṭaḥ tribhraṣṭāyāḥ smitāspadam || k14.140 ||
āstāṃ vaḥ parihāso'yaṃ taṃ yuktyāhaṃ karomi te |
khalāste viṣamasthānāṃ ye biḍambanapaṇḍitāḥ || k14.141 ||
ityuktvā nagarīṃ gatvā sa nṛpāya nyavedayat |
sośumbā te nadītīre tapoyukteti manmatiḥ || k14.142 ||
atha nināya tāṃ rājā vitīryābharaṇāmbaram |
doṣamācchādayatyeva rāgadveṣaḥ śarīriṇām || k14.143 ||
saivādyotpalavarṇeyamudāyī śīghrago'pyasau |
prāgjanmāntarapuṇyena bhikṣḥuvratamupāgatau || k14.144 ||
abhavadatirasārdraṃ mānāsṃ rāgayoge
yadu madanavidheyaṃ rāgayuktaṃ yadasyāḥ |
virataśamavirārā tena tasminmuhūtre
kṛtanarapatirūpānandinaṃ māṃ vavande || k14.145 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ devāvatārāvadānaṃ nāma caturdaśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


15. śilānikṣepāvadānam |

balabhatuladhairyavīryaṃ sāścaryaṃ bhavati saprabhāvāṇām |
mahadāśrayayogādyasmai sarvaṃ mahimatvamāyāti || k15.1 ||
purā kuśīpurīṃ ramyāṃ mallānāṃ balaśālinām |
svecchāvihārī bhagāvān pratasthe sugataḥ svayam || k15.2 ||
te tadāgamanaṃ śrutvā kalyāṇaṃ kuśalaiṣiṇaḥ |
vartmasaṃśodhanaṃ cakrurupacārapadodyatāḥ || k15.3 ||
deśaṃ bhūṣayatāṃ teṣāṃ saṃsiktaṃ candanodakaiḥ |
tṛṇakaṇṭakapāṣāṇasarkarāreṇuvarjitam || k15.4 ||
madhye samāyayau bhūminimagnā mahatī śilā |
avasannā visannā ca vadhūrvindhyagireriva || k15.5 ||
tāmutpāṭayatāṃ teṣāṃ kuddālabhujarajjubhiḥ |
māso jagāma na tvasyāḥ sahasrāṃśe'pyabhūta kṣatiḥ || k15.6 ||
atha samsārasaṃtāpapraśamāmṛtadīdhitiḥ |
āyayau bhagavān sarvamānasollāsabāndhavaḥ || k15.7 ||
ghanāndhakāravirativyaktasatphaladarśanaḥ |
prasādasaṃvibhaktāśaḥ prakāśa iva śāradaḥ || k15.8 ||
sa tān dṛṣṭvā pariśrāntān viphalakleśapiḍāītān |
śrutvā ca tadvyavasitaṃ tānūce praṇatānanān || k15.9 ||
aho kleśaphalārambhaḥ prayāsavyavasāyinām |
saṃsārakarmaṇīvāsmin vyāpāre vaḥ samudyamaḥ || k15.10 ||
prārambhe viṣamakleśaṃ kriyamāṇaṃ sasaṃśayam |
siddhamapyanupādeyaṃ na prājñāḥ karma kurvate || k15.11 ||
ityuktvā caraṇāṅguṣṭhaghaṭṭitāṃ vāmapāṇinā |
vinyasya dakṣiṇe pāṇau bhagavān vipulāṃ śilām || k15.12 ||
visṛjya brahamlokāntamaparyantaparākramaḥ |
cacārāścaryacaryāyāṃ dūtamiva jagattraye || k15.13 ||
kṣiptāyāṃ sahasā tasyāṃ tenātyadbhutakāriṇā |
udabhūdganodbhūta iva vyāptajanaḥ svanaḥ || k15.14 ||
anityaḥ sarvasaṃskāra ityabhrāntavidhāyinaḥ |
sarvadharmā nirātmānaḥ śāntanirvāṇameva tat || k15.15 ||
iti sphuṭodite śabde śilā bhagavataḥ kare |
girīndraśīrṣakākārā sthitā punaradṛśyata || k15.16 ||
kṣaṇena sā bhagavatā kṣiptā vadanamārutaiḥ |
kṛtā visāriṇī dikṣu paramāṇuparaṃparā || k15.17 ||
punarekīkṛtāmeva bhagavān paramāṇubhiḥ |
śilāmanyatra nidadhe vismayaṃ ca jagattraye || k15.18 ||
āścaryaniścaladṛśastato mallā balirjitam |
vīryaṃ bhagavato vīkṣya praṇatāstaṃ babhāṣire || k15.19 ||
aho mahāprabhāvo'yaṃ balavīryodayastava |
niścayādhigame yasya na pragalbhāḥ surā api || k15.20 ||
anugrahapravṛttena balena guruṇā tava |
adhogatinimagneyaṃ janateva dhṛtā śilā || k15.21 ||
vīryaprajñābalādīnāṃ pramāṇāvadhiniścayam |
api jānāti te kaścidāścaryatarakāriṇaḥ || k15.22 ||
iti bruvāṇānāścaryaniścalānavalokya tān |
tasyāṃ śilāyāmāsīnaḥ provāca bhagavān jinaḥ || k15.23 ||
ekībhūtabalaṃ yaddhi bhūtānāṃ bhuvanatraye |
sugatasya tadekasya loke naiva samaṃ balam || k15.24 ||
ambhāṃsi kumbhairambhodherjaganti paramāṇubhiḥ |
śakyānyalaṃ laṅghayituṃ prabhāvo na tu saugataḥ || k15.25 ||
saṃkhyāṃ sumeroryo vetti tulāmānena tattvataḥ |
sugatānāṃ na jānāti so'pi sudguṇagauravam || k15.26 ||
kathayitveti bhagavān saṃprāpte suramaṇḍale |
saśakrapadmanilaye cakre kuśaladeśanam || k15.27 ||
mallāstadupadeśena tattadbodhisamāśrayāt |
saśrāvakākhyāṃ pratyekasamyaksaṃbuddhatāṃ yayuḥ || k15.28 ||
srotaḥprāptiphalaṃ kauścitsakṛdāgāmi cāparaiḥ |
anāgāmiphalaṃ cāṇyaiḥ prāptamarhatpadaṃ paraiḥ || k15.29 ||
ityāśyayānuśayadhātugatiṃ nirīkṣya jñātvā tathāprakṛtimapratimopadeśam |
teṣāṃ cakāra bhagavāṃścaturāryasatyasamyakprakāśaviśadaṃ kuśalodayāya || k15.30 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ śilānikṣepāvadānaṃpañcadaśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


16. maitreyavyākaraṇāvadānam |

asaṃgamo nāma viśuddhidhāmo śreyāṃsi sūte kuśalābhikāmaḥ |
saṃsāravāmaḥ sukṛtābhirāmo manomalairvairarajovirāmaḥ || k16.1 ||
purā gaṅgāṃ samuttīrya nāgānāṃ phaṇasetunā |
bhagavān sugataḥ pāraṃprāpya bhikṣūnabhāṣata || k16.2 ||
iha ratnamayaḥ pūrvaṃ yūpo'bhūdadbhutadyutiḥ |
darśayāṃi tamatraiva draṣṭuṃ vaḥ kautukaṃ yadi || k16.3 ||
ityuktvā bhagavān bhūmau pāṇinā divyalakṣmaṇā |
spṛṣṭvā nāgagaṇotkṣiptaṃ ratnayūpadarśayat || k16.4 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā bhikṣavaḥ sarve nirnimeṣekṣaṇāściram |
babhūvuścitralikhitāmiva niścalavigrahāḥ || k16.5 ||
tatkathāmatha taiḥ pṛṣṭaḥ provāca bhagavān punaḥ |
atītānāgatajñānaṃ dantālokaiḥ kirannivaḥ || k16.6 ||
devaputraḥ purā kaścitkāle svargaparicyutaḥ |
mahāpraṇādanāmābhūnnṛpatiḥ śakraśāsanāt || k16.7 ||
dharmavṛttānusaraṇasmaraṇāya mahītale |
ucitaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ kiṃcitsa yayāce śatakratum || k16.8 ||
tataḥ surapatervākyādviśvakarmā tadālaye |
bhāsvaraṃ vidadhe ratnayūpaṃ puṇyamivonnatam || k16.9 ||
tatastaddarśanāsakte jane kautukaniścale |
kṛṣyādikarmaṇyucchinne rājñaḥ koṣakṣayo'bhavat || k16.10 ||
tatastena kṣitibhujā kṣipto'yaṃ jāhnavījale |
tiṣṭhatyadyāpi pātāle ratnaiḥ sūryairivācitaḥ || k16.11 ||
bhaviṣyatyasya kālena paricchinnaḥ parikṣayaḥ |
na tajjagati nāmāste pariṇāme yadakṣayam || k16.12 ||
āgāmisamaye puṃsāṃ varṣāśītisahasrake |
śaṅkhaśubhrayaśāḥ śaṅkho nāma rājā bhaviṣyati || k16.13 ||
puṇyalabdhamimaṃ yūpaṃ nṛpaḥ kalpadrumopamaḥ |
sa purohitaputrāya maitreyāya pradāsyati || k16.14 ||
maitreyo'pyanmumādāya kṛtvā sapadi khaṇḍaśaḥ |
arthicintāmaṇirlokamadaridraṃ kariṣyati || k16.15 ||
yūpaṃ datvātha maitreyaḥ samyaksaṃbuddhatāṃ gataḥ |
anuttarajñānanidhirbhaviṣyati surārcitaḥ || k16.16 ||
śaṅko rājā sahasrāṇāmaśītyā parivāritaḥ |
sāntaḥpurāmātyagaṇo rājāpi pravrajiṣyati || k16.17 ||
prāgjanmapraṇidhānena śaṅkhasya kuśalodayaḥ |
kṛtasyāvaśyabhogyatvātpariṇāme phaliṣyati || k16.18 ||
madhyadeśe purā rājā vāsavo vāsavopamaḥ |
dhanasaṃmatanāmā ca nṛpo'bhūduttarāpathe || k16.19 ||
tayorvibhavasaṃgharṣo bhūto vairāgnitaptayoḥ |
abhūdyuddhasamārambhasaṃbhārarabhasaṃ manah || k16.20 ||
nagaraṃ ca praviśyātha sametya dhanasaṃmataḥ |
cakre garajathānīkairgaṅgātīraṃ nīrantaram || k16.21 ||
tatra ratnaśīkhī nāṃ samyaksaṃbuddhatāṃ gataḥ |
dṛṣṭastenārcyāmānāṅghrirbrahmaśakrādibhiḥ suraiḥ || k16.22 ||
so'cintayadaho rājā vāsavaḥ pṛthipuṇyavān |
viṣayānte vasatyeṣayo hi tridaśavanditaḥ || k16.23 ||
tatastasyānubhāvena tatra bhūpālayostayoḥ |
yayau vairarajaḥ śāntyā mithyāmohaparikṣayam || k16.24 ||
kṝtasaṃghiḥ pareṇātha vāsavaḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ |
bhagavantaṃ samabhyetya sarvabhogaurapūjayat || k16.25 ||
praṇidhānaṃ ca vidadhe pūjānte praṇamāmi tam |
ahaṃ kuśalamūlena syāmetena mahāniti || k16.26 ||
asminnavasare ghore śankhaśabde samudgate |
provāca taṃ ratnaśikhī praṇataṃ purataḥ sthitam || k16.27 ||
śankho nāma mahīpālaścakravartī bhaviṣyasi |
paryante bodhiyuktaśca kuśalaṃ samāvāpsyasi || k16.28 ||
evaṃ satpraṇidhānatahkṣitipatiḥ puṇyodayādvāsavaḥ
śaṅkho nāma nṛpa sa ratnaśikhinādiṣṭaḥ śriyaṃ prāpsyati |
maitreyaḥ praṇayātkariṣyati tathā bodhau viśuddhāṃ dhiyaṃ
kalyāṇābhiniveśapuṇyataraṇirāḍya hi satsaṃgamaḥ || k16.29 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ maitreyavyākaraṇāvadānaṃ nāma ṣoḍaśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


17. ādarśamukhāvadānam |

cittaprasādavimalapraṇayojjvalasya svalpasya dānakusumasya phalāṃśakena |
hemādrirohaṇanagendrasudhābdhidānasaṃpatphalaṃ na hi tulākalanāmupaiti || k17.1 ||
purā manojñe sarvajñaḥ śrāvastyāṃ jetakānane |
anāthapiṇḍadārāme vijahāra mahāśayaḥ || k17.2 ||
āryo mahākāśyapākhyastacchiṣyaḥ karuṇānidhiḥ |
nagaropavanasyāntaṃ janacārikayā yayau || k17.3 ||
tatra sudrugatiryoṣinnagarāśravalambikā |
apaśyatkuṣṭharogārtā kāśyapaṃ taṃ yadṛcchayā || k17.4 ||
sā taṃ dṛṣṭvā prasādinyā śraddhayā samacintayat |
pātre'sya piṇḍapātārhā kiṃ na jātāsmi puṇyataḥ || k17.5 ||
vijñāya tasyā āścaryaśraddhāyuktaṃ manoratham |
prasārya pātraṃ jagrāha piṇḍaṃ taṃ karuṇākulaḥ || k17.6 ||
tīvracittaprasādena bhaktasārasamaraṇe |
kuṣṭhinyā nipapātāsyāḥ pātre śīrṇakarāṅguliḥ || k17.7 ||
tataḥ sā pātakamiva tyaktvānityakalevaram |
devānāṃ tuṣitākhyānāṃ nilaye samajāyata || k17.8 ||
śakrastadadbhutaṃ jñātvā dānapuṇypditādaraḥ |
yatnātkāśyapasatpātraṃ sudhayā samapūrayat || k17.9 ||
sudhārpaṇe'pyasau bhikṣurnispṛhastṛṇalīlayā |
praśamāmṛtasaṃpūrṇaścakre pātramadhomukham || k17.10 ||
bhajante praṇayaprītiṃ kṛpaṇeṣu kṛpākulāḥ |
santaḥ saṃpatsamādhmātavadane mīlitādarāḥ || k17.11 ||
śrutvā tāṃ tuṣite devanikāye niratāṃ nṛpaḥ |
prasenajitbhagavataścakre bhojyādhivāsanām || k17.12 ||
rājñastasya gṛhe dṛṣṭvā lakṣmīmāścaryakāriṇaḥ |
āryānandena bhagavān pṛṣṭastatpuṇyamabhyadhāt || k17.13 ||
purā gṛhapateḥ sūnurdāridyrāddāsatāṃ gataḥ |
kṣetrakarmāṇi saṃsaktaḥ kṣutkṣāmaḥ klāntimāyayau || k17.14 ||
svajananyā samānītāṃ niḥsnehalavaṇāṃ cirāt |
kulmāṣapiṇḍimāsādya bhoktuṃ sādaramāyayau || k17.15 ||
dhautahastaḥ kṣaṇe tasmin saṃprāptāya yadṛcchayā |
dadau pratyekabuddhāya tāṃ prasannena cetasā || k17.16 ||
jātaḥ sa eva kālena bhūpālo'yaṃ prasenajit |
tasya dānakaṇasyaivaṃ vibhūtiḥ prathamaṃ phalam || k17.17 ||
śrutveti bhagavadvākyaṃ bhikṣūrvismayamāyayau |
rājāpi vipulāṃ pūjāṃ cakre bhagavataḥ puraḥ || k17.18 ||
rājārhairakhilairbhogaiḥ kṛtvā bhaktinivedanam |
sa koṭīstailakumbhānāṃ dīpamālāmakalpayat || k17.19 ||
dīpamekaṃ dadau tatra svalpakaṃ durgatāṅganā |
snehakṣayātprayāteṣu sarveṣu na jagāma yaḥ || k17.20 ||
vicintya praṇidhānena tayā vimalacetasā |
bhāvinīṃ śākyamunitāṃ sarvajño'syāhsamabhyadhāt || k17.21 ||
ratnadīpāvaliṃ datvā rājā bhagavataḥ purah |
upaviśya praṇamyāgre praṇayāttaṃ vajijñapat || k17.22 ||
bhagavatpraṇidhānena tattatpuṇyānubhāvataḥ |
na kasyānuttarā samyaksaṃbodhirbhavadarpitā || k17.23 ||
bhavatprasādapraṇayātprāptumicchāmi tāmaham |
nirvikalpaphalāvāptyai sevyante kalpapādapāḥ || k17.24 ||
iti rājavacaḥ śrutvā bhagavān samabhāṣata |
durlabhānuttarā samyaksaṃbodhiḥ pṛthivīpate || k17.25 ||
sūkṣmā mṛṇālatantubhyo giribhyo'pi garīyasī |
samudrebhyo'pi gambhīrā sā sukhena na labhyate || k17.26 ||
na dānairbahubhirlabdhaṃ mayaivānyeṣu janmasu |
cittaprasādaviśadaṃ jñānaṃ tatkāraṇaṃ jaguḥ || k17.27 ||
caturdvīpādhipatyena mayā māndhātṛjanmani |
ciraṃ dānaphalaṃ bhuktaṃ bodhirnādhigatā tu sā || k17.28 ||
dānena cakravartīśrīḥ sā sudarśanajanmani |
bhuktā mayāmahīyasī bodhirnādhigatā tu sā || k17.29 ||
purā datvā gajānaṣṭau velāmadvijajanmāni |
mayā prāptaṃ mahatpuṇyaṃ bodhirnādhigatā tu sā || k17.30 ||
kurūpaḥ kuśalātmāhaṃ rājaputraḥ purābhavam |
yaḥ piśāco'yamityuktvā nijalatnyā vivarjitaḥ || k17.31 ||
śriyai śrīskandho bhūtyāge prītiryasya sadā sthitā |
sa duḥkhī rūpavaikalyātkka vā sarvaguṇodayaḥ || k17.32 ||
taṃ rūpavirahe dehatyāgārūḍhaṃ śacīpatiḥ |
divyacūḍāmaṇiṃ datvā cakre pañcaśaropamam || k17.33 ||
ṣaṣṭiḥ purasahasrāṇi tasya yajñeṣu yajvanaḥ |
hemayūpābhirūpāṇi prāpurmerubalaśriyam || k17.34 ||
tasminmayātidānādrikṛte kuśalajanmani |
tāni puṇyānyavāptāni bodhirnādhigatā tu sā || k17.35 ||
mayā satyaprabhāveṇa triśaṅkunṛpajanmani |
kṛtā vṛṣṭiḥ sudurbhikṣā bodhirnādhigatā tu sā || k17.36 ||
mithilāyāṃ mahādevanṛpajanmani yajvanā |
mayāptā puṇyasaṃpattirbodhirnādhigatā tu sā || k17.37 ||
mithilāyāṃ purā puṇyaṃ nimibhūpālajanmani |
prāptaṃ dānatapoyajñairbodhirnādhigatā tu sā || k17.38 ||
purā nandasya nṛpateścatvāraḥ piśunāḥ sutāḥ |
babhūvurādarśamukhaḥ pañcamaśca guṇādhikaḥ || k17.39 ||
kālenāpannaparyantaḥ sa bhūpatiracintayat |
ete madante catvāro rājyaṃ nārhanti karkaśāḥ || k17.40 ||
putre mamādarśamukhe rājyaśrī pratibimbitā |
suvṛtte labhate śobhāṃ prajñāvaimalyaśālini || k17.41 ||
dhyātvetyamātyān so'vādītsa bhavadbhirnareśvaraḥ |
kṛto'ntaḥpuravargeṇa yo'bhyutthānena pūjyate || k17.42 ||
maulirna kampate yasya sameva maṇipādukaiḥ |
dvāradrumādrivāpīṣu nidhiṣaṭkaṃ sa paśyati || k17.43 ||
ityuktvā tridivaṃ yāte nṛpatau mantriṇaḥ kramāt |
taduktrairlakṣaṇaiścakrurādarśamukhamīśvaram || k17.44 ||
dharmanirṇayakāryeṣu yaṃ vādiprativādinah |
vilokyaiva svayaṃ tasthurnyāyairjayaparājaye || k17.45 ||
purā nirabhiasṃghena prātavaiśasakilbiṣam |
brāhmaṇaṃ daṇḍinaṃ nāma dayāluḥ prayayau puraḥ || k17.46 ||
guyugārthe gṛhasthena mṛtena vaḍavāhateḥ |
kuṭhārapātataḥ patnyā takṣavāsī vivāditaḥ || k17.47 ||
śauṇḍikenātmajavadhāddīkṣitaṃ tulyanigraham |
tadvipakṣabhayenoktvā tatsaṃtyaktaṃ vyamokṣayat || k17.48 ||
amānuṣāṇāṃ sattvānāmadhyāśayaviśeṣataḥ |
cakāra cittaśodhanaṃ tattatsaṃdehanirṇayam || k17.49 ||
avṛṣṭimṛṣṭe durbhikṣe yena dvādaśavarsake |
vihitaṃ sarvasattvānāmaśanaprāṇavartanam || k17.50 ||
ityamūnmama puṇyāptirādarśamukhajnmani |
na tu sā samyaksaṃbodhirvibodhitā mahodayā || k17.51 ||
bahujanmaśatābhyāsaprasāsena mahīyasā |
adya tu jñānavaimalyaṃ mayāptaṃ lutpasaṃvṛti || k17.52 ||
jñānaprajñādhigamyā kimapi paratarānuttarā satyāsaṃvit
samyaksaṃbodhireṣā na ca khalu nṛpate labhyate dānapuṇyaiḥ |
mohaśyāmāvirāme gataghanagaganavyaktavaimalyabhājāṃ
nirvyājānandabhūmirbhavati bhavatamaśchedinī sā dinaśrīḥ || k17.53 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāmādarśamukhāvadānaṃ nāma saptadaśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


18. śāriputrapravrajyāvadānam |

nedaṃ bandhunoṃ suhṛtsodaro vā nedaṃ mātā na pitā vā karoti |
yatsaṃsārāmbhodhesetuṃ vidhatte jñānācāryaḥ ko'pi kalyāṇahetuḥ || k18.1 ||
kalandakanivāsākhye ramye veṇuvanāśrame |
bhagavān viharan buddhaḥ pure rājagṛhe purā || k18.2 ||
kilitaṃ copayiṣyaṃ ca dvau parivrājakau purā |
prapannau bhikṣubhāvena cakāra śamasaṃvṛtau || k18.3 ||
tataśca śāriputrasya bhikṣoḥ saṃdeśanāṃ vyaghāt |
yayā sākṣātkṛtārhattvo so'bhūnmokṣagatikṣamaḥ || k18.4 ||
tasya tadadbhutaṃ dṛṣṭvā dhanārhaṃ sarvabhikṣubhiḥ |
papracchuḥ pūrvavṛttāntaṃ sa ca tebhyo vyabhāṣata || k18.5 ||
brāhmaṇasyāgnimitrasya bhāryā guṇavarābhavat |
śūrpiketi kṛtaṃ pitrā krīḍānāma ca bibhratī || k18.6 ||
bhrātā prathamaśīlākhyaḥ tasya śūrpasamābhidhaḥ |
pratyekabuddhatāṃ yātaḥ kadācidgṛhamāyayau || k18.7 ||
sa tayā bharturādeśādgṛhībhaktyādhivāsitaḥ |
praṇatipraṇayācāraistoṣitaḥ paricaryayā || k18.8 ||
kadāciccīvare tasya kurvāṇasya vipātraṇam (?) |
sūcīkarmavahāddṛṣṭvā praṇidhānaṃ samādadhe || k18.9 ||
yatheyaṃ kartarīṃ tīkṣṇā yathā gambhīragāmiṇī |
sūcī tathāparā prajñā mama syāditi sādarā || k18.10 ||
pratyekabuddhavinayātpraṇidhānena tena ca |
gatāsmin janmani saiva saprajñaśāriputratām || k18.11 ||
sa eṣa śāriputro'dya bhikṣuṣtīkṣṇatarāgradhīḥ |
kalyāṇapātratāṃ yātaḥ kalpavallī hi sanmateḥ || k18.12 ||
vākyaṃ bhagavataḥ śrutvā papracchurbhikṣavaḥ punah |
kasmānnāṭyakule jātaḥ śāriputro narādhame || k18.13 ||
tatastān bhagavānūce pūrvasminneṣa janmani |
abhūnmahāmatirnāma rājaputraḥ satāṃ mataḥ || k18.14 ||
śrīmato'pi matistasya pravrajyāyāmajāyata |
paripākaprasannānāṃ kāluṣyāya na saṃpadaḥ || k18.15 ||
pravrajyā rājaputrāṇāṃ yūnāṃ naiva kulocitā |
ityuktvā janakaḥ prītyā taṃ yatnena nyavārayat || k18.16 ||
kadācitkuñjarārūḍhaḥ sa vrajan janavartmani |
dṛṣṭvā daridraṃ sthaviraṃ kāruṇyādidamabravīt || k18.17 ||
adhanyā dhanīno loke bandhubandhanayantritāḥ |
pravrajyāṃ nāpnuvantyeva tvaṃ tu kena nivāritaḥ || k18.18 ||
sa nyavedayanme daridrasya na pātraṃ na ca cīvaram |
dhanopakaraṇānyeva śamopakaraṇānyapi || k18.19 ||
rājasūnuriti śrutvā gatvā munitapovanam |
pravrajyāṃ kārayitvāsya pradadau pātracīvaram || k18.20 ||
so'cireṇaiva kālena yātaḥ pratyekabuddhatām |
rājaputraṃ samabhyetya divyāmṛddhimadarśayat || k18.21 ||
tasya prabhāvamālokya sa pradadhyau nṛpātmajaḥ |
aho mahodayatvānme pravrajyā durlabhābhavat || k18.22 ||
dāridyrādavivekāccnīcānāmapi durlabhā |
jāyeyamadhame kule tasmadasmi vivekavān || k18.23 ||
sa eva śāriputro'yaṃ jñātastatpraṇidhānataḥ |
pravrajito bhagavatā kāśyapenānyajanmani || k18.24 ||
tenāyaṃ niyamapraṇayavinayī samyakprasādodayād
ādiṣṭaḥ kuśalāya satyanidhinā prajñāvatāmagraṇīḥ |
kāle śākyamunerbhaviṣyati mataḥ śiṣyatvayogādvaraṃ
maudgalyāyana eṣa cātra kathitaḥ saṃvinmayānāṃ vadaḥ || k18.25 ||
anyajanmani daridraḥ kārmikaḥ kenacidapi dayayā maharṣiṇā |
dattapātracīvaro'bhavaddarśitarddhirāsīdatulaprabhāvavān || k18.26 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ śāriputraprajajyāvadānaṃ nāmāṣṭādaśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


19. śroṇakoṭikarṇāvadānam |

sa ko'pi puṇyātiśayodayasya varaḥ prabhāvaḥ paramākṣayo yaḥ |
pratyakṣalakṣyaḥ śubhapakṣasākṣī janmāntare lakṣaṇatāmupaiti || k19.1 ||
ramye purā bhagavati śrāvastyāṃ jetakānane |
anāthapiṇḍadārāme vihāriṇi tathāgate || k19.2 ||
babhūva vāsavagrāme balasenābhidho gṛhī |
pūritāśaḥ phalabharaiśchāyātarurivārthinām || k19.3 ||
jāyāyāṃ jayasenāyāṃ kāle kamalalocanaḥ |
ajāyata sutastasya puṇyairmīrta ivotsavaḥ || k19.4 ||
sajahā ratnadīpārcirabhūtkarṇasya karṇikā |
nābhūnmūlyatulā yasya hemakoṭiśatairapi || k19.5 ||
śravaṇānakṣatrajāto'sau ratnakoṭyarhakarṇikāḥ |
sa śroṇakoṭikarṇākhyaḥ kumāro'bhūdguṇocitaḥ || k19.6 ||
sa nirmalaruciḥ kāntaḥ kalābhiṃ paripūritaḥ |
amandānandaniṣyandī na kasyendurivābhavat || k19.7 ||
sa yuvā vāryamāṇo'pi pitrā dhanadasaṃpadā |
jananī sāśrunayanā paraṃ puruṣayā girā || k19.8 ||
priyaṃvado'pi nirbhartsyo viṣavarṣīva candramāḥ |
mahāsārthena ratnārthī dūradvīpāntaraṃ yayau || k19.9 ||
makarākaramuttīrya vrajatasyasya nirjane |
karmormiviplavenābhūtsvasārthavirahaḥ pathi || k19.10 ||
sārtho'pi tamanāsādya vinivṛttaṃ śucā śanaiḥ |
svadeśamarjitakleśaḥ praviveśa viśṛṅkhalāḥ || k19.11 ||
so'pi taptamaruśreṇīlakṣaṇāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ diśam |
śrāntaḥ praśāntaviśrāntirvāpigāhaṃ vyagāhata || k19.12 ||
so'cintayadaho vitte pratinittārjanodyamaḥ |
mamāyamanayenaiva jātakleśaḥ phalodayaḥ || k19.13 ||
aho dhanārjanāveśaḥ saṃtoṣavirahānnṛṇām |
sarvāpavādasaṃvādo nindyānāṃ vipadāṃ padam || k19.14 ||
hemācale'pi saṃprāpte na paryāptirhanārjane |
saṃsāre vāsanābhyāsadveṣamohaḥ śarīriṇām || k19.15 ||
pṛthuprayāsavirasā dīpitā śrīrivāyatā |
tṛṣṇāṃ tanoti nitarāmiyaṃmarumahītale || k19.16 ||
aho bata kuraṅgāṇāṃ tṛṣṇāndhānāṃ pade pade |
mamāpi janayantyeva mohaṃ marumarīcikāḥ || k19.17 ||
iyaṃ tṛṣṇā śramaścāyamimā nirudakā bhuvaḥ |
kiṃ karomi kka gacchāmi paśyāmi jvalitā diśaḥ || k19.18 ||
iti saṃcintya sa śanaiḥ prasarpan salilāśayā |
āyāsamiva sākāraṃ dadarśa puramāyasam || k19.19 ||
ghoraṃ dvāri sthitaṃ tatra saṃtrāsasyeva sodaram |
dadarśa puruṣaṃ kālakarālaṃ raktalocanam || k19.20 ||
sa tena pṛṣṭaḥ pānīyaṃ yadā novāca kiṃcana |
tadā svayaṃ praviṣṭena pretaloko vilokitah || k19.21 ||
dagdhakaṣṭhopamān dhūlimalaliptānnirambarān |
śuṣkāsthinsāyuśeṣāṅgān pretān dṛṣṭvā sa vivyathe || k19.22 ||
tairyācitahsa pānīyaṃ pānīyavirahārditaḥ |
parityajya nijāmārtimabhūttadduḥkhaduḥkhitaḥ || k19.23 ||
tīvratṛṣṇāturānūce sa tānārtapralāpinah |
mamāsminmarukāntāre niṣkṛpasya kutaḥ payaḥ || k19.24 ||
kṛcchre'smin duḥsahe nyastāḥ ke yūyaṃ kena karmaṇā |
yuṣmadaprakriyabandhaścātaḥ kṛcchraścarāmyaham || k19.25 ||
nidrā niḥśalyakalpasya sukhasikteva tasya yā |
nārtānnapaśyati dṛṣṭvā teṣāṃ kleśakṣaye kṣamā || k19.26 ||
te tamūcurviruddhena karmaṇā mohasaṃcayāt |
anivartya vayaṃ martyāḥ kṣiptā vyasanasaṃkaṭe || k19.27 ||
adhikṣepātkṣepakṣatipataradhairyairmadabharādanāryairmānavyasanerṣyābhiravāryavyatikaraiḥ |
kṛtāsmābhirnityaṃ suhanahṛdaye nirdayatayā
vacobhirnārācairviṣaparicitaiḥ śalyakalanā || k19.28 ||
dānaṃ nadattaṃ hṛtameva vittaṃ hiṃsānimittīkṛtameva cittam |
asmārbhiraṅgairvihitā vikārāḥ parasya dārāpahṛtiprakārāḥ || k19.29 ||
te vayaṃ kuhakāsaktā dakṣāḥ kṣudreṣu karmasu |
prayātāḥ pretanagare ghore'smin kleśapātratām || k19.30 ||
iti teṣāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā so'nyatra ca tathāvidhān |
pretān dṛṣṭvānabhipretān karuṇakulito'bhavat || k19.31 ||
nirgamya durgamāttasmātpurātpuṇyabalena saḥ |
vimalaṃ śītalacchāyamāsasāda vanāntaram || k19.32 ||
atha dūradhvasaṃtaptaḥ pariśrāntaḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
nipapātācalādandhastṛṣṭārta iva bhāṣkaraḥ || k19.33 ||
dine puṇya iva kṣīṇe niḥśeṣāśāprakāśake |
saṃmohamalinaṃ loke tamaḥ pāpamivodyayau || k19.34 ||
kṣīṇabhṛṅgavihaṅgānāṃ nalinīnāṃ prasaṅginī |
vikāsāsṃpasā mudrā nidreva samajāyata || k19.35 ||
kāruṇyādiva śītāṃśurjyotsnāmṛtaśalākayā |
sphāratāraṃ jagannitraṃ cakre vitimitaṃ tataḥ || k19.36 ||
kṣayodayaparāvṛttirdrśitānekavibhramaḥ |
saṃsāradinayāminyorjahāseva sudhākaraḥ || k19.37 ||
netrānandasudhāvarṣe sukhasparśe niśākare |
digvadhūvadanādarśe harṣe mūrta ivotite || k19.38 ||
śroṇakoṭirdadarśāgre vimānamānanadyutim |
kautukādanyarūpeṇa svargādbhuvamivāgatam || k19.39 ||
tasminnapaśyatsamadāścatasrastridaśāṅganāḥ |
diśaścandrodayānandavihārāyeva saṃgatāḥ || k19.40 ||
tāsāṃ madhye varākāraṃ ramamāṇaṃ vyalokayat |
taruṇapremasaṃbhāramiva sākāratāṃ gataḥ || k19.41 ||
ratnamaṇḍaleyūrakirīṭikacirāmśubhiḥ |
ālikhantamivāścaryamamaryādaṃ diśāṃ mukhe || k19.42 ||
tasya tāmadbhutāṃ dṛṣṭvā saṃbhogasukhasaṃpadam |
mene sa sukṛtākhyasya taroḥ sphītāṃ phalaśriyam || k19.43 ||
prītyā tena kṛtātithyaḥ svādupānāśanādibhiḥ |
tāṃ śroṇakoṭikarṇo'tha nināya rajanīṃ sukham || k19.44 ||
tārākusumavātāliprabhā prābhātikī tataḥ |
anityateva śaśinaścakre lakṣmīparikṣayam || k19.45 ||
kṣayaṃ kṣapāyāṃ yātāyāṃ bhānau bhuvanacakṣuṣi |
udite sarvabhūtānāṃ sukhaduḥkhaikasākṣiṇi || k19.46 ||
vimānaṃ suranāryaśca kṣasṇādadṛśyatāṃyayuḥ |
vinaṣṭavadanacchāyaḥ puruṣaścāpatatkṣitau || k19.47 ||
tatastasyāpatatpṛṣṭhe śunāṃ saṃgho'tibhīṣaṇaḥ |
trailokyaśāpapāpotthaḥ kleśarāśirivākhilaḥ || k19.48 ||
sa taigrīvāmukhārabdhamāṃsagrāsāgrakarṣaṇaiḥ |
ākrandirudhirakṣīrairbhakṣyamāṇaḥ kṣayaṃ yayau || k19.49 ||
dinānte punarāyāntaṃ tadvimānamapaśyata |
catasro'psarāsastāśca puruṣaḥ sa ca kāntimān || k19.50 ||
taṃ śroṇakoṭikarṇo'tha papracchātyantavismitaḥ |
sakhe kimetadāścaryaṃ dṛśyate kathyatāmiti || k19.51 ||
sa tena pṛṣṭaḥ provāca vayasya śrūyatāmidam |
tvāṃ śroṇakoṭikarṇākhyaṃ jānāmi sukṛtocitam || k19.52 ||
abhavaṃ vāsavagrāme duṣkṛtī paśupālakaḥ |
paśūnāṃ māṃsamutkṛtya vikrītaṃ satata mayā || k19.53 ||
piṇḍapātāya saṃprāpto māmāryaḥ karuṇānidhiḥ |
kātyāyanākhyaḥ provācaḥ viramāsmātkukarmaṇaḥ || k19.54 ||
hiṃsāmayo hyayaṃ kleśo duḥsahaḥ sāhasaiṣiṇām |
svaśarīre patatyeva chinnamūla iva drumaḥ || k19.55 ||
ityahaṃ vāryamāṇo'pi tenānāryaḥ kṛpātmanā |
yadā na virataḥ pāpāttadā sa prāha māṃ punaḥ || k19.56 ||
divā tvaṃ kuruṣe hiṃsāṃ sarvathā yadi nirdayaḥ |
rātrau śīlasamādānaṃ gṛhāṇa samayānmama || k19.57 ||
ityuktvā tena yatnena sarvasattvahitaiṣiṇā |
dattā śīlasamādānamayī puṇyamatirmama || k19.58 ||
kālena kālavaśagaḥ prāptaḥ so'hamimāṃ daśām |
taptāṅgārasudhāvarṣairiva kīrṇo divāniśam || k19.59 ||
rātrau śīlasamādānaphalaṃ hiṃsāphalaṃ dine |
caryā matpuṇyapāpābhyāṃ patitaḥ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ || k19.60 ||
tasya me kuru kāruṇyaṃ sakhe kalusakāriṇaḥ |
gatvā svadeśaṃmatputraṃ brūhi madvacasā rahaḥ || k19.61 ||
asti me gṛhakoṭānte nikhātaṃ hemabhājanam |
taduddhṛtya parityaktapāpavṛtti vidhīyatām || k19.62 ||
āryakātyāyano nityaṃ piṇḍapātena pūjyatām |
ityuktastena vinayāttathetyuktvā jagāma saḥ || k19.63 ||
sa dadarśa vrajan divyavimānamaparaṃ punaḥ |
ratnapadmalatākāntaṃ dvitīyamiva nandanam || k19.64 ||
tasmin sāṅgamivānaṅgaṃ saṃgataṃ divyayoṣitā |
apaśyadvāsarārambhe puruṣaṃ ratnabhūṣitam || k19.65 ||
tena prītyupacāreṇa kṛtātithyastathaiva saḥ |
dinaṃ nināya dīrghaṃ ca kleśamadhye sudhāmayam || k19.66 ||
atha vyomavimānāgrātpatite padminīpatau |
apūryata jagadghorairduḥkhairiva tamobharaiḥ || k19.67 ||
tataḥ kṣapāpatirjyotsnāṃ vamanneva kṣapājaḍaḥ |
śanakaiḥ pāṇḍurogīva gauradyutiradṛśyata || k19.68 ||
sukumāre dināloke rātrau rākṣasayoṣitā |
bhakṣite'lakṣyata śaśī kapālabalasaṃnibhaḥ || k19.69 ||
vyāpte candrikayā loke kālacandranacarcayā |
vimānamagamatkkāpisā ca svargamṛgekṣaṇā || k19.70 ||
vimānapatitaḥ so'pi puruṣaḥ sarvarūpayā |
śanaiḥ saptabhirāvartaiḥ śatapadyā viveṣṭitaḥ || k19.71 ||
sā tasya mūrdhni vivaraṃ kṛtvā mastiṣkaśoṇitam |
āsvādayantī śanakaiścakāra śuṣiraṃ śiraḥ || k19.72 ||
athāruṇakaracchanne socchvāsavadāne dine |
bībhatsadarśanakleśādiva mīlitatārake || k19.73 ||
prādurāsan punardivyavimānaṃ sā ca kāminī |
yuvā sa cādbhutatanurdivyābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ || k19.74 ||
pṛṣṭo'tivismayāttena svavṛttāntaṃ jagādsaḥ |
dvijo'haṃ vāsavagrāmanivāsī manasābhidhaḥ || k19.75 ||
taruṇī prātiveśyasya patnī malayamañjarī |
abhūnmama bhujaṅgasya svairiṇī vallabhā bhṛśam || k19.76 ||
paradāraratergrāme vyagrā me kṣamate matiḥ |
minagnā viṣayagrāme samagrā me kṣayaṃ gatā || k19.77 ||
āryakātyāyanaḥ pāpaṃ jñātvā māṃ cauryakāmukam |
dayāvidheyaḥ kāruṇyātprovāca vijane śanaiḥ || k19.78 ||
parāṅganāṅgasaṃsargaprītyā rūparateḥ kṣaibam (?) |
anaṅgāgnau patannāśaṃ pataṅgamiva mā gamaḥ || k19.79 ||
aho āsaktaraktānāṃ saṃpatanapramādinām |
kāmināṃ hiṃsakānāṃ ca paradārādaraḥ param || k19.80 ||
pṛthu pravepathu (?) svāpaśramavihvalānāṃ gṛdhrāṅganāmukhanikhātanakhakṣatānām |
saṃmohane paravadhūvihitaspṛhāṇāṃ romāñcakāriṇi paraṃ narake ca kāmaḥ || k19.81 ||
tasmādasmānnivartasva vatsa kutsitakarmaṇaḥ |
jāyate pātakaṃ sparśe śunāmevāśucau ratiḥ || k19.82 ||
ityahaṃ kṛpayā tena niṣiddho'pyaviśuddhadhīḥ |
aniruddhena rāgeṇa baddhastāmeva nātyajam || k19.83 ||
vijñāya māmavirataṃ tataḥ kātyāyano dadau |
mahyaṃ śīlasamādānaṃ dinacaryāhitodyataḥ || k19.84 ||
dinaśīlasamādānātparastrīgamanānniśi |
iyaṃ me puṇyapāpotthā sukhaduḥkhamayī sthitīḥ || k19.85 ||
gatena vāsavagrāmaṃ vācyaḥ putro mama tvayā |
suvarṇamagniśālāyāmasti gūḍhaṃ dhṛtaṃ mayā || k19.86 ||
vṛttiḥ kāryā tadudbhṛtya pūjyaḥ kātyāyanaśca saḥ |
praṇayāditi tenoktaḥ śroṇakoṭiryayau tataḥ || k19.87 ||
so'paśyaddivyalalanāmagre maṇivimānagām |
lakṣmīṃ lāvaṇyadugdhābdheranāyāsodgatāmiva || k19.88 ||
tasya vimānapādeṣi caturṣu snāyusaṃyutam |
sa dadarśātidurdaśaṃ baddhaṃ pretacatuṣṭayam || k19.89 ||
sāpi taṃ pratyabhijñāya saṃbhāṣya snigdhayā girā |
surocitaṃ dadau tasmai rasavatpānabhojanam || k19.90 ||
bhuñjānasaṃjñayāṃ dūrātpretairdainyena yācitaḥ |
sa dadau kṛpayā tebhyaḥ kākebhya iva piṇḍikāḥ || k19.91 ||
piṇḍo busatvamekasya prayāto'nyasya lohatām |
svamāṃsatvaṃ tṛtīyasya caturthasya prapūyatām || k19.92 ||
vilokya tatkṛpāviṣṭaḥ sa teṣāṃ kaṣṭaceṣṭayā |
papraccha tanmukhacchāyāṃ vicchāyīkṛtapaṅkajām || k19.93 ||
pṛṣṭā tadadbhutaṃ tena uvāca sā mṛgekṣaṇā |
na śroṇakoṭirṇaiṣāṃ dattaṃ bhavati tṛptaye || k19.94 ||
brāhmaṇasyāsya bhāryāhaṃ pūrvapādābalambinaḥ |
nandanāmnaḥ sunandākhyā vāsavagrāmavāsinaḥ || k19.95 ||
dvitīyapādasaṃsaktaḥ putro me niṣṭhurābhidhaḥ |
paścātpādāvalambinyau dāsī ceyaṃ snuṣā ca me || k19.96 ||
nakṣatrayogapūjāyāṃ purā sajjīkṛate mayā |
bhaikṣyopahāre me gehamāryakātyāyano'viśat || k19.97 ||
mayā cittaprasādinyā piṇḍapātena so'rcitaḥ |
kurvanneva yayau kāntyā vaimalyānugrahaṃ diśām || k19.98 ||
tataḥ snātvā samāyātastūrṇaṃ patirayaṃ mama |
patpiṇḍapātamākarṇya pramidāya mayoditam || k19.99 ||
kopāduvāca māṃ kasmādviśikhaḥ śramaṇaḥ śaṭhaḥ |
apūjiteṣu pūjyeṣu busārhaḥ pūjitastvayā || k19.100 ||
iti mohādanenokte putro'pyeṣa jagādmām |
pāke pūrvāśanāyogyaḥ sa kiṃ nāśnātyayoguḍān || k19.101 ||
iyaṃ snuṣā me satataṃ pūrvabhakṣyāvabhoginā |
mayokte śapathaṃ cakre svamāṃsādanavādinī || k19.102 ||
iyaṃ dāsī ca bhaikṣyāṇā cairyāttadvyayakāriṇī |
ākṣiptā cākarotsatyaṃ pūyaśoṇitavādinī || k19.103 ||
tatra te pretatāṃ yātāḥ svavākyasadṛśāśanāḥ |
ahaṃ tvāryaprasādena divyabhogopabhoginī || k19.104 ||
tvayā tvāryaśamāptena vaktavyā duhitā mama |
santi hemanidhānāni gṛhe catvāri te pituḥ || k19.105 ||
tānyuddhṛtya yathāyogaṃ bhajasvasvajanisthitim |
pūjanīyaḥ piturbhrātā nāmnā kātyāyana sadā || k19.106 ||
sa ḥ śroṇakoṭikarṇastvaṃ gaccha deśaṃ tyaja śramam |
varṣā dvādaśa saṃpūrṇāḥ svagṛhānnirgatasya te || k19.107 ||
ityuktvā taṃ samādiśaya tasya pretacatuṣṭayam |
suptasyaiva muhūrtena svadeśāptimakārayat || k19.108 ||
utthitaḥ so'pi sahasā svadeśodyānakānanāt |
viyogaśokātpitarau śuśrāvāndhyamupāgatau || k19.109 ||
bhikṣudvijātithigaṇe pūjyamāne surālaye |
svakaṃ pitṛgṛhaṃ dṛṣṭvā paraṃ vismayamāyayau || k19.110 ||
niścitya sarvaṃ bhāvānāṃ kṣaṇikatvādanityatām |
sneharāgaṃ samutsṛjya tatrasthaḥ samacintayat || k19.111 ||
aho saṃmohanidreyaṃ nirantato divāniśam |
svapnamāyāvilasitaiḥ karotyadbhutavibhramam || k19.112 ||
janmavartmapradā mātā pitā bījavapatkhagaḥ |
pānthapūjāsanaṃ kāyaḥ ko'yaṃ niyamasaṃgamaḥ || k19.113 ||
śriyaḥ saṃsārābhrabhramaparicitāḥ kāñcanarucā
āśā dig(?) nirbandhāstaḍita iva nirlepacapalāḥ |
vapuḥ sarvāpāyaiḥ kṣayabhayanikāyaiḥ parigataṃ
jarārogodvegaistadapi na virāgastanubhṛtām || k19.114 ||
śriye svastisamāptaye svajanasyāyamañjaliḥ |
dākṣiṇyaiḥ kṣamataṃ śrīṣu pravrajyaiva priyā mama || k19.115 ||
iti dhyātvā sa pitarau samāśvāsyāptalocanau |
buddhau dharmapathe śuddhe śamadhāmni nyaveśayat || k19.116 ||
sārthabhraṣṭaścirāyātkṛśo'pi svajanasya ca |
aluptasattvavibhavānnakṛpāspadatāṃ yayau || k19.117 ||
anukampasva yadyetaṃ saṃsārakleśavihvalam |
sataḥ kasyānukampyāste saṃpatsaṃparkanitspṛhāḥ || k19.118 ||
paśupālakaviprastrīsaṃdeśādi yathoditam |
nigadya tebhyaḥ kanakaprāptipratyayalakṣaṇam || k19.119 ||
śāntaḥ kātyāyanaṃ prāpya pravrajyāṃ sa samādade |
mugdhānāṃ yadviṣādāya tatprasādāya dhīmatām || k19.120 ||
sa samāsādya viśadaṃ sritaḥprāptiphalaṃ tataḥ |
sakṛdāgāmyanāgāmiphalamarhatphalaṃ tathā || k19.121 ||
traidhātuko vītarāgaḥ samaloṣṭāśmakāñcanaḥ |
ākāśapāṇitulyo'bhūdasicandanayoḥ samah || k19.122 ||
śrāvastyāṃ veṇugahane jinaṃ jetavane sthitam |
bhagavantaṃ yayau draṣṭuṃ so'tha kātyāyanājñayā || k19.123 ||
praṇitātakṛtātithyaḥ prītyā bhagavatā svayam |
sa śroṇakoṭīkarṇo'tha babhāṣe harṣanirbharaḥ || k19.124 ||
bhagavān dharmakāyena dṛṣṭo'yaṃ śrotravartmani |
adhunā rūpakāyena puṇyairālokito mayā || k19.125 ||
analpasukṛtaprāpyamidaṃ taddarśanāmṛtam |
pītvā na tṛptimāyānti vañcitā eva te param || k19.126 ||
aspṛhasyāpi te mūrtiḥ kurute kasya na spṛhām |
nirlopasyāpi te dṛṣṭiraho harṣeṇa limpati || k19.127 ||
tvatkathā tvadanudhyānaṃ tvatprāptistvanniṣevaṇam |
etāḥ kuśalamūlānāṃ sphītāḥ phalasamṛddhayaḥ || k19.128 ||
iti śrutvā bhagavatā prasādenābnhinanditaḥ |
tadādiṣṭaṃ śamārāmaṃ sa vihāramavāptavān || k19.129 ||
tasyāspadaṃ samabhyetya praṇayādbhagavānapu |
śrutvāsya madhuraṃ dharmaṃ svādhyāyaṃ praśaśaṃsa saḥ || k19.130 ||
tāṃ śroṇikoṭikarṇasya dṛṣṭvā praśamasaṃpadam |
bhikṣubhirbhagavān pṛṣṭaḥ pūrvavṛttamabhāṣata || k19.131 ||
vārāṇasyāṃ purā samyaksaṃbuddhe kāśyapābhidhe |
nirvāṇadhātau niḥśeṣakāryatvātparinirvṛte || k19.132 ||
kṛkināmanṛpaścaityaṃ tasya ratnairakārayat |
svayaṃ tatpuṇyasaṃbharaṃ svargaṃ vaktumivodgatam || k19.133 ||
śīrṇasthāpitasaṃskāre tasmin saṃjñādhṛtaṃ dhanam |
tatputraḥ prāptarājyo'tha na dadau lobhamohitaḥ || k19.134 ||
athottarāpathāyātaḥ sarthavāho'rthadābhidhaḥ |
pradadau pṛthivīmūlyaṃ tatkṛte karṇabhūṣaṇam || k19.135 ||
kālāntaropagato'pi datvā cānyaddhanaṃ mahat |
praṇidhānaṃ sa kṛtavān bhūyāsaṃ puṇyavāniti || k19.136 ||
sa śroṇakoṭikarṇo'yaṃ puṇyaiḥ prātapadaṃ mahat |
tadvidhenaiva saṃyātaḥ karṇabhūṣaṇalakṣaṇaḥ || k19.137 ||
prasthānasamaye mātā śrāvitā paruṣaṃ vacaḥ |
yasmādanena tenāsya babhūvāsya śramo mahān || k19.138 ||
madhyeṣu mahataḥ śuklaguṇasatkarmavāsasaḥ |
kṛṣṇakarmalavāṃśo'pi sphuṭa evāvadhāryate || k19.139 ||
sukṛtasacivaḥ sattvetsāhaḥ pravāsasakhī dhṛtiḥ
viṣamataraṇe vīryaṃ seturvipadyadhikā kṛpā |
śamaparicitā paryante ca prasādamayī matiḥ
pariṇatiriyaṃ puṇyaprāpteḥ sphuratphalaśālinī || k19.140 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ śroṇakoṭikarṇāvadānaṃ nāmonaviṃśatitamaḥ pallavaḥ ||


20. āmrapālyavadānam |

dvijihvasaṅge kathamasti vṛttiranekamukhye kathamasti saukhyam |
karmāntabandhe'sti kathaṃ svaśaktiḥ prajñāprakarṣe kathamastyapāyaḥ || k20.1 ||
mithilāyāṃ videheṣu jalasattvābhidho nṛpaḥ |
abhūdbhujabhujaṅgasya viśrāntapṛthivībharaḥ || k20.2 ||
khaṇḍo nāma mahāmātyastasyākhaṇḍalasaṃpadaḥ |
babhūvāśeṣaṣāṅguṇyaparijñānabṛhaspatiḥ || k20.3 ||
nītijñagauravāttasya nṛpe vyaktaṃ nalokini |
sadābhavanmukhaprekṣī sarvaḥ kāryavaśājjanaḥ || k20.4 ||
gatānugatikatvena pravāhapraṇayī bharaḥ |
vardhate vāryamāṇasya svajanasya jalasya ca || k20.5 ||
sarvaṃ tanmayamālokya janaṃ mātsaryamūrcchitāḥ |
mantriṇaḥ saṃhatāstasya vinipātamacintayan || k20.6 ||
te praviśyāśramaṃ rājñastāṃ tasya prabahviṣṇūtām |
muhuḥ śaṅkāspadaṃ kṛtvā śaśaṃsurbhedakovidāḥ || k20.7 ||
tadgirā śaṅkito rājā tasya vaimukhyamāyayau |
abalābālabhūpālā varṇapratyayāḥ param || k20.8 ||
aśaṅkyādapi śaṅkante śaṅkādoṣe'pyaśaṅkitāḥ |
aviśeṣajñacapalā bhūpālāḥ kākaśaṅkinaḥ || k20.9 ||
prabhorviraktiliṅgāni vilokyāmātyapuṃgavaḥ |
svasutau gopasiṃhākhyau saśaṅkaḥ svairamabravīt || k20.10 ||
dhūrtairme vītaviśvāsaḥ piśunairnṛpatiḥ kṛtaḥ |
pratyayaṃ naiti hṛdaye vidāryāpi pradarśite || k20.11 ||
viraktaḥ sthagitālāpadarśanaśravaṇaḥ prabhuḥ |
śepha iva vṛddhasya yātaḥ śithilatāṃ mama || k20.12 ||
piśunodbhūtabhedasya premṇaḥ saṃdhirna vidyate |
na maṇiḥ śliṣyāti punaḥ pāṣāṇaśakalīkṛtaḥ || k20.13 ||
dvijihṇakuṭilakrāntaḥ prabhuścandanapādapaḥ |
na yātyarthakriyākārī guṇavānapi sevyatām || k20.14 ||
kathaṃ nṛpanidhānārthī kuśalaṃ bhajate naraḥ |
ghoradveṣaviṣāviṣṭadvijihvāghātavihvalaḥ || k20.15 ||
tasmādvrajāmaḥ saṃtyajya dveṣadoṣeṇa bhūpateḥ |
śaṅkāśalyamaye vṛtte me'smin deśe sthitena kim || k20.16 ||
dakṣā rakṣākṣamāḥ śūrāḥ prabhutārthāḥ susaṃhatāḥ |
santaḥ santi viśālāyāṃ vāsastatra mamepsitaḥ || k20.17 ||
iti bruvāṇaḥ putrābhyāṃ tathetyuktaḥ sa sānugaḥ |
udyānagatimānena prayayau saparicchadaḥ || k20.18 ||
prayāṇaṃ nṛpatirjñātvā nivartanasamudyataḥ |
yatnenāpi na taṃ prāpa notsṛṣṭaṃ labhyate punaḥ || k20.19 ||
mūrkhāḥ satsu kṛtāvajñā vimuhyante kṣaṇena taiḥ |
yāti teṣāṃ tu sarvatra ko'pi nārthī kṛtārthatām || k20.20 ||
dhīmānamātyaḥ prāpto'tha kṛṣṭo vaiśālikairguṇaiḥ |
pūjitaḥ praṇayācāraiḥ saṃghamukhye pade sthitaḥ || k20.21 ||
tadbuddhivibhavāptaśrīḥ so'tha tatpramukho gaṇaḥ |
kadācidanayāllekhe na parābahvapātratām || k20.22 ||
atha kālena siṃhasya mantrisūnoḥ kanīyasaḥ |
ajāyata sutā kāntā cailā nāma guṇocitā || k20.23 ||
dvitīyā copacailākhyā sutā jātāsya sundarī |
nanmanyeva tayoḥ prāha nimittajño vicakṣaṇaḥ || k20.24 ||
cailāyāstanayo bhāvī pitṛhantā mahīpatiḥ |
guṇavānupacailāyāḥ pūrṇalakṣahṇavāniti || k20.25 |
jyeṣṭho mantrisutaḥ śauryādgopaḥ prauḍhamadoddhataḥ |
udyānamardanakṣepairgaṇānāṃ dveṣyatāṃ yayau || k20.26 ||
tatpiturgauravāttasmai sānujāya vimanyavaḥ |
viśālaśālatāmante jīrṇodyānadvayaṃ daduḥ || k20.27 ||
sugatapratimāṃ cakre tatraikaḥ sukṛtocitām |
vihāraṃ vaibhavodāraṃ bhuvanābharaṇaṃ paraḥ || k20.28 ||
atha pitrā balotsiktaḥ sutaḥ pratyantamaṇḍale |
gaṇakopabhayādgopaḥ karmāntopārjane dhṛtaḥ || k20.29 ||
kālena tridivaṃ yāte tasminmantrivare gaṇaiḥ |
kanīyasastu sādhutvātsiṃhastasya pade dhṛtaḥ || k20.30 ||
gopaḥ piturasaṃprāpya padaṃ gaṇavimānitaḥ |
taddeśavāsavirasaḥ parihāramacintayat || k20.31 ||
vāstavya kaṇṭakākīrṇe vyāghrāghrāte varaṃ vane |
anekasvāmisaṃbhinnajane na tu viśṛṅkhale || k20.32 ||
nānāmatakriyālāpaḥ kathamārādhyate gaṇaḥ |
samīhitaṃ yadekasya datanyasmai na rocate || k20.33 ||
itmānī sa saṃcintya gatvā rājagṛhaṃ puram |
bimbisāraṃ narapatiṃ guṇaśriyamaśiśriyat || k20.34 ||
sa tena mānitaḥ prītyā tasya viśrambhabhūrabhūt |
cirarucyeva tatkālamābhāti guṇasaṃgatiḥ || k20.35 ||
rājño'tha bimbisārasya vallabhā pañcatāṃ yayau |
tadviyogāgnisaṃtaptaṃ taṃ vicintya sa buddhimān || k20.36 ||
upacailāṃ sutāṃ bhrātustadvivāhocitā vadhūm |
gūḍhacārī tadādeśātvaiśālakapurīṃ yaya || k20.37 ||
kanyā gaṇopabhogyauva na kasmaicitpradīyate |
iti vaiśālikaiḥ pūrvaṃ svadeśe niyamaḥ kṛtaḥ || k20.38 ||
tatpure dvārarakṣāyai yakṣasthānāvalambinī |
parapraveśe kurute śabdaṃ ghaṇṭī paṭīyasī || k20.39 ||
sa praviśya tato bhrāturgūḍhamudyānacāriṇīm |
upacailāṃ samāhatuṃ gataścailāmavāptavān || k20.40 ||
yātastaṃ rathamāruhya ghaṇṭāśabdādabhidryutam |
sa hatvā vīrapuruṣānavāpa nṛpateḥ puram || k20.41 ||
tamūce devakanyeyaṃ prātā vimanasātmanā |
pitṛhantā suto hyāsyā nimittajñena sūcitaḥ || k20.42 ||
tasmādeṣā narapatermahiṣī na tavocitā |
tvayi jīvati jīvanti prajānāṃ sarvasaṃpadaḥ || k20.43 ||
ityuktastena tāṃ dṛṣṭvā tyuktaṃ naiva śaśāka saḥ |
niruddhaḥ karmaṇā hyeva tanmukhālekhyalekhayā || k20.44 ||
so'vadatkka kadā dṛṣṭa putreṇa nihataḥ pitā |
svayaṃ mayābhiṣektavyaḥ suto yadybhaviṣyati || k20.45 ||
ityuktvā nṛpatiḥ kanyāṃ pariṇīyābhavatsukhī |
kṛtakarmorminirmāṇe prabhavanti na buddhayaḥ || k20.46 ||
bhoginastasya kālena tasyāṃ sūnurajāyata |
jyotiṣkacarite yasya vṛttamuktaṃ pitṛdruhaḥ || k20.47 ||
tapovanamṛgādhānamṛgayāvyasane vane |
evaṃvidho hyabhūttasya muniśāpaḥ sutākṛtiḥ || k20.48 ||
atrāntare mahānnāma vaiśālikagaṇāgraṇīḥ |
kanyāmāmravanātprāpa kadalīskandhanirgatām || k20.49 ||
sā tasya bhavane kāntā vardhamānā śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
vidadhe vipulāṃ prītiṃ dānacintāmaccetasi || k20.50 ||
praṇayādāmrapālīti bandhubhiḥ sā kṛtābhidhāḥ |
sūnuhīnamina tyaktvā bālyaṃ yauvanamādade || k20.51 ||
tadvivāhodyatasyātha na sehe tatpiturgaṇaḥ |
gaṇopabhogyā kanyeti samayasya vyatikramam || k20.52 ||
pitaraṃ duḥkhasaṃtaptaṃ sametyātha jagādsā |
bhavāmi gaṇabhogyaiva kiṃ tveṣa samayo yadi || k20.53 ||
ekasyopari nānyasya praveśaḥ svapade sthitiḥ |
paṇaḥ kārṣāpaṇaśataiḥ pañcabhiḥ pratyahaṃ mama || k20.54 ||
saptāhenaiva vicayaḥ karyo veśmani nānyadā |
ityasmin samaye va dhyaḥ sarvaścaiva vyatikramī || k20.55 ||
iti tatsamayaṃ jñātvā tatpiturvacasā gaṇaḥ |
akārodbāḍhamityuktvā dṛḍhaniścayamādarāt || k20.56 ||
tataḥ saratnabhavane varābharaṇabhūṣite |
hemaharmyasamārūḍhā dideśa dinacandrikām || k20.57 ||
tataḥ paṇīkṛtaḥ kāmī yo yastāṃ samupāyayau |
tasya tasyābhavattasyāḥ prabhāveṇaujasaḥ kṣayaḥ || k20.58 ||
draṣṭumeva na śekuste kiṃ punaḥ spraṣṭumākulāḥ |
bhujaṅgabhogasaṃruddhāṃ tāṃ candanalatāmiva || k20.59 ||
tataḥ sā sundarī bheje yauvanasyāpi yauvanam |
guruṇā stanabhāreṇa madhyabhaṅgabhayapradam || k20.60 ||
smarasaṃbhogarahitaṃ tattasyā rūpamadbhutam |
śvabhrahemalatāpuṣpamiva niṣphalatāṃ yayau || k20.61 ||
kautukāśāvinodāya nānādeśāntarāgataiḥ |
akāri citrakārairbhūpālapratikṛtirgṛhe || k20.62 ||
vidhāya citralikhitān sā krameṇa nareśvarān |
dadarśa bimbirāsaya rūpaṃ ratipateciva || k20.63 ||
tamālokyaiva sahasā samudbhūtamanobhavā |
sa yena likhitastatra taṃ papraccha kutūhalāt || k20.64 ||
ko'yaṃ sakhe prītilatāmādhavo vasudhāpatiḥ |
prīṇāti locane yasya sudhāparicitā ruciḥ || k20.65 ||
dhanyā kā nāma bhūbharturasya praṇayabhāgiṇī |
lakṣyaṃ saubhagyajaṃ garvamurvaśyāḥ saṃhṛtaṃ yayā || k20.66 ||
iti pṛṣṭatayā svairaṃ tāmūce citrakovidaḥ |
bhūpatirbimbasāro'yaṃ sāraṃ sukṛtasaṃpadām || k20.67 ||
śuaryarūpatulārohe devāḥ ke nākanāyakāḥ |
śaṅke karoti naivāsya manmatho vā manoratham || k20.68 ||
ityukte tena sā tasthau bhūpālanyastalocanā |
sahasaivābhilāṣeṇa navīnābhimukhīkṛtā || k20.69 ||
atrāntare bimbisāraḥ svairaveśmani nijane |
kathānte gopamavadatkiṃcitsmitasitādharaḥ || k20.70 ||
śrūyatāṃ yanmama sakhe kiṃcinmanasi vartate |
niryantramitrasvacchandavādaḥ ko'pi sudhārasaḥ || k20.71 ||
vaiśālikaurvarārohā gaṇaiḥ sādhāraṇikṛtā |
rambhorūḥ śrūyate kāntā rambhāgarbhasamudbhavā || k20.72 || k20.tatprabhāvavinaṣṭāśaistejasvipraṇayocitā |
sā tairna dūṣitādyāpi mātaṅgairiva padminī || k20.73 ||
śravaṇādeva sānandamaparyuṣitakautukam |
na karoti manaḥ kasya tatstrīratnamayonijam || k20.74 ||
abhilāṣi manastasyāṃ śrotāya jātaṃme saha cakṣuṣā |
tadguṇaśrutidhanyāya śrotrāya spṛhayāmyaham || k20.75 ||
ityukte bhūmipatinā gopastaṃ prayabhāṣata |
bhujaṅgagaṇasaṃruddhaḥ sa rājanmānmatho nidhiḥ || k20.76 ||
atyalpaskhalitaṃ prāpya duḥsahāpātadurgamaḥ |
eṣa te viṣamaḥ panthā darśito viṣameṣuṇā || k20.77 ||
labhate sā na nirgantuṃ na yuktaṃ gamanaṃ ca te |
kimasmin viratopāye vadāmyubhayasaṃśaye || k20.78 ||
ityuktastena nṛpatirnotkaṇṭhāgrāhamatyajat |
vidvāṃso'pyucitāṃ nītiṃ na smaranti smarāturāḥ || k20.79 ||
vaiśālikapurīṃ yāto gopena sahito'tha saḥ |
praviveśānyaveśena mandiraṃ hariṇīdṛśaḥ || k20.80 ||
sā citradarśanenaiva dṛṣṭvā paricitaṃ dṛśoḥ |
naranāthaṃ savailakṣyalakṣaṇaṃ kṣitimaikṣata || k20.81 ||
lajjāniruttare tasyāḥ kampavyatikare param |
raṇantī rasanā cakre svāgataṃ nṛpaterivaḥ || k20.82 ||
vilokya dhanyatāmānī tatra citre nijaṃ vapuḥ |
tāṃ lāvaṇyanadīṃ rājā nayanāñjalinā papau || k20.83 ||
lajjāveśena sundaryāmābhijātyena bhūpatau |
ābaddhamainayoḥ kṣipraṃ gopastāṃ sasmito'vadat || k20.84 ||
ayaṃ te citralikhitākāradhyānāvadhānataḥ |
vyaktaṃ bhakṣiviśeṣena devaḥ pratyakṣatāṃ gataḥ || k20.85 ||
tvayāyaṃ likhitaścitre tvamanena tu cetasi |
na jāne yuvayoḥ ko nu prayātaḥ premadūtatām || k20.86 ||
ityādibhiḥ kathābandhaiḥ parīpūrṇapramodayoḥ |
yadyadeva smarādiṣṭaṃ tattadāsvādatāṃ gatam || k20.87 ||
ghaṇṭāravākule loke rājā pracchannakāmukah |
saptarātramanālokye tasthau tadbhavane rahaḥ || k20.88 ||
lateva puṣpitā kāle tasmādgarbhamavāpya sā |
cakre viditavṛttāntaṃ taṃ lajjāvanatā śanaiḥ || k20.89 ||
āsanne viśmavicaye datvāsmai nṛpatiryayau |
bhāviputraparijñānapratyayādaṅgulīyakam || k20.90 ||
yāte bhāsvadvapiṣu nṛpatau saṃmate locanānāṃ sadyaḥ prodyadvirahatimirākrāntimīrākrāntimīlanmukhābjā |
sābhūtsāyaṃtanatanutarāpāravātābhibhūtā
śokocchvāsavyatikaravatī hāsahīnā niśeva || k20.91 ||
kapolaṃ pāṇipadmena saṃkalpena mahīpatim |
navaṃ tānavamaṅgena vahantī nimimīla sā || k20.92 ||
tataḥ kālena kalyāṇī pratibimbopamaṃ pituḥ |
ajījanatsā natayaṃ vinayaṃ sādhudhīriva || k20.93 ||
vardhamāne śanaistasmin kāle bimba ivaindave |
bimbisārasya putro'yamiti lokeṣu paprathe || k20.94 ||
apavādaparaistaistaistaṃ pratyanucittairyadā |
bādhante śiśavaḥ krīḍāprasaṅge'marṣasaṃyatāḥ || k20.95 ||
tataḥ sā preṣayāmāsa putraṃ vidyārjanocitam |
vaṇiksārthena mahatā sāṅgulīyaṃ pituḥ padam || k20.96 ||
bimbisāro'pi saṃprāpya sadṛśākāramātmajam |
harṣadṛptaḥ pariṣvajya cakre tasya parigraham || k20.97 ||
vṛttāṇte viśrute tasminnāmrapālyāhsakautukaiḥ |
bhikṣubhirbhagavān pṛṣṭastatkathāmavadajjinaḥ || k20.98 ||
pure rājagṛhe rājavallabhodyānakānane |
babhūva mālatī nāma pūrvamudyānapālikā || k20.99 ||
sā kadācitprasādārdraṃ puraḥ prāptaṃ yadṛcchayā |
pratyekabuddhaṃ rājaṣiṃ cūtapuṇpaipūjayat || k20.100 ||
ayonijā nṛpasyāhaṃ patnī syāmiti tatra sā |
praṇidhānaṃ puraścakre tasya cittaprasādinī || k20.101 ||
puṇyapuṣpaphalabhogaśālinī saiva divyatanurāmrapālikā |
ityudāracaritā niśamya te bhikṣavaḥ sapadi vismayaṃ yayuḥ || k20.102 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāmāmrapālyavadānaṃ nāma viṃśatitamaḥ pallavaḥ ||


21. jetavanapratigrahāvadānam |

dṛṣṭaṃ muṣṭiniviṣṭapāradakaṇākāraṃ narāṇāṃ dhanaṃ
dhanyo'sau yaśasā sahākṣayapadaṃ yadyasya vidyotate |
dīnānāthagaṇārpaṇopakaraṇībhūtaprabhūtaśriyaḥ
puṇyārāmavihāracaityabhagavadbimbapratiṣṭhādibhiḥ || k21.1 ||
śrīmān babhūva śrāvastyāṃ datto nāma gṛhābhipaḥ |
sutastasya sudatto'bhūdākaraḥ puṇyasaṃpadām || k21.2 ||
sa bālya evālaṃkāraṃ yācakebhyaḥ sadā dadau |
prāgjanmavāsanābhyāsaḥ kasya kena nirvāyate || k21.3 ||
nityamābharaṇatyāgātparaṃ pitrā nivāritaḥ |
nadīsamuddhṛtānyasmai sadaivānyānyadarśayat || k21.4 ||
nidhidarśī sa sarvatra pitari tridivaṃ gate |
dīnānāthapradānena babhūvānāthapiṇḍadaḥ || k21.5 ||
kṛtadānahsa kālena putravān putravtsalaḥ |
abhūtputravivāhārthī kanyānveṣaṇayatnavān || k21.6 ||
kanyakāṃ yācituṃ kāṃcitpuraṃ rājagṛhaṃ tataḥ |
madhuskandhābhidhaṃ dakṣaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ visarsarja saḥ || k21.7 ||
āsādya magadhān rājagṛhaṃ nagarametya saḥ |
mahādhanaṃ gṝhapatiṃ yayāce kanyakāṃ dvijaḥ || k21.8 ||
anāthapiṇḍado nāma śrāvastyāmasti viśrutaḥ |
tatputrāya sujātāya kanyāyāṃ dehītyuvāca saḥ || k21.9 ||
so'bravīdeśa saṃbandhaḥ paramo'smatkulocitaḥ |
vaṃśe'smākaṃ tu kanyānāṃ śuklamādīyate mahat || k21.10 ||
śataṃ śataṃ rathāgryāṇāṃ gajāśvāśvatarasya ca |
dāśīnicayaniṣkānāṃ dīyatāṃ yadi śakyate || k21.11 ||
ityukte tena taṃ vipraṃ sasmitaḥ pratyabhāṣata |
anāthapiṇḍadagṛhe dāsye śulkaṃ tadalpakam || k21.12 ||
brāhmaṇenākhile tasmin kanyāśulke pratiśrute |
tamādarādgṛhapatirbhojanāya nyamantrayat || k21.13 ||
sa bhuktvā vividhaṃ tatra bhakṣyabhojyamayantritaḥ |
rātrau visūcikākrāntaścukrośa vipulavyathaḥ || k21.14 ||
ye'nnamaśnanti laulyena niśi nidrāsukhāpaham |
janmakarma kathaṃ kuryuḥ paralokasukhāya te || k21.15 ||
tatyājāśucibhītyā taṃ gṛhātparijano bahiḥ |
āspadaṃ nairapekṣyasya jātyā dāsajanaḥ śaṭhaḥ || k21.16 ||
śubhena karmaṇā tasya saṃprāptastena vartmanā |
samaudgalyāyanaḥ śāriputraḥ kāruṇyapeśalaḥ || k21.17 ||
taṃ vaṃśasya dalāgreṇa nirlikhyāpi tathā mṛdā |
prakṣālya dharmamādiśya tau tasya yayatuḥ śanaiḥ || k21.18 ||
so'pi cittaṃ tayoragre prasādya tyaktavigrahaḥ |
caturmahārājikeṣu deveṣu samājāyata || k21.19 ||
tatra viśravaṇādeśānmartyaloke niketane |
sa cakre śibiradvāre pūjādhiṣṣṭhānasaṃnidhim || k21.20 ||
lekhavijñātasaṃbandhaviścayo'tha yathoditam |
anāthapiṇḍadaḥ śulkamādāya svayamāyayau || k21.21 ||
sa saṃbandhigṛhaṃ prāpya dadarśāścaryakāriṇīm |
śikharākārarājārhabhakṣyasaṃbhārasaṃpadam || k21.22 ||
sa vismayādgṛhapatiṃ papraccha svacchamānasaḥ |
bhūribhakṣyptsavaḥ ko'yamapi rājā nimantritaḥ || k21.23 ||
sa taṃ babhāṣe bhagavān buddhaḥ saṃghaparigrahaḥ |
mayā nimantritaḥ saṃghe so'yaṃ mama mahotsavaḥ || k21.24 ||
iti buddhābhidhānena jātaromāśncakaṇṭakaḥ |
indusyandirivālkinnaḥ sahasaiva babhūva saḥ || k21.25 ||
aviditaparamārthe kasyacinnāmamātre
sphurati sahajabhāvaḥ ko'pi janmānubandhaḥ |
abhinavaghananāde vyaktaharṣabhilāṣaḥ
spṛśati lalitanṛtyodvṛttavṛttiṃ mayūraḥ || k21.26 ||
so'vadadvadanāmbhojasaṃjātābhinavadyutiḥ |
ka eṣa bhagavān buddhaḥ kaśca saṃgho'bhidhīyate || k21.27 ||
iti pṛṣṭo gṛhapatistena provāca sasmitaḥ |
aho bata na jānīṣe śāstāraṃ bhuvanatraye || k21.28 ||
saṃsārapāśabhītānāṃ śaraṇyaṃ śaraṇaiṣiṇām |
jina yastaṃ na jānāti sa loke vañcitaḥ param || k21.29 ||
kiṃ tena mohalīnena viphalīkṛtajanmanā |
ajñānataraṇopāyaṃ vayo yena vyayīkṛtam || k21.30 ||
gotamo bhagavān buddhaḥ śākyarājakuladbhivaḥ |
saṃbudhyānuttaraṃ samyaksaṃbodhimanagārikaḥ || k21.31 ||
paścātpravrajitānāṃ ca tasyaivānugrahātparam |
bhikṣūṇāṃ gatarāgāṇāṃ samūhaḥ saṃgha ucyate || k21.32 ||
sa eṣa buddhapramukhaḥ saṃghaḥ svakuśalaiṣiṇāḥ |
mayā puṇyapaṇaṃ prāpyaṃ praṇayena nimantritaḥ || k21.33 ||
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā tatsmṛtvānāthapiṇḍadaḥ |
buddhālambanabhāvena niśi nidrāṃ samāyayau || k21.34 ||
rajanyāṃ yāmaśeṣāyāṃ samākṛṣṭa ivetsukaḥ |
prabhātamiti sa jñātvā puradvāreṇa niryayau || k21.35 ||
śibikādvāramāsādya saṃprāpta iva devatām |
madhuskandhena nirdiṣṭaṃ śreyaḥpanthānamāptavān || k21.36 ||
bhagavāṃstaṃ tataḥ prāpya sa tṛṣṇārta ivāmṛtam |
babhūvānupamāsvādaṃ pramodāmodanirvṛtaḥ || k21.37 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā sādaraṃ dūrātchāyātarumivādhvagaḥ |
avāpa gatasaṃtāpaḥ śrāntiṃ viśrāntiśītalām || k21.38 ||
tasya taddarśaneva vimalābhigataḥ manaḥ |
śaratsamāgameneva ghanadhvāntojjhitaṃ nabhaḥ || k21.39 ||
sa ko'pi puṇyaśīlānāmanubhāvaḥ prasādinām |
bhavanti yatprasādena nirvighnāścittavṛttayaḥ || k21.40 ||
so'cintayadaho mohavihīnasya hi me tathā |
anucchedavikāro'yaṃ jātaḥ praśamasaṃpadaḥ || k21.41 ||
vañcito'smi na yatpūrvaṃ dṛṣto'yaṃ bhagavānmayā |
nādhanyānāmiyaṃ yāti mūrtirlocanagocaram || k21.42 ||
amṛtamadhirodārā dṛṣṭirdyutiḥ śaśipeśalā taruṇakaruṇāyattā vṛttiḥ prasādamayī matiḥ |
ayamatiśayaṃ pratyāsannaḥ karoti virāgatāṃ vigatarajasāṃ niḥsaṃsāraḥ priyo'pi parigrahaḥ || k21.43 ||
iti cittaprasādena cintayannupasṛtya saḥ |
vidadhe tasya sānandaḥ pādapadmābhivandanam || k21.44 ||
bhagavānapi tatprāptiprasādānandalakṣaṇam |
uvāha vadanacchāyaṃ pūrṇakāruṇyapūritam || k21.45 ||
dṛṣṭimāśvāsajananīṃ kāmaṃkāmapi tasya saḥ |
visasarjojjvalāṃ janmarajaḥśuddhyai sudhānadīm || k21.46 ||
athāsya bhagavān bhradrāṃ vidadhe dharmadeśanām |
caturṇāmāryasatyānāṃ pratibhāvavidhāyinīm || k21.47 ||
sa śāsanāddhi saṃvyastasamastakleśasaṃtatiḥ |
nijaṃ janma nivedyāsmai praṇatastamabhāṣataḥ || k21.48 ||
atikrānto'smi bhagavan bhavantaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
vipannavāsanābhyāsaḥ saṃsāre na rame param || k21.49 ||
karotyakuśalaṃ dūre śubhamāśu prayacchati |
sūcayatyucitācāraṃ mahatamavalokanam || k21.50 ||
sukhārhaṃ tvadvihārāya vihāraṃ paramādarāt |
ratnasārapurodāraṃ svapuraṃ kārayāmyaham || k21.51 ||
karotu tatra bhagavān satataṃ sthityanugraham |
dhanairāsevito'smābhiḥ saparyāparicaryayā || k21.52 ||
ityarthanāṃ tathetyasya bhagavān pratyapadyata |
praṇayuprārthanābhaṅgapragalbhā na hi sādhavaḥ || k21.53 ||
bhagavantamathāmantrya śrāvastīṃ sa purīṃ yayau |
tadādiṣṭena sahitaḥ śāriputreṇa bhikṣuṇā || k21.54 ||
tatra jetakumāreṇa hiraṇyārghoṇa bhūyasā |
dattaṃ kāñcanamādāya vihāraṃ tamasūtrayat || k21.55 ||
bhaktyutsāhādathārambhakṛtasāhāyyakahsuraiḥ |
vihāraṃ tridivākāraṃ cakārānāthapiṇḍadaḥ || k21.56 ||
tatra jetakumāro'pi bhaktyā bhagavataḥ param |
yaśaḥpuṇyapratiṣṭhāyai vidadhe dvārakoṣṭhakam || k21.57 ||
athatīrthyāstamālokya vihārārambhamadbhutam |
sāpavādavivādena cakrurdveṣākulāḥ kalim || k21.58 ||
raktākṣapramukhasteṣāṃ mātsaryātkṣudrapraṇḍitaḥ |
sapakṣa iva kṛṣṇāhiścakitaḥ purataḥ sadā || k21.59 ||
ruddhe vihārasaṃbhāre tena vādajayāvadhi |
anāthapiṇḍadagirā śāriputraḥ samāyayau || k21.60 ||
raktākṣo'tha tamāhūya prabhāvotkarṣadarśane |
indrajālabalotphullaṃ sahakāramadarśayat || k21.61 ||
śāriputraprabhāvotthairvipulaistanmukhānilaiḥ |
unmūlitaḥ śakalatāṃ tīrthyotsāha ivāpa saḥ || k21.62 ||
raktākṣavihitāṃ phullakamalāṃ padminīṃ tataḥ |
paṅkaśeṣāṃ dvipaścakre śāriputravinirmitaḥ || k21.63 ||
ratkākṣavakṣonikṣiptaḥ saptaśīrṣamahoragaḥ |
śāriputreṇa nikṣiptastārkṣyapakṣāgramārutaiḥ || k21.64 ||
tadāhūto'tha vetālaḥ śāriputreṇa kīlitaḥ |
prerito mantravirtyeṇa raktākṣaṃ hantumudyayau || k21.65 ||
vetālābhihatastrāsānnaśyanmānamadajvaraḥ |
śaraṇaṃ pādapatitaḥ śāriputraṃ jagāma saḥ || k21.66 ||
raktākṣastena bhaṅgena śaraṇyaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
pravrajyāyāṃ vītarāgaḥ śuddhāṃ bodhismavāptavān || k21.67 ||
tīrthyāstvanye paridveṣakrodhapāramitāṃśavaḥ |
tatra karmakaravyājāttasthurbhikṣavadhodyatāḥ || k21.68 ||
te'tha dharmadruhaḥ kāle śāriputreṇa lakṣitāḥ |
taddṛṣṭipātamātreṇa babhūvurmaitramānasāḥ || k21.69 ||
āśayānuśayaṃ dhātuṃ prakṛtiṃ ca vicārya saḥ |
dharmadeśanayā teṣāṃ dideśānuttarāṃ daśām || k21.70 ||
atha tasya vihārasya nirvighnārambhakarmaṇi |
anāthapiṇḍadaṃ prāha śāriputraḥ smitānanaḥ || k21.71 ||
vihārasūtrapātasya tulya eva kṣaṇe mahān |
haumo vihāraḥ saṃvṛttastuṣite devasadmani || k21.72 ||
etadākarṇya saṃjātaprasādadviguṇāntaraḥ |
hemaratnavarāgāraṃ vihāraṃ samakārayat || k21.73 ||
vibhavairatha rājārhaiḥ pathi tenopakalpitaiḥ |
vijñaptistridivaiḥ sārdhamāyayau bhagavān jinah || k21.74 ||
tadāgamanaharṣeṇa prasanne bhavanatraye |
anāthapiṇḍadastasmai vāridhārāmapātayat || k21.75 ||
tasmin yadā na pradeśe vāridhārā papāta sā |
tadā bhagavato vākyāttvaritaṃ patitānyataḥ || k21.76 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā kautukātpṛṣṭo bhikṣubhirbhagavān punaḥ |
uvāca śrūyatāmetadvāristambhasya kāraṇam || k21.77 ||
anena pūrvabuddhebhye asminnevedamāspadam |
pratipāditamityeṣā vāridhārānyataścyutā || k21.78 ||
anenaiva purā samyaksaṃbuddhāya vipaśyine |
ayameva varārāmapradeśaḥ pratipāditaḥ || k21.79 ||
buddhāya śikhine prādātpuṣyajanmanyayaṃ punaḥ |
tato dadau viśvabhuve jināya raghijanmani || k21.80 ||
bhavadattābhidho bhūtvā kakucchandāya dattavān |
dadau bṛhaspatirbhūṭvā kanakākhyāya tāyine || k21.81 ||
kāśyapāya punaścāyaṃ prādādāṣāḍhajanmaniḥ |
anenaivādhunā mahyaṃ deśo'yaṃ pratipāditaḥ || k21.82 ||
kālena sudhanākhyo'yaṃ maitreyāya pradāsyati |
sattvavān kṣāntiśīlatvānnidhānānyeṣa paśyati || k21.83 ||
punaścāyaṃ gṝhapatirbhūtvā hemapradābhidhaḥ |
cakre pratyekabuddhasya saṃskāraṃ parinirvṛtau || k21.84 ||
ratnakumbhe tadasthīni dhṛtvā tatpraṇidhānataḥ |
adhunā ratnakośārhaḥ saṃjāto'yaṃ suvarṇabhās|| k21.85 ||
śrutveti śāsturvacanābhidhānaṃ te bhikṣavaḥ sāramivāmṛtasya |
kartuḥ pratiṣṭhārjitapūrṇapuṇyapuṣpādhivāsena bhṛśaṃ nananduḥ || k21.86 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ jetavanapratigrahāvadānaṃ nāma ekaviṃśaṃ pallavaḥ ||


22. pitāputrasamādānam |

aho mahārhaṃ maṇivanmahattvaṃ bhavyā bhajante guṇagauraveṇa |
vinā guṇaṃ yadvapuṣāṃ gurutvaṃ sthūlopalānāmiva niṣphalaṃ tat || k22.1 ||
śrīmān purā śākyapure narendraḥ śuddhodanaḥ śuddhisudhānidhānam |
vairāgyayogātsugatatvamāptaṃ smṛtvā sutaṃ sutsukatāmavāpam || k22.2 ||
so'cintayatpuṇyaguṇādhivāsaṃ sarasvatīvāsasaroruhaśri |
manaḥ prasādasya vilāsasaudhaṃ drakṣāmi sūnorvadanaṃ kadāham || k22.3 ||
ehīti taddarśanalālasena ye ye mayā jetavanaṃ visṛṣṭāḥ |
te te tadālokananirnimeṣaṃ tatraiva tiṣṭhantyamṛtaṃ pibantaḥ || k22.4 ||
saṃpreṣitastasya mayā sa nāyayau yo'pyātmatulyaṃ praṇayādudāyī |
sa lekhahastastridivābhirāme tatraiva lekhatvamirābhiyātaḥ || k22.5 ||
saṃdeśavākyaṃ prahitaṃ mayā yattadvismṛtaṃ tasya mukhena nūnam |
sarvo hi nāma svahitābhilāṣī dhatte parārthe kila śītalatvam || k22.6 ||
vilokanenaitya niṣiñca tūrṇaṃ pīyūṣapūreṇa mamāṅgasaṅgam |
niḥsaṅgato viśramatāṃ muhūrtaṃ dayāvidheyaḥ kuru bandhukāryam || k22.7 ||
ityetadākarṇya kathāṃ sa kuryātkṣaṇaṃ vilambaṃ mama darśane'pi |
taccetasaḥ pallavapeśalasya na hi svabhāvaḥ praṇayāvabhaṅgaḥ || k22.8 ||
manoratheneti puraḥ prayāte taddarśanāyeva dharādhināthe |
pravrajyā vyañjitatatprasādaḥ samāyayau harṣarasādudāyī || k22.9 ||
dṛṣṭvā tamānandavipūrṇamānasaṃ pravrajyayā tatsadṛśānubhāvam |
utkaṇṭhitaḥ kuṇṭhitadhairyavṛttiḥ saṃmohamūrcchāṃ nṛpatiḥ prapede || k22.10 ||
sa labdhasaṃjñaḥ śiśiraiḥ payobhiḥ papraccha taṃ kiṃ nu sameṣyatīti |
so'pyabravīddeva dinairbhavantamasaṃbhṛtaiḥ sādarameṣyatīti || k22.11 ||
tataḥ prayāteṣu dineṣu keṣu vyomnā śanairbhikṣugaṇānuyātaḥ |
sahāyayau nākasadāṃ nikāyaiḥ sarvārthasiddhairbhagavān kumāraḥ || k22.12 ||
dyusundarīpāṇisarojamuktamandāramālākalitaścakāśe |
svargīyagaṅgāsphuṭaphenakūṭavilāsahāsāṅga ivāmarādriḥ || k22.13 ||
saṃghaṭṭabhinnābhrasakhaiḥ skhalidbhiḥ saśabdajāmbūnadakiṅkiṇīkaiḥ |
babhurvimānaiḥ kakubhāṃ mukhāni bhaktyeva śāsturvihitastavāni || k22.14 ||
nirantarairantarivārkatāraiḥ suraiḥ savidyādharasiddhasaṃghaiḥ |
paryāptasaṃsaktasitātapatrairvyāptaḥ samāptiṃ gaganaṃ jagāma || k22.15 ||
taṃ sarvalokopakṛtiprapannaṃ sarvākṛtiṃ sarvamayāvabhāsam |
samāpatantaṃ nabhaso'tha digbhyaḥ kṣiteśca sarve dadṛśuḥ kṣaṇena || k22.16 ||
praharṣarāśiṃ janalocanānāṃ puṇyapramāṇaṃ sukṛtotsavānām |
lokastamālokanidhiṃ vilokya samullalannucchalitādbhutormiḥ || k22.17 ||
āścaryabhūtaṃ ruciraprabhāvamudāyinā sūcitamākalayya |
jagadguruṃ bhūmipatiḥ kumāraṃ kṛtāñjalistaṃ praṇanāma dūrāt || k22.18 ||
athāvatīryāryajanānuyātaḥ saṃpūjyamānaḥ praṇayena rājñā |
sphīṭaprabhābhāsitadigvibhāgāṃ nyagrodhinīṃ ratnabhuvaṃ viveśa || k22.19 ||
hemāsanaṃ śāsanasaṃniviṣṭalokatrayaḥ saṃgatapādapīṭham |
sa tatra ratnāṅkuracitrapatraṃ bhāsvadvapurmerun || k22.20 ||
tanmānasendornayanāmṝtaughaṃ manorathaprārthanayopayātam |
vilokayannirvṛtinirnimeṣaḥ kṣaṇaṃ kṣitīśastridaśatvamāpa || k22.21 ||
sa taṃ jagādāśruniruddhakaṇṭhaḥ sotkarṣaharṣākulitaṃ kumāram |
hārāgraratnapratibimbasaktaṃ praveśayan prītirasādivāntaḥ || k22.22 ||
saṃtoṣaśītācalavatsvabhāvātsarve ramante kuśalasthalīṣu |
kṛtastvayāsmākamayaṃ tu kasmātsatsūpakārī virahopadeśaḥ || k22.23 ||
snehātpramodādguṇagauravācca dhīrdhāvatīyaṃ tvayi me prasahya |
āliṅganāya sthirasaṃgamāya pādapraṇāmāya ca tulyameva || k22.24 ||
yadvastu kiṃcidgaditaṃ mayā tatśrotavyameva praṇayoparodhāt |
guṇojjhitaṃ vā virasakramaṃ vā na snehamohasya bhavatyavācyam || k22.25 ||
pratyarṇaratnapratibimbitārkaprauḍhaprabhāprāvaraṇānyamūni |
tvaṃ hemaharmyāṇi vihāya kasmātvigāhase śūnyavanāntarāṇi || k22.26 ||
kāntākarāvarjitahemakumbhasatsaurabhāmbhaḥpravarābhiṣiktaḥ |
ekaḥ kathaṃ snāsi vikāsipāṃśusaṃtaptatoyāsu marusthalīṣu || k22.27 ||
gaṇḍasthalātkuṇḍalaratnakānti kiṃ lambitaṃ maṇḍanameva vetsi |
kasmādakasmāttava niḥsukhasya na candanaṃ nandanaminduśubhram || k22.28 ||
mahāvitāne śayane nṛpārhe śeṣe na kiṃ śeṣaviśeṣaśubhre |
lakṣmīnavāliṅganabhogayogyā kathaṃ tanuste sahate kuśayyām || k22.29 ||
kāntāsmitormipratimāṃśukārhaṃ kiṃ cīvarasyocitametadaṅgam |
pāṇau ca līlākamalāspade'smin pātraṃ kathaṃ te priyamadya jātam || k22.30 ||
ayaṃ vihārastava kaṇṭhapīṭhaḥ sotkaṇṭhakāntābhujabandhanārhaḥ |
saṃbhogalakṣmīkṣapitapramodaḥ karotyakasmātpraṇayāvabhaṅgam || k22.31 ||
rūpaṃ vilakṣīkṛtapuṣpacāpaṃ mattebhakumbhoccakucā vibhūtiḥ |
ratervilāsppavanaṃ vayaśca kenāsamaste kalito virāgaḥ || k22.32 ||
śratveti taṃ śīlanidhirbadhāṣe śaśāṅkalekhālalitasmitena |
saṃkrāntanānānṛparatnarāgāṃ kurvannalakṣāmiva rājalakṣmīm || k22.33 ||
rājan jarārogahateva na syāttaraṅgalolā yadi jīvavṛttiḥ |
tatkasya na syādaniśaṃ praharṣapīyūṣavarṣī viṣayābhilāṣaḥ || k22.34 ||
śamāmṛtāsvādanasusthirāṇāmapātanaṃ śūnyavanāntabhūmeḥ |
vibhūtilīlāmadavihvalānāṃ harmyāṇi paryantanipātanāni || k22.35 ||
sakuṅkumaiḥ snāntiḥ nṛpāḥ payobhiḥ sarāgatāṃ yaiḥ satataṃ prayānti |
saṃtoṣaśīlastu manaḥ prasādaśuddhāmbudhautā vimalībhavanti || k22.36 ||
śrotraṃ śrutenaivana kuṇḍalena dānena pāṇirna tu kaṅkaṇena |
vibhāti kāyaḥ karuṇākulānāṃ paropakāreṇa na candanena || k22.37 ||
etāni mohāhatavallabhāni saṃsaktamuktāṃśusitasmitāni |
satāṃ na bhogyāni bhavanti bhūbhṛducchiṣṭaśiṣṭāni vibhūṣaṇāni || k22.38 ||
rāgāturāṇāṃ ritupāpitānāṃ nidrā dhanadhyānavatāṃ na nāma |
śayyāsu susparśavatīṣu rājñā sarvatra śāntaḥ sukhameva śete || k22.39 ||
nirmokakāntena varāmśukena bhujaṅgavṛttirna tu cīvareṇa |
pātraṃ pavitrāṇi samāplitāni pīyūṣamaitrāṇyaśanāni sūte || k22.40 ||
chatrāṇi vakrkraṃ bhṛśamaprakāśaṃ manivilolaṃ vyajanānilaughāḥ |
saṃsaktajāṅyaṃ hṛdayaṃ nṛpāṇāṃ kurvanti hārā haricandanārdrāḥ || k22.41 ||
viyogarogānugatā vibhūtiḥ kāntāhkṣaṇāntā viraso vilāsaḥ |
yasminnapāyaḥ satatānuśāyī sa kasya bhogaḥ subhagopayogaḥ || k22.42 ||
jāḍyaṃ sajṛmbhaṃ janayatyajasraṃ tanoti tṛṣṇābhramamohamūrcchāḥ
karotyasahyaṃ sarasatvameva bhogopabhogaḥ prasabhaprayogaḥ || k22.43 ||
yadā sukhaśrīrnavacandralekhā prabhātapuṣpāṇyapi yaivanāṇi |
karmormimālāgrahaṇaṃ śarītaṃ tadā mamāyaṃ gamito'nurāgaḥ || k22.44 ||
sacāmarāhsadhvajapuñjapaṭṭāḥ savājivālā dvipakarṇatālāḥ |
svabhāvalolāḥ kila rājalakṣmyaḥ sarve vilāsāhkṣaṇabhaṅgasaṅgāḥ || k22.45 ||
uktveti tattatkuśalāya rājñaścittaprasādaṃ paramaṃ vidhāya |
sa śāntikallolasudhāpravāhaṃ kiran dṛśā pārṣadamāluloke || k22.46 ||
manīṣiṇāṃ śākyakulodgatānāṃ saptāyutāni pratipādya dharmam |
cakre sahasrāṇi ca sapta tatra saṃprāptaparyāptaviśeṣabhāñji || k22.47 ||
śaklodanādyaiḥ kuśalopapannaiḥ gaṇe'tha tattulyasahasrasaṃkhyaiḥ |
droṇodanādyairamṛtpdanādyaiścittaprasādaḥ sumahānavāptaḥ || k22.48 ||
kecidyayuḥ śrāvakabodhiyuktapratyekabodhau niratāśca kecit |
samyaktathānuttarabodhisaktāḥ pare babhūvurgaganaprasannāḥ || k22.49 ||
srotaḥ pariprāptaphalaṃ tato'nye sakṛttathāgāmiphalaṃ tathānye |
anye'pyanāgāṃiphalaṃ tadāpurarhatphalaṃ kleśavimuktimanye || k22.50 ||
ekastu tatrārjitapāpaśāpastamaḥsamūhopahitapramohaḥ |
māyeyamityāha hasan janānāṃ satyasthitiṃ saṃsadi devadattaḥ || k22.51 ||
nṛpaṃ tu vātsalyanilīnameva putrodayātpratyupajātadarpam |
maudgalyabhikṣurjinaśāsanena maharddhibhirvītamadaṃ cakāram || k22.52 ||
dṛṣṭvāpi rājā bhagavatprabhāvaṃ nātyadbhutaṃ pauruṣameva mene |
abhyāsalīnāni janasya nūnaṃ sotkarṣakṛtyāni na vismayāya || k22.53 ||
athāparedyurbhagavān surendrasaṃpādite hemamahāvimāne |
sumeruśīrrṣṇīva samānakāntau siṃhāsane ratnamaye nyaṣīdat || k22.54 ||
brahmendramukhyeṣu tataḥ sureṣu tatropaviṣṭeṣu pṛthuprabheṣu |
babhustaduṣṇīṣaśikhāvilāsaiścandrāṃśumālājaṭīlā ivāśāḥ || k22.55 ||
anyonyasaṃghaṭṭavilolahārairdhanāvahāraistridaśairviśadbhiḥ |
nirantarāṃ tāṃ bhavametya rājā dvāreṣu mārgānna caturṣu lebhe || k22.56 ||
sabhrūbhramaistatra kuveramukhyairnivāryamāṇābhimatapraveśah |
vicchāyavakraḥ skhalitābhidhāyī bhūbhṛtparaṃ niṣpratibho babhūva || k22.57 ||
praveśitastairjinaśāsanena kadācidāsādya tadagrabhūmim |
śuddhodanastapraṇipatya mūrdhnā cittaprasādena puro'sya tasthau || k22.58 ||
śāstā tu tasmai caturāryasatyaprabodhikāṃ dharmakathāṃ dideśa |
jñānena yā viṃśatiśṛṅgamasya satkāyadṛgbhūdharamapyabḥāṅkṣīt || k22.59 ||
tataḥ sa gatvā kṛtakṛtyajanmā śuklodanaṃ prāpta bhajasva rājyam |
svasyāvratīttaṃ bhagavatpradiṣṭaṃ tacchāsanaṃ modayituṃ na rājyam || k22.60 ||
droṇodane rājyaparāṅbhukhe'pi vairāgyayogādamṛtodane ca |
jagrāha śuddhodanasaṃpradiṣṭāṃ tāṃ rājalakṣmīmatha bhadrakākhyaḥ || k22.61 ||
rājārhabhogairatha pūjayitvā jinaḥ janeśaḥ śucisaṃpraṇitaiḥ |
nyagrodhadhāma pratipādya cāsmai śuddhodanaṃ śuddhamanoratho'bhūt || k22.62 ||
droṇodanasyāpi sutau yuvānau rājājñayā preraṇayā ca mātuḥ |
ekastu yaḥ pravrajito'niruddhaḥ paro mahānnāma gṛhī babhūva || k22.63 ||
athābhavaccetasi bhadraksya rājño viraktasya vanābhilāṣaḥ |
vivekabhājāṃ praśamapravṛttaṃ navāpi lakṣmīrna mano ruṇaddhi || k22.64 ||
tataḥ samāhūya sa devadattaṃ rājyābhiṣekapratipannacittam |
uvāca me pravrajanasya kālaḥ samāgataḥ kiṃ bhavatābhidheyam || k22.65 ||
taṃ pratyuvācāttavivekadambhaḥ susaṃvṛtaṃ saṃsadi devadattaḥ |
rājanna rājye'sti mamābhilāṣaḥ pravrajyayā tvatsadṛśo bhavāmi || k22.66 ||
śrutveti rājā kuṭilasya tasya mithyāvinītasya kadarthavākyam |
udīritaṃ śākyagaṇastavāyaṃ saṃkalpasākṣīti hasannuvāca || k22.67 ||
athārthatāpopahataḥ pradadhyau bhogānurāgāditi devadattaḥ |
mayā kimetadavipātamuktaṃ bhajeta vā pravrajito'pi rājyam || k22.68 ||
rājyaṃ samutsṛjya nijaṃ vrajantaḥ śākyaṃ kumārāḥ saha bhadrakādyāḥ |
śuddhodanaṃ niryayurāyavṛttaprītiṃ puraskṛtya rathairdvipaiśca || k22.69 ||
vrajatsu sarveṣvatha devadattaḥ kirīṭasaktaṃ pṛthipadmarāgam |
jahāra raktāktamivāmiṣārthī śyenaḥ prabhāpallavitāmbarārkam || k22.70 ||
naimittikairuktamathāsya lakṣmadṛṣṭvā tadugraṃ narakaprayāṇam |
cittaṃ sadoṣaṃ kila durnimittaṃ nimittamanyatpunaruktameva || k22.71 ||
kokālikhaṇḍotkaṭamorakāṇāṃ tithyādināmnāṃ madadurmadānām |
saṃsūcitānyatyadhikāni tatra tathāvidhānairbahulakṣaṇāni || k22.72 ||
bhūpapramodādatha bhadrako'pi tairdevadatapramukhaiḥ sahaiva |
pravrajyayā cīvarapātrayogātcakāra vairāgyamayīmiva kṣmām || k22.73 ||
rājñastathā rājakumārakāṇāmutsṛṣṭahārāṅgadakuṇḍalānām |
sāsro virāgādavatārya teṣāṃ keśānupālī kila kalpako'bhūt || k22.74 ||
mūrkhaḥ sa nīco'pi jinājñayaiva pravrajyayā pūjyataro babhūva |
cittaprasādasya parasya manye na kāraṇaṃ paṇḍitatā najātiḥ || k22.75 ||
sāmīcikāyāmatha bhadrako'pi jñātvā nṛpaḥ pārṣadikaṃ tamenam |
nīcasya pādau kathamasya vande mahīpati sanniti niścalo'bhūt || k22.76 ||
tamabravīdaskhalitābhimānaṃ vikalpabhinnaṃ bhagavān vihasya |
pravrajyayā mohamahānuvandhī saṃtyajyate jātimayo'bhimānaḥ || k22.77 ||
śrutveti rājñā saha rājaputraiḥ kṛte praṇāme pṛthivī cakampe |
na devadattaḥ paruṣābhidhāyī padau vavande bhagavadgirāsya || k22.78 ||
kampātkṣitervismitamānasena pṛṣṭastato bhikṣugaṇena śāstā |
uvaca rājā kila kalpasya janmāntare'pyasya kṛtaḥ praṇāmaḥ || k22.79 ||
purā yuvā kāśipure vilokya bhadrābhidhānāṃ gaṇīkāṃ daridraḥ |
sevāṃ vyaghātsundarakastadāsyai rāgo hi sarvavysanopadeṣṭā || k22.80 ||
tayā visṛṣṭaḥ kusumoccayāya punahpunarbhṛṅga ivādhikārthī |
tatsaṅgamānaṅgamanorathena śrāntaḥ sa babhrāma vanāntareṣu || k22.81 ||
atrāntara śrāntataraḥ kṣitīśaḥ prāpto vanāntaṃ mṝgayārasena |
taṃ brahmadattaḥ prasamīkṣya gītaṃ tasyāśṛṇocchannatanurlatābhiḥ || k22.82 ||
navanavakusumāśayā kimevaṃ madhukaraṃ tāpahato'si gaccha tūrṇam |
vikasitakamalānanābjinī sā bhavati hi saṃkucitā dināvasāne || k22.83 ||
tasyā hi gītaṃ nṝpatirniśamya smitaprabhāghaṭṭitahārakāntiḥ |
uvāca taṃ tīvrakarārkatāpaḥ ko'yaṃ sakhe gītarasābhioyogah || k22.84 ||
so'pyabravīdbhūmipate na nāma tapto ravistaptatarastu kāmaḥ |
svakarmaduḥkhāni vihanti loke na grīṣmadagdhāni marusthalāni || k22.85 ||
ityarthavadvākyaguṇārpaṇena sa bhūpatervallabhatāmavāpa |
saṃvādasaṃsparśasubhāṣitaṃ hi keṣāṃ casatkārapadaṃ na yāti || k22.86 ||
tenātha rājā vijane śramāturaḥ śītopacārairapanītatāpaḥ |
prītyā tamādāya tataḥ sahaiva svarājadhānīmagamatkṛtajñah || k22.87 ||
tatrāsya jīvaprada ityudantasaṃtoṣa saṃpūritacittavṛttiḥ |
rājyārdhadānābhimukhaḥ sa tasthau cittānuvṛttasya kimasya deyam || k22.88 ||
rājyārdhadānaprasṛte'tha tasminnācintayatsundarakaḥ kṛpāyām
bhadrāṃ vinā rājyasukhena kiṃ me dhanyo hi tatprītisudhābhiṣiktaḥ || k22.89 ||
mahmaṃ na rājyādyapi rocate'rdhamakhaṇḍitālpāpi hi śobhate śrīḥ |
ekārthayoge hi sadā vivādaḥ dvayorhi bhogaiḥ kalireva mūrtaḥ || k22.90 ||
tasmānnṛpaṃ kuṇṭhamahaṃ nipātya samastarājyena bhavāmi pūrṇaḥ |
kṣaṇaṃ vicintyetyanutāpataptaḥ tīvraṃ manaḥ svasya punaḥ pradadhyau || k22.91 ||
kiṃ cintitaṃ nindyaparaṃ mayaitatko'yaṃ prakāraḥ khalu tīkṣṇatāyāḥ |
kṛtaghnasaṃkalpakalaṅkalepādaho nu lajjā nijacetaso'pi || k22.92 ||
svastyastu rājyāya namaḥ sukhebhyaḥ saṃmohamātā kṣamatāṃ ca lakṣmīḥ |
yeṣāmanāsvāditacintitānāmevaṃvidhā dhīḥ prathamaḥ svabhāvaḥ || k22.93 ||
bhramaṃ vidhatte vidadhāti mūrcchāṃ nipātayatyeva tamastanoti |
āghrātamātraiva karoti puṃsāmaho vināśaṃ viṣavallarī śrīḥ || k22.94 ||
ciraṃ vicintyeti sa jātacittaḥ pratyekabodhirvimalaḥ prabhāte |
abhyarthamāno'pi nareśvareṇa rājyaṃ najagrāha nivṛttatṛṣṇaḥ || k22.95 ||
pratyekabuddhatvamavāptamenaṃ kālena dṛṣṭvā nṛpatirmaharddhiḥ |
tatpādapadmacyutamaulimālyaścittaprasādocitamityavecat || k22.96 ||
sa ko'pi satkarmavipākajanmā vandyo vivekaḥ praśamābhiṣekaḥ |
yasya prabhāvādvirataspṛhāṇāṃ tyājyeva ratnākaramekhalā bhūḥ || k22.97 ||
śrutveti rājñā kathitaṃ tadarthajātaṃ tadabhyarthanayā vidhāya |
tatkalpakaḥ śāntipadaṃ prapede sevāntaraṅgaḥ kila gaṅgapālaḥ || k22.98 ||
prāptaṃ tamayuttamakarmayogātpravrajyayā sajjanapūjyabhāvam |
rājā vavande praṇataḥ pṛthivyāḥ kampastadābhūdapi ṣaḍvikāraḥ || k22.99 ||
so'yaṃ rājā vihitavinatirbhadrako brahmadatto
paśyopālī sa kila kuśalī kalpako gaṅgapālaḥ |
ityāścaryaṃ bhagavaduditaṃ bhikṣavaste niśamya
svacchaṃ cittaṃ sukṝtaśaraṇe menire hetumeva || k22.100 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ pitāputrasamādānaṃ nāma dvāviṃśatitamaḥ pallavaḥ ||


23. viśvaṃtarāvadānam |

cintārantādadhkarucayaḥ sarvalokeṣvanindyā
vandyāste'nyaiḥ puruṣamaṇayaḥ ke'pyapūrvaprabhāvāḥ |
yeṣāṃ naiva priyamapi paraṃ putradārādi datvā
sattvārthānāṃ bhavati vadanamlānatā dainyadūtī || k23.1 ||
bhagavān bhikṣubhiḥ pṛṣṭaḥ purā śākyapure jinaḥ |
jagāda pūrvavṛttāntaṃ devadattakathāntare || k23.2 ||
purī babhūva viśvākhyā viśvāsavasatiḥ śriyaḥ |
viśvopakārasaktasyasukṝtasyeva janmabhūḥ || k23.3 ||
saṃjayākhyo'bhavattasyāmamitratimirāṃśumān |
netrānandasudhāsūtirvicitracarito nṛpaḥ || k23.4 ||
tasya viśvaṃtaro nāma vadānyastanayo'bhavat |
apūrvatyāginā yena hṛtaṃ kalpatayoryaśaḥ || k23.5 ||
īrṣyāvirahitāstulyaṃ vidagdhena prsādhitāḥ |
satyena bhāratī yena dānena śrīḥ śrutena dhīḥ || k23.6 ||
