adyāpi yasya dikkāntākarṇābharaṇatāṃ gatam |
vibhāti ketakīgarbhapalāśaviśadaṃ yaśah || k23.7 ||
sa kadāciddadau divyaratnālakāramarthine |
athaṃ vijayasāmrājyamanorathaharaṃ tviṣā || k23.8 ||
datte rathavare tasmin vismayenākhilo janaḥ |
babhūvākrāṇtahṛdayaścintayā ca nareśvaraḥ || k23.9 ||
mahāmātyānathāhūya harṣahīno mahīpatiḥ |
uvācopacitodvegacintākrāntamanorathah || k23.10 ||
datto rathaḥ kumāreṇa sa jaitraḥ śatrumardanaḥ |
yatprabhāvārjitā seyaṃ mahārathavarūthinī || k23.11 ||
lakṣmī sukhaniṣaṇṇā me yātā niścalatāṃ tayā |
rathe sauryapathe tasmin jayakuśnje ca kuñjare || k23.12 ||
iti rājavacaḥ śrutvā tamabhāṣanta mantriṇaḥ |
rājan doṣastavaivāyaṃ vātsalyena pramādyataḥ || k23.13 ||
dharmaḥ kasya na harṣāya dānaṃ kasya na saṃmatam |
kiṃ tu mūlahatādvṛkṣānnivartante palārthinaḥ || k23.14 ||
vikrītaḥ paradeśe ca rathastena dvijanmanā |
ityuktvā mantriṇaḥ sarve śalyaviddhā ivābhavan || k23.15 ||
atha kālena saṃprāpte vasante madanotsave |
vipāke sukṛtasyeva hṛdayānandadāyini || k23.16 ||
svayaṃgrahopajīvyasya madhormadhukarārthinaḥ |
lokaḥ puṣpavanairyāto yaśobhiriva śubhratām || k23.17 ||
aśokaṃ lokasacchāyamupakārodyataṃ drumam |
madhu vidhūtaṃ saṃnaddhe kalikālaṃ kṛtaṃ jagat || k23.18 ||
rājaputraḥ samāruhya kuñjaraṃ rājavardhanam |
yayau phullān vane druṣṭumarthikalpatarustarūn || k23.19 ||
vrajantaṃ prati sāmantaprayuktātaṃ dvijāḥ pathiḥ |
babhāṣire samabhyetya svastivādapuraḥsarāḥ || k23.20 ||
cintāmaṇirgīyase tvaṃ ślāghyo jagati jaṅgamaḥ |
yasya saṃdarśanenārthī gāḍhamāliṅgyate śriyā || k23.21 ||
dvāveva viśrutotkarṣavideṣau bhadrajanmani |
dānārdrahastastvaṃ loke gajaścāyaṃ sthironnatiḥ || k23.22 ||
asmabhyaṃ sukṛtpdāra kuñjaro'yaṃ pradīyatām |
tvadanyena vadānyena dātumeṣa na śakyate || k23.23 ||
ityarthitastaiḥ sotsāhaḥ sa tebhyastu dadau dvipam |
sajīvamiva sāmrājyaṃ saśaṅkhadhvajacāmaram || k23.24 ||
datvā bodhipradhānena praṇidhānena śuddhadhīḥ |
ratharatnaṃ dvipendraṃ ca so'bhūdānandanirbharaḥ || k23.25 ||
śrutvaiva nṛpatirdattaṃ viśrutaṃ jayakuśnjaram |
rakṣāprakārarahitāṃ rājalakṣmīmamanyata || k23.26 ||
sa rājyabhraṃśabhītena kupitena mahībhujā |
niṣkāsitaḥ kumāro'tha pratasthe praṇipatya tam || k23.27 ||
mādrīdayitayā sārdhaṃ jālinaṃ nāma dārakam |
kṛṣṇābhidhāṃ tathā kanyāmādāya sa yayau vanam || k23.28 ||
vane'pi śeṣa ṃ sa dadau vāhanādikamarthine |
samaṃ hi mahatāṃ sattvaṃ saṃpatsu ca vipatsu ca || k23.29 ||
mādyrāṃ kadācidyātāyāṃ puṣpamūlaphalāptaye |
brāhmaṇaḥkaścidabhyetya rājaputramabhāṣata || k23.30 ||
paricārakāhīnāya caturau bālakāvimau |
dehi mahyaṃ mahāsattvaṃ sarvado hyasi viśrutaḥ || k23.31 ||
śrutvaitadavicāryaiva dārakau dayitau param |
datvā sa tasmai sahasā sehe tadvirahavyathām || k23.32 ||
dhanaputrakalatrādi loke kasya navallabham |
dānādanyadvadānyānāṃ na dayāvatām || k23.33 ||
atha mādrī samabhyetya bālakau bālavatsalā |
apaśyantī puraḥ patyuḥ patitāpannamūrcchitā || k23.34 ||
sā labdhasaṃjñā dīptena vyāptā śokakṛśānunā |
śiśupradānavṛttāntaṃ śrutvaivābhūtpalāpinī || k23.35 ||
tasyāścetasi duḥkhāgnirapatyasnehaduḥsahaḥ |
priyapremānusṛtyaiva prayayau puṭapākatām || k23.36 ||
atrāṇtare samabhyetya viprarūpaḥ sureśvaraḥ |
bhṛtyārthī dayitāṃ patnīṃ rājaputramayācata || k23.37 ||
yācitastena sahasā śucaṃ jāyāviyogajām |
dhiyā saṃstabhya sattvābdhiḥ sa tasmai vitatāra tām || k23.38 ||
sadyaḥ pradānataralāṃ saṃtrastāṃ hariṇīmiva |
so'vadaddayitāmantaḥ kalayan bodhivāsanām || k23.39 ||
samāśvāsihi kalyāṇi na śokaṃ kartukarhasi |
svapnapraṇayakalpo'yamasatyaḥ priyasaṃgamaḥ || k23.40 ||
śuśrūṣayā dvijasyāsya dharme te ramatāṃ matiḥ |
vilolalokayātrāyāṃ dharmaḥ sthirasuhṛtsatām || k23.41 ||
dṛṣṭvā sarve svajanasujanā bāndhavāścānubhūtāḥ
nyastā kaṇṭhe kṣaṇaparimalamlāyinī mitrālā |
dāre putre kṣapitamaniśaṃ yauvanaṃ jīvitaṃ ca
prāpto nāptaśtiraparicayaḥ ko'pi dharmādṛte'nyaḥ || k23.42 ||
ityuktvā vallabhāṃ lobhaparityāgāduvāha saḥ |
dyutiṃ vadanapadmena dhairyavṛttiṃ ca cetasā || k23.43 ||
viyogaśokavikalāṃ mādrīṃ dṛṣṭvā kṛpākulaḥ |
nijarūpaṃ samādhāya śacīpatiruvācatām || k23.44 ||
viṣādaṃ mā kṛthāhputri devo'haṃ tridaśeśvaraḥ |
arthibhyastvā dadātyeṣa tasmādasi mayarthitā || k23.45 ||
adhunā saiva patyustvaṃ nyāsībhūtā mayārpitā |
taṃ dadātyeṣa nānyasmau dīyate katham || k23.46 ||
kariṣyāmi tavāvaśyaṃ dārakābhyāṃ samāgamam |
ityudīrya sahasrākṣaḥ sahasāṇtaradhīyataḥ || k23.47 ||
atha tau dārakau vipraḥ samādāyārthalipsayā |
viśvāmitrapuraṃ gatvā lobhādvokretumudyatah || k23.48 ||
viśyāmitraḥ parijñāya rājaputrasya dārakau |
jagrāha mahatārthena bāṣpasaṃruddhalocanaḥ || k23.49 ||
kālena tridivaṃ yāte viśvāmitramahīpatau |
bheje viśvaṃtaro rājyaṃ paurāmātyagaṇārthitaḥ || k23.50 ||
rājye viraktasya tasya dānavyasaninaḥ param |
sattvena vardhamānarddhirna kaścidyācako'bhavat || k23.51 ||
tadvittapūrṇavibhavo brāhmaṇaḥ so'pi jambukah |
kṛtaghnaḥ svaprabhāvānme saṃpadityabhyadhājjanam || k23.52 ||
viśvaṃtaraḥ sa evāhaṃ devadattaḥ sa ca dvijaḥ |
uktveti cakre bhagavān bhikṣūṇāṃ dānadeśanām || k23.53 ||
ālambanaṃ śvabhraśatāvapāte ghorāndhakāre suciraprakāśaḥ |
āśvāsanaṃ duḥsahaduḥkhakāle dānaṃ narāṇāṃ paralokabandhuḥ || k23.54 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ viśvaṃtarāvadānaṃ nāmatrayoviṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


24. abhiniṣkramaṇāvadānam |

hasati sakalalolālokasargāya bhānuḥ paramamamṛtavṛṣṭayai pūrṇatāmeti candraḥ |
iyati jagati pūjyaṃ janma gṛhṇāti kaścitvipulakuśalasetuḥ sattvasaṃtāraṇāya || k24.1 ||
purā śuddhodanaḥ śrīmān rājā śākyapure'bhavat |
yaśaḥśaśāṅkajanakaḥ sudhāsindhurivāparaḥ || k24.2 ||
khalāsaktā svabhāvena lakṣmīrguṇigaṇārpitā |
āścaryakāriṇā yena kṛtā satpakṣapātinī || k24.3 ||
adyāpyakhiladiktīryavanāsaktairvivekibhiḥ |
yaśobhiḥ śucibhiryasya munivratamivohyate || k24.4 ||
syāmahaṃ śuddhamāteti purā praṇidhitaḥ kila |
viśvakarmasuto martyamājagāmāmaladyutiḥ || k24.5 ||
kīrtiḥ satpuruṣasyeva tasyābhūdvallabhā param |
mahāmāyābhidhā devī candrasyeva kumudvatī || k24.6 ||
sāpaśyaddantinaṃ śvetaṃ svapne kukṣau vihāyasā |
gatamārohaṇaṃ śaile praṇatiṃ ca mahājanāt || k24.7 ||
atrāntare bodhisattvastuṣitāttridaśālayāt |
garbhaṃ tasyāḥ samāpede svayaṃ lokānukalpayā || k24.8 ||
vahantī bodhisattvaṃ sā garbhe bhuvananandanam |
induṃ dugdhābdhiveleva babhūva pāṇḍuradyutiḥ || k24.9 ||
ikṣvākurājavaṃśyena tena garbhasthitena sā |
bhūrnidhānavatīvābhūdbhavyalakṣaṇalakṣitā || k24.10 ||
babhūva dohadastasyā dānapuṇyamayodayaḥ |
aṅkure'pyavisaṃvādi sahakārasya saurabham || k24.11 ||
atgha kālena saṃpūrṇaṃ sā lumbinīvane sthitā |
asūta tanayaṃ devī divākaramivāditiḥ || k24.12 ||
māturgarbhamalāspṛṣṭaṃ kukṣiṃ bhittvā sa nirgataḥ |
tāṃ cakre'tha svabhāvena svasthāṅgī vigatavyathām || k24.13 ||
nirgacchanneva ruddho'sau balajijñāsayā kṣaṇam |
śakreṇa vajrasārāṅgastasyāśakyatvamāyayau || k24.14 ||
jātamātaḥ śuiśurgatvā svayaṃ sapta padāni saḥ |
diśo vilokayannūce suvyaktākṣarayā girā || k24.15 ||
iyaṃ nirvṛtiḥ pūrvā ca gatirlokeṣu dakṣiṇā |
paścimā jātirapyeṣā saṃsārādiyamuttarā || k24.16 ||
iti tasya bruvāṇasya pṛthivī samakampata |
tamakṣayabalaṃ dhartumaśakteva jagadgurum || k24.17 ||
chatraṃ tasya yaśaḥ śubhraṃ sattvasmeraṃ sacāmaram |
vyomāmbudhārādhautasya jagṛhustasya devatā || k24.18 ||
asminnavasare pṛṣṭaḥ svastrīyeṇāsitābhidhaḥ |
nāradena prabhāṃ dṛṣṭvā kiṣkindhādristhito muniḥ || k24.19 ||
kasmādarkaśatāloka ivālokaḥ pradṛśyate |
tamodaridrāṃ yeneti girayaḥ saguhāgṛhāḥ || k24.20 ||
vismayāditi tenoktaḥ so'vadaddivyalocanaḥ |
jātaḥ puṇyaprabhāso'yaṃ bodhisattvasya janmani || k24.21 ||
acireṇaiva taṃ vatsa drakṣāvaḥ kuśalāptaye |
ityuktvā munirānandādviśrāntisukhito'bhavat || k24.22 ||
putrajanmani sarvārthasiddhiṃ śuddhodanaḥ param |
dṛṣṭvā sarvārthasiddho'yamiti nāmāsya nirmame || k24.23 ||
śākyavardhananāmābhūdyakṣaḥ śākyapurāśrayaḥ |
yatpraṇāmeṇa śākyānāṃ śiśavo nirupadravāḥ || k24.24 ||
tatsthityā preṣitaḥ pitrā praṇāmāya saguhyakaḥ |
taṃ bodhisattvamālokya nipapātāsya pādayoḥ || k24.25 ||
athotsaṅge samādāya hṛṣṭastaṃ pṛthivīpatiḥ |
lakṣaṇāni nimittajñaistasya dehe vyalokayat || k24.26 ||
lakṣaṇajñāstataḥ sarve nṛpamūcuḥ savismayāḥ |
deva divyakumāro'yaṃ lakṣaṇairupalakṣyate || k24.27 ||
jāyate lakṣaṇairetairviśvaviśrāntaśāsanaḥ |
śakrādhipaścakravartī bhagavān sa tathāgataḥ || k24.28 ||
dīrghāṅgulidalau cakralāñchanau supratiṣṭhitau |
aruṇau caraṇāvasya kāntau kamalakomalau || k24.29 ||
rājahaṃsa iva prāṃśuḥ sajālāṅgulipallavaḥ |
eṣa jānuyugaḥ śrīmānājānubhujabhūṣitaḥ || k24.30 ||
sakośabastiguhyaśca nyagrodhaparimaṇḍalaḥ |
dakṣiṇāvartaromāṅkaḥ pariṇāhasamonnatiḥ || k24.31 ||
rajomalalavāspṛṣṭastajāmbūnadadyutiḥ |
hastapādāṃsakaṇṭhāgraspaṣṭasaptacchadākṛtiḥ || k24.32 ||
siṃhapūrvārdhakāyaśca bṛhatspaṣṭāṅgavigrahaḥ |
catvāriṃśatsamāprotaśukladantaḥ sunāsikaḥ || k24.33 ||
dīrghapratanujihvaśca meghadundubhiniśvanaḥ |
abhinīlākṣagokṣmaḥ sahajoṣṇīṣamastakaḥ || k24.34 ||
sitorṇāṅko bhruvorbhāgaḥ svastikoraḥsthalojjvalaḥ |
lekhāśṛṅgābjahasto'yaṃ chatrākāraśirāḥ śiśuḥ || k24.35 ||
rājannayaṃ te tanayaścakravartī bhaviṣyati |
samyaksaṃbodhisaṃbuddhaḥ sarvajño vā bhaviṣyati || k24.36 ||
ityuktvā teṣu jāteṣu lebhe harṣaṃ mahīpatiḥ |
saptabhidivasaiḥ śāsturjananī tridivaṃ yayau || k24.37 ||
tasya janmani śākyānāṃ munīnāmiva śāntatā |
dṛṣṭvā yadā śākyamunirnāmābhūtsa tadā śiśuḥ || k24.38 ||
devānāmapi devo'yamiti niścitya tejasā |
devātideva ityasya nāma cakre mahīpatiḥ || k24.39 ||
nāradenātha sahitastattvadarśī tapovanāt |
taṃ samabhyāyayau draṣṭumādarādasito muniḥ || k24.40 ||
sa bodhisattvaṃ bālārkamiva kalpaprakāśanam |
dṛṣṭvā vikāsivakrkraśrīrlebhe kamalatulyatām || k24.41 ||
so'brāvīdvihitātithyaṃ nṛpatiṃ praṇataṃ muniḥ |
rājan guṇagaṇeneva spṛhaṇīyo'si sūnunā || k24.42 ||
etāni lakṣaṇānyasya mokṣalakṣmīsamāgamam |
vadanti cakravartiśrīḥphalaṃ naiṣāṃ vinaśvaram || k24.43 ||
asya bodhiprabhāveṇa saṃbuddhasymukhāmbujam |
dhanya padmākarasyeva netrapātraṃ kariṣyati || k24.44 ||
vibudhāḥ śuddhasattvasya bodhidugdhamahodadheḥ |
dhanyā vāgamṛtairasya bhaviṣyantyupajīvinaḥ || k24.45 ||
puṇyabhāji jagatyasminneka evāsmi vañcitaḥ |
etatsaṃdarśanaṃ yasya pūrṇakālasya durlabham || k24.46 ||
ityuktvā nṛpamāmantrya gatvā vyomnā tapovanam |
suprasannaṃ manaḥ kṛtvā dehatyāgamacintayat || k24.47 ||
sa paryantopadeśāya nāradenābhicoditaḥ |
tamuvācāmṛtaṃ vatsaṃ kumāraḥ kathayiṣyati || k24.48 ||
adhigamyāmṛtaṃ tasmānnṛpasūnoranāmayam |
tariṣyasi bhavāmbodhimityuktvā tanumatyajat || k24.49 ||
śrīrasatkriyāṃ tasya kṛtvā sapadi nāradaḥ |
yayau vārāṇasīṃ siddhyai lebhe kātyāyanābhidhām || k24.50 ||
vardhamānaḥ kumāro'tha sarvavidyāsu pāragaḥ |
lipipravīṇo'bhinavāṃ lipiṃ brāhmīṃ vinirmame || k24.51 ||
nāgāyutasamaprāṇe tasmin jagati viśrute |
vaiśālikaiḥ priyāyāsmai preṣito mattakuñjaraḥ || k24.52 ||
bhaviṣyati cakravartītyasya prātamupāyanam |
devadattaḥ paridveṣāttaṃ jaghāna mahāgajam || k24.53 ||
cyutaṃ cakarṣaṃ dvidataṃ nandaḥ sapta padāni tam |
kumārastu tadutkṣipya prākārādbahirakṣipat || k24.54 ||
ekanārāvanirbhinnasaptatālamahītalaḥ |
chedyabhedyāstraśastreṣu sa evātiśayaṃ yayau || k24.55 ||
tatatulyaguṇāṃ patnīṃ rājasūnuryaśodharām |
avāpa viśrutāṃ loke śuddhaśīla ivonnatim || k24.56 ||
atrāntare mahāvātavicyutaḥ saptayojanaḥ |
saritpravāhasaṃrodhaṃ vidadhe vipuladrumaḥ || k24.57 ||
sāṃ sphāratarusaṃruddhā rohikā nāma nimnagā |
śīlabhraṣṭeva vanitā prayayau pratilomatām || k24.58 ||
rājaputrastamutkṣipya bhaṅktvā vikṣupya taṃ drumam |
nyavārayatprajāmatsyajalakallolaviplavam || k24.59 ||
tataḥ kadācidudyāne haṃsaṃ niśitayantriṇā |
devadattena nihataṃ kumāraḥ samajīvayat || k24.60 ||
saṃtāpaṃ tadvivādena devadatto'dhikaṃ yayau |
na sahante hi kuṭilāstulyakulagoṇonnatim || k24.61 ||
kadācidgopikā nāma kumāraṃ rājakanyakā |
kandarparūpamālokya kimapyautsukyamāyayau || k24.62 ||
śuddhodanaḥ sutasthaitāṃ jñātvā cittocitāṃ vadhūm |
apūrayadvivāhena manmathasya manoratham || k24.63 ||
naimittikāstato'bhyetya nṛpamūcuḥ suniścayāḥ |
putraste cakravartī vā munirvā saptame'hani || k24.64 ||
tacchrutvā nṛpatiḥ sūnoścakrāvrtipadāptaye |
pravrajyācakitaścintāṃ dinasaṃkhyāmayīṃ yayau || k24.65 ||
lolāṃ sarvaḥ srhiyaṃ vetti śāntasthirasukhāmapi |
atthāpi bhogaraktānāṃ saṃpatsvevādaraḥ param || k24.66 ||
tataḥ kadācidudyānavihārāya nṛpātmajaḥ |
prayayau rathamāruhya valgutuṅgaturaṅgamam || k24.67 ||
sa vivarṇaṃ jarājīrṇaṃ kīrṇaśīrṇaśiroruham |
suśuṣkaparuṣākāraṃ dadarśa puruṣaṃ pathi || k24.68 ||
sa taṃ dṛṣṭvā nijaṃ kāyamālokyācintayacciram |
aho paryāptapāko'yaṃ kāyasyāsya jugupsitaḥ || k24.69 ||
vayaḥ paryāptamāpto'pi paryāptaṃ nāśrayatyayam |
atīva palitavyājājjarā vṛddhaṃ hasatyasau || k24.70 ||
śarīre saṃtatasnāyupāśaprotāsthipañjare |
vṛddhaḥ puṣṇāti manye'hamaho mohavihaṅgamam || k24.71 ||
sārathe kiṃ karotyeṣa kiṃ na yāti tapovanam |
asṃkocameti vṛddhasya dehena saha sā matiḥ || k24.72 ||
vṛddho'valambate yaṣṭiṃ na tu dharmamayīṃ dhiyam |
jarākuṭilakāyasya svabhāvo nirvivekatā || k24.73 ||
jugupsāṃ janayatyeṣa vṛddhaḥ praskhalitākṣaraiḥ |
vacobhiścyutadantābhairgalallālālavākulaiḥ || k24.74 ||
naṣṭā dṛṣṭiḥ kṛśaḥ kāyaḥ śaktirluptā hatā śrutiḥ |
tathāpi mohāddṛṣṭaiva vṛddhasya taruṇīpriyā || k24.75 ||
dhatte dhavalatāṃ vṛddhaḥ kimetāmatigarhitām |
lolā paraṃ viraktāpi yadyasyātipriyā tanuḥ || k24.76 ||
iti cintayatastasya nirvedaḥ samajāyata |
manyamānasya sāpāyaṃ kāyaṃ nicayamāpadām || k24.77 ||
punaśca samaye'nyasminnapaśyadvyādhitaṃ naram |
vipakkakūṇapaprāyaṃ sapūyamiva pāṇḍaram || k24.78 ||
sa pradadhyau tamālokya samuddiśya nijāṃ tanum |
aho nu sahajaivāsmin kāye rogagaṇodgatiḥ || k24.79 ||
idameva mahaccitramiyaṃ māṃsamayī tanuḥ |
na yāti kledavaiklavyaṃ kṣaṇaṃ paryuṣīte'pi yat || k24.80 ||
iti dhyātvā sa sodvegaḥ śarīravicikitsayā |
babhūva rājyasaṃbhopgarāge vigatitādaraḥ || k24.81 ||
athānyasmin kṣaṇe mālyavastrācchāditavigraham |
dadarśa dehasatkāravyagrabandhujanaṃ śavam || k24.82 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā sahasodvegadayāduḥkhaghṛṇākulaḥ |
ciraṃ niḥsārasaṃsāraparihāramacintayat || k24.83 ||
eṣa pretavanaṃ yāti saṃsaktāṃ hṛdaye vahan || k24.84 ||
aho nu viṣayābhyāsavilāsādhyavasāyinām |
nṛṇāmantyakṣaṇe kaṣṭā kāṣṭhapāṣāṇatulyatā || k24.85 ||
udvegavāribhavasāgarabudbude'smin kālānilākulitakarmalatāgrapuṣpe |
māyāvadhūnayanavibhramasaṃvibhāge puṃsāṃ ka eṣa vapuṣi sthiratābhimānaḥ || k24.86 ||
noktaṃ kiṃcitparahitayutaṃ na śrutaṃ dharmayuktaṃ
naiva ghrātaṃ kuśalakusumaṃ satyarūpaṃ na dṛṣṭam |
naiva spṛṣṭaṃ śamapadamiti vyaktamāsaktacintā-
viśrānto'yaṃ vahati sahasā niścalatvaṃ gatāyuḥ || k24.87 ||
rājasūnurvicintyeti śarīraṃ vipadāplutam |
aśeṣaviṣayāsaṅge parāṃ naḥ snehatāṃ yayau || k24.88 ||
athāgre nirmitaṃ devaiḥ sa śuddhāvāsakāyikaiḥ |
vyalokayatpravrajitaṃ pātrakāṣāyadhāriṇam || k24.89 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvaiva babhūvāsya pravrajyābhimukhī matiḥ |
īpsitālokanaprītyā svabhāvo hi vibhāvyate || k24.90 ||
virāgyakāraṇaṃ tatra nṛpasūnoḥ pade pade |
vilokya sārathiḥ sarvaṃ kṣitipāya vyanedayat || k24.91 ||
kumāro'tha piturvākyātgrāmālokanakautukī |
vrajan pathi nidhānāni vivṛtāni vyalokayat || k24.92 ||
tatpūrvapuruṣanyastaiḥ strīkarairutthitānyapi |
yadā sa nāgrahīttāni tadā viviśurambudhim || k24.93 ||
tataḥ sa karṣakān pāṃśuvyāptapāṇḍuśiroruhān |
vidīrṇapāṇicaraṇān kṣutpipāsāśramāturān || k24.94 ||
halakaddālaviṣamollekhapakṣavraṇāditān |
vilokya kleśavivaśān babhūva karuṇākulaḥ || k24.95 ||
vidhāya dayayā teṣāṃ draviṇauradaridratām |
sa vṛṣāṇāṃ vṛṣarataḥ kleśamuktimakārayat || k24.96 ||
tataḥ pratinivṛtto'tha sānujaḥ pārthivātmajaḥ |
madhyāhnapṛthusaṃtāpe taralastaraṇitviṣaḥ || k24.97 ||
rathaghoṣonmukhaśikhiśyāmīkṛtadigantaraḥ |
svedavārikaṇākīrṇaḥ prabhāsnigdhavanasthalīm || k24.98 ||
so'varuhya rathāttatra gaṇḍaskhalitakuṇḍalah |
viśrāṇtyai viśrutayaśā jambucchāyāmaśiśriyat || k24.99 ||
sa babhārorasi vyaktāṃ svedāmbukaṇasaṃtatim |
vapurāśleṣalalitāṃ hārasyeva kuṭumbinīm || k24.100 ||
chāyāsu parivṛttāsu śanakaiḥ sarvaśākhinām |
tasya jambūtarucchāyā na cacāla tanustanoḥ || k24.101 ||
sā tasya śītalacchāyā tāpaklāntimavārayat |
saṃsāraviratasyeva tīvravairāgyavāsanā || k24.102 ||
putradarśanasotkaṇṭhastaṃ deśamatha bhūpatiḥ |
ājagāma gajotsarpatrastabhramaracāmaraḥ || k24.103 ||
chāyāṃ sa niścalāṃ dṛṣṭvā kumārasya prabhāvataḥ |
gauravādbhutasaṃprāptaḥ praṇataṃ praṇanāma tam || k24.104 ||
tataḥ sa sahitaḥ pitrā nagaraṃ gantumudyataḥ |
apaśyatpuraparyante śmaśānaṃ śavasaṃkulam || k24.105 ||
sa dṛṣṭvā kuṇapākīrṇamaśivaṃ śivakānanam |
sodvegaṃ sārathiṃ prāha sthagitasyandanaḥ kṣaṇam || k24.106 ||
sārathe paśya jantūnāṃ kāyāpāyamatīṃ daśām |
dṛṣṭvedamapi rāgārdraṃ mano mohapramādinām || k24.107 ||
parastrīdarśanāttṛptaṃ netramāsvādya sādaram |
asyāsatyavatī jihvā paśya kākena kṛsyate || k24.108 ||
asyāḥ stanamukhanyastanakhollekhasukhasthitiḥ |
khṇḍayatyadharaṃ gṝdhraḥ kāmīva madanirbharah || k24.109 ||
ete dṛṣṭaniṣakṭavāyasaśakṛnniṣṭhivinaḥ pādapā
mūrcchantīva vipākapūyakuṇapāghrāṇena niṣkūṇitāḥ |
dṛṣṭvā gṛdhravidāryamāṇamasakṛtkīrṇārdratantra śavaṃ
bhūyo vātavilolapallavakarairācchādayantīva ca || k24.110 ||
kṣībasyevācalasya drutahṛtahṛdayā jambukī kaṇṭhasaktā
raktābhivyaktakāmā kamapi nakhamukhollekhamāsutrayantī |
āsvādyāsvādya yūnaḥ kṣaṇamadharadalaṃ dattadantavraṇāṅkaṃ lagnānaṅgakriyāyāmiyamatirabhasotkarṣamāviṣkaroti || k24.111 ||
ityuktvā jātaviratirbhavabībhatsakutsayā |
kalayan kleśanirvāṇaṃ praviveśa purāntaram || k24.112 ||
tatra harmyagatāpaśyattaṃ kanyābhijanojjvalā |
mṛgajā nāma mṛgajānodinī mṛgalocanā || k24.113 ||
sarāgataralā dṛṣṭḥ śrotrasaṃcāriṇī param |
abhūttaddarśane tasyāḥ sahasaiva virekiṇī || k24.114 ||
sā tadālokanenaiva bālā lajjāsahiṣṇūnā |
smareṇeva samākṛṣṭā sakhīṃ prāha puraḥsthitām || k24.115 ||
kā dhanyā lalanā loke sparśenāsya śaśitviṣaḥ |
yasyā madanasaṃtaptā tanurnirvāṇameṣyati || k24.116 ||
nirvāṇaśabdaṃ śrutvaiva rājaputraḥ samīhitam |
tāṃ dadarśonmukhaḥ padmavanānīva diśan dṛśā || k24.117 ||
sa tasyāstena vacasā vapuṣā va prasāditaḥ |
hāraṃ suvṛttaṃ cittaṃ ca vikṣepāsyai guṇojjcalam || k24.118 ||
ālokanānukūlyena bhāvaṃ vijñāya bhūpatiḥ |
putrasyāntaḥpurapade tāmādāya nyaveśayat || k24.119 ||
ṣaṇṇāṃ kāṇtāsahasrāṇāṃ vṛtamantaḥpuraṃ tataḥ |
viveśa rājatanayaḥ priyāṃ śāntiṃ vicintayan || k24.120 ||
atrāṇtare narapatiṃ prāhurnaimittikāḥ sphuṭam |
munirvā cakravartī vā prātaste bahvitā sutaḥ || k24.121 ||
tataḥ saṃcintya nṛpatiḥ pravrajyāṃ cakitaḥ param |
akārayatpuradvāraguptiṃ ruddhagamāgamām || k24.122 ||
droṇodanamukhān bhrātṝn dvāreṣu viniveśya saḥ |
nagarasya svayaṃ makhye tathā sāmātyasainikaḥ || k24.123 ||
rājaputrādatha prāptagarbhā devī yaśodharā |
vabhāṣe śāradīva dyauḥ pratyāsannendupāṇḍurā || k24.124 ||
ekarātrāvaśeṣe'tha nagaradvārarakṣaṇe |
śamapravṛttārkamabhūtpravrajyābhimukhaṃ dinam || k24.125 ||
ciraṃ vicarya saṃsāraṃ śāntiṃ yāte divaspatau |
kāṣāyāmbaramālambya yayau saṃdhyāvadṛśyatām || k24.126 ||
aśeṣāśātamomohavirāmavimalāṃ śanaiḥ |
indurgāmuditaścakre pūrṇālokavilokinīm || k24.127 ||
sarāgatāpe vabhasaścetasīva gate ravau |
śuddhenduhṛdayasyābhūtprasādaḥ ko'pyaviplavaḥ || k24.128 ||
athāsminnantare kāntāsaṃtatāntaḥpurodare |
ratnaharmyapraviṣṭendudyutisaṃdohahāsini || k24.129 ||
niḥsāravirasaṃ sarvaṃ rājasūnurvilokayan |
jagāa gaganasvacchasvacchandocchalitasmṛtiḥ || k24.130 ||
gaṇo'yaṃ nārīṇāṃ madanadahanolkāparikaraḥ
parityājyastīvravyasanaśatasaṃtāpasacivaḥ |
idānīṃ yuktā me tarutalalatāśītalare
parityaktāgārapraśamasukhasāre pariṇatiḥ || k24.131 ||
etāścandradyutimadamayā yāmi nāryo vane'smin
nidrāmudrāniyamitadṛśaḥ saṃstarasrastavatrāḥ |
svapnotpannānucitavacanāḥ keśasaṃchāditāṃsāḥ
kṣipraṃ mandānilavicalitān lajjayantīva dīpān || k24.132 ||
saralasrastagātrāṇāṃ nirlajjānāṃ vivāsasām |
suptānāṃ ca mṛtānāṃ ca bhedaḥ ko nāma dehinām || k24.133 ||
iti tasya bruvāṇasya saṃjāte gamanodyame |
mithaḥ kathā samabhavannagaradvārarakṣiṇām || k24.134 ||
bho bhoḥ kaḥ ko'tra jāgarti jāgrato nāsti viplavaḥ |
prabhucittagrahavyagrāḥ samagrā eva jāgrati || k24.135 ||
jāgarti saṃsāragṛhe manīṣī mohāṇdhakāre svapiti pramattaḥ |
jjīvitameva loke mṛtasya suptasya ca ko viśeṣaḥ || k24.136 ||
iti marhyasthitaḥ śrutvā rātrau rājasutaḥ kathāḥ |
prasthitaṃ satpatheneva nijaṃ mene manoratham || k24.137 ||
nivṝtterlakṣaṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā sa svapnaṃ kṣaṇanidrayā || k24.138 ||
tataḥ prabuddhā sahasā trastā devī yaśodharā |
tatkālopanataṃ svapnaṃ dayitāya nyavedayat || k24.139 ||
paryaṅkābharaṇāngāni svapne bhagnāni me vibho |
śrīrvrajantī mayā dṛṣṭvā candrārkau ca torihitau || k24.140 ||
ityākarṇya sa tāmūce mugdhe satyavivarjitaḥ |
saṃsāra eva svapno'yaṃ svapne svapno'pi kīdṛśaḥ || k24.141 ||
svapne'dya nābhisaṃjātā latā vyāptā vihārasā |
merūpadhānaśirasā pūrvapaścimavāridhau || k24.142 ||
bhujābhyāṃ caraṇābhyāṃ ca dakṣiṇābdhirmayā dhṛtaḥ |
bhadre svapnaḥ śubho'yaṃ te strīṇāṃ bhartuśubhaṃ śubham || k24.143 ||
ityukte bodhisattvena noce kiṃcidyaśodharā |
punaśca nidrābhimukhī babhūva mīlitekṣaṇā || k24.144 ||
śakrabrahmamukhāḥ sarve sametyātha sudhābhujaḥ |
cakrire bodhisattvasya sattvotsāhaprapūraṇam || k24.145 ||
tairdevaputrāścatvāraḥ samādiṣṭā mahājavāḥ |
sahāyā gamane tasya bhūśailābdhidhṛtikṣamāḥ || k24.146 ||
śakrādiṣṭena yakṣeṇa pāñcikākhyena nirmitaiḥ |
saharmyāsaktasopānairavatīrya viniryayau || k24.147 ||
suptaṃ sārathimādāya chandakākhyaṃ prabodhya saḥ |
utsādamiva jagrāha kaṇṭhakākhyaṃ turaṅgamam || k24.148 ||
taṃ tīkṣṇaruciraṃ lakṣmīkaṭākṣataralaṃ harim |
sa cakre saṃyamālīnaṃ mūrdhi saṃspṛśya pāṇinā || k24.149 ||
śamodyame sumanasā sako'pyantarbahiḥ samah |
śiśavo'pi vimuñcanti yatprabhāveṇa cāpalam || k24.150 ||
balajijñāsayā nyastaṃ tenātha caraṇaṃ kṣitau |
na te kampayituṃ śekurdevaputrāḥ savismayāḥ || k24.151 ||
chandakena sahāruhya nistaraṅgaṃ turaṅgamam |
sa jagāhe mahadvyoma vimalaṃ svamivāśayam || k24.152 ||
prayayau taralāvartinartitoṣṇīṣapallavaḥ |
saṃsarpipavanollasaiḥ śokocchvāsa iva śriyaḥ || k24.153 ||
tasyābharaṇaratnāṃśulekhābhiḥ śabalaṃ nabhaḥ |
jagrāha sūtrapatrālīvicitramjiva cīvaram || k24.154 ||
kīrṇāśrubindukalitā vilolanayanotpalāḥ |
vrajantaṃ dadṛśurdṛśyāstamantaḥpuradevatāḥ || k24.155 ||
saṃsāramiva vistīrṇaṃ puraṃ sanṛpabāndhavam |
dūrātpradakṣiṇīkṛtya kṣamyatāmityabhāṣata || k24.156 ||
kṣapāyāṃ kṣaṇaśeṣāyāṃ jane nidrābhimudrite |
taṃ dadarśa mahānnāma prabuddho rājabāndhavaḥ || k24.157 ||
divi dṛṣṭvā vrajantaṃ taṃ śaśāṅkaśaṅkayā hṛtaḥ |
ūce ciraṃ vicāryoccairbāṣyāśncitavilocanah || k24.158 ||
citrametadviraktavtaṃ bandhujīvopamasya te |
kumāra rucirākāra na yuktaṃ yuktakāraṇam || k24.159 ||
vaṃśotkarṣaviśeṣārthū nibaddhāśaḥ pitā tvayi |
kasmānnirāśa kriyate sarvāśābharaṇa tvayā || k24.160 ||
iti śākyasya mahataḥ śrutvā vākyaṃ nṛpātmajaḥ |
tamūce bāndhavaprītirbandho vandhanaśṛṅkhalā || k24.161 ||
ayaṃ kāyaḥ kṣayaṃ yāti mithyāgṛhasukhapriyaḥ |
viṣayograviṣrtānāmamṛtāyatanaṃ vanam || k24.162 ||
hastākṛṣṭastriphaṇiphaṇabhṛnmastakanyastamṛtyu
kaṇṭhābaddhitkaṭaviṣalatāpallavālolamālaḥ |
dīptāṅgāraprkaragahanaṃ gāhate durgamārgaṃ
saṃsāre'smin viṣayanicaye sapramodaḥ pramādī || k24.163 ||
ityudīrya vrajan vyomnā vilaṅghya nagaraṃ kṣaṇāt |
bahirbhūtalamabhyetya sa yayau vājinā javāt || k24.164 ||
mahatā śākyamukhyena bodhitasyātha bhūpateḥ |
antaḥpure ca kāntānāmudbhūtaḥ karuṇaḥ svaraḥ || k24.165 ||
atha brahmendradhanadapramukhastridaśairvṛtaḥ |
rājasūnurvanaṃ prāpa gatvā dvādaśayojanam || k24.166 ||
avaruhyātha turagātgvimucyābharaṇāni saḥ |
uvāca sūcitānandaśchandakaṃ vadanatviṣā || k24.167 ||
gṛhītvābahraṇāni tvaṃ hayaṃ ca vraja mandiram |
medānīmasti me kṛtyametairmāyānibandhanaiḥ || k24.168 ||
vane'sminnahamekākī śamasaṃtoṣabāndhavaḥ |
ekaḥ saṃjāyate jantureka eva vipadyate || k24.169 ||
viṣamaviṣayayogaṃ bhogamutsṛjya re kaḥ sarasarati viśeṣakleśaśoṣapravṛttaḥ |
paribhavabhuvane'sminneṣa naḥ saṃniveśaḥ śamitamadanakāntiḥ śāntimeva śrayāmi || k24.170 ||
ityuktvābharaṇānyasya dīptānyaṅke mumoca saḥ |
tyaktaśokānvitānīva muktāpakkaṇasaṃcaye || k24.171 ||
cūḍāṃ niskṛṣya khaṅgena sa cikṣepa nabhaḥsthale |
śakraśca taṃ samādāya nināya divamādarāt || k24.172 ||
keśaḥ kleśa ivotkṛtto yatra tena mahātmanā |
keśapratigrahaṃ caityaṃ sadbhistatra niveśitam || k24.173 ||
chandako'pyaśvamādāyaṃ prayātaḥ saptabhirdinaiḥ |
śanaiḥ prāpa puropāntaṃ śokārtaḥ samacintayat || k24.174 ||
śūnyaṃ turagamādāya parityajya nṛpātmajam |
draṣṭuṃ śaknomi nṛpatiṃ kathaṃ putrapralāpinam || k24.175 ||
vicintyeti hayaṃ tyaktvā sa tatraiva vyalambata |
śūnyāsanaḥ paraṃ vājī mūrtaḥ śoka ivāviśat || k24.176 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvāntaḥ purajanaḥ sāmātyaśca mahīpatiḥ |
pratipralāpamukharāścakrire nikhilā diśaḥ || k24.177 ||
udbhūtārtasvaraiḥ kaṇṭhaiḥ sotkaṇṭhaiḥ sa viṣādavān |
sarvairgṛhītakīrṇāśrurvājī jīvitamatyajat || k24.178 ||
sa bodhisattvasaṃsparśapuṇyaprāptipavitritaḥ |
jagrāha brāhmaṇakule janma saṃsāramuktaye || k24.179 ||
śakradattaṃ kumārastu yatra kāṣāyamagrahīt |
kāṣāyagrahaṇaṃ tatra caityaṃ cakre mahājanaḥ || k24.180 ||
vibhavamabhavavṛttyai janma janmapramuktyai
vijanamapi janānāṃ mohagartānnivṛttyai |
iti sa kuśalakāmaḥ kāmamutsṛjya bheje
guṇakṛtajanarāgaḥ ślāghyatāṃ tyaktarāgaḥ || k24.181 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāmabhiniṣkramaṇāvadānaṃ caturviśaḥ pallavaḥ ||



25. māravidrāvaṇāvadānam |

jayanti te janmabhayapramuktā bhavaprabhāvābhibhavābhiyuktāḥ |
yaiḥ sundarīlocanacakravartī māraḥ kṛtaḥ śāsanadeśavartī || k25.1 ||
tatastapovane cāsmin bodhisattve tapojuṣi |
tadupasthāpakāḥ pañca vārāṇasyāṃ pravavrajuḥ || k25.2 ||
spṛhaṇīyo munīndrāṇāmatha śākyamuniḥ śanaiḥ |
svayaṃ senāyanīgrāmaṃ janacārikayā yayau || k25.3 ||
tatra senābhidhānasya kanye gṛhapateḥ sute |
nandā nandabalākhyā ca cāruvṛtte babhūvatuḥ || k25.4 ||
śuddhodanasya bhūbhartuste śrutvā viśrutaṃ sutam |
cakrāte tadvivāhārthaṃ vrataṃ dvādaśavārṣikam || k25.5 ||
āmodinīnāṃ hṛdaye sadā sūtravadāsthitaḥ |
mālānāmiva bālānāmabhilāṣaḥ svabhāvajaḥ || k25.6 ||
dhenūnāṃ pītadugdhānāṃ dugdhaṃ tābhyāṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
gṛhīṭvā sphaṭikasthālyā vratānte pāyasaṃ śubham || k25.7 ||
vidhivatpāyase siddhe viprarūpaḥ sureśvaraḥ |
taṃ samabhyāyayau deśaṃ devaśca kamalāsanaḥ || k25.8 ||
harṣādatithibhāge'tha kanyakābhyāṃ samuddhṛte |
śakro'vadatsarvaguṇodayāyāgre pradīyatām || k25.9 ||
matto'yamadhikastāvadbrahmaṇaḥ prathamo'pi ca |
ityukte surarājena provāca caturānanaḥ || k25.10 ||
matto'dhiko deva āste śuddhāvāsanikāyikaḥ |
ityukte brahmaṇā te'pi jagadurgaganasthitāḥ || k25.11 ||
sarvaprativiśiṣṭo'sau bodhisattvastapaḥkṛśaḥ |
nadyāṃ nirājanākhyāyāṃ vigāhya salile sthitaḥ || k25.12 ||
etadākarṇya kanyābhyāmāhūya maṇibhājane |
avatīryārpitaṃ bhaktyā tadasmai madhupāyasam || k25.13 ||
bodhisattvastadādāya ratnapātrīṃ dadau tayoḥ |
datteyaṃ na punargrāhyetyuktvā jagṛhaturna te || k25.14 ||
sā tena nadyāṃ nikṣiptā nāgairnītā prabhāvatī |
vikṣobhyāpyāhṛtā tebhyastārkṣyarūpeṇa vajriṇā || k25.15 ||
prasādī bodhisattvo'tha kanyāyugalamabhyadhāt |
dānasya praṇidhānena bhavatyoḥ kiṃ samīhitam || k25.16 ||
te tamūcaturānandanidhiḥ śuddhodanātmajaḥ |
sarvārthasiddho'bhimataḥ kumāraḥ patirāvayoḥ || k25.17 ||
udyamaṃ māralīlāyāḥ sarasaṃ tadvacastayoḥ |
na lilimpa manastasya pādmaṃ dalamivodake || k25.18 ||
sa jagāda kumāro'sau na kiṃ pravrajitaḥ śrutaḥ |
na tasya lolanayanāḥ priyāhśriya iva striyaḥ || k25.19 ||
ityanīpsitamākarṇya dīrghaṃ niśvasya kanyake |
ūcaturdānadharmo'yaṃ siddhau tasyaiva jāyatām || k25.20 ||
adṛṣṭasnehasaṃśliṣṭaḥ praviṣṭo'ntaḥ parāṅbhukhaḥ |
na nāma sucirābhyastaḥ pakṣapāto nivartate || k25.21 ||
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā bodhisattvaḥ prasannadhīḥ |
prayātaste samāmantrya viśrāntyai kānanāntaram || k25.22 ||
pāyasāmṛtabhāgena labdhadivyabalodayaḥ |
pracchāyatarusucchāyamāruroha mahīdharam || k25.23 ||
paryaṅkabandhamādhāya tatra tasmin sukhaṃ sthite |
ahaṃkāra ivātyuccaśirāhso'drirvyagīryata || k25.24 ||
viśīrṇabhūdhare tasmin sa pradadhyau viṣaṇṇadhīḥ |
sapakṣālāni karmāṇi mayā kāni kṛtānyaho || k25.25 ||
iti cintāśatocchvāsaṃ tamūcurvyomadevatāḥ |
na tvayā vihitaṃ sādho karma kiṃcidasāṃpratam || k25.26 ||
acchinnottaptakuśalaṃ dhartuṃ na kṣamate kṣitiḥ |
sa tvamuttaptakuśalaḥ proccaśailaśatāgdguruḥ || k25.27 ||
nirañjanīṃ samuttīrya saritaṃ vraja niścalam |
siddhidaṃ bodhisattvānāṃ deśaṃ vajrāasnābhidham || k25.28 ||
devatādiṣṭamārgeṇa prasthitasyāsya bhūtale |
pādanyāsairabhūttasya hemapadmaparaṃparā || k25.29 ||
pṛthivī vrajatastasya prollasatsalilākulā |
raṇantī kāṃsyapātrīva pronnanāma nanāma ca || k25.30 ||
tāni tāni nimittāni pravṛttāni dadarśa saḥ |
yeṣāmanuttarajñānanidhānasādhanaṃ phalam || k25.31 ||
nirañjanāya bhuvane nāgo'ndhaḥ kālikābhidhaḥ |
buddhotpāditadṛrabhūmeḥ śabdamākarṇya niryayau || k25.32 ||
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpannaṃ dīptajāmbūnadadyutim |
sa bodhisattvamālokya provāca racitāśnjaliḥ || k25.33 ||
nalinanayana kāntastvaṃ vane yauvane'smin
viharasi virahārtiṃ saṃpadāmarpayitvā |
aśamaśamaviśeṣonmeṣasaṃtoṣahetur
bhavasi bhavasamudre dehināṃ satyasetuḥ || k25.34 ||
athāmuñcantyete bhayataralatāmatra hariṇāḥ
yathā līlācakraṃ viharati samīpe khagagaṇaḥ |
sasattvāsattvānā kimapi hṛdayāśvāsasadanaṃ
tathā manye bauddhaṃ vapuridamanāyāsasukhadam || k25.35 ||
karikalabhakahpadmaprītyā karoti hareḥ karaṃ
sukhayati śikhī snigdhālāpaṃ kalāpaśikhānilai |
bhavati hariṇī lolāpāṅgā puraḥ praṇayonmukhī
praśamasamayasyeyaṃ puṇyaprasādamayī sthitiḥ || k25.36 ||
adyaiva buddhatvamavāpya śuddhaṃ tvaṃ bodhimeṣyatya(?)khilāṃ trilokīm |
sadyaḥprasādapramadāvadātāṃ kumudvatī pūrṇa ivāmṛtāṃśuḥ || k25.37 ||
anyonyaṃ dinanāthadīptamahasaḥ sadyastavālokanāl
lokānāṃ kanalaprabodhakalayā divyaprakāśaspṛśām |
niryātī hṝdayānnibaddhamadhupaśreṇiva saṃbandhana-
trastāntarna punaḥ kariṣyati padaṃ mohāndakārāvaliḥ || k25.38 ||
iti bruvāṇāṃ vinayānnāgarājaḥ prasannadhīḥ |
bodhisattvaṃ samābhāsya samuttīrya nadīṃ yayau || k25.39 ||
vajrāsanapadaṃ prāpya bodhimūlamanākulam |
dakṣiṇāgraiḥ kuśaiścakre śaktadattaiḥ sa saṃstaram || k25.40 ||
tatropaviśya paryaṅkabaddho niścalaniścayaḥ |
manthāvasānaviśrāntaḥ sa dugdhābdhirivābabhau || k25.41 ||
asādhāraḥ kṣamādhāraḥ sa dhīrasaralākṛtiḥ |
ruruce kāñcanaruciḥ paro merurivācalaḥ || k25.42 ||
asāvakṣayaparyantaḥ paryaṅko'yaṃ mama sthiraḥ |
babandheti sa saṃkalpaṃ kṛtvā pratimukhīṃ smṛtim || k25.43 ||
atrāṇtare samabhyetya māraḥ saṃyamamatsaraḥ |
lekhahārastatra tūrṇaṃ bodhisattvamabhāṣataḥ || k25.44 ||
akāmakāmatā keyaṃ loke bandhanadā matā |
akālakalikākārā matiste kāsya kāmanā || k25.45 ||
gṛhītaṃ hataśaṅkena devadattena te puram |
niruddhantaḥpuraśreṇīrbaddhaḥ śuddhodano nṛpaḥ || k25.46 ||
iti śrutvaiva vacanaṃ śokāmarṣaviṣojjhitaḥ |
aśikṣitavikāreṇa cetasā sa vyacintayat || k25.47 ||
aho batāntarāyaṃ me māraḥ kartuṃ samudyataḥ |
nartayatyeṣa durvṛttaḥ śisvaṇḍikrīḍayājagat || k25.48 ||
māra māra virāmaste daurjanyasya na jāyate |
ekena hiṃsāyajñena prāpteyaṃ kamratā tvayā || k25.49 ||
yajñadāṇatapaḥślāghāṃ nātmanaḥ kartumutsahe |
svaguṇodīraṇamlānaṃ puṇyapuṣpaṃ hi śīryati || k25.50 ||
iti nirbhatsitastena cittastenaḥ śarīriṇām |
sāmarṣaḥ prayayau māraḥ samārambhādhatodyamaḥ || k25.51 ||
athādṛśyanta lalitā lālityāñcitalocanāḥ |
bhramadbhṛṅgaraṅgiṇyaḥ kāntāścūtalatā iva || k25.52 ||
cārutaccaritātṛptāstisrastāḥ kāmakanyakāḥ |
sarāgaṃ pādanalinīnyāsaiścakrustapovanam || k25.53 ||
vilocanena hariṇī kariṇī gativibhramaiḥ |
tatra tābhirmukhāmbhojairnalinī malinīkṛtā || k25.54 ||
yauvanābharaṇairaṅgauranurāgāvilepanaiḥ |
lāvaṇyavasanaistāsāṃ kāmo'bhūdapyacetasām || k25.55 ||
vajrāsanasamādhānadhyānaniścalalocanam |
taṃ vilokyābhavattāsāṃ vismayadhyānadhāraṇā || k25.56 ||
tā bodhisattvasaṃkalpānmadarāgamayaṃ vayaḥ |
parityajyaiva sahasā salajjā bhejire jarām || k25.57 ||
pratīpagamanāttāsāmatha bhagnamanorathaḥ |
manmathaḥ prathitārambhaḥ sainyasaṃbḥāramādade || k25.58 ||
sarvapraharaṇairvyāptaṃ nānāprāṇimukhairbhayaiḥ |
ṣaṭtriṃśatkoṭīvipulaṃ balaṃ tasya samudyayau || k25.59 ||
svayamākarṇaniṣkṛṣṭakopakrūraśarāsanaḥ |
māraḥ sphāravikāreṇa bodhisattvaṃ samādravat || k25.60 ||
śastravṛṣṭistadutsṛṣṭā saha pāṃśuviṣāśmabhiḥ |
prayayau bodhisattvasya mandārāmbujatulyatām || k25.61 ||
punarmārabalotsṛṣṭā śastravṛṣṭirghṛtakṣame |
cakrire devatāstasya vajrapratisamāśrayam || k25.62 ||
smaro'pi naṣṭasaṃkalpaḥ samādheḥ śrotrakaṇṭakam |
ghaṇṭāpaṭuraṭatpatraṃ nirmame sphaṭīkadrumam || k25.63 ||
taṃ tāramukharaṃ vṛkṣaṃ māraṃ ca sabalāyudham |
cakravāṭe samutkṣipya cikṣipurvyomadevatāḥ || k25.64 ||
bhagavānatha saṃprāptaprasannajñānanirmalaḥ |
sarvavitsarvagaḥ saravjātismṛtiparo'bhavat || k25.65 ||
sa tatrānuttarajñānasamyaksaṃbodhimāpitaḥ |
dadarśa sarvabhutāṇi gatiṃ karmorminirmitām || k25.66 ||
atha śākyapure māraḥ pravādamasṛjaddivaḥ |
bodhisattvaḥ prayāto'staṃ tapaḥkleśavaśāditi || k25.67 ||
tatra śuddhodano rājā putrasnehaviṣāturaḥ |
nipapāta tamākarṇya vajrāhata iva kṣitau || k25.68 ||
antaḥpure sahanṛpe prāṇatyāgakṛtakṣaṇe |
suvṛttapakṣapātinyastamūcurvyomadevatāḥ || k25.69 ||
putrastavāmṛtaṃ pītvā samyaksaṃbuddhatāṃ gataḥ |
tenāvalokitasyāpi nāste bhṛtyubhayaṃ kutaḥ || k25.70 ||
iti sāntaḥpurāmātyaḥ śrutvā narapatirvacaḥ |
abhūtpratyāgataprāṇaḥ sudhāsikta iva kṣaṇāt || k25.71 ||
tasminmahotsavānande bodhisattvavadhūḥ sutam |
kāntaṃ yaśodhaāsūta rāhugraste niśākare || k25.72 ||
rāhulākhyaḥ sa bālo'pi nṛpaterjanmaśaṅkinaḥ |
jananyā saśilaḥ śuddhyai nikṣipto'mbhasi pupluve || k25.73 ||
bhagavānapi saptāhaṃ sthito niścalavigrahaḥ |
vajraparyaṅkabandhena devānāṃ vismayaṃ vyadhāt || k25.74 ||
sa brahmakāyikākhyābhyāṃ devatābhyāṃ virodhitaḥ |
avadatparamānandasudhāsaṃdohasundaraḥ || k25.75 ||
aho tava mayā jñātā pūrvameṣā sukhasthitiḥ |
yayā surāsuraiśvaryasukhaṃ duḥkhagaṇāyate || k25.76 ||
lāvaṇyāmbhaḥplāvitāṅgāstaruṇyaḥ pīyūṣārdraḥ svargasaṃbhogavargaḥ |
asyāśeṣatyāgahelāsukhasya spardhābandhe pāṃśuniḥsāra eva || k25.77 ||
saṃtapto'haṃ viṣayaviṣamakleśasaṃsārapānthaḥ
klāntaḥ śāntyāśritimupagataścandanacchāyayeva |
saṃjāteyaṃ sakalakaraṇavyāpinī nirvṛtime
viśrāntānāṃ śamahimavane kiṃ sukhasyopamānam || k25.78 ||
asminnavasare puṇyaparipākeṇa tadvanam |
vaṇijau pṛthusārthena prāptau trapusabhallikau || k25.79 ||
devatāpretitau bhaktyā bhagavantamupetya tau |
praṇatau piṇḍapāto'yaṃ gṛhyatāmityabhāṣatām || k25.80 ||
dayāvidheyaḥ sarvajñastadākarṇya vyacintayat |
pūrvaiḥ pātre gṛhīto'yaṃ na pāṇī pātravarjite || k25.81 ||
iti cintayatastasya mahārājābhidhāḥ surāḥ |
datvā sphaṭikapātrāṇi catvāri tridivaṃ yayuḥ || k25.82 ||
kṛtvātha bhagavān pātre piṇḍapātapratigraham |
anugrahaṃ tayoścakre śaraṇyatrayaśāsanāt || k25.83 ||
vitatasukṛtasākṣī puṇyanikṣepadakṣaḥ kṣayitavipadaśeṣaḥ prārthanākalpavṛkṣaḥ |
bhavati kuśalamūlaiḥ kasyacidbhāgyabhājaḥ śubhapariṇatidīkṣādakṣiṇaḥ sādhusaṅgaḥ || k25.84 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ māracidrāvaṇāvadānaṃ nāma pañcaviṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||



26. śākyotpattiḥ |

vaṃśaḥ sa ko'pi vipulaḥ kuśalānubandhī yaścāruvṛttamucitaṃ guṇasaṃgrahasya |
ratnaṃ viśuddharucisūcitasatprakāśaṃ muktāmayaṃ jagadalaṃkaraṇaṃ prasūte || k26.1 ||
nyagrodhārāmanirataṃ purā kapulavāstuni |
śākyāḥ svavaṃśaṃ papracchurbhagavantaṃ tathāgatam || k26.2 ||
taiḥ pṛṣṭaḥ svakulotpattiṃ sa maudgalyāyanaṃ puraḥ |
vaktuṃ nyayuṅkta kṛtvāsya vimalajñānadarśanam || k26.3 ||
sa vilokya yathātattvamatītaṃ jñānacakṣuṣā |
tānavocata saṃsmṛtya śrūyatāṃ śākyasaṃbhavaḥ || k26.4 ||
aśeṣe'smin jalamaye jagatyekārṇave purā |
sthite pavanasaṃsparśātpayaḥ paya ivābhavat || k26.5 ||
jale tasmin ghanatayā yāte kaṭhinatāṃ śanaiḥ |
abhūdvarṇarasasparśaśabdagandhamayī mahī || k26.6 ||
tasyāmābhāsvarā devāścyutāḥ karmaparikṣayāt |
tattulyavarṇasaṃbhūtāḥ sattvāḥ sattvabalādhikāḥ || k26.7 ||
aṅgulyā rasamāsvādya tattṛṣṇātīvramohitāḥ |
āhāradoṣātsaṃprāpourgururūkṣavivarṇatām || k26.8 ||
annaprasavinī teṣāṃ krameṇābhūdvasuṃdharā |
tamobhiśca viluptānāṃ kṣetrāga || k26.9 ||
tatasteṣāṃ kṣatatrāṇātkṣatriyaḥ kṣitipālane |
mahāsaṃmatanāmābhūjjanasya mahato mataḥ || k26.10 ||
tasyānvaye mahatyāsīnṛpaḥ śrīmānupoṣadhaḥ |
amlānakīrtikusumaḥ pārijāta ivodadhau || k26.11 ||
cakravartī sutastasya māndhātābhūdayonijaḥ |
jagatyekātapatrasya yasya vaṃśo mahānabhūt || k26.12 ||
vaṃśe sahasravaṃśasya kṛkistasyābhavannṛpaḥ |
cittaprasādamakarodbhagavān yasya kāśyapaḥ || k26.13 ||
ikṣvākuranvaye tasya tasya cābhūdvirūḍhakaḥ |
prītyā kanīyasaḥ sūnorjyeṣṭhāstena vivāsitāḥ || k26.14 ||
ekībhūya tataḥ sarve svadeśavigataspṛhāḥ |
kumārāḥ kapilākhyāsya maharṣerāśramaṃ yayuḥ || k26.15 ||
dhyānakālāṇtarāyāṇāṃ bālyāduccaiḥ pralāpinām |
so'nyatra nirmame teṣāṃ puraṃ kapulavāstviti || k26.16 ||
kālena putravātsalyādanutāpena bhūpatiḥ |
ānīyantāṃ kumārāste sacivānityabhāṣata || k26.17 ||
tamūrcurmantriṇaḥ sarve rājan prāptapurottamāḥ |
pratyānetumaśakyāste jātāpatyapṛthuśriyaḥ || k26.18 ||
iti teṣāṃ pitustatra śakyāśakyavicintane |
babhūvuḥ śākyasaṃjñāste nṝpurasteṣu vaṃśakṛt || k26.19 ||
tadvaṃśeṣu pañcapañcasahasreṣu mahībhujām |
atīteṣu kṣitipatiḥ śrīmān daśaratho'bahvat || k26.20 ||
tasyānvaye siṃhahanurbabhūva pṛthivīpatiḥ |
na raṇe siṃhamiva yaṃ sehire rājakuñjarāḥ || k26.21 ||
jyeṣṭhaḥ śuddhodanastasya sutaḥ śuklodanaḥ paraḥ |
droṇodanastadanujaḥ kanīyānamṛtodanaḥ || k26.22 ||
kanyāścatasraḥ śuddhākhyā śuklā droṇāmṛtā tathā |
śuddhodanasya bhagavān sūnurnandastathāparaḥ || k26.23 ||
śuklodanasya tanayau dvau tiṣyākhyo'tha bhadrikaḥ |
droṇodanasya dvau putrāvaniruddho mahāṃstathā || k26.24 ||
ānandadevadattākhyāvamṛtodanasaṃbahvau |
śuddhāsutaḥ supraśuddhaḥ śuklāsūnuśva mālikaḥ || k26.25 ||
droṇāputraśca bhadrāṇirvaiśālyakhyo'mṛtāsutaḥ |
rāhulo bhagavatsūnuryasmin vaṃśaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ || k26.26 ||
ityujjvalajñānamayena tena vaṃśaṃ yathāvatkathitaṃ niśamya |
śākyā babhūvarbhagavatprabhāvaiḥ
saṃbhāvitotkarṣaviśeṣaśuddhāḥ || k26.27 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ śākyotpattīrnāma ṣaḍviṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


27. śroṇākoṭīviṃśāvadānam |
sa ko'pi sattvasya vivekabandhoḥ puṇyopasannasya mahān prabhāvaḥ |
nāpaiti yaḥ kāyaśateṣu puṃsaḥ kastūrikāmoda ivāṃśukasya || k27.1 ||
jine sarvajagajjantusaṃtāpakaruṇāhrade |
pure rājagṛhe veṇuvanāntaravihāriṇi || k27.2 ||
śrīmānabhūdbhūmipatiścampāyāṃ potalābhidhaḥ |
jahāra dhanadarpāndhyaṃ dhanena dhanadasya yaḥ || k27.3 ||
ajāyata sutastasya manorathaśatānvitaḥ |
dhanānāmīpsitāvāptya sukhasakhyo vibhūtayaḥ || k27.4 ||
śravaṇarkṣeṇa jātasya sa sūnostasya janmani |
prītyā dadau daridrasya koṭīnāṃ viṃśatiṃ tadā || k27.5 ||
sa śroṇakoṭiviṃśākhyaḥ śiśuḥ khyātastadābhavat |
vibhavaḥ sukṛtenaiva yena vaṃśo vibhūṣitaḥ || k27.6 ||
prāptavidyaḥ sa sarvārthaiḥ saha vṛddhimupāgataḥ |
cakāra sukhaviśrāntiṃ vyavahāradharaḥ pituḥ || k27.7 ||
sa kadācitprabhāpuñjamiavsūryasya maṇḍalāt |
avatīrṇaṃ puraprātaṃ maudgalyāyanamabravīt || k27.8 ||
ko bhavānarkasaṃkāśaḥ prakāśitadigantaraḥ |
tridaśeśaḥ śaśāṅko vā devo vā draviṇeśvaraḥ || k27.9 ||
sa taṃ babhāṣe na suraḥ śiṣyo bhagavatastvaham |
buddhasya vibudhādhīśavandyamānaguṇaśriyaḥ || k27.10 ||
svacchaṃ tasya prayacchantaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ mahāmuneḥ |
sattvaśuddhodayāvāptaṃ bhogyaṃ bhagavataḥ priyam || k27.11 ||
iti śroṇasya bhagavannāmnā śravaṇagāminā |
ravibhakrasya jātyāpi romāñcaḥ samajāyataḥ || k27.12 ||
prāgjanmavāsanāyuktaḥ svabhāvo yasya yaḥ sthitaḥ |
sa tasyodīraṇenāpi sphuṭa eva vibhāvyate || k27.13 ||
sa tasmai viṃśatisthālībhāgaṃ nākajanocitam |
prahiṇodbhaktisaṃsaktaśraddhāyuktena cetasā || k27.14 ||
tatastaṃ preṣitaṃ tena bhagavān bhaktisaṃsadā |
anugrahāgrahavyagraḥ samagraṃ svayamagrahīt || k27.15 ||
asminnavasare bhaktyā sthālībhāgaṃ nṛpocitam |
samādāya svayaṃ rājā bimbisāraḥ samāyayau || k27.16 ||
śroṇaprahītabhogānāmāmodaṃ tatra pārthivaḥ |
āghrāya śakraprahitaṃ sarvaṃ divyamamanyata || k27.17 ||
pātraśeṣaṃ bhagavatā dattamāsvādya bhūpatiḥ |
śroṇena preṣitaṃ śrutvā tadanu vismayaṃ yayau || k27.18 ||
bhagavantaṃ praṇamyātha rājadhānīṃ nareśvaraḥ |
praviśyācintayatsphītāṃ divyāṃ śroṇasya saṃpadam || k27.19 ||
gacchāmi taṃ svayaṃ gatvā draṣṭavyo'sau amhāyaśāḥ |
iti niścitya sacivaiḥ sa yātrārambhamādiśat || k27.20 ||
potalaḥ kṣitipaṃ jñātvā svayamāgamanodyatam |
nītijñaṃ putramekāṇte śroṇakoṭimabhāṣata || k27.21 ||
putra tvāṃ draṣṭumāyāti varṇāśramagururnṛpaḥ |
ityeṣa bhṛśamutkarṣaḥ sadoṣaḥ pratibhāti me || k27.22 ||
kṣitīśā lakṣatāṃ yātaṃpakṣapātodyatā iva |
avilambitamāghnanti śarā iva guṇacyutāḥ || k27.23 ||
bhṛtyānāmapi vidveṣyo bhavatyatiśayonnatiḥ |
abhimānaikasārāṇāṃ kiṃ punaḥ pṛthivībhūjām || k27.24 ||
rūpe vayasi saubhāgye prabhāve vibhave śrute |
svasutasyāpi saṃgharṣānnotkarṣaṃ sahate janaḥ || k27.25 ||
jane dveṣamaye putra guṇamācchādya jīvyate |
ācchāditaguṇaḥ padmaḥ priyastīkṣṇarucerapi || k27.26 ||
uddhataḥ kasya na dveṣyaḥ praṇataḥ kasya na priyaḥ |
drumaṃ pātayati stabdhaṃ namtraṃ rakṣati mārutaḥ || k27.27 ||
sa cābhigamyo bhūpālastvāṃ yadi svayameṣyati |
tadeṣaṃ darpamohaste śreyase na bahviṣyati || k27.28 ||
tasmāditaḥ svayaṃ gatvā praṇamyaṃ praṇama prabhum |
nakṣatrarāśisadṛśaṃ datvā hāramupāyanam || k27.29 ||
pituḥ śrutveti vacanaṃ śroṇakoṭirhahīpatim |
prayayau nāvamāruhya draṣṭuṃ ratnavibhūṣitaḥ || k27.30 ||
sa rājadhānīmāsādya prāpya dṛṣṭvā ca bhūpatim |
dadau hāraṃ parṇamyāsmai harṣahāsamiva śriyaḥ || k27.31 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā svayamāyātaṃ nṛpatiḥ snigdhayā dṛśā |
hemaromāṅkacaraṇaṃ vismayādityabhāṣata || k27.32 ||
aho puṇyamaheśākhyaḥ ko'pi tvaṃ sattvabāmdhavaḥ |
yasya saṃdarśanenaiva manovṛttiḥ prasīdati || k27.33 ||
guṇebhyaḥ paramaiśvaryamaiśvaryātsukhamuttamam |
sukhebhyaḥ paramārogyamārogyātsādhusaṃgamaḥ || k27.34 ||
api dṛṣṭastvayā sādho bhagavān veṇukānane |
tatpādapadmayugalaṃ draṣṭumarhasi me mataḥ || k27.35 ||
ityukte kṣitināthena saujanyātpakṣapātinā |
taṃ śroṇakoṭīviṃśo'pi praṇayātpratyabhāṣata || k27.36 ||
asmādatulyakalyāṇātprasādāddevadeva te |
adhunā yogyatāyātā bhagavaddarśane mama || k27.37 ||
ityukte tena sahitaḥ pratasthau sthitikovidaḥ |
bhaktyā tathāgataṃ draṣṭuṃ padbhyāmeva mahīpatiḥ || k27.38 ||
aspṛṣṭapādasya bhuvā śruṇasyājanmavāsarāt |
mahārhavastraiḥ prasthāne bhṛtyairācchāditā mahī || k27.39 ||
bhagavadbhaktivinayādgauravācca sa bhūpateḥ |
vastrāṇyavārayadbhṛtyairavācca iva kṣitau || k27.40 ||
vāriteṣvatha vastreṣu divyavastrairvṛtā mahī |
aprayatnopakaraṇāḥ saṃpadaḥ puṇyaśālinām || k27.41 ||
nivārya dinyavastrāṇi bhūmau tenārpite pade |
vicacālācalā pṛthvī saśailavanasāgarā || k27.42 ||
tataḥ sa bhūmipatinā saha prāpya jināśramam |
bhagavantaṃ vilokyāsya vidadhe pādavandanam || k27.43 ||
upaviṣṭasya tasyāgre hṛṣṭasyālokanāmṛtaiḥ |
cakre śamavivekasya bhagavānabhiṣecanam || k27.44 ||
āśayānuśayaṃ dhātuṃ prakṛtiṃ ca vicārya saḥ |
satyasaṃdarśanāyāsya vidaśe dharmadeśanām || k27.45 ||
satkāyadṛṣṭiśailo'sya tayā viṃśatiśṛṅgavān |
jñānavajreṇa nirbhinnaḥ srotaḥprāptipadaspṛśaḥ || k27.46 ||
pravrajyāyāṃ tatastasya jātāyāṃ sahasā svayam |
bhagavantaṃ praṇamyātha vismitaḥ prayayau nṛpaḥ || k27.47 ||
tīvravrate'pi śroṇasya kadācitsamajāyata |
vāsanāśeṣasaṃskārādbandhabhogasukhasmṛtiḥ || k27.48 ||
sa tamāhūya bhagavān vilakṣaṃ prāha sasmitaḥ |
ko'yaṃ parivitarkaste pratisaṃlīnacetasaḥ || k27.49 ||
viśliṣṭātyantakṛṣṭā vā tantrī bahvati visvarā |
samā mādhuryamāyāti tasmātsāmyaṃ samāśrayet || k27.50 ||
ityādeśādbhagavataḥ sarvasāmyamupāgataḥ |
sa prāpa vimalajñānaṃ paścāttāpavivarjitaḥ || k27.51 ||
tasya tāmadbhutāṃ siddhiṃ vilokya pṛthuvismayāḥ |
papracchurbhikṣavaḥ sarve bhagavantaṃsa cābhyadhāt || k27.52 ||
śroṇasya śrūyatāṃ śreyaḥkarma janmāntarārhitam |
na hyapuṇyānubhāvānāṃ bhavantyadbhutasaṃpadaḥ || k27.53 ||
vipaśvī bhagavān samyaksaṃbuddhaḥ sugataḥ purā |
purīṃ bandhumatīṃ nāma janacārikayā yayau || k27.54 ||
tatra bhaktyā sukṛtibhirbhaktāyopanimantritaḥ |
vāreṇa pratyahaṃ gehaṃ yayau teṣāṃ sahānugaḥ || k27.55 ||
tato daridraḥsaṃprāptavāro brāhmaṇadārakaḥ |
indrasomābhidhaścakre yatnātyadyogyabhojanam || k27.56 ||
sa prayatnena mahatā bhojyaṃ bhaktipavitritam |
āchādya vastrairvasudhāṃ prahvastasmai nyavedayat || k27.57 ||
tadbhogapraṇidhānena jātaḥ so'yaṃ mahādhanaḥ |
sauvarṇaromacaraṇaḥ śroṇakoṭī suropamaḥ || k27.58 ||
na vastrarahitā bhūmiḥ spṛṣṭānena kadācana |
kampastaccaraṇasparśādata evābhavadbhuvaḥ || k27.59 ||
iti sugatavacaḥ sudhāvadātaṃ daśanamayūkhamivonmiṣatsvabhāvam |
praṇihitahṛdayaḥ paraṃ nipīya sthirakuśalāya babhūva bhikṣusaṃghaḥ || k27.60 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ śroṇakoṭīviṃśāvadānaṃ saptaviṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


28. dhanapālāvadānam |

daurjanyaduḥsahaviśālakhalāpakārairnaivāśaye vikṛtirasti mahāśayānām |
vyālolvaṇakṣitibhṛdākulito'pi sindhurnaivotsasarja hṛdayādamṛtasvabhāvam || k28.1 ||
pure purā rājagṛhe bahgavān veṇukānane |
kalandakanivāsākhye vijahāra manohare || k28.2 ||
tatra vitrāsitānekaśatrinistriṃśabāndhavaḥ |
ajātaśatrunāmābhūdbimbisārasuto nṛpaḥ || k28.3 ||
śākyavaṃśyaḥ suhṛttasya devadattābhidho'bhavat |
kṣudramantreṇa yasyāsītsa vetāla ivotkaṭaḥ || k28.4 ||
sa kadācitsukhāsīnaṃ rahaḥ prāha mahīpatim |
na rājan phalito'dyāpi mamāpi tvatsamāśrayaḥ || k28.5 ||
nithopacārarahitaḥ sukhaniryantratantrayoḥ |
mitho manorathatrāṇānmitraśabdaḥ pravartate || k28.6 ||
ya eṣa śākyaśramaṇaḥ sukhe veṇuvane sthitaḥ |
taṃ hatvā prāptumicchāmi tatpadaṃ devavanditam || k28.7 ||
kṣīyate na yayā śatrurlabhyate na yayā yaśaḥ |
vardhate na yayā mānaḥ kiṃ tayā mitrasaṃpadā || k28.8 ||
mahādhanābhidhānena tatpaureṇa nimantritaḥ |
sa puraṃ prātarāgantā dāmbhikaḥ saha bhikṣubhiḥ || k28.9 ||
rājamārgaṃ praviśataḥ sa tasya vyālakuñjaraḥ |
utsṛjyatāmabhimukhaḥ krodhāndho dhanapālakaḥ || k28.10 ||
ityukte devadattena nṛpatirmitravatsalaḥ |
buddhaprabhāvaṃ saṃcintya nāha kiṃcidavāṅbhukhaḥ || k28.11 ||
devadatto'pi nirgatya rājasauhārdadurmadaḥ |
prāha hastimahāmātraṃ hāraṃ datvāsya toṣaṇam || k28.12 ||
śramaṇaḥ prātarāgantā puraṃ bhikṣuśatairvṛtaḥ |
preraṇīyastvayā tasmai gaja ityāha bhūpatiḥ || k28.13 ||
devadattavacaḥ śrutvā tathetyuce dvipādhipaḥ |
śreṇī hi meṣamūrkhāṇāmekayātānupātinī || k28.14 ||
jñātvāpi tamabhiprāyaṃ sarvajñaḥ pāpacetasām |
prātaḥ samāyayau sārdhaṃ bhikṣūṇāṃ pañcabhi śataiḥ || k28.15 ||
atha hastipakotsṛṣṭaḥ kṛṣṭaprāsādapādapaḥ |
tamabhyadhāvadāviddhaḥ krodhāndhaḥ krūrakuñjaraḥ || k28.16 ||
anāyattaḥ paricayādaṅkuśasya gurorapi |
khalaviddhāniva dveṣī madena malinīkṛtaḥ || k28.17 ||
sevavyasanasaktānāmasakṛtkarṇacāpalāt |
prāṇāpahārī bhṛṅgāṇāṃ bhṛtyānāmiva duṣpatiḥ || k28.18 ||
mandaropadrave tasmin drumadrohiṇyabhidrute |
vidrute sahasā loke hāhākāro mahānabhūt || k28.19 ||
tasyāñcatkarṇatālānilatulitasaratsāndrasindūrapūraiḥ
saṃpūrṇe rājamārge cyutacakitavadhūraktavastrāsamānaiḥ |
uddaṇḍoccaṇḍaśuṇḍabhramaṇaravalasatsādhvasāyāsitāśā
vyālolālakābhabhramaramiladvibhramaḥ saṃbhramo'bhūt || k28.20 ||
purapramāthavyathite jane kolāhalākule |
āruroha mahāharmyaṃ devadattaḥ pramattadhīḥ || k28.21 ||
bhagavadgrahaṇaṃ draṣṭuṃ so'bhavadbhṛśamutsukaḥ |
unmūlanena guṇināṃ mātaṅgaḥ parituṣyati || k28.22 ||
vidruteṣu gajatrāsātteṣu sarveṣu bhikṣuṣu |
ānanda eka evābhūdbhikṣurbhagavato'ntike || k28.23 ||
tatra pañcānanāhpañca niryayurbhagavatkarāt |
karālakesarasaṭāstannakhāṃśucitā iva || k28.24 ||
dvipastadgandhamāgrāya pardāpasmāravāraṇam |
abhūtsrutaśakṛnmūtraḥ sahasaiva parāṅmukhaḥ || k28.25 ||
javena vidrutastatra dantī darpadaridratām |
prāptaḥ pradīpadahanāḥ sa dadarśa diśo daśa || k28.26 ||
sa vilokya jālavahnijvalajjvālākulaṃ jagat |
pādapadmāntikaṃ śāstuḥ śītalaṃ samupāyayau || k28.27 ||
saṃkocābhiruceḥ sacintamanasaḥ pradhvastavaktradyuterdainyāpannavihīnadānamadhupaprārabdhakolāhalaḥ |
lobhāndhasya mahāvyayotsava iva kleśoṣmaniśvāsinastasyābhūtparitāpaniślathagaterbhārāyamāṇaḥ karaḥ || k28.28 ||
taṃ pādamūlamāyāntaṃ bhītaṃ kāruṇyasāgaraḥ |
śāstā kareṇa pasparśa cakrasvastikalakṣmaṇā || k28.29 ||
kumbhavinyastahastastaṃ provāca bhagavān jinaḥ |
putra svakarmaṇainemāṃ prāpto'si tvamimāṃ daśām || k28.30 ||
vivekālokahaladaḥ kāyo'yaṃ māṃsabhūdharaḥ |
bhāraste mohasaṃbhāraḥ pāpādupanataḥ paraḥ || k28.31 ||
ityukte karuṇardreṇa bhito bhagavatā gajaḥ |
sa labdhaścāsamālānalīno niścalatāṃ yayau || k28.32 ||
devadattasya saṃkalpe kuñjare ca mahotkaṭe |
bhagne nirvighnaharṣo'bhūtsamudgatādbhuto janaḥ || k28.33 ||
tataḥ kṛtvā gṛhapatergṛhe bhojyapratigraham |
bhagavān bhikṣubhiḥ sārdhaṃ kānanaṃ gantumudyayau || k28.34 ||
abhisṛtya gajendro'pi jinasya caraṇābjayoḥ |
kṛtvā kareṇa saṃsparśaṃ vapustatyāja kauñjaram || k28.35 ||
cāturmahārājikeṣu deveṣu viśadadyutiḥ |
upapannaḥ sa sahasā sugataṃ draṣṭumāyayau || k28.36 ||
svamāśramapadaṃ prāptaṃ bhagavantamupetya saḥ |
praṇanāmārkasaṃkāśaṃ pradīptamaṇikuṇḍalaḥ || k28.37 ||
tasya keyūramukuṭaprabhāpallavapūritāḥ |
śakracāpairiva vyāptā virejurghanarājayaḥ || k28.38 ||
vinayādupaviśyāgre sa śāstuḥ srastakalmaṣaḥ |
taṃ divyapuṣpairākīrya sattva śubhrairabhāṣataḥ || k28.39 ||
bhagavan bhavataḥ pādapadmasaṃsparśanena me |
durdaśāduḥkhasaṃtāpaḥ śāntaḥ saṃtoṣaśālinaḥ || k28.40 ||
śamaślāghyā kāpi vyasanaviṣadoṣoṣmaśamanī
sudhāvṛṣṭirdṛṣṭirbata bhagavataḥ snigdhamadhurā |
yayā spṛṣṭaspṛṣṭaṃ kharataravikāravyatikaraṃ
vimutyāntaḥśāntiṃ śrayati hatamoha paśurapi || k28.41 ||
iti tasya bruvāṇasya bhagavān bhavaśāntaye |
satyadarśanasaṃśuddhāṃ vidadhe dharmadeśanām || k28.42 ||
maulimuktāṃśuśubhreṇa śirasā caraṇadvayam |
sa śāstuḥ prayayau natvā hasanniva bhavabhramam || k28.43 ||
gate tasminmukhaśaśiprakāśitanabhastale |
bhagavān bhikṣubhiḥ pṛṣṭastadvṛttāntamabhāṣata || k28.44 ||
pūrvakalpāntare śāstuḥ kāśyapākhyasya śāsane |
abhūtpravrajito'pyeṣa śikṣāpadanirādaraḥ || k28.45 ||
anādarātkuñjaratābhogaḥ saṃghopasevanāt |
satyadṛṣṭibalenānte saṃprāptaḥ śāsanagrahaḥ || k28.46 ||
prāgjanmavihitaṃ karma kasyacinna nivartate |
karmopadiṣṭasaṃbandhabhaktibhogaiḥ sacetasaḥ || k28.47 ||
tasmin vyatikare ghore sarvaistyakro'smi bhikṣibhiḥ |
na tvānandena tatrāpi śrūyatāṃ pūrvasaṃgatiḥ || k28.48 ||
śaśāṅkaśītasarasi bhrātarau rucirau purā |
pūrṇamukhaḥ śukhaśceti rājahaṃsau babhūvatuḥ || k28.49 ||
kadācidbrahmadattasya vārāṇasyāṃ mahīpateḥ |
brahamamatīṃ puṣkariṇīṃ ramyāṃ pūrṇamukho yayau || k28.50 ||
sa tasyāṃ lolakamalakiñjalkaparipiñjaraḥ |
vijahāra sarojinyāṃ haṃsānāṃ pañcabhiḥ śataiḥ || k28.51 ||
pūrvapuṇyānubhāvena taṃ rūpātiśayojjvalam |
dadarśaṃ kāryāṇyutsṛjya jano niścalalocanah || k28.52 ||
taṃ śrutvā bhūpatistatra sthitaṃ taddarśanotsukaḥ |
kuśalān grahaṇe tasya vyasṛjajjālajīvinaḥ || k28.53 ||
tasmin gṛhīte nalīnīlīlāsmitasitatviṣi |
śatāni pañca haṃsānāṃ tyaktvā taṃ prayayurjavāt || k28.54 ||
ekastu tasya saujanyādabaddho'pi subaddhavat |
tadarthaṃ vyathitastasthau premapāśavaśīkṛtaḥ || k28.55 ||
tatastaiḥ prāpitaṃ rājā rājahaṃsaṃ vilokya tam |
snehabaddhaṃ dvitīyaṃ ca vismitastāvavalokayat || k28.56 ||
haṃsaḥ pūrṇamukhaḥ so'hamānandastasya cānugaḥ |
gatāstadadya ca tyaktvā māṃ gaṃsā eva bhikṣavaḥ || k28.57 ||
pūrvasminnabahvatkāle vārāṇasyāṃ mahīpatiḥ |
tutturnāma manaḥpaṭṭe likhitaṃ yadyaśaḥ paraiḥ || k28.58 ||
sahasrayodhastasyābhūddākṣiṇātyo niratyayaḥ |
karadaṇḍīti vikhyātaḥ saṃgrāmāgresaraḥ priyaḥ || k28.59 ||
kadācidghorasamare pañcāmātyaśatāni tam |
nṛpaṃ tyaktvā yayurbhītyā karadaṇḍī tu nātyajat || k28.60 ||
ahameva sa bhūpālaḥ sacivāste ca bhikṣavaḥ |
karadaṇḍī sa evāyamānando na jahāti mām || k28.61 ||
janmāntare'pi siṃho'haṃ māsaṃ kūpe nipātitaḥ |
upekṣitaḥ kṣaṇādbhṛtyaiḥ śṛgālairye'dya bhikṣavaḥ || k28.62 ||
ekena ca nakhaiḥ khātaṃ dīrghaṃ kṛtvāsmi mokṣitaḥ |
jambukena sa evāyamānando'dya mamānugaḥ || k28.63 ||
kūṭapāśanibaddhasya mṛgayūthapateḥ purā |
lubdhakāgamane eva jagmustadanugā mṛgāḥ || k28.64 ||
anuraktā na tatyāja taṃ mṛgī sāśrulocanā |
prītiśṛṅkhalayā baddhā yātā nispandatāmiva || k28.65 ||
atha lubdhakamāyāntaṃ dṛṣṭvā mṛgavadhodyatam |
sāvadanmama bāṇena prathamaṃ hara jīvitam || k28.66 ||
iti spaṣṭagirā tasyāḥ snehena ca sa vismitaḥ |
mumoca lubdhakaḥ prītyā hariṇaṃ hariṇīsakham || k28.67 ||
mṝgayūthapatiḥ so'hamānandaḥ sā kuraṅgikā |
ityeṣa prītisaṃbandhaḥ prāgvṛttamanuvartate || k28.68 ||
śrutveti vākyaṃ sugatasya sarve lajjānilīnā iva bhikṣavaste |
sānandamānandamukhāravindaṃ prabhābhirāmaṃ dadṛśuḥ spṛhārdrāḥ || k28.69 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ dhanapālāvadānamaṣṭaviṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


29. kāśīsundarāvadānam |

jayati sa sattvaviśeṣaḥ sattvavatāṃ sarvasattvasukhahetuḥ |
dehadalane'pi śamayati kopāgniṃ śāntimuccairyaḥ || k29.1 ||
dharmopadeśe bhagavān kauṇḍinyasyāgravartinaḥ |
bhikṣoḥ kathāprasaṅgena pṛṣṭpbhikṣubhirabhyadhāt || k29.2 ||
brahmadattasya tanayo vārāṇasyāṃ mahīpateḥ |
kāśisundaranāmā va kālabhūśca babhuvatuḥ || k29.3 ||
yauvarājyabharaikarhaḥ kumāraḥ kāśisundaraḥ |
dharmādharmamayaṃ rājyaṃ vicāryācintayacciram || k29.4 ||
kṣaṇakṣayiṇi tāruṇye jīvite vīcicañcale |
rājye svapnavivāhe'sminmohamūle na me matiḥ || k29.5 ||
rāgapralāpabahule māyāmphamahe muhuḥ |
veśyārodananiḥsāre saṃsāre nāsti satyatā || k29.6 ||
pravrajyāmanaghastasmādagārādanagārikam |
nistriṃśavṛttisaṃsaktābhirāgaḥ kiṃ vibhūtibhiḥ || k29.7 ||
rājasūnurvicintyeti vivekavimalāśayaḥ |
uvācābhyetya bhūpālamaraṇyagamanotsukaḥ || k29.8 ||
mama saṃbhogavargo'yaṃ rājannaivopayujyate |
yauvarājyābhiṣekārhaḥ samārambho nivāryatām || k29.9 ||
krodhāgnitaptāḥ sutarāmetāstāta na me matāḥ |
baddhabandhabhayāyāsajananyo rājasaṃpadaḥ || k29.10 ||
vyāptāḥ krūratarācārairjvalitāḥ pārthivaśriyaḥ |
kurvanti kasya nodvegaṃ śmaśānāgniśikhā iva || k29.11 ||
chatrasaṃchāditā lokāścāmarānilalolitāḥ |
patanti pātakaśvabhre madakṣībāḥ kṣitīśvarāḥ || k29.12 ||
mṛdubhogāṃśukābhyāsasukumāre mahībhujām |
kāye patati paryante kleśaḥ kuliśadāruṇaḥ || k29.13 ||
cintāsaṃtatasaṃtāpatīvratṛṣṇāpralāpinām |
rājyajvarajuṣāṃ naiṣāṃ mohamūrcchā nivartate || k29.14 ||
vakrāṇāṃ ratnadīptānāṃ dvijihānāṃ pade pade |
chidrasaṃdarśināṃ rājñāṃ vyāpāraḥ paramāraṇam || k29.15 ||
nṛpavaṃśaśatocchiṣṭāṃ manyate māmananyagām |
itīva śrīḥ kṣītīśānāṃ hāracāmarahāsinī || k29.16 ||
vyañjitavyajanocchvasā lakṣmīrmuktāśrusaṃtatiḥ |
rājñāṃ mohābhṛtātītabhūpatismaraṇādiva || k29.17 ||
tasmādvrajāmi pravrajyāvivarjitajanasthitiḥ |
saṃtoṣaśītalacchāyaṃ saṃtāpaśamanaṃ vanam || k29.18 ||
aviśrāntasya saṃsārapathapānthasya durvahaḥ |
kāyo'sya yatsadāpāyaḥ kiṃ punaḥ pṛthivībharaḥ || k29.19 ||
iti putravacaḥ śrutvā bhūptirbhṛśamapriyam |
pravrajyāśabdacakitaḥ sodvegastamabhāṣataḥ || k29.20 ||
asya vaṃśasya mahataḥ sāmrājyasya vṛddhaye |
āśānibaddhavṛddhena tvayi putra mayā param || k29.21 ||
vatsa saṃkalpabhangaṃ me na kāle krtumarhasi |
idaṃ tava mahacchāyaṃ yauvanaṃ na vanocitam || k29.22 ||
samantrābhyāsayuktānāṃ śaktānāṃ sādhudarśane |
jitendriyāṇāṃ saravtra nṛpāṇāmavanaṃ tapaḥ || k29.23 ||
svapade'pi sarojasya niḥsaṅgasalilasthitiḥ |
dṛṣṭvā vane'pyaśokasya lalanācaraṇāhatiḥ || k29.24 ||
svagehasulabhairbhogairyāvaddṛṣṭivisūcikā |
tāvadete parityuktaṃ śakyante viṣayāḥ kṣaṇam || k29.25 ||
sukhamantaḥ parityajya svajanaṃ gṛhanirgataḥ |
abhyastabhogacirahakleśaṃ na sahate janaḥ || k29.26 ||
śrūyate smaryate dharmaḥ kriyate ca sukhādgṛhe |
vane śuṣyanti śuṣkāṇāṃ śravaṇāsmaraṇakriyāḥ || k29.27 ||
kṣaratkṣatajapādasya darbhasaṃdarbhasūcibhiḥ |
tataḥ kiṃ duḥkhamaparaṃ paralole bhaviṣyati || k29.28 ||
bhuñjānaṃ janamīkṣante yātāścarmāsthiśeṣatām |
paradataṃ sadāśnanti pretā iva tapasvinaḥ || k29.29 ||
vane nivasanaṃ putra pāṃśuprāvaraṇaṃ samam |
pālanaṃ brahmacaryasya makarākaraśoṣaṇam || k29.30 ||
dāvāgnidhūmavikaṭabhrukuṭimukheṣu gonāsavāsaghanaghūkaguhāgṛheṣu |
siṃhāhatadviradalohitalohiteṣu tyaktvā gṛhaṃ bhavati kasya dhṛtirvaneṣu || k29.31 ||
kāmī saṃyamamicchati smarati ca śyāmārateḥ saṃyamī
tṛptastīvrataravrateṣu ramate bhakṣyaṃ kṣudhā kāṅkṣḥti |
ekākī janamīhate janavanodvegī vanaṃ vāñchati
tyaktvānveṣaṇatatparāḥ punarapi prāpyāvamānaṃ janāḥ || k29.32 ||
na māṃ putra parityajya gahanaṃ gantumarhasi |
bhavantu tava śatrūṇāṃ vanavāsamanorathāḥ || k29.33 ||
vyaktamauktikahāsinyaḥ karavālalatā iva |
tyaktā na punarāyānti māninyo nṛpasaṃpadaḥ || k29.34 ||
ityukro'pyasakṛtpitrā niścayānna cacāla saḥ |
vajraratnaśikhākalpaḥ saṃkalpo hi mahātmanām || k29.35 ||
jananībhiramātyaiśca bandhupauramahattamaiḥ |
sa prārthito'pyabhūnmaunī nirāhāro dinatrayam || k29.36 ||
rājabhogī tapasvī vā jīvatveṣa nijecchayā |
kāmānuvartī lolo'yamityūciḥ sacivā nṛpam || k29.37 ||
sa kathaṃcidanujñātaḥ sāśrunetreṇa bhūbhujā |
yayau paurajanākrandamaunī munitapovanam || k29.38 ||
vairāgyaparipākeṇa tatra maitrīpavitritām |
bheje sa sarvasattveṣu vivekadayitāṃ dayām || k29.39 ||
babhūvustatprabhāveṇa tatra sarvavanaukasām |
jātivairājalatyāgaśītalāścittavṛttayaḥ || k29.40 ||
tyakte prāṇavadhe prasaktahariṇīvṛndaiḥ pulindaiḥ paraṃ
siṃhairvāraṇadāraṇanyuparame sarvāṅgasaṅgīkṛte |
māyūrāvaraṇairdaridrajaghanā muktākalāpojjhitās
tatrocchvāsavirāgaśuṣyadadharā jātāḥ kirātāṅganāḥ || k29.41 ||
kṣamāṃ tyaktvābdhivasanāṃ saravbhūtakṣamāśritaḥ |
so'bhavatkṣāntivādīti viśutaḥ kāśisundaraḥ || k29.42 ||
atrāntare mahīharṣe brahmadatte divaṃ gate |
udvega iva bhūtānāṃ bhūpālaḥ kalibhūrabhūt || k29.43 ||
athadabhrabhramadbhṛṅgabhrūbhaṅgamalinānanaḥ |
munisaṃyamavidveṣī vasantaḥ pratyadṛśyata || k29.44 ||
madanonmādabhutasya prodbhūtasya mṛgīdṛśām |
mānavidhvaṃsadūtasya cutasya ruruce ruciḥ || k29.45 ||
raktāśokasya pārśvasthalatāliṅganaśaṅkitaḥ |
īrṣyāluriva puṣpāṇi jahāra malayānilaḥ || k29.46 ||
udyānayauvane tasmin kāle kokilasaṃkule |
sāntaḥpuro nṛpaḥ prāyādvanālokanakautukī || k29.47 ||
nānāvarṇapatatpuṣpaprakārapracitāni saḥ |
paśyan vanāni ramyāṇi śanaiḥ prāpa tapovanam || k29.48 ||
vanasthalīṣu kāntāsu tatra kanyāsakhaściram |
vihṛtya rativiśrāntaḥ kṣasṇaṃ nidrāmavāptavān || k29.49 ||
apūrvakusumasmerāścinvānāstatra mañjarī |
antaḥpurāṅganāśceruḥ saṃcāriṇyo latā iva || k29.50 ||
atrāntare kṣāntivādī viviktoddeśanirvṛtaḥ |
ekānte niścalastasthau śāntimantarvicintayan || k29.51 ||
amandānandaciṣyandī vandanīyo manīṣiṇām |
kṛśo'pyakṛśasaundaryaḥ śaśīva prathamoditaḥ || k29.52 ||
pariṇāmamanojñena rekhāsaṃsthānaśobhinā |
purāṇacitrarūpeṇa naiva śūnyā tadākṛtiḥ || k29.53 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā rājalalanāścittadarpaṇamārjanam |
tatraiva niścalāstasthustāścitralikhitā iva || k29.54 ||
prabuddho'tha nṛpaḥ kṣipraṃ nāpaśyaddayitāḥ puraḥ |
dadarśa vanamanviṣya parivārya sthitā munim || k29.55 ||
bhujaṅgastā vilokyaiva śvasannīrṣyāviṣākulaḥ |
visasarja varocūpahalāhalamivotkaṭam || k29.56 ||
kastvaṃ munivyañjanayā citrakṛtrimamātrayā |
muṣṇāsi mugdhagṛdayā nūnaṃ nārīpratārakaḥ || k29.57 ||
parastrīharaṇe dhyāna japastadvighnavāraṇe |
dhūrtānāṃ paramopāyaḥ saralāśvāsanaṃ tapaḥ || k29.58 ||
mukhamādhuryadhūrtasya vṛttirvalkalinastava |
aho nu mohajananī vane viṣataroriva || k29.59 ||
munikalpasamākalpaścaritaṃ punarīdṛśam |
siddhiṃ saṃbhāvitāṃ vāpi vetti kastattvamāntaram || k29.60 ||
ityukte bhūbhujā krodhādakrodhamadhurāśayaḥ |
nirvikāreṇa manasā kṣāntivādī jagādtam || k29.61 ||
kṣāntivādī munirahaṃ na māṃ śankitumarhasi |
etāsu nirviśeṣo me kāntāsu ca talāsu ca || k29.62 ||
iti tenoktamākarṇya kṣāntaṃ paśyāmi te'dhunā |
iti bruvāṇaściccheda hastau tasyāsinā nṛpaḥ || k29.63 ||
viśase'pi kṣamāśīlaṃ nivikāraṃ vilokya tam |
cakarta caraṇau tasya praśamāya samatsaraḥ || k29.64 ||
pradiśantyaśivaṃ mārge jihvayā dūṣayanti ca |
lumpantyaṅgāni paryante khalāḥ kauleyakā iva || k29.65 ||
tāḍane'pi kṣamāsaktāḥ skandhacchede'pi mauninaḥ |
śītalāstīvratāpe'pi saralāḥ saralā iva || k29.66 ||
nikṛttapāṇicaraṇaḥ sa saṃstabhya pṛthuvyathām |
rakṣanmanyumanaḥkṣobhaṃ kṣamayā samacintayat || k29.67 ||
tyaktānyakarmaṇānena cchinnānyaṅgāṇi me yathā |
saṃsāraviṣamakleśacchedaṃ kuryāmahaṃ tathā || k29.68 ||
kopamohādavajñāya nṛpatau bhrātaraṃ munim |
purīṃ prayāte rajasā śokamlāneva bhūrabhūt || k29.69 ||
tatastadduḥkhakupitā rājñe tatkṣāntidevatā |
cakre nagaryāṃ durbhokṣamarakāvṛṣṭiviplavam || k29.70 ||
naimittikebhyo vijñāya rājāmuniparābhavāt |
devatākoapajaṃ doṣaṃ taṃ prasādayituṃ yayau || k29.71 ||
nipatya pādayostasya kṣamasvetyabhidhāya saḥ |
paścāttāpaviṣādena niścetana ivābhavat || k29.72 ||
tamabravītkṣāntivādī rājanmanyurna me'ṇvapi |
karmarekhāparicchedādīdṛśī bhavitavyatā || k29.73 ||
sarvāṇi na gaṇayati svacchandā bhavitavyatā |
na dhairyaṃ na guṇaṃ nārthaṃ na tapo nāpi gauravam || k29.74 ||
antrasthitaprasavabījaparaṃparāṇi bhinnāni kālaparipākavicitritāni |
janmasthale vipulamūlabalasya janturbhuṅkte phalāni nijakarmamahīruhasya || k29.75 ||
tvayi tasmānna me kaścidvikāro'sti mahīpate |
satyenānename paśya rudhiraṃ kṣīratāṃ gatam || k29.76 ||
aṅgacchede'pyakaluṣi babhūva yadi me manaḥ |
satyenaitena ściṣṭāni tānyevāṅgāni santu me || k29.77 ||
iti śuddhadhiyastasya tībrasayopayācanāt |
śliṣṭānyaṅgāni tānyeva sahasā svāsthyamāyayuḥ || k29.78 ||
mukuṭaspṛṣṭacaraṇastamuvāca nṛpastataḥ |
mune mahāprabhāvo'si tapasā tatkimicchasi || k29.79 ||
puṇyahastāvalambena mphāndhaṃ karuṇānidhe |
pāpāvasāne patitaṃ māṃ tvamuddhartumarhasi || k29.80 ||
ityarthitaḥ kṣitīśena pratyabhāṣata taṃ muniḥ |
saṃtāraṇāya magnānāṃ baddhānāmapi muktaye || k29.81 ||
āśvāśanāya bhītānāṃ nirvāṇāya vimuhyatām || k29.82 ||
yadā tu samyaksaṃbodhiṃ tāmavāpnoṣyanuttarām |
mohacchedaṃ kariṣyāmi tadā jñānāsinā tava || k29.83 ||
prayayau munirityuktvā tamāmantrya svamāśramam |
tameva manasā dhyāyan rājadhānīṃ nṛpo'pyagāt || k29.84 ||
kṣāntivādī sa evāhaṃ kauṇḍinyaḥ kālabhūrayam |
āsādya samyaksaṃbodhimṛddhṛto'yaṃ mayādhunā || k29.85 ||
iti sugatamukhāravindanirmitamadhuramadhupratimaṃ vacaḥ prasannam |
bhramarabhava ivoditapramodaḥ kimapi babhūva nipīya bhikṣusaṃghaḥ || k29.86 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ kāśīsundarāvadānaṃ nāma ūnatriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


30. suvarṇapārśvāvadānam |

ślāṣyaḥ ko'pi sa sattvasārasaralaḥ saujanyapuṇyasthitir
nindya ko'pi sa dharmamārgagamane vighnaḥ kṛtaghnaḥ param |
citraṃ yaccaritaṃ vicārya suciraṃ romāñcacarcācitas
tulyaṃ yāti janaḥ sabāṣpanayanastadvarpane mūkatām || k30.1 ||
devadattaprasaṅgena bhikṣubhirbhagavān purā |
pṛṣṭaḥ kathāmakathayatpūrvavṛttāntasaṃśrayām || k30.2 ||
mahendrasenanāmābhūdvārāṇasyāṃ nareśvaraḥ |
yayuḥ kṣitīśvarāḥ sarve yasya lakṣmyā vilakṣatām || k30.3 ||
candraprabhābhavattasya divyakīrtiriva priyā |
yasyāḥ patyuḥ prabhāveṇa svapnāḥ satyatvamāyayuḥ || k30.4 ||
babhūva samaye tasminmṛtayūthapatirvane |
suvarṇapārśva ityāptanāmā hemamayacchaviḥ || k30.5 ||
nīlaratnodarāspharamuktāhāranibhaprabhā |
abhūddṛṣṭicchaṭā yasya bhūṣaṇaṃ kānanaśriyaḥ || k30.6 ||
pravālavalliśṛṅgasya citraratnacitatvacaḥ |
tasyāścaryasudhāmbhodhilaharī ruruce ruciḥ || k30.7 ||
bodhisattvāvatārasya tasya kāntamabhūdvapuḥ |
pūrvaṃ sukṛtacitrasya lakṣaṇaṃ hi surūpatā || k30.8 ||
dīrghadṛṣṭirbabhūvāsya vayasyo vṛddhavāyasaḥ |
labdhakānveṣaṇatrāse digvilokanatatparaḥ || k30.9 ||
tau kathābhirmithaḥ prītyā vivikteṣu vijasratuḥ |
prākpuṇyairjāyate vāṇī tiraścāmapi māṇuṣī || k30.10 ||
sa kadācijjalānveṣī yathūnāthaḥ sahānugaiḥ |
taṭinyā veṇumālinyāḥ pulinaṃ samupāyayau || k30.11 ||
tasya tārataraṃ śrutvā dīrghamākrandaniśvanam |
hariṇā dudruvuḥ sarve grīvāvalanavibhramaiḥ || k30.12 ||
suvarṇapārśvastu tadā kṛpāpāśavaśīkṛtaḥ |
tatraiva niścalastasthau marmaviddha iveṣuṇā || k30.13 ||
taduddharaṇasaṃnaddhaṃ dīrghadṛṣṭiṃ vilokya tam |
kākaḥ provāca na sakhe yukto'taṃ te samudyamaḥ || k30.14 ||
puṣpopamā vipatkāle kṛtārthāḥ kuliśopamāḥ |
kṛtamete na manyante svakāyasuhṛdaḥ khalāḥ || k30.15 ||
ityasau vāryamāṇo'pi kākena karuṇākulaḥ |
avatīryāśu saritaṃ saralastamatārata(?) || k30.16 ||
vimucya bandhanānyasya sa śṛṅgābhyāmaśaṅkitaḥ |
taṃ pādapatitaṃ dīnamavadadgantumudyatam || k30.17 ||
na tvayā kathanīyo'hamatrasthaḥ kasyacitsakhe |
prārthayante suvarṇaṃ māṃ carmalubdhā hi lubdhakāḥ || k30.18 ||
ityuktastena vinayāttattathetyabhidhāya saḥ |
yayau kuṭilakākhyastaṃ praṇamya prastutastutiḥ || k30.19 ||
atrāntare narapateḥ patnī candraprabhā niśi |
svapne dadarśāsanasthaṃ mṛgaṃ saddharmavādinam || k30.20 ||
satysvapnātha sā devī prabuddhā nṛpamabravīt |
suvarṇahariṇaḥ svapne deva dṛṣṭo mayādbhutaḥ || k30.21 ||
tamahaṃ draṣṭumicchāmi sākṣādupagataṃ mṛgam |
aṅkādiva mṛgāṅkasya nirgataṃ rāhuśaṅkayā || k30.22 ||
ityuktaḥ praṇayātprīto devyā ca pṛthivīpatiḥ |
mṛgagrahāya vyasṛjatlubdhakān draviṇapradaḥ || k30.23 ||
tataḥ pratinivṛttāste vanamanviṣya lubdhakāḥ |
niṣphalāgamanakruddhaṃ sakampā jagadurnṛpam || k30.24 ||
iyatīṃ jagatī deva vicitā nicitācalaiḥ |
bhrāntā vayamaviśrāntā na labhyastadvidho mṛgaḥ || k30.25 ||
āścaryaracanākṛṣṭalocanaścārulocanaḥ |
svapnasaṃpannarūpo'sau hiraṇyahariṇaḥ kutaḥ || k30.26 ||
mano vinodane tasmin yadi deva prasīdati |
kurvantu kāñcanamṛgaṃ kuśalāḥ ke'pi śilpinaḥ || k30.27 ||
iti śrutvā sa nṛpatirdadadbahutaraṃ dhanam |
mṛgānveṣaṇasaṃkalpe babhūvābhiniveśavān || k30.28 ||
tataḥ kuṭilako'bhyetya nṛpaṃ śrutvā bahupradam |
uvāca draviṇādāne lubdhakebhyo'pi lubdhadhīḥ || k30.29 ||
prasādaḥ kriyatāṃ deva mṛgaṃ saṃdarśayāmyamah |
dṛṣṭaḥ kanakasārāṅga sāraṅgaḥ samayā vane || k30.30 ||
ityākarṇya kṣitipatiḥ praharṣitphullalocanaḥ |
bhadra saṃdarśaya kkāsau kkāsāvityavadanmuhuḥ || k30.31 ||
tamevāgresaraṃ kṛtvā mṛgamārgapradarśakam |
sasainyaḥ sa yayau svacchacchatracandrodayācalaḥ || k30.32 ||
dīrghadṛṣṭirdadarśātha kākastaruśiraḥsthitaḥ |
gajavājivrajodīrṇareṇupravāraṇaṃ vanam || k30.33 ||
suvarṇapārśvamabhyetya jagāda mṛyayūthapam |
hitamuktaṃ mayā pūrvaṃ na śrutaṃ na kṛtaṃ tvayā || k30.34 ||
sa eṣa puruṣaḥ prāptaḥ saṃnaddhaiḥ saha dhanvibhiḥ |
mayā nivāritenāpi saṃhāreṇa na tṛpyate || k30.35 ||
adhunā kka nu gantavyaṃ kiṃ kartavyaṃ bhayodbhave |
hitaṃ kimanuvartavyaṃ tulyaṃ martavyameva vā || k30.36 ||
kṛtaghnaḥ krūracaritaḥ kṣudro'yaṃ saṃghapātakah |
tvayaivātmavināśāya rakṣito viṣapādapaḥ || k30.37 ||
svaśarīrapradasyāpi saṃhāreṇa na tṛpyate |
sasattvasāgaragrāsī kṛtaghno vāḍavānalaḥ || k30.38 ||
upakāraḥ kṛtaghneṣu viśvāsaḥ kuṭulātmasu |
upadeśaśca mūrkheṣu karturdoṣāya kevalam || k30.39 ||
iti kākena kathite pratyāsanne ca pārthive |
acintayatprāptakālaṃ hitaṃ yūthasya yūthapah || k30.40 ||
subhaṭānāmiyaṃ senā vigāhedgahanaṃ mahat |
karoti matprasaṅgena muhūrtenaiva nirmṛgam || k30.41 ||
tasmātsainyapradhānasya gacchāmi svayamantikam |
ekasyaiva vadho me'stu sarve jīvantvamī mṛgāḥ || k30.42 ||
iti niścitya sa yayau samīpaṃ bhūpatermṛgaḥ |
paraprāṇaparitrāṇe tṛṇaṃ prāṇāṃ mahātmanām || k30.43 ||
tamāyāntaṃ drutaṃ dṛṣṭvā hṛṣṭaḥ kuṭulakahpuraḥ |
so'yamityāśu pāṇibhyāṃ rājñe dūre vyadarśayat || k30.44 ||
tatkṣaṇe droṇaśāpena vajreṇeva nipātinā |
karu paricyutau tasya pāpapādapapallavau || k30.45 ||
tadvṛttaṃ vismitaḥ śrutvā mṛgeṇa kathitaṃ nṛpaḥ |
abhūtkṛtaghnacarite dhikkāramukharānanaḥ || k30.46 ||
tataḥ kṣitipatiḥ prītyā parayā mṛgam |
taṃ nināya svanagarīṃ gauraveṇa garīyasā || k30.47 ||
rājadhānīmathāsādya tasmai ratnāsanaṃ nṛpaḥ |
datvā sāntaḥpurāmātyastasyāgre samupāviśat || k30.48 ||
sa bodhisattvo hariṇastasyāṃ parṣadi divyadhīḥ |
dharmaṃ dideśa yenābhūjjanaḥ śikṣāpadānvitaḥ || k30.49 ||
suvarṇapārśvaḥ sāraṅgaḥ so'yamevābhavaṃ purā |
yo'bhavatkuṭilaḥ krūro devadattaḥ sa cādhunā || k30.50 ||
iti sukṛtacitaṃ bhagavatā bhavabhītibhidā
kathitamudārasattvarucirasya tataścaritam |
praśamamayaṃ niśamya kuśalāya sa bhikṣugaṇaḥ
kimapi babhūva puṇyaparipākavivekaruciraḥ || k30.51 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ suvarṇapārśvāvadānaṃ triṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


31. kalyāṇakāryavadānam |

pratyakṣalakṣaṇaparīkṣita eṣa loke saṃlakṣyate sujanadurjanayorviśeṣaḥ |
arkaḥ prakāśaviśadaṃ vidadhāti viśvamandhīkaroti nikhilaṃ jagadandhakāraḥ || k31.1 ||
sarvajñaḥ pūrvavṛttāntamaśeṣamavalokayan |
asminneva kathārambhe bhagavān punarabravīt || k31.2 ||
nṛpaḥ pātaliputrākhye pure bhūmipuraṃdaraḥ |
abhūtpuraṃdaro nāma mandiraṃ puṇyasaṃpadaḥ || k31.3 ||
kalyāṇakārī tasyābhūtsūnurguṇagaṇonnataḥ |
akalyāṇābhidhānaścadvitīyo nirguṇaḥ sutaḥ || k31.4 ||
kalyāṇakārī sarvārthikaruṇākalpapādapaḥ |
chatrāvaśeṣāmakaroddāṇaśīlaḥ śriyaṃ pituḥ || k31.5 ||
tasmai manoramāṃ nāma puṇyaseno mahīpatiḥ |
dūtaṃ visṛjya lekhena svasutāṃ vacasā dadau || k31.6 ||
pratyāsannavivāho'tha nṛpaṃ rājasuto'bhyadhāt |
prāptaḥ pariṇayastāta saṃpratyeṣa na me mataḥ || k31.7 ||
vātsalyapeśalatayā madāttāḥ prakṛtiśriyaḥ |
mayā dānavyasaninā koṣaste śuṣirīkṛtaḥ || k31.8 ||
tasmātpravahaṇenāhaṃ samuttīrya mahodadhim |
ratnadvīpaṃ vrahāmyeva divyaratnārjanodyataḥ || k31.9 ||
divyasaṃpadamāsādya kariṣye dārasaṃgraham |
kalatramakṛtārthasya trāsanaṃ sukhasaṃpadām || k31.10 ||
iyuktvā sa pituḥ prāpya śāsanaṃ caraṇānataḥ |
jagāhe jaladhiṃ lolakallolāliṅgitāmbaram || k31.11 ||
bhrātā tamanuvajrāja saujanyavyañjano'nujaḥ |
nirguṇaḥ saguṇadveṣadrohamantarvicintayan || k31.12 ||
jyeṣṭhastamabravīdvatsa samudre karmaviplavāt |
ahaṃ grāhyastvayā skandhe jāte pravahaṇakṣaye || k31.13 ||
iti bhrātrā kṛtāśvāsastathetyūve sa taṃ śaṭhaḥ |
doṣodyataḥ praṇayitāṃ khalaḥ samavalambate || k31.14 ||
tataḥ pravāhaṇārūḍhaḥ pavanairanukūlatām |
yātaiḥ puṇyairiva prāpya ratnadvīpaṃ nṛpātmajaḥ || k31.15 ||
dovyaratnāni saṃprāpya pratyāvṛtte tataḥ śanaiḥ |
rājaputre pravahaṇaṃ drāgabhañjatprabhañjanaḥ || k31.16 ||
bhagne pravahaṇe tasmin bhrātaraṃ pūrvasaṃvidā |
sa jagrāha śaṭhaḥ kaṇṭhe taṃ bhujaṅga ivānujaḥ || k31.17 ||
karmavāteritastūrṇaṃ kūlaṃ prāpya nṛpātmajaḥ |
avāpa sahasā nidrāmāndhyaprathamadūtikām || k31.18 ||
tasya suptasya ratnāni dṛṣṭvā baddhānyathānujaḥ |
prahartuṃ vyasanacchidre krūraḥ samupacakrame || k31.19 ||
utpāṭya gāḍhabaddhasya sa tasya nayanāmbujam |
taṃ tārakaṃ bhayāmbhodhau cakāra gatatārakam || k31.20 ||
āttaratne gate tasmin vājasūnurgatadyutiḥ |
mātaṅgonmūlitāmbhoja ivābhūtkamalākaraḥ || k31.21 ||
sa babhūva nirālokaḥ śokatīvratamovṛtaḥ |
kṛṣṇapakṣakṣapārambha iva sūryenduvarjitaḥ || k31.22 ||
atrāntare samāyātastaṃ deśaṃ gokuśādhipaḥ |
rājaputraṃ vilokyāndhamabhūtsaṃkrāntatadvyathah || k31.23 ||
sa taṃ nītvā svanilayaṃ paricaryāparaḥ pārḥ |
tasyāsīdguṇasaujanyasnehāveśavaśīkṛtaḥ || k31.24 ||
tatra śokasujāṃ śāntyai sadā cetovinodinīm |
vīṇāṃ svarapravīṇo'sau pūrvābhyastamavādayat || k31.25 ||
satsaṃgamaḥ pṛthuvivekakathābhoyogaḥ
kāvyāsavaḥ priyasuhṛtpraṇayo vihāraḥ |
vīṇāsvanahkusumakāntavanāntavāsaḥ
śokāgnitaptamanasāmamṛtāvagāhaḥ || k31.26 ||
tasya gopapateḥ patnī tīgavīṇāvicakṣaṇā |
paśyantī rājatanayaṃ prayayau sābhilāṣatām || k31.27 ||
kṛtopadeśā satataṃ kuṭilā vīṇayeva sā |
mūrcchantī navarāgeṇa sotkaṇṭhā samacintayat || k31.28 ||
subhago'yaṃ mamātīva dṛśi citte ca cartate |
nivartate na me tāpaḥ premṇi cenna pravartate || k31.29 ||
dhanyeyaṃ nakhasaṃpātaiḥ kkaṇantī rāgiṇīmuhuḥ |
yātāsya vallakī puṇyairaṅkārohaṇayogyatām || k31.30 ||
iti saṃcintya sā svairaṃ tamuvāca savibhamam |
spṛśantī tatkarāmbhojaṃ sakampakarapallavā || k31.31 ||
lalanāsulabhāṃ lajjāṃ mamedaṃ tvadgataṃ manaḥ |
akṛtajña iva prītiṃ na saṃsmarati mānada || k31.32 ||
na śīlaṃ na kulācāraṃ nābhimānaṃ na saṃśayam |
apekṣante smarakṣiptā vailakṣyarahitāḥ striyaḥ || k31.33 ||
praṇayānmama saṅkalpaṃ saphalaṃ kartumarhasi |
bhavanti mānitāḥ prītyai devatā iva yoṣitaḥ || k31.34 ||
iti tasya vacaḥ śrutvā bhinnasvaraviśṛṅkhalam |
capalāṃ rājaputrastāṃ trastāntaḥkarṇo'vadat || k31.35 ||
neyaṃ mātaḥ samucitā sataḥ śīlaparicyutiḥ |
dhikkilbiṣaviṣaspṛṣṭaḥ naṣṭaśīlasya jīvitam || k31.36 ||
parāṅganāpariṣvaṅgamaṅgairaṅgīkaroti yaḥ |
āliṅgati pataṅgo'yaṃ narakāgniśikhāṃ punah || k31.37 ||
paropakāraniratāhparadāranirādarāḥ |
ye'pyahiṃsāvyasaninaste jīvanti mṛtāḥ pare || k31.38 ||
iti tenoktamākarṇya sābhūdbhagnamanorathā |
nidhanābhyadhikaḥ prītipratiṣedho hi yoṣitām || k31.39 ||
tataḥ svapatimabhyetya bhujaṅgī bhaṅgamāgate |
manorathe manyuviṣaṃ vamantīva jagāda sā || k31.40 ||
paravatsalatā sādho doṣāya saralasya te |
ko hyavijñātaśīlānāṃ svādhīnīkurute gṛham || k31.41 ||
pareṣu bhṛśamāśvāsaṃ spṛśataste na śobhanam |
guptaṃ cittaṃ ca vittaṃ ca jano jānāti kasya kaḥ || k31.42 ||
paradārasahasrākṣastvayāndhaḥ sa gṛhe dhṛtaḥ |
dīnāndhajanavātsalyātpaśya tasyocitaṃ phalam || k31.43 ||
adyāhaṃ tena vijane saṃgame bhṛśamarthitā |
yadyasya nayane syātāṃ syātkathaṃ me palāyanam || k31.44 ||
ityuktaḥ sa tayā kopāttapto gopapatirbhṛśam |
dvare niṣkāsya taṃ cakre gehaṃ cittaṃ ca śītalam || k31.45 ||
pitā tyajati yatputraṃ suhṛnmitraṃ nihanti yat |
bandhucchedāsidhārāṇāṃ taddārāṇāṃ vijṛmbhitam || k31.46 ||
bhruvordṛśoryatkauṭilyaṃ yattaikṣṇyaṃ yacca cāpalam |
kucayoryacca kāṭhinyaṃ tatsarvaṃ hṛdi yoṣitām || k31.47 ||
kalyāṇakārisārthena pathaḥ saṃtāritaṃ śanaiḥ |
pitari tridivaṃ yāte śuśrāva bhrātaraṃ nṛpam || k31.48 ||
sa puraṃ puṇyasenasya śvaśurasya mahīpateḥ |
kālena prāppa dūrādhvakleśapraśamabāndhavam || k31.49 ||
atrāntare sutā tatra mahībharturmanoramā |
vācā dattā purā yasmai tasminnabdhicyute śrute || k31.50 ||
āhūteṣu narendreṣu niviṣṭeṣu yathākramam |
āruhya ratnaśibikāṃ svayaṃvarabhuvaṃ yayau || k31.51 ||
vilokayantī bhūpālān sā śanaiścalalocanā |
yadṛcchayāgataṃ tatra rājaputraṃ dadarśa tam || k31.52 ||
andho'pi tasyāḥ sahasāsa yayau priyatāṃ dṛśoḥ |
grahamadhye kumudvatyā meghāndho'pi priyaḥ śaśī || k31.53 ||
nṛpeṣu pratiyāteṣu vilakṣeṣvaphalāgamāt |
mahīpatisutāntastaṃ vavre guṇavinirgatam || k31.54 ||
sāpu hāraṃ parikṣipya kaṇṭhe tasyāyatekṣaṇā |
śanakaurmadhurālāpā tvadvaśāsmītyuvāca tam || k31.55 ||
strīvṛttacakitaḥ so'pi vijane tāmabhāṣata |
prajñādaridrayā nedaṃ striyā yuktaṃ kṛtaṃ tvayā || k31.56 ||
smarasauhārdamitreṣu padmanetreṣu rājasu |
sthiteṣu vandhyajanmāndhaḥ kasmādasmi vṛtastvayā || k31.57 ||
anyavakrkrāvalokinyo jāyāścakṣuṣmatāmapi |
vadhūḥ kiṃ punarandhasya dine'pyanyābhisārikā || k31.58 ||
lalanābhirna me kṛtyaṃ pratyayastāsu nāsti me |
kulakūlanipātinyo nimnagāḥ kuṭilāḥ stiyaḥ || k31.59 ||
ityuktā tena paruṣaṃ lajjālolā nṛpātmajā |
tamūce nātha sarvatra na śaṅkāṃ kartumarhasi || k31.60 ||
dṛṣṭadoṣaḥ kkacinnāryāṃ yadi tvamatiśaṅkitaḥ |
aduṣṭāpi tvayā nāma tadvyāptā kriyate katham || k31.61 ||
tvayyeva yadi me prītirananyaśaraṇaṃ manaḥ |
tena satyena te netramekaṃ bhavatu nirmalam || k31.62 ||
ityuktamātre sudṛśā dakṣiṇaṃ tasya lecanam |
satyānubhāvenābhūttatpraphullakamalopamam || k31.63 ||
rājaputraḥ prahṛṣṭo'tha tāṃ prasādya silocanām |
uvāca tanmukhāmbhojalāvaṇyaguṇacismitḥ || k31.64 ||
kalyāṇakārī subhagaḥ sa evāhaṃ nṛpātmajaḥ |
yasmau purā tvaṃ guruṇā vacasā pratipāditā || k31.65 ||
sa evāhaṃ yadi paraṃ nirvairaḥ pāṭane dṛśaḥ |
tena satyena nayanaṃ svasthaṃ bhavatu me'param || k31.66 ||
satyopayācaneneti sahasaivāsya locanam |
dvitīyamapi vaimalyamavāpa saha cetasā || k31.67 ||
tato viditavṛttena puṇyasenena bhūbhujā |
kṛtasāhāyyakaḥ prāpa svarājya sa priyāsakhaḥ || k31.68 ||
kalyāṇkārī yaḥ so'haṃ devadattaḥ sa cānujaḥ |
tena pūrvānubhāvena tadvidho'dyāpi vartate || k31.69 ||
ityudāramupakāranirmalaṃ bodhisattvacaritaṃ niśamya te |
bhikṣavaḥ khalviceṣṭitaṃ ca tattulyamapratimavismayaṃ yayuḥ || k31.70 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ kalyāṇākāryavadānamekatriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


32. viśākhāvadānam |

vāmāḥ sajjanavāmāḥ prāyeṇa bhavanti nīrogiṇyaḥ |
timironmukhī sarāgā kṣipati raviṃ bhūdharātsaṃdhyā || k32.1 ||
devadattasya carite bahujanmāntarāśraye |
kathite'pi punaḥ prāha bhagavān jñānasāgaraḥ || k32.2 ||
purā kaliṅgaviṣaye nṛpatiḥ śatrubhaṅgakṛt |
śrīmānabhūdaśokākhyaḥ prakhyātāsaṃkhyavikramaḥ || k32.3 ||
tasya śākhaḥ praśākhaśca catvāraḥ sadṛśāḥ sutāḥ |
anuśākho viśākhaścetyabhavan bhuvi viśrutāḥ || k32.4 ||
tāruṇyamattāste pitrā sapatnīkāḥ pravāsitāh |
savikāranikāreṇa putrasneho'pi naśyati || k32.5 ||
te śanaiḥ kṣīṇapātheyā durdaśāmiva duḥsahām |
vikaṭāmaṭavīṃ prāpyaṃ kṣutkṣāmāḥ samacintayan || k32.6 ||
etāḥ striyo vipatkāle gulphabandhanaśṛṅkhalāḥ |
kṛcchralabdhe ca bhāginyaḥ sthitāḥ parṇāśane'pi naḥ || k32.7 ||
iti cintayatāmāsītteṣāṃ strībadhaniścayah |
durdaśādagdhabhāgyānāṃ ghorā saṃjāyate matiḥ || k32.8 ||
teṣāṃ madhyādviśākhastu pāpasaṃkalpaśaṅkitaḥ |
bhāryāmādāya kṛpayā palāyya prayayau pṛthak || k32.9 ||
sā kalaṅkavatī nāma bhṛśaṃ vaiklavyamāgatā |
dūrādhvadhāvanaśrāntā mūrcchitā nyapatadbhuvi || k32.10 ||
tataḥ sā karuṇārdreṇa bhartrā prāṇakṣayakṣaṇe |
śarāvedhasamudbhūtaṃ pāyitā nijaśoṇitam || k32.11 ||
tāṃ raktapānasaṃprāptajīvitāṃ sattvasāgaraḥ |
svāṅgaṃ niṣkṛtya māṃsena prāṇavṛttimakārayat || k32.12 ||
nirjalaṃ ghorakāntāraṃ samuttīrya krameṇa tau |
pracchāyapādapaśyāmaṃ prāptau girinadītaṭam || k32.13 ||
viśrāntayostayostatra kṛttapādakaro naraḥ |
tīvrākrandī nadīvegenohyamānaḥ samāyayau || k32.14 ||
dṛṣṭvaiva karuṇāśliṣṭaḥ kaṣṭāṃ vipadamāśritaḥ |
vigāhya saritaṃ dorbhyāṃ viśākhastamatārayat || k32.15 ||
tataḥ pratyāgataprāṇāṃ toyamūlaphalādibhiḥ |
sa taṃ cakre dinaireva saṃrūḍhacchedanirvyatham || k32.16 ||
svastho'pi gativaikalyānnaiva gantuṃ kkacitkṣamaḥ |
sa tasthaui tatra tatpatnyā kāle kalitabhojanah || k32.17 ||
rājaputrastu jāyāyāmabhūdviralasaṃgamaḥ |
siṃhālparatayaḥ śūrāḥ prāyeṇa vijigīṣavaḥ || k32.18 ||
divyauṣadhirasāhāraparipūrṇatanuḥ śanaiḥ |
tatpatnī vikalāyāsmau cakāra surataspṛhām || k32.19 ||
snehena nopalipyante na badhyante guṇena ca |
gurave na ca sajjanti svecchasparśasukhāḥ striyaḥ || k32.20 ||
sā tena niśi niḥśabdaṃ ramamāṇā ghanastanī |
niḥśaṅkasuratātṛptā patiṃ vighnamamanyata || k32.21 ||
sā patyuḥ svairiṇī tena vidadhe vadhasaṃvidam |
pāpeṣu śikṣākuśalāḥ kaluṣāḥ kila yoṣitaḥ || k32.22 ||
sā śiraḥśūlamatulaṃ vadantī svasya chadmanā |
cakre likhitapāpāsyā lalāṭe paṭṭabandhanam || k32.23 ||
tasyāḥ śirorujāṃ tīvrāṃ vyatītaḥ pārthivātmajaḥ |
kāruṇyāttatpratīkāre tāṃ tāṃ yuktimacintayat || k32.24 ||
viṣādacintāstimitaṃ śvasantaṃ sā jagādtam |
śītārtakūjadbhramarā himamlāneva padminī || k32.25 ||
evaṃvidhaṃ me kanyāyāhśiraḥśūlaṃ purābhavat |
pāṣāṇabhedalepena bhiṣagbhiśca nivāritam || k32.26 ||
pāṣāṇabhedavyāpto'yaṃ prāgbhāgo'symahībhṛtaḥ |
rājjvāvatīrya bhavatā gṛhyatāṃ yadi śakyate || k32.27 ||
dhārayiṣyāmi pāṇibhyāmahamālambanaṃ tava |
ityuktaḥ praṇayātpatnayā tathetyāha nṛpātmajaḥ || k32.28 ||
tatpāṇidhṛtarajjvātha vihitālambanaḥ śanaiḥ |
so'vatīrṇaḥ śilāsphālagarjadgirinadītaṭam || k32.29 ||
bheṣajādānasaṃsaktaḥ saṃtyaktālambanastayā |
sa papāta mahāśvabhre strīcittacapalāmbhasi || k32.30 ||
śubhasya karmaṇaḥ śeṣādabhagnatanureva saḥ |
uhyamānaḥ pravāheṇa dhīraścitamacintayat || k32.31 ||
nārīcittābhamāvartaṃ darśayantyā nijāśayam |
mama strīniyame nūnamupadeśaḥ kṛto'nayā || k32.32 ||
durvijñeyāḥ pratatamatibhiḥ svapnasaṃkalpakalpā
rāgadveṣavyasanaviṣamāyāsavinyāsasaktāḥ |
kāmātkāmī sakalajanatāmohane saṃpravṛttāḥ
pātāyaiva kṣasṇaparicitasyāpi māyāḥ striyaśca || k32.33 ||
iti saṃcintayanneva nadīvegena bhūyasā |
prāpitaḥ sukṛteneva sa purīṃ puṣkarāvatīm || k32.34 ||
tasminnavasare tatra niṣputre nṛpatau mṛte |
nimittajñairmahāmātyairgṛhītaḥ sa sulakṣaṇaḥ || k32.35 ||
abhiṣiktaḥ sa taistatra vidhivanmaṅgalodakaiḥ |
abhūdvivāhavidveṣī dṛṣṭastrīcaritādbhutaḥ || k32.36 ||
kalaṅkavatyapi girau bodhisattvavivarjite |
mandvīryauṣadhiḥ kāle vṛtticchedākulābhavat || k32.37 ||
skandhe bhagnāṅgamāropya sā grāmapuravartmasu |
janaṃ pativratāsmīti girā bhikṣāmayācata || k32.38 ||
parivratāgauraveṇa sarvastasyau dadau bahu |
mithyāśīlapravādo'pi sūte vipadi saṃpadam || k32.39 ||
aṭantī sā śanaiḥ prāptā nagarīṃ puṣkarāvatīm |
satīti vanditā sarvairvṛpadvārāntike yayau || k32.40 ||
rājā strīvṛttavidveṣī vandate tu pativratām |
iti saṃcintya tadbhaktyā nṛpaṃ prāha puhohitaḥ || k32.41 ||
dūredeśāntarāddeva prāptā kāpi pativratā |
yayeyaṃ caraṇanyāsairbhūtadhātrī pavitritā || k32.42 ||
he deva paśya tāṃ śādhvīṃ skandhāropitabhartṛkām |
pativratāpraṇāmena puṃsāmāyurvivardhate || k32.43 ||
nṛpaḥ purohiteneti tatsaṃdarśanamarthitaḥ |
tamuvāca na jānīṣe brāhmaṇaḥ sarale bhavān || k32.44 ||
snigdhā strīti pravādo'yaṃ nirvyājeti matibhramaḥ |
satīti vyomapuṣpāptiḥ pāpā strīti na saṃśayaḥ || k32.45 ||
niṣphalāścagnurohiṇyaḥ saralā janasaṃgame |
nāryo vetasavallarya iva nirmūlabandhanāḥ || k32.46 ||
bhedadrohaikaśīlābhyo duḥśīlābhyaḥ svabhāvataḥ |
namaḥ strībhyo namaḥ strībhyo namaḥ strībhyo namo namah || k32.47 ||
dṛṣṭastrīvṛttadoṣāya taccintāvyathitānmane |
apyeṣā pṛthivī mahyaṃ ratnapūrṇā na rocate || k32.48 ||
nagamṛgavadhūmugdhāstīkṣṇāḥ paraṃ paravañcane
tanuvitaraṇe saktāḥ puṃsāṃ haranti ca jīvitam |
dadhati ca bhayaṃ puṣpāyāte pibanti ca pāvakaṃ
saralakuṭilā naiva jñātā vicāraśataiḥ striyaḥ || k32.49 ||
tathāpi yadi nirbandhastava paśyāmi tāṃ striyam |
ityuktvā harmyamāruhya tāṃ dadarśa nareśvaraḥ || k32.50 ||
tāmeva sa parijñāya pāpāṃ kṛttāṅgasaṅginīm |
sacivebhyaḥ kṣitipatistadvṛttāntaṃ nyavedayat || k32.51 ||
nṛpaṃ sāpi parijñāya pāpā kṣaṇamadhomukhī |
nirastā prayayau tūrṇaṃ pihitaśravaṇairjanaiḥ || k32.52 ||
viśākhanāmā naranāthasūnuḥ so'haṃ vadhūḥ sāsya ca devadattaḥ |
śrutvetivṛttaṃ kathitaṃ jinena bhikṣuvrajastaccaritaṃ nininda || k32.53 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ viśākhāvadānaṃ dvātriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


33. nandīpanandāvadānam |

sa ko'pi puṇyapraśamānubhāvaḥ śuddhātmanāmastyamṛtasvabhāvaḥ |
yasya prabhāveṇa bhavanti sadyaḥ krūrā api krodhaviṣapramuktāḥ || k33.1 ||
purā tathāgate jetavanārāmavihāriṇi |
tadājñayā bhikṣugaṇe girikānanacāriṇi || k33.2 ||
sumerupariṣaṇḍāyāṃ sthitvā dhyānaparāyaṇāḥ |
āyayurbhikṣavaḥ pāṇḍuvicchāyavadanāḥ kṛśāḥ || k33.3 ||
te'tha kṛtvā bhagavataḥ pādapadmābhivandanām |
ūcire bhikṣubhiḥ pṛṣṭā dehadaurbalyakāraṇam || k33.4 ||
sumeruṃ triguṇāvṛttyā veṣṭayitvā vyavasthitau |
adṛṣṭau vainateyasya nāgau nandopanandakau || k33.5 ||
tau sadā trividhocchvāsaṃ sṛjataḥ kīrṇapāvakam |
bhavanti bhasma sparśena sahasaiva śilā api || k33.6 ||
vayaṃ tadviṣaniśvāsairnidagdhā dhyānayoginaḥ |
vivarṇavadanacchāyāhkevalaṃ kṛśatāṃ gatāḥ || k33.7 ||
iti taiḥ kathite śāstā bhikṣusaṃghārthitaḥ puraḥ |
nāgayordamane yogyaṃ maudgalyāyanamādiśat || k33.8 ||
sa sumeruṃ samāsādya śṛṅgairāliṅgitāmbaram |
suptau dadarśa nāgendrau yogenāntarhitākṛtiḥ || k33.9 ||
yodhyamānau śanaistena bubudhāte yadā na tau |
