tadā mahānāgavapurbhūtvā sa samaveṣṭayat || k33.10 ||
prabuddhau pīḍitau tena dṛṣṭvā taṃ bhīṣaṇākṛtim |
viṃdrutau nararūpeṇa tasthaturbhayavihvalau || k33.11 ||
nāgarūpaṃ parityajya kṛtvā rūpaṃ svakaṃ tataḥ |
palāyamānāvanyonyaṃ tau maudgalyāyano'vadat || k33.12 ||
nāgau kka gamyate tūrṇa bhayaṃ saṃtyajyatāmidam |
na sa nāgaḥ sthitastatra yena vidrāvitau yuvām || k33.13 ||
sarvathā yadi nastyea tadbhayādyivayordhṛtiḥ |
kriyate kiṃ śaraṇyasya na buddhasyābhivandanam || k33.14 ||
iti tenoktamākarṇya vinayāttau tamūcatuḥ |
prasādaḥ kriyatāmārya bhagavaddarśanena nau || k33.15 ||
iti bravāṇau nāgendrau nītvā bhagavato'ntikam |
sa ta dvṛttāntamāvedya praṇamya samupāviśat || k33.16 ||
bhagavānatha nāgendrau babhāṣe śaraṇāgatau |
praṇāmaṃ cakratū ratnaprabhābhūṣītabhūtalau || k33.17 ||
śikṣāpadāni saṃprāpya sarvabhūtābhayapradau |
śaraṇaprāptisamayādadhunā nirbhayau yuvām || k33.18 ||
ityālokanamātreṇa doṣadveṣavivarjitau |
kṛtau bhagavatā samyakjagmatustaṃ praṇamya tau || k33.19 ||
saṃdarśanenaiva mahāśayānāṃ prabhāpadeśena śarīralagnaiḥ |
sīṃsrā api dveṣaviṣoṣmataptāḥ śamāmṛtaiḥ śītalatāṃ vrajanti || k33.20 ||
tatpūrvajanmavṛttāṇtaṃ bhikṣubhirbhagavān jinaḥ |
prabhāvavismayātpṛṣṭaḥ sarvadarśī jagādā tān || k33.21 ||
kṛkirnāma purā śrīmān vārāṇasyāṃ nareśvaraḥ |
kāśyapākhyādbhagavataḥ prāptavān dharmaśāsanam || k33.22 ||
amātyayoḥ sa vinyasya rājyaṃ nandopanandayoḥ |
babhūva bodhisaṃsaktaḥ satyadarśananirvṛtaḥ || k33.23 ||
dharmadahrmamayaṃ rājyaṃ kṛtvā tau tasya mantriṇau |
sarvopakaraṇairyuktaṃ vihāraṃ kāśyapāya ca || k33.24 ||
kālena jātau nāgendrāvetau nandopanandakau |
vihārārpaṇapuṇyena padaṃ merurabhūttayoḥ || k33.25 ||
iti jinanigaditaphaṇivaracaritaṃ paratanupariṇatiparicitasukṛtam |
śrutavati śamanayamuniparinivahe viṣadharaniyamanaguṇanutirudabhūt || k33.26 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ nandopanandāvadānaṃ trayastriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


34. gṛhapatisudattāvadānam |

dattaḥ parahitabhāvanayā yadu tanudhanakaṇaśeṣaḥ |
aparikṣayaguṇakalpanayā bhavati supuṇyaviśeṣaḥ || k34.1 ||
atha vyatīte kasmiṃścitkāle bhagavato'ntike |
nandopanandayordharmadeśanāṃ śrotumāyayau || k34.2 ||
rājā prasenajiddraṣṭuṃ bhagavantamupāgataḥ |
tābhyāmakṛtasatkārapraṇāmaḥ komapāyayau || k34.3 ||
sa praṇamya jinaṃ gatvā pradadhyau nigrahaṃ tayoḥ |
śastravṛṣṭiṃ saṃsṛjantau tau ca vyomnā samāgatau || k34.4 ||
sarvajñapreṣitastūraṇaṃ maudgalyāyana etya tām |
śastravṛṣṭiṃ narapateścakre padmotpalāvalim || k34.5 ||
punargatvā bhagavataḥ samīpaṃ pṛthivīpatiḥ |
saṃprāptau kṣamayāmāsa tasyādeśātphaṇīśvarau || k34.6 ||
athārthitaḥ pārthivena bhagavān rājamandiram |
bhaktipūtaṃ yayau bhoktuṃ bhaktaṃ bhikṣugaṇaiḥ saha || k34.7 ||
bhakṣyeṣu pacyamāneṣu rātrau tatrāgnivilpavaḥ |
jāto jinaprabhāveṇa sahasā śāntimāyayau || k34.8 ||
bhuktvā gate bhagavati kṣitipaḥ svapure'bhyadhāt |
jvalanajvālanaṃ rātrau vārayan daṇḍasaṃvidā || k34.9 ||
atrāṇtare gṛhapatiḥ sudattasyātmaji yuvā |
mithyādoṣadṛddhibalo nāma rājñābhighātitaḥ || k34.10 ||
pūrvaṃ bhagavataḥ śāstuḥ śāsanānugraheṇa saḥ |
labdhajñānadhṛtiḥ putraduḥkhe'pyāsīdaduḥkhitaḥ || k34.11 ||
aputraḥ svadhanaṃ bhūri dīnebhyaḥ pratipādya saḥ |
cakārātirasādekapaṇaśeṣaṃ śanaiḥ śriyaḥ || k34.12 ||
paṇalābhakṛtāśeṣadharmaḥ svalpaprado'tha saḥ |
abhūdgṛhī sudattākhyo gṛhaṃ hi svaplamucyate || k34.13 ||
kadāciddarśanāyātaṃ bhagavān purataḥ sthitam |
taṃ svalpadānanāmnaiva lajjitaṃ dayayāvadat || k34.14 ||
alpadānaṃ gṛhapaterna lajjāṃ kartumarhasi |
yāti śraddhārpito dānakaṇaḥ kanakaśailatām || k34.15 ||
purā bahutaraṃ dattaṃ velamena dvijanmanā |
śraddhāvirahasāmānyānna tathā vṛddhimāyayau || k34.16 ||
yaḥ sarvaṃ bhojayedbhaktyā jambudvīpagataṃ janam |
yaścaikaṃ bodhisaṃyuktaṃ tasya puṇyaṃ tato'dhikam || k34.17 ||
iti vākyaṃ bhagavatastathyaṃ śrutvābhinandya ca |
nijagehaṃ gṛhapatiḥ prayayau praṇipatya tam || k34.18 ||
dīpaṃ datvā paṭhan rātrau tatarbuddhānuśāsanam |
daṇḍāya rājapuruṣaiḥ sa nīto'gnipravartanāt || k34.19 ||
daṇḍasya saṃbhavādbaddhaṃ bandhanāgāravartinam |
taṃ draṣṭumāyayurdevā rātrau śakrabrahmādayaḥ || k34.20 ||
sa tairdhanaṃ gṝhāṇeti prārthito nāgrahīdyadā |
tadā dharmopadeśo'yaṃ pravṛttastsya mandire || k34.21 ||
rājāpi tatprabhāveṇa dṛṣṭvā prajvālitaṃ puram |
muktvā taṃ bandhanāgārānna dadarśa jalaṃ kkacit || k34.22 ||
sa gataḥ sugataṃ draṣṭuṃ kadācittatpuraḥ sthitaḥ |
nṛpaṃ jinapraṇāmāya prāptaṃ paścādvyalokayat || k34.23 ||
agre bhagavataścakre praṇayaṃ na sa bhūpateḥ |
jagatpūjyasya purataḥ pūjāmarhati nāparaḥ || k34.24 ||
jinamāmantrya nṛpatiḥ praṇataḥ svapuraṃ gataḥ |
vivāsanaṃ gṛhapaterādideśa nijātpurāt || k34.25 ||
prasādinī sudattasya devatā nṛpatiṃ tataḥ |
kṣudrajantubhirutsṛṣṭaiścakre daṃśaviṣākulam || k34.26 ||
sa trastastairnṛpaḥ prāptaḥ sāmātyāntaḥ purānugaḥ |
gatvā prasādayāmāsa sudattaṃ jinaśāsanāt || k34.27 ||
iti sa satatapratyāsattyā parāmṛtanirbharaṃ
bhagavaduditaṃ śāntiṃ bheje sudattagṛhādhipaḥ |
svamiva labhate vighnayāsapravāsavivarjitaṃ
vimalamanasāmantevāsī vivekamahānidhim || k34.28 ||

iti kṣemendravicaritāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ gṛhapatisudattāvadānaṃ caturstriśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


35. ghoṣīlāvadānam |

phalaṃ samānaṃ labhate sa dāturyāti kṣaṇaṃ dānasahāyatāṃ yaḥ |
paropakārapraṇayodyatānāṃ nāpuṇyakarmā sacivatvameti || k35.1 ||
ramye purā bhagavati śrāvastyāṃ jetakānane |
anāthapiṇḍadārāmavihārādhirate jine || k35.2 ||
kauśāmbyāṃ vatsanṛpatirbabhūvodayanābhidhaḥ |
gāyatyadyāṣi yatkīrtirvidyādharavadhūjanaḥ || k35.3 ||
abhavadviṣaye tasya karmāntākarajīvanaḥ |
gṛhasthaḥ sudhano nāma dhanādhānavicakṣaṇaḥ || k35.4 ||
nṛpaḥ kadāciṃdāsthāne taṃ kāryārthinamāgatam |
vacasaiva parijñāya jagādvihitādaraḥ || k35.5 ||
jānāmyahaṃ gṛhapate hiraṇyopacitasvaram |
vipulaḥ saṃcayajñasya suvarṇanidhirasti te || k35.6 ||
ityuktaḥ sasmitaṃ rājñā sa tamūce kṛtāñjaliḥ |
satyamastyeva me rājan gṛhe kāñcanasaṃcayaḥ || k35.7 ||
sadvṛttacintānirate deve vātsalyapeśale |
kiṃ vā nāsti janāsyāsya pitari tvayi goptari || k35.8 ||
dhanino yāntyadhanatāṃ nidhanaṃ yānti cādhanāḥ |
vyāghratāṃ yāti dedbhūbhṛdāmiṣāghrāṇanirghṛṇaḥ || k35.9 ||
niḥsaṅkairarjyate vittamarjitaṃ ca vibhajyate |
vibhaktaṃ bhujyate spaṣṭaṃ janairdharmadhane nṛpe || k35.10 ||
upapannaṃ vacaḥ śrutvā dhīmatastasya bhūpatiḥ |
tamūce daśanodyotaiḥ prasādaṃ darśayanniva || k35.11 ||
matimān kāryasacivastvaṃ me bhavitumarhasi |
dhīdhūryaistvadvidhaireva dhāryate dharaṇībharaḥ || k35.12 ||
iti rājavacaḥ śrutvā śudhanastamabhāṣata |
navayaṃ rājasevāsu sabhāmaṇḍalapaṇḍitāḥ || k35.13 ||
svācchandyodyānavicchedaḥ sunidrāsukhavikrayaḥ |
sevā hi puṃsāṃ saṃsāraduḥkhadainyabhayaṃkaraḥ || k35.14 ||
īśvarairmastakanyastacaraṇaḥ kṛtakṛtyatām |
pādapīṭha ivāyāti sevako'haṃ sthitaḥ sadā || k35.15 ||
sevāyāsaprayāsena prāptānāmapi saṃpadām |
saṃbhogaḥ piśunāsaṅgī prabhubhrūbhangabhaṅguraḥ || k35.16 ||
etāśca nāvatiṣṭhante prayatnena dhṛtā api |
darpogradurgrahagrāhadurgrahā nṛpasaṃpadaḥ || k35.17 ||
kṣaṇaṃ navanavāśliṣaviśeṣapraṇayodyatāḥ |
avācavāraramaṇīramaṇīyā vibhūtayaḥ || k35.18 ||
ityuktapratiṣedho'pi sa rājñā sacivaḥ kṛtaḥ |
atikrāmati ko nāma prabhaviṣṇoḥ samīhitam || k35.19 ||
prāptaprauḍhapadaṃ rājñā nītaṃ sarvādhikāritām |
vidveṣadūṣitāḥ sarve mantriṇastaṃ na sehire || k35.20 ||
dharmajijñāsayā rājñā piśunapreritena saḥ |
naivākarodasatkāryaṃ niyukto'pi puna punaḥ || k35.21 ||
āghātaṃ prekṣitaḥ so'tha mithyākopena bhūbhujā |
tathāpyadharmasaṃyuktaṃ na śāsanamatanyata || k35.22 ||
ekajanmasukhāyaiva bahujanmaśatārditam |
na sādhuninditaṃ karma karomīti jagāda saḥ || k35.23 ||
bhayadharmopadhāśuddhaḥ pratimuktaḥ sa bhūbhujā |
dānasatramavicchinnamakarodakhilārthinām || k35.24 ||
sarvatra viśrute tasya dānasatre yaśasvinah |
kalpavṛkṣādaraḥ puṃsāṃ paraṃ pratanutāṃ yayau || k35.25 ||
atrāntare munigaṇāstīrthārthā dakṣiṇāpathāt |
āgatā kṛcchrakāntāramaviśannirjanaṃ vanam || k35.26 ||
tatra tṛṣṇāturāḥ sarve mūrcchitāḥ śayanāśritāḥ |
ayācanta jalaṃ mohāduccairniścetanānapi || k35.27 ||
devagandharvanāgebhyaḥ kaścidyo'tra dayāmbudhiḥ |
sthitaḥ prayacchatu jalaṃ so'smākamiti te'bruvan || k35.28 ||
tataḥ sa ratnakeyūrakkanatkaṅkaṇasaddhvaniḥ |
bhujasthamemabhṛṅgārastarumadhyādviniryayau || k35.29 ||
te tasmādamṛtāsvādaṃ pāṇipadmāvanāmitāt |
ākaṇṭhaṃ salilaṃ pītvā jahṛṣurlabdhajīvitāḥ || k35.30 ||
prārthitaḥ punarabhyetya papracchuste savismayāḥ |
adṛśyavṛkṣanilayādudbhūtaṃ ko bhavāniti || k35.31 ||
so'bravīdviśrutayaśāḥ śrāvastyāmāśayaḥ śriyaḥ |
anāthapiṇḍado nāma sarvado'sti gṛhādhipaḥ || k35.32 ||
saucikena mayā pūrvaṃ tadgṛhāntikavāsinā |
bhujamudyabhya tadveśma darśitaṃ nityamarthinām || k35.33 ||
tena puṇyena devatvaṃ prāpto'tra viharāmyaham |
bāhurmaṃ vibhātyeṣa dakṣiṇaḥ so'rthidakṣiṇaḥ || k35.34 ||
te tamāmantrya munayaḥ punaḥ saṃprasthitā vane |
kṣudhitāḥ snigdhasacchāyaṃ dadṛśuḥ pādapaṃ param || k35.35 ||
tamapyuccairayācanta bhojanaṃ tadvadeva te |
uccacāra ca gambhīrā vāṇī vismayakāriṇī || k35.36 ||
atra puṣkariṇītīre droṇyāṃ divyānnabhojanam |
saṃpūrṇamasti tadgatvā bhujyatāṃ yadabhīpsitam || k35.37 ||
iti tena samādiṣṭaṃ bhuktvā te divyabhojanam |
ko bhavaniti papracchustaṃ divyatarusaṃśrayam || k35.38 ||
so'pyācacakṣe śrāvastyāṃ gṛhastho'nāthapiṇḍadaḥ |
asti tasyāhamabhavaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ saṃghahojane || k35.39 ||
caturaḥ paricaryāyāṃ dadhikumbhapracārakaḥ |
tadbhojanānte saṃprāpte svalpaśeṣānnabhojanaḥ || k35.40 ||
bhikṣuṇāṃ gauraveccāraṃ dṛṣṭvāhaṃ rājabhojanam |
ātmanaścānnamakṣāramabhavaṃ khinnamānasaḥ || k35.41 ||
anāthapiṇḍadagirā bhojane gauravāśayā |
tato mayāṣṭāṅgayuktaṃ gṛhītaṃ poṣadhaṃ vratam || k35.42 ||
asamāptavratenātha bhuktaṃ laulyānmayā niśi |
tenāhamabhavaṃ loke vijñātaḥ khaṇḍapoṣadhaḥ || k35.43 ||
khaṇḍenāpi vratenāhaṃ devaputratvamāgataḥ |
iti te tadvacaḥ śrutvā munayo vismitā yayuḥ || k35.44 ||
acintayan vrajantaste tīvreṇa tapasā vayam |
suciraṃ kevalaṃ kliṣṭāṃ nādyāpi kuśalaspṛśaḥ || k35.45 ||
adhunā poṣadhaṃ prāptuṃ vratameva yatāmahe |
nirapāyasukhopāye svahite kasya nādaraḥ || k35.46 ||
iti saṃcintayantaste kauśāmbīmabhito gatāḥ |
sudhanasya gṛhaṃ prāpurviśrutaṃ gṛhamedhinaḥ || k35.47 ||
tatra tena kṛtātithyā nivedyāsmai tadadbhutam |
anāthapiṇḍadaṃ draṣṭuṃ tenaiva sahitā yayuḥ || k35.48 ||
śrāvastyāṃ te samāsādya pūjitāstena sādaram |
asmai nyavedayan sarvaṃ yathādṛṣṭaṃ yathāśrutam || k35.49 ||
sa tān vratārthina sarvān suhṛdaṃ sudhanaṃ ca tam |
nināya dharmasacivaḥ prīto bhagavato'ntikam || k35.50 ||
bhagavānapi tadvākyāccakre teṣāmanugraham |
satyadarśanasaṃbuddhā yena te sugatiṃ yayuḥ || k35.51 ||
teṣu yāteṣu sudhanaṃ pakṣapātādrayā dṛśā |
vilokya bhagavān samyagvidadhe jñānabhājanam || k35.52 ||
satyasaṃdarśanāvāptaviśeṣakuśalodayaḥ |
gatvā jināya kauśāmbyāṃ sa vihāramakārayat || k35.53 ||
yasmādbhagavatādiṣṭastasmin yātaḥ sahāyatām |
bhikṣuścundābhidhastasmātso'bhūccundavihārabhūḥ || k35.54 ||
rādhābhidhā tadā dāsī vihāraparicārikā |
dayayā bhagavānasyāḥ śīrṇavastraṃ samagrahīt || k35.55 ||
adāsī syāmiti śraddhāpraṇidhānārpitastayā |
sa cīvaro bhagavataḥ prayayāvekavarṇatām || k35.56 ||
sudhanasyojjvalaṃ dṛṣṭvāṃpuṇyasaṃbhāramadbhutam |
bhikṣubhirbhagavān pṛṣṭastatpūrvodayamabhyadhāt || k35.57 ||
sundhānākhyo gṛhapatirvārāṇasyāmabhūtpurā |
nodārakuśnjarasyābhūdyasya dānaparikṣayaḥ || k35.58 ||
anāvṛṣṭihate kāle tasya dvādaśavārṣike |
avāritamabhūcchatramavicchinnānnamarthinām || k35.59 ||
tasya padmākaro nāma koṣāgārapatirgṛhe |
dānasāhāyyakaṃ cakre sahasthā hi samṛddhayaḥ || k35.60 ||
pratyekabuddhasaṃghasya bhaktakālanivedakah |
dharmadūtābhidhastasya mantrī dhīmānapasthitaḥ || k35.61 ||
karmavyākṣepatastasya jāte kālavyatikrame |
kadācitkukurasteṣāṃ kālasaṃjñāṃ vyadhātpuraḥ || k35.62 ||
sundhāno'dya sa evāhaṃ koṣṭhiko'nāthapiṇḍadaḥ |
saṃghasya dharmadūto yaḥ sa evodayano nṛpaḥ || k35.63 ||
saṃjñānideśako yaśca kukuraḥ sudhano'pi saḥ |
rājñā ghoṣeṇa vijñāto ghoṣilāparanāmabhṛt || k35.64 ||
caritamityucitaṃ bhavabhedinā bhagavatā kathitaṃ kila bhikṣavaḥ |
sukṛtasaurabhasārasudhārasaṃ sumanasā śravaṇāñjalibhiḥ papuḥ || k35.65 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ ghoṣilāvadānaṃ pañcatriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


36. pūrṇāvadānam |

vibudhasarasiḥ padmaiḥ śobhite paṅkajinyā śuciparisarajātaṃ spṛśyate na sthale'bjam (?) |
sahajaparicitānāṃ nityamantargatānāṃ bhavati sitaguṇānāṃ kāraṇaṃ naiva jātiḥ || k36.1 ||
śrāvastyāṃ sarvasattvānāṃ svastidhyānaparāyaṇe |
jinakalpadrume jetavanārāmasthite purā || k36.2 ||
śūrpārakākhye nagare ratnasaṃcayasāgaraḥ |
bhavo nāmābhavatsārthaparirmatimatāṃ varaḥ || k36.3 ||
bhavilo bhavabhadraśca bhavanandī ca viśrutāḥ |
ketakyāṃ tasya jāyāyāṃ babhūvustanayāstrayaḥ || k36.4 ||
rogayogādupagaraḥ sa kadācinmumūrṣutām |
pāruṣyādbhṛśamudvignaiḥ patnīputrairupekṣitaḥ || k36.5 ||
bhaktyā dāsī tu tasyaikā paricaryāparā param |
mallikākhyābhavattasyāḥ sevayā svāsthyamāyayau || k36.6 ||
snehopakārapraṇataḥ kṛtajñaḥ sa tayānvitaḥ |
ṛtau saṃgamamabhyetya tasyāḥ putramajījanat || k36.7 ||
yadā tajjanmani pituḥ sarvārthāḥ pūrṇatāṃ yayuḥ |
sa tadā pūrṇanāmābhūdbālaḥ pūrṇendusundaraḥ || k36.8 ||
jyeṣṭhāstrayaḥ kṛtidvāhā yayurabdhiṃ dhanārthinaḥ |
pūrṇastu bhāṇḍaśālāyāṃ pituścakre dhanārjanam || k36.9 ||
tataḥ pratinivṛttāste prāptārthaḥ sāgarātpunaḥ |
gaṇanāṃ hemalakṣāṇāṃ kṛtvā svamudire puram || k36.10 ||
samudragamane teṣāṃ yāvānāsīddhanāgamaḥ |
pūrṇasya svagṛhe paṇyaurbabhūvābhyadhikastataḥ || k36.11 ||
taddṛṣṭvā janakasteṣāṃ vṛddhaḥ paryantavāsare |
tamūcehitamāyatyāṃ tṛṣṇā naikakṣayodayaḥ || k36.12 ||
dṛṣṭaṃ bhavadbhiḥ sāmudralābhe kṛtapariśramaiḥ |
pūrṇenoārjitaṃ vittamakleśena mahīyasā || k36.13 ||
śubhakarmavipākena bhavantyarthā dhanārthinām |
hastātpalāyate'nyasya prāpnoti patitaṃ paraḥ || k36.14 ||
sanmārgasyāparityāgādyuktāyuktavivecanāt |
deśakālaparijñānātsatāṃ sarvatra saṃpadaḥ || k36.15 ||
bhavanti svagṛhe śanyāḥ sudhiyo dharmabhāginaḥ |
gatvā ratnākaraṃ cānye labhante prāṇasaṃśayam || k36.16 ||
yatnena boddhavyā sadbhiḥ dhanasyopaniṣtparā |
adrohaśuddhabuddhīnāṃ svādhīnānāṃ dhanācchriyaḥ || k36.17 ||
rakṣaṇīyo bhavadbhiśca bhedaḥ satatasaṃhateḥ |
bhinnātskhalati kalyāṇaṃ kulātkumbhādivodakam || k36.18 ||
athābhinnendhanasyāgnernaśyante sadṛśāstviṣaḥ |
tathā vipulavaṃśasya bhinnajñātervibhūtayāḥ || k36.19 ||
bhrātṝṇāṃ saṃtato bhedaḥ kathaṃ nāma nivartate |
adhyāpitānāṃ patnībhirdveṣavidyāṃ sadā niśi || k36.20 ||
unnatānāṃ svavaśānāṃ dvaidhaṃ tāvanna jāyate |
yāvatkuṭhāradhāreva yoṣidviśati nāntaram || k36.21 ||
bhrāturarthānuvādena guruṃ pāruṣyakutsayā |
mitramekābhilāṣeṇa nayanti dvaidhatāṃ striyaḥ || k36.22 ||
tadvadanti hasantyo'pi bhrūvilāsena yoṣitaḥ |
yatprayāti suhṛtsnehamūlonmūlanahetutām || k36.23 ||
hitamuktveti putrāṇāṃ bhūtaye'bhimataṃ bhavaḥ |
anityatāpariyuktaḥ kāle nidhanamāyayau || k36.24 ||
avibhakte dhane śaktā deśāntaradhanārjane |
jyeṣṭhā babhūvuḥ pūrṇastu gṛhe vittamacintayat || k36.25 ||
kālena gṛhamāptānāṃ vastrāśanavivādinām |
strīmantradattakarṇānāṃ bhedasteṣāmajāyata || k36.26 ||
vitte vibhajyamāne'thatairvidveṣavaśīkṛteḥ |
dāsīsuto'yamtyuktvā nītaḥ pūrṇo niraṃśatām || k36.27 ||
so'pi pūrṇaśanaḥ kāle śītasaṃkucitaṃ pathi |
dadarśa grīṣmatāpr'pi vivaśaṃ dārubhārakam || k36.28 ||
ādāya dārumūlyena sa tasmāddārubhārakam |
divyacandanamadrākṣīddahanasyāpi śītadam || k36.29 ||
sukṛtenaiva mahatā tena labdhamahādhanaḥ |
sa sevyaḥ sārthavāhānāṃ pūjyo'bhūtpṛthivīpateḥ || k36.30 ||
ratnākaraṃ sa ṣaṭkṛtvaḥ prayātaḥ sarvado'rthinām |
cakāra sarvavaṇījāṃ taraśulkādyanugraham || k36.31 ||
srhāvastīvāsibhiḥ sārthavaṇigbhiḥ punararthitaḥ |
yayau pravahaṇārūḍhaḥ samudradvīpamāśu saḥ || k36.32 ||
pratyāvṛtte pravahaṇe so'tha śuśrāva gāyatām |
vaṇijāṃ sthāvirāḥ śailagāthāḥ sugatasaṃśrayāḥ || k36.33 ||
kasyaitā iti te tena pṛṣṭāḥ sarve babhāṣire |
etā bhagavatā gītā gāthā buddhena dhīmatā || k36.34 ||
iti buddhābhidhāmeva śrutvā harṣamavāpa saḥ |
puṃsāṃ svavāsanārūḍhaṃ vyaktimāyātyudīritam || k36.35 ||
tairvistareṇa kathitāmākarṇya bhagavatkathām |
so'bhavattadgatamanāstaddarśanasamutsukaḥ || k36.36 ||
sa śanairgṛhamāgatya tyaktvā sarvaparicchadam |
anāthapiṇḍadaṃ draṣṭuṃ śrāvastyāṃ suhṛdaṃ yayau || k36.37 ||
abhilāṣaṃ nivedyāsmai pravrajyāyāṃ jitendriyaḥ |
jagāma sahitastena bhaktyā bhagavato'ntikam || k36.38 ||
dṛṣṭvaiva tatra sarvajñaṃ mohadhvāntadivākaram |
tatpādadarśanenaiva mene sa kṛtakṛtyatām || k36.39 ||
vijñāya tasya bhagavān saṃkalpaṃ tamabhāṣata |
daśanajyotsnayā kurvan vivekavimalā diśaḥ || k36.40 ||
ehi bhikṣi nirāśaṅke nirvipakṣe kṣayojjhite |
ākhyāte dharmavinaye brahmacaryāṃ carepsitam || k36.41 ||
iti prasādaśīlena jinenodīrite purah |
pravrajyā sahasaivāsya papātālakṣitā tanau || k36.42 ||
tataḥ sa śatrau mitre ca praśamātsamatāṃ śritaḥ |
śāstuḥ śāsanamādāya praṇipatya jagāma tam || k36.43 ||
śroṇāparāntakaṃ nāma deśaṃ śrūrajanāśrayam |
svayaṃ parīkṣituṃ kṣāntiṃ janena sa samāyayau || k36.44 ||
tato dṛṣṭvā tamāyāntaṃ mṛgayāyāmamaṅgalam |
lubdhakaścāpamākṛṣya hantuṃ kridhātsamādravat || k36.45 ||
nirvikāraṃ nirudvegaṃ sa taṃ vigatasādhvasam |
prahareti bruvāṇaṃ ca dṛṣṭvaiva śamamāptavān || k36.46 ||
śāmyatastasya sahasā prasādī lubdhakasya saḥ |
dharmaṃ dideśa yenāsau bodhiṃ prāpa sahānugaiḥ || k36.47 ||
sa tatra sugatārhāṇāṃ sarvopaskarasaṃpadām |
śatāni paśnca ramyāṇāṃ vihārāṇāmakārayat || k36.48 ||
pūrṇo'pi jñānasaṃpūrṇaḥ prāptistridaśapūjyatām |
vairāgyalakṣmyā yukto'bhūnmunīnāṃ spṛhaṇīyayā || k36.49 ||
atha tasyāgrajo bhrātā bhavilaḥ kṣīṇavittatām |
kālenopagataḥ prāyātsamduraṃ draviṇāśayā || k36.50 ||
tataḥ pravahaṇārūḍhaḥ so'nukūlaiḥ samīraṇaiḥ |
gośīrṣacandanavanaṃ prāpitaḥ svalpavāsaraiḥ || k36.51 ||
kuṭhārikaśataistasmin pañcabhiśchettumudyate |
taccandanavanaṃ divyaṃ bhujaṅgagaṇāsaṃkulam || k36.52 ||
tatsvāmī yakṣasenānāṃ maheśvaramiti śrutaḥ |
kopādudasṛjadghoraṃ kālikākhyaṃ mahānilam || k36.53 ||
marutā mahatā tena prāpitāḥ prāṇasaṃśayam |
cakrandurvaṇijaḥ sarve śarvaśakramukhān surān || k36.54 ||
tānārtarāvamukharān bhavilaḥ sārthanāyakaḥ |
uvāca saṃcintya ciraṃ paścāttāpasamākulaḥ || k36.55 ||
pūrṇaḥ kanīyān bhrātā sa hitaiṣī māṃ purāvadat |
bahukleśo hyalpasukhaḥ kka gantavyastvayāmbudhiḥ || k36.56 ||
akṛtvā dhīmatastasya vacanaṃ satyadarśinaḥ |
cyuto'haṃ dhanalobhena ghore'smin vyasanārṇave || k36.57 ||
śrutvaitadvaṇijaḥ sarve prabhāvaṃ lokaviśrutam |
pūrṇasya manasā dhyātvā tameva śaraṇaṃ yayuḥ || k36.58 ||
namastubhyaṃ jagatkleśaviṣadhoṣāpahāriṇe |
pūrṇayodīrṇakaruṇāsudhāsaṃpūrṇacetase || k36.59 ||
ityekasvararāveṇa teṣāṃ saṃpūrite'mbare |
kṣaṇena gatvā tadvṛttaṃ pūrṇaḥ prāha svadevatāḥ || k36.60 ||
śroṇāparāntakagataḥ śrutvā teṣāṃ sa viplavam |
vyomnā samādhisaṃnaddhaḥ prāpa pravahaṇaṃ kṣaṇāt || k36.61 ||
tasmin paryaṅkabandhanena sthito merurivācalah |
pralayottālavegasya gatiṃ vāhorjahāra sah || k36.62 ||
pūrṇena pavanaṃ ruddhaṃ jñātvā yakṣagaṇāgraṇīḥ |
taṃ prasādya yayau tyaktvā tebhyaścandanakānanam || k36.63 ||
pūrṇaprasādādādāya bhavilaścandanadrumān |
hṛṣṭastenaiva sahitaḥ śūrpāraṃ svapuraṃ yayau || k36.64 ||
pūrṇo'thsaṃmate bhrātustatra gośīrṣacandanaiḥ |
cakre candanamālākhyaṃ prāsādaṃ sugato'citam || k36.65 ||
tatra pūrṇena bhagavān dhyātastūrṇaṃ vihāysā |
samāyayau jetavanādullaṅghya śatayojanīm || k36.66 ||
āgacchato bhagavataḥ puraḥprasṛtayā jagat |
dehakāntyā kapiśitaṃ sarvaṃ himamivābhavat || k36.67 ||
puropāntanivāsinyastaṃ vilokya gṛhāṅganāḥ |
tīvracittaprasādena praśamonmukhatāṃ yayuḥ || k36.68 ||
kuśalopacitāṃ tāsāṃ bhagavān satyadeśanām |
cakre bhavādṛtāṃ kāntāstāhprāpuḥ kuśalaṃ yayuḥ || k36.69 ||
ṛddhyā bhagavatastatra caityaṃ paurāṅganābhidham |
tābhirvihitamadyāpi vandante caityavandakāḥ || k36.70 ||
munīnāṃ bhagavān kṛtvā tathā valkalino muneḥ |
pravrajyānugrahaṃ śuddhāṃ vidadhe dharmadeśanām || k36.71 ||
tataścandanamālākhyaṃ prāsādaṃ bhagavān jinaḥ |
praviśya sphāṭikaṃ cakre janasaṃghabharakṣamam || k36.72 ||
atha ratnāsanāsīnaḥ sa tatra karuṇānidhiḥ |
vidadhe sarvasattvānāṃ śāntyai nirvāṇadeśanām || k36.73 ||
atrāntare sānucarau munīndrau kṛṣṇāgautamau |
abhyetya dharmaśravaṇeśāstuḥ śāsanamāpatuḥ || k36.74 ||
tatra kṛtvātha bhagavān prāsādasya pratigraham |
punarjetavanaṃ gacchannudyayau saha bhikṣubhiḥ || k36.75 ||
vrahanmārīcilokasthāṃ maudgalyāyanamātaram |
sagdirā cāryasatye tāṃ dharmamārge nyaveśayat || k36.76 ||
atha jetavanaṃ prāptaṃ bhagavantaṃ savismayāḥ |
pūrṇasya puṇyaṃ papracchurbhikṣavaḥ so'pyabhāṣata || k36.77 ||
kāśyapasya purā samyaksaṃbuddhasyānyajanmani |
vihārādhikṛtaḥ pūrṇaḥ saṃghopasthāyako'bhavat || k36.78 ||
sa kadācidasaṃmṛṣṭāṃ dṛṣṭvā vaihārikīṃ bhuvam |
prāha pravrajitaṃ tīvraṃ krodhādupadhivārikam || k36.79 ||
dṛptasyopadhivāro'dya vihāro'sminnamārjitaḥ |
kasya dāsīsutasyeti bruvāṇastamabhartsayat || k36.80 ||
tena pāruṣyapāpena bhuktvā narakadurgatim |
dāsīsuto'bhavatpūrṇaḥ pañca janmaśatāni saḥ || k36.81 ||
saṃghopāsanamevāsya puṇyāyābhūnmahīyasā |
arhattvaṃ yena niḥśeṣabhavakleśojjhitaḥ śritaḥ || k36.82 ||
iti prabhāvaṃ kathitaṃ jinena pūrṇasya puṇyopacayapraṇitam |
śrutvādbhutaṃ saṃsadi bhikṣusaṃghaḥ puṇyaprasaṃśābhirato babhūva || k36.83 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ pūrṇāvadānaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


37. mūkapaṅgvavadānam |

ākiṃcanyasukhāya niḥspṛhatayā vairāgyalakṣmījuṣaḥ
sarvaṃ yānti vihāya kāyasacivāḥ santaḥ praśāntyai vanam |
tatrāpi vrataḍambare parikarārambhāya cetsaṃcayas
tatkaḥ kośaparicchadopakaraṇairgehe'parādhaḥ kṛtaḥ || k37.1 ||
jine jetavanārāmavihārābhirate purā |
śākyarājakumārāṇāṃ pravrajyāsaṃjuṣāṃ puraḥ || k37.2 ||
citracīvarasatpātrayogapaṭṭādisaṃcayam |
prabhūtataramālokya bhagavān samacintayat || k37.3 ||
aho bataiṣāṃ nādyāpi bandhaheturnivartate |
abhimānamayaḥ kāye priyaḥ parikaragrahaḥ || k37.4 ||
kāye kāyapariṣkārastasyopakaraṇāvalī |
tasyāḥ parikarādānamaho nu bandhaśṛnkhalā || k37.5 ||
iti saṃcintya bhagavānnaikāntavihitasthitiḥ |
kāruṇyādupasannānāṃ kuśalāya samudyataḥ || k37.6 ||
māsatrayamupasthānaṃ kartavyaṃ yena kenacit |
adarśanāya bhikṣūṇāmakaroditi saṃvidam || k37.7 ||
pravṛtte niyame tasmin bhikṣurāraṇyakavrataḥ |
āyayāvupasenākhyaḥ kāryārthaṃ tanucīvaraḥ || k37.8 ||
pravāritaḥ sa saṃprāpya dhanyaḥ sugatadarśanam |
kṛtakṛtyaḥ kṣasṇaṃ sthitvā pratasthe praṇipatya tam || k37.9 ||
vrajantametya papracchurbhikṣavaḥ parivārya tam |
tavārya darśanamaho dattaṃ bhagavatā katham || k37.10 ||
māsatrayaṃ darśane'sya niyamaḥ śāsanena yaḥ |
sa kathaṃ bhavatā bhagnaḥ saṃghasyonmārgagāminā || k37.11 ||
śrutvā tadvacanaṃ teṣāmupasenaḥ smitottaram |
tānuce namayā kaścitkṛtaḥ samayaviplavaḥ || k37.12 ||
ahamukto bhagavatā darśanāvasare svayam |
āraṇyakasya me bhikṣorniṣedho nāsti darśane || k37.13 ||
paricchadopakaraṇatyāganirmuktabandhanāḥ |
avāryadarśanā vṛkṣamūlikāḥ pāṃśukūlikāḥ || k37.14 ||
idamadya paraṃ prātariti ye saṃcaye ratāḥ |
pātravīcaravargeṣu teṣāṃ nāstīha darśanam || k37.15 ||
pāthobhiḥ prasarattuṣāraśiśiraistṛṣṇāturāste paraṃ
te nityaṃ ca nidhānadhāmni vivṛte'pyanyādalaṃ durgatāḥ |
teṣāṃ candanapādapādupanataḥ saṃtāpakaḥ pāvako
yaireṣa praśamavratopakaraṇe baddho'bhimānagrahaḥ || k37.16 ||
ityuktamupasenena śrutvā te śākyabhikṣavaḥ |
vailakṣyakṣayitotsāhāḥ sahasaiva vyacintayan || k37.17 ||
etadbhagavatādiṣṭamasmānuddhiśya nāparān |
vicitracīvaracayaprāvārā vayameva yat || k37.18 ||
viratecchāḥ priyāhśāsturmahecchā vayamapriyāḥ |
tasmādicchāṃ parityajya bhavāmastasya saṃmatāḥ || k37.19 ||
iti saṃcintya te sarve cārucīvarasaṃcayam |
prāvṛtyābhyadhikaṃ tyaktvā yayurbhagavato'ntikam || k37.20 ||
icchāvirāme bhagavān vyadhātteṣāmanugraham |
jñānavajreṇa satkāyadṛṣṭiśailaṃ bibheda yaḥ || k37.21 ||
śākyarājakumārāṇāṃ śrotaḥprāptiphalaspṛśām |
bhikṣubhiḥ pūrvavṛttāntaṃ pṛṣṭaḥ prāha tathāgataḥ || k37.22 ||
vārāṇasyāmabhūtpūrvaṃ brahmadatto mahīpatiḥ |
dānārdrahasto yadbāhuḥ kṣmāmadhāddigdvipopamaḥ || k37.23 ||
tasya brahmāvatī muktālateva guṇaśālinī |
kīrtiḥ satpuruṣasyeva viśrutā vanitābhavat || k37.24 ||
pratibimbopamaṃ patyuḥ sā sutaṃ vimalāśayā |
jalakrīḍāgatā kāle suṣuve divyalakṣaṇam || k37.25 ||
udakākhyaḥ sa bālo'bhūtsaṃjātaḥ salilāntare |
vardhamānaḥ pitustulyaṃ yauvarājyānorathaiḥ || k37.26 ||
śatāni pañcāmātyānāṃ tasya janmadinaṃ samam |
avāpustulyarūpāṇāṃ putrāṇāṃ śatapañcakam || k37.27 ||
nijaṃ jātismaraḥ smṛtvā prāgvṛttaṃ sa śiśuḥ śanaiḥ |
acintayatprāptakālaṃ hitaṃ sukṛtamātmanaḥ || k37.28 ||
ṣaṣṭivarṣāṇi kṛtvā tu yauvarājyamahaṃ purā |
abhavaṃ kṛcchrasaṃtaptaściraṃ narakasaṃkaṭe || k37.29 ||
janmanyasminnapi punaryauvarājyamupasthitam |
sarvathā prārthyamāno'pi na kariṣyāmi pātakam || k37.30 ||
iti saṃcintya sa ciraṃ rājabhogaparāṅmukhaḥ |
piturudvegajananīmagrahītpaṅgumūkatām || k37.31 ||
sarvalakṣaṇayukto'pi rājavṛtterabhājanam |
sa mūkapaṅgurnāmābhūdbandhūnāṃ duḥkhavardhanaḥ || k37.32 ||
prāpteṣu mantriputreṣu śastraśāstrabalodayam |
rājaputraḥ pravṛddho'pi nodatiṣṭhanna cāvadat || k37.33 ||
pṛṣṭastataḥ kṣitīśena vaidyāstaddoṣabheṣajam |
avadan vaikalyaṃ rājan rājasūnorna dṛśyate || k37.34 ||
abhyāsādyadi jāto'sya doṣo'pi sukhasevinaḥ |
tadeṣa bhayasaṃvegāduttiṣṭhati ca vakti ca || k37.35 ||
iti vaidyairabhihitaṃ tathetyuktvā kṣitīśvaraḥ |
mithyaiva vadhyavasudhāṃ bhayāya vyasṛjatsutam || k37.36 ||
sa bhartsyamānahpuruṣaistamuvāca rathasthitam |
api kaścidvasatyasyāṃ vārāṇasyāṃ na vā janaḥ || k37.37 ||
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā tairnītaḥ sa nṛpāntikam |
tatra pitrārthyamāno'pi mūka evābhavatpunaḥ || k37.38 ||
punarvadhyabhuvaṃ nītaḥ śavaṃ dṛṣṭvā jagādsaḥ |
astyeṣa jīvati śavaḥ kiṃ vā sarvātmanā mṛtaḥ || k37.39 ||
śrutvaitattaiḥ pituḥ pārśve nyasto maunaṃ vyadhātpunaḥ |
punarvadhabhayāccaiva nītaḥ provāca tān pathi || k37.40 ||
rāśirya eṣa dhānyasya sa hi bhukto'nubhujyate |
ityuktavākyo'pi punarnoce kiṃcitpitu paraḥ || k37.41 ||
tataḥ khyātapratikṣepe tasyādiṣṭe mahībhujā |
so'vadadvaradānena vacmi pradbhyāṃ vrajāmi ca || k37.42 ||
athāsya rājñā hṛṣṭena varadāne pratiśrute |
sa padbhyāṃ svayamabhyetya spaṣṭaṃ pitaramabravīt || k37.43 ||
nāhaṃ paṅgurna mūko'haṃ naiva cāhaṃ jaḍāśayaḥ |
kiṃtu janmāntarakleśaṃ smṛtvā vaihvalyamāśritaḥ || k37.44 ||
yauvarājyasukhaṃ bhuktvā ṣaṣṭivarṣāṇyahaṃ purā |
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi nyavasaṃ narakodare || k37.45 ||
rājabhītyā mayā tasmātkṛteyaṃ mūkapaṅgutā |
pravrajyayā brahmacaryaṃ carāmyeṣa varo mama || k37.46 ||
iti tenoktamākarṇya tamuvāca mahīpatiḥ |
amūka ityāptadhṛtirvirakta iti duḥkhitaḥ || k37.47 ||
dharmamūlamidaṃ rājyaṃ putra na tyaktumarhasi |
yajñadānaprajātrāṇaiḥ puṇyapūrṇā nṝpaśriyaḥ || k37.48 ||
ekaputrastvayā putra parityāgarasādaham |
nidrādaridratāṃ nītaḥ śokaśayyāsamāśrayaḥ || k37.49 ||
saṃpūrṇacandrarucirāṃ vyaktamauktikahāsinīm |
kathaṃ saṃpadamutsṛjya pravrajyābhimatā tava || k37.50 ||
kathaṃ śayyāḥ parityajya prājyarājyasukhocitāḥ |
vanāntavāsavyasanī sevase pāṃśulāḥ sthalīḥ || k37.51 ||
kāntālīlāmukuramaṇimanmandirīṃ rājadhānīm
etāṃ tyaktvānanu vanabhuvaḥ saṃpatadvyāghraghorāḥ |
sarvaprītyai jaradajagarāśvāsavipluṣṭapatrāḥ
kliṣṭacchāyāhpraviralalatāstāḥ kathaṃ te bhavanti || k37.52 ||
pituḥ śrutveti vacanaṃ rājaputrastamabravīt |
dantakāntādhararuciṃ vairāgyaṃ grāhayanniva || k37.53 ||
śītalā nirmalajalāhsaṃtoṣaśaśiśītalāḥ |
vane vairāgyasubhagā bhuvaḥ kasya na vallabhāḥ || k37.54 ||
paradārā iva kṣiprasukhāvarjitadurjanāḥ |
narakapratyayāyāme sāpāyā na priyāḥ priyāḥ || k37.55 ||
dhyānaṃ mantraḥ parijñānamindriyāṇāṃ canirjayaḥ |
rājñāṃ hiṃsāprayatnena yogo'yaṃ narakapradaḥ || k37.56 ||
hasantyaḥ saṃsāraṃ kusumakalilāḥ kānanabhuvaḥ
svabhāvena prītiṃ vidadhati budhānāṃ śamamayīm |
dṛḍhaṃ cintāśrāṇtā vyajanapavanocchvāsabahulā
vibhūtirbhūpānāṃ śiraḥsaktāmanityatām || k37.57 ||
anujānīhi māṃ tāta vrajāmyeṣa tapovanam |
jānīhi sarvabhāvānāṃ śiraḥsaktāmanityatām || k37.58 ||
iti putravacaḥ śrutvā tattatheti vicintayan |
uvācopacitāścaryastaṃ manīṣī mahīpatiḥ || k37.59 ||
vivekavimalaṃ putra tvamicchāsi sa cedvanam |
hitvāme saṃśayaṃ tāvatpaścādyuktiṃ kariṣyasi || k37.60 ||
vrajatā badhyavaśudhāṃ tiryaguktaṃ tvayā vacaḥ |
pracuraṃ tadabhiprāyaṃ vaktumarhasi tattvataḥ || k37.61 ||
iti kṣitibhujā pṛṣṭaḥ so'bravīttanmayoditam |
vasatyatra na kaścittvā madvadhādyo nivartayet || k37.62 ||
sukṛtī jīvati śavaḥ sa pāpastu mṛto'mṛtaḥ |
prākpuṇyaṃ bhakṣyate mūlātsadhanairdhānyarāśivat || k37.63 ||
ityāśayānmayā tāta taduktaṃ vacanaṃ priyam |
* * * * * * * * || k37.64 ||
iti śrutvā kṣitipatistaṃ pariṣvajya sādaraḥ |
ucitaṃ kriyatāṃ putra kuśalāyetyabhāṣataḥ || k37.65 ||
tataḥ sa pitrānujñātaḥ sāśrunetreṇa kānanam |
prayayau mantriputrāṇāṃ sahitaḥ pañcabhiḥ śataiḥ || k37.66 ||
maharṣerantike tatra pravrajyāṃ prāpya sānugah |
teṣāṃ kālena so'paśyatkuṇḍavalkalasaṃcayam || k37.67 ||
tataḥsa saṃcayadveṣī tadadarśanasaṃvidā |
ekākī vijane tasthau kaṃcitkālaṃmahāmatiḥ || k37.68 ||
darśanābhāṣaṇe baddhaniyamo'pi yadṛcchayā |
prāptaṃ svāgatamityuktvā papraccha kuśalaṃ mṛgam || k37.69 ||
punaśca pūjitaṃ dṛṣṭvā muniṃ tena mṛgavratam |
amātyatanayāḥ sarve vilakṣāḥ samacintayan || k37.70 ||
mṛgo mṛgavrataścāyaṃ pūjitau niṣparigrahau |
etāvanajinau daṇḍasaṃbḥārāḍambarojjhitau || k37.71 ||
etadarthamanenāsmaddarśane niyamaḥ kṛtaḥ |
vratopakaraṇavyagrānnūnamasyāpi vārayet || k37.72 ||
iti saṃcintya sarvaṃ te vratopacārasaṃcayam |
nadyāṃ prakṣipya vārāyāṃ yayuḥ śuddhāstadantikam || k37.73 ||
tyaktvā gṛhabhūvaṃ teṣāmāśayānuśayocitām |
sa dhātuṃ prakṛtiṃ jñātvā vidadhe dharmadeśanām || k37.74 ||
rājaputraḥ sa evāhaṃ śākyāste mantrisūnavaḥ |
punastyāgopadeśo'yamadyāpyeṣāṃ mayā kṛtaḥ || k37.75 ||
iti śākyakumāravṛttametatkathitaṃ bhikṣugaṇaḥ svayaṃ jinena |
avadhārya parāmapūjayattāṃ karuṇāmāśritavatsalasya tasya || k37.76 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ mūkapaṅgvavadānaṃ nāma saptatriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


38. kṣānyavadānam |

te jayanti dhṛtiśīlinaḥ paraṃ nirvikārarucisūcitādbhutāḥ |
śeṣavatpṛthulabhāranirvyathāḥ ye vahanti sukṛtakṣamāḥ kṣamām || k38.1 ||
purā purā puṇyaviparyayeṇa ripuḥ prajānāṃ janitaprakampaḥ |
udunbanāmā nibiḍopatāpairyakṣa kṣayāyaiva kṛtakṣaṇo'bhūt || k38.2 ||
akālakālaṃ tamanāthabandhurlokānukampī bhagavān prasahya |
śikṣopadeśaṃ śaraṇaṃ prapannaṃ śamābhidhāyī vinaye nyayuṅkta || k38.3 ||
tasmin praśānte bhuvanopatāpe draṣṭuṃ prahṛṣṭaḥ sugataṃ sametya |
saṃcāriṇaṃ nākapatiḥ praṇamya tatkālajātasmitamityuvāca || k38.4 ||
kasmādakasmātsmitacandralekhā mukhāmbuje bhāti tavādbhuteyam |
akāraṇaṃ sattvasudhāsamudrā na lokasāmānyatayāṃ hasanti || k38.5 ||
śrutveti vākyaṃ tridaśeśvarasya tattaṃ sarvadarśī bhagavān babhāṣe |
asmin pradeśe nijapūrvavṛttaṃ smṛtvā smtaṃ jātamidaṃ mamendram || k38.6 ||
purā muniḥ kṣāniratirvane'sminnuvāsa nirvāsitaroṣadoṣaḥ |
yo'bhūdbhuvo rāgarajaḥsvabhāve vidveṣavānindurivāravinde || k38.7 ||
athottarāśādhipatirvasante vanāntarālokanakautukena |
sāntaḥpuraḥ kelisukhāya kāmī tadāśramopāntamahīmavāpa || k38.8 ||
rāgī kalirnāma sa bhūmipālaḥ pādaprahārairvadanāsavaiśca |
lebhe vilāseṣu nitambinīnāmaśokaśobhāṃ bakulaśriyaṃ ca || k38.9 ||
diśastapolopapṛthuprakopabhrūbhaṅgavṛndairiva tāpasānām |
tatra bhramadbhirbhramarairbabhūvuḥ kāmāgnidhūmairiva sāndhakārāh || k38.10 ||
līlāvilolāḥ pavanākulālīstanāvanamrāḥ stabakā latānām |
raktādharāḥ pāṭalapallavānāṃ prāpurvilāsaṃ lalanā latānām || k38.11 ||
rājāṅganāḥ kautukavibhrameṇa vane carantyastamṛṣiṃ vilokya |
acañcaladhyānasamādhisaktaṃ vimuktarāgaṃ parivārya tasthuḥ || k38.12 ||
taddeśamabhyetya nareśvaro'tha dṛṣṭvā vabhūbhiḥ parivāritaṃ tam |
īrṣyāprakopānaladurnirīkṣyaḥ ciccheda tasyāśu sa pāṇipādam || k38.13 ||
chinnāṅgavargo'pi sa nirvikāraścukopa bhūpāya na nāma dhīraḥ |
nyavārayatkrūrataraṃ ca tasmai gandharvayakṣoragadevasaṃgham || k38.14 ||
tataḥ prayāte nṛpatau puraṃ svāṃ sametya sarve munayo vanebhyaḥ |
taṃ tatra kṛtāvayavaṃ vilokya kṣāntā api krodhadhutā babhūvuḥ || k38.15 ||
śāpapradānābhimukhānnivārya kṣantavyamityeva sa tānuvāca |
kṣāmāsamāliṅgitamānasānāṃ kopakriyābhiḥ kriyate na saṅgaḥ || k38.16 ||
vikāravego'pi na pāṇipādacchede mamābhūdyadi vītamanyoḥ |
satyena tenākṣatadeha eva syāmityavādītsa punaḥ prasādī || k38.17 ||
tataḥ kṣaṇātsaṃgatapāṇipādaṃ rūḍhavraṇaṃ pretya sadodayena |
apūjayatkṣāntiguṇaṃ stavena taṃ devatā sattvasitaiśca puṣpaiḥ || k38.18 ||
rājāpi tatkilbiṣakālakūṭavisphoṭasaṃghaṭṭavinaṣṭaceṣṭaḥ |
pūrotkaṭāvartavivartamānaḥ saṃvartapākaṃ narakaṃ jagāma || k38.19 ||
yo'bhūtpurā kṣāntiratirmaharṣiḥ so'haṃ kaliryaśca sa devadattaḥ |
atītavṛttasmaraṇena śakra nākāraṇaṃ jātamidaṃ smitaṃ me || k38.20 ||
iti bhagavataḥ śrutvā vākyaṃ sa vismayamānasaḥ
pramadavikacavyaktotsāhā vahannayanāvalīḥ |
taraṇikiraṇasparśeneva sphuṭaḥ kamalākaras
tridaśavasatiṃ prītaḥ prāyātpatistridivaikasām || k38.21 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ kṣāntyavadānamaṣṭatriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


39. kapilāvadānam |

atyantamunnatimatāṃ mahatāṃ vināśadoṣasya durjanasamāgama eva hetuḥ |
kūladrumāḥ kila phalaprasavaiḥ sahaiva sadyaḥ patanti jalasaṃgatibhinnamūlāḥ || k39.1 ||
rucirāgāraśālinyāṃ vaiśālyāṃ bhagavān purā |
valgumatyāstaṭe nadyā vicacāra tathāgataḥ || k39.2 ||
tasyāḥ kaivartasarthena gambhīre'mbhasi dustare |
kadācidghoramakaraḥ kṣiptvā jālaṃ samuddhṛtaḥ || k39.3 ||
aṣṭādaśaśirāḥ siḥsadviraprakharānanaḥ |
nṛṇāṃ sahasrairākṛṣṭaḥ parvatākāravigrahaḥ || k39.4 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā tatra vitrastāhsrastākarṣaṇarajjavaḥ |
āścaryaniścaladṛśo na tasthurna yayuḥ kṣaṇam || k39.5 ||
gaṇanāṃ gahanāścaryaviśeṣaśataśālinām |
saṃsāre karmavaicitrye vikārāṇāṃ karoti kaḥ || k39.6 ||
atrāntare tamuddeśaṃ bhagavān bhūtabhāvanaḥ |
jinahsamāyayau sarvajanatrāṇakṛtakṣaṇah || k39.7 ||
savṛddhabālalalanaṃ janaṃ kautukasaṃgatam |
dṛṣṭvā tatrākarottīre bhagavānāsanagraham || k39.8 ||
bhikṣusaṃghaiḥ parivṛtaṃ dṛṣṭvā tatra tathāgatam |
jano'bhūdunmukhaḥ sarvaḥ pratyāvṛttaḥ ivodadhiḥ || k39.9 ||
taṃ vilokyaiva vinatāḥ kaivartāḥ prāṇibandhanam |
viśālajālaṃ sahasā saṃsāramiva tatyajuḥ || k39.10 ||
matsyakumbhīnakrādisaṃsāraṃ tadgirāmbhasi |
tyaktvā te viratāṭopā babhūvuḥ kilbiṣadviṣaḥ || k39.11 ||
tairnyastaṃ bhagavān dṛṣṭvā mahākamaramagrataḥ |
taṃ jagāda sṛjan dantakāntyaiva karuṇānadīm || k39.12 ||
api tvaṃ kapilaḥ putra kiṃ nasmarasi duṣkṛtam |
vacoduścaritasyāyaṃ paripāko'nubhūyate || k39.13 ||
sāpyakalyāṇamitraṃ te jananī kkādya vartate |
sarvajñenetyabhihitaḥ smṛtvā jātimuvāca saḥ || k39.14 ||
vibho bhavāmi kapilaḥ smarāmi nijaduṣkṛtam |
vacoduścaritasyāyaṃ paripāko'nubhūyate || k39.15 ||
yātā me narakaṃ mātā narakādeśinī purā |
ityuktvā makarastatra ruroda paruṣasvaram || k39.16 ||
taṃ śokasāgare magnaṃ babhāṣe bhagavān punaḥ |
akāle kiṃ karomyadya tiryagyonigatasya te || k39.17 ||
apuṇyaprārambhe rabhasahasitollāsavihite
pramattānāṃ yāte narakaparipākapraṇayitām |
aśānteḥ saṃtāpaṃ ruditaśaraṇāni pratiniśaṃ
bhṛśaṃ kleśāveśairdiśati viṣatulyairanuśayaḥ || k39.18 ||
kṣaṇaṃ duḥkhakṣayāyaiva mayi cittaṃ prasādaya |
prasannamānasaḥ kāle yāsyasi tridaśālayam || k39.19 ||
śṛṇu vatsa hitaṃ cedaṃ vicārya kuru cetasi |
anityāḥ sarvasaṃskārāḥ śāntinirvāṇamakṣayam || k39.20 ||
ityājñayāḥ bhagavatastasmin yāte prasannatām |
janasaṃghaḥ sa suciraṃ babhūvāścaryaniścalaḥ || k39.21 ||
āryānandaḥ praṇayinā janenābhyarthitastataḥ |
tatpūrvavṛttaṃ papraccha bhagavantaṃ kṛtāñjaliḥ || k39.22 ||
sa tena pṛṣṭaḥ provāca vimapajñānalocanaḥ |
asyākuśalaśīlasya vṛttāntaḥ śrūyatāmayam || k39.23 ||
bhadrakākhye purā kalpe varṣāyutayugāyuṣi |
jane babhūva bhagavān kāśyapākhyastathāgataḥ|| k39.24 ||
kṛkirnāma mahīpālaḥ kalpaśāla ivārthinām |
abhavatsamaye tasmin vārāṇasyāṃ bahupradaḥ || k39.25 ||
kadācidvibudhāsthāne sahasrākṣamivāparam |
āsīnaṃ vādisiṃhākhyastaṃ vidvānāyayau dvijaḥ || k39.26 ||
avilambitasaṃprāptadarśanāsanasatkṛtiḥ |
sa dattāśīrnarapatiṃ śiṣyaśreṇivṛto'bhyadhāt || k39.27 ||
svasti svastimate budhādhipasabhāsīnāya tubhyaṃ vibho
lubdhāḥ saccaritāmṛte tava paraṃ saṃdarśane rāgiṇaḥ |
sadveṣāḥ parabhūpanāmni mukharāste sudguṇodīraṇe
kasmātsarvaguṇāśrayeṇa bhavatā duṣairvayaṃ yojitāḥ || k39.28 ||
yadyācakā api nirantararatnavarṣe nānārthisārthaparipūrakatāṃ prayānti |
sarvaṃ bhavānupamapuṇyanidhe vadānya nirdainyadānavibhavasya vijṛmbhitaṃ te || k39.29 ||
rājan kiṃcitparicitaguṇaiḥ sevayā sadgurubhyaḥ
prāpto'smābhirvibudhavijayī ko'pi vidyāṃśaleśaḥ |
asyāṃ vidvatkamalabharasaumyaprabhāyāṃ sabhāyāṃ
tasyotkarṣaṃ katipayapadaṃ pratyayaṃ darśayāmaḥ || k39.30 ||
nijaguṇagaṇane dhīrlajjate sajjanānāṃ mukharayati tathāpi prauḍhavādābhilāṣaḥ |
iyati jagati rājan kṣipramānviṣyatāṃ me prativacanaruciśedasti kaścidvipaścit || k39.31 ||
saṃdarbhagarbhagambhīramiti tasyotkaṭaṃ vacaḥ |
śrutvā kṣitipatiḥ kṣipraṃ vilakṣaḥ samacintayat || k39.32 ||
aprāptapratimallo'yaṃ yadi yāyānmadoddhataḥ |
tadeṣa mamadeśasya yaśaḥkhaṇḍanadiṇḍimaḥ || k39.33 ||
guṇāpamānakṛdyatra mūrkho bhavati bhūpatiḥ |
na karoti janastatra vidyārjanapariśramam || k39.34 ||
vivekavimalāloke dharmārāme mahīpatau |
loke vidyāḥ pravartante sadācārakriyā iva || k39.35 ||
tasmādasya prayatnena kartavyo madanigraha |
vidyādaridratā deśe doṣa eva viśāṃpateḥ || k39.36 ||
iti saṃcintya nṛpatirvipraṃ karvaṭavāsinam |
ānināya mahāmatyairanviṣaya viduṣāṃ gurum || k39.37 ||
abhūbhṛtsabhāmupādhyāya pretya taṃ tarkakarkaśam |
cakāra vādisiṃhasya darpakesarakartanam || k39.38 ||
tasya tena jitasyāśu vijitāśeṣavādinaḥ |
maunasūtraṃ samāpede lajjiteva sarasvatī || k39.39 ||
ārūḍhāḥ śubhramahasaṃ nakṣatrāṇāmivodayāḥ |
uparyupari dṛśyante guṇotkarṣā manīṣiṇām || k39.40 ||
vādisiṃhaṃ visṛjyātha datvā bhūri dhanaṃ nṛpaḥ |
dadau dvijāya jayine karvaṭaṃ nagaropamam || k39.41 ||
labdharājagajāśvo'tha carukeyūrakaṅkaṇaḥ |
upādhyāyaḥ svabhavanaṃ praviveśa saha śriyā || k39.42 ||
bhujairjitā bhūmibhujāṃ vaṇijāṃ sāgarārjitāḥ |
vidyāvatāṃ virājante guṇotkarṣarjitāḥ śriyaḥ || k39.43 ||
kālena śrīmatastasya putrajanmotsavo'bhavat |
sukhe'pi sukhasaṃpattirlakṣaṇaṃ puṇyakarmaṇām || k39.44 ||
kapilo nāma sa śiśustejaḥpiṅgaśiroruhaḥ |
vardhamānamatirvidvān piturabhyadhiko'bhavat || k39.45 ||
kule mahati vaiduṣyaṃ vibhavodbhavaḥ |
vibhave satsutotkarṣaḥ phalaṃ sukṛtaśākhinaḥ || k39.46 ||
kadācidvyādhisaṃyogātpratyāsannatanukṣayaḥ |
vijane putramāhūya so'vadatputravatsalaḥ || k39.47 ||
bālye guṇārjanaṃ putra paralokasukhārjanam || k39.48 ||
uttamarṇa iva prāpte kāle sugaṇitāvadhau |
adhunā vivaśaḥ kkāhaṃ kka sā vidyā kka taddhanam || k39.49 ||
guṇapuṣpe sukhaphale baddhamūle dhanairjane |
vane vajra ivākālakālaḥ patatiḥ duḥsahaḥ || k39.50 ||
kṣapayati sakalābhirjanma vidyākalābhiḥ kṣaṇikasukhanimittaṃ saṃnidhatte ca vittam |
paśuśiśuṣu manuṣyaḥ prīyate mohaśiṣyaḥ tanuvirahamūrhūrte sarvamanyatsa cānyaḥ || k39.51 ||
idaṃ tu te hitaṃ vacmi snehamohavaśīkṛtaḥ |
saṃsārasāraśaraṇaṃ vatsa vetsi bahuśrutaḥ || k39.52 ||
santaḥ praṇamyāḥ paruṣaṇna vācyaṃ kāryaḥ prayatnena paropakāraḥ |
pāpāvapāte satataṃ hi puṃsāmetāni puṇyānyavalambanāni || k39.53 ||
alobhaśobhābharaṇā vibhūtiradveṣasaktiḥ svasukheṣvamohaḥ |
mūlatraye'smin kuśaladrumasya vasatyaśeṣākhilasatphataśrīḥ || k39.54 ||
yāvattapati tīkṣṇāṃśurasmin bhuvanamaṇḍale |
tāvattvatsadṛśaḥ putra vidvān vādī na vidyate || k39.55 ||
bhikṣubhistu na kartavyastvayā vādaḥ kadācanah |
gambhīrajñānadurbodhaprabuddhā bauddhabuddhayaḥ || k39.56 ||
purā bhikṣurmayā pṛṣṭaḥ padasyārthaṃ jahāsa mām |
praśnaṃ kartuṃ na jānīṣe vidvāniti jagāda ca || k39.57 ||
tasmādbhikṣuvivādaste paraṃ pāṇḍityapīḍanam |
balaprabhāvakāmo hi giriṃ mūrdhnāṃ na tāḍayet || k39.58 ||
ityuktvā tanayaṃ vipraḥ paralokabhuvaṃ yayau |
kāyāvasathapānthānāṃ dehināṃ na cirasthitiḥ || k39.59 ||
vāgmī kālena kapilaḥ khaṇḍitākhilapaṇḍitaḥ |
nṛpādbahugrṇaṃ prāpa dhanamānamahodayam || k39.60 ||
tataḥ kadācidekānte kapilaṃ kācarābhidhā |
saṃprāptaṃ vādisāmrājyaṃ jagāda jananī śanaiḥ || k39.61 ||
vādidarpacchidā putra digdvīpajayinā tvayā |
durjanāḥ śramaṇāḥ kasmāddarpāndhāḥ parivarjitāḥ || k39.62 ||
parotkarṣādhirūḍhasya pratipakṣe kṣamārateḥ |
akṣamo'yamiti vyaktaṃ kṣaṇena kṣīyate yaśaḥ || k39.63 ||
iti māturvacaḥ śrutvā so'vadadbiduṣo vacaḥ |
na vādaḥ śramaṇaiḥ kāryaḥ pitrāhamiti vāritaḥ || k39.64 ||
iyaṃ durjīvikāsmākaṃ patrālambanavādinām |
kriyate guṇamānyānāṃ mānamlānirmukhe yayā || k39.65 ||
dhigetaccaṇḍapāṇḍityaṃ guruvidveṣaduḥsaham |
mahatāṃ sukhabhaṅgāya sadā tasmin samudyamaḥ || k39.66 ||
yasyāṃ na mayā sā buddhiḥ sā śrīrlobhaṃ nihanti yā |
darpo na yasya vidyā sā śaktiryā ca kṣamāvatī || k39.67 ||
evameva na kartavyaḥ parairvidveṣavigrahaḥ |
kiṃ mātarjagatāṃ pūjyabhikṣubhiḥ khyātalakṣmabhiḥ || k39.68 ||
vijetuṃ na ca te śakyāḥ praṃānapariniṣṭhitāḥ |
pratipakṣairavikṣiptaṃ yeṣāṃ nairātmyaśāsanam || k39.69 ||
iti putravacaḥ śrutvā kupitā tamuvāca sā |
abhūttava pitā nūnaṃ pāpaśramaṇacetakaḥ || k39.70 ||
mahati brāhmaṇakule jātaḥ prājño bahuśrutaḥ |
bhikṣapakṣe nipatitaḥ kathaṃ tvamapi tādṛśaḥ || k39.71 ||
pṛthupramāṇakhaṅgena kuru śramaṇanigraham |
avidāryābhrasaṃghātaṃ tīrkṣṇāṃśurna virājate || k39.72 ||
itsa prerito māturgirā tadbhaktiyantritaḥ |
bhikṣūṇāmāśramapadaṃ śanairgantuṃ samudyayau || k39.73 ||
vrajan sa saṃmukhāyātaṃ bhikṣuṃ jijñāsayā pathi |
granthasāraṃ pramāṇaṃ ca papraccha samayocitam || k39.74 ||
sa tena pṛṣṭaḥ provāca gāḍhaśabdārthanirṇayam |
lakṣatrayapramāṇaṃ na śastraṃ tīrthikadurlabham || k39.75 ||
kutaḥ pāre'tivartante kka ca vartmātivartate |
sukhaduḥkhe ca lokasya kkacitsamabhibandhataḥ || k39.76 ||
iti gambhīraśabdārthaṃ śāsturbhagavato vacaḥ |
anupāsitasarvajñairjñāyate na yathā tathā || k39.77 ||
etadākarṇya kapilaḥ ślokagāmbhīryavismitaḥ |
yayau bhagavataḥ puṇyaṃ kāśyapasya tapovanam || k39.78 ||
tathā bhikṣugaṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā prasannahṛdayānanaḥ |
acintayattadaśraddhāṃ vihāya gatamatsaraḥ || k39.79 ||
eteṣāṃ dveṣakāluṣyātkrauryaṃ kaḥ kartumarhati |
yeṣāṃ saṃdarśanenaiva vaimalyaṃ labhate manaḥ || k39.80 ||
iti saṃcintya sa ciraṃ tadvivādaparāṅmukhaḥ |
dūrādhvakhinnaḥ svagṛhaṃ gatvā provāca mātaram || k39.81 ||
mithyaivāhaṃ tvayā mātaḥ preritaḥ kalikarmaṇi |
ajayāḥ śramaṇā loke gūḍhārthagranthavādinaḥ || k39.82 ||
ślokamātraṃ mayā śrutvā bhikṣorekasya vartmani |
ajñātārthena vailakṣyātsuciraṃ vīkṣitāṃ kṣitiḥ || k39.83 ||
tadgrantheṣvakṝtābhyāsastān vaktuṃ kahpragalbhate |
kathayanti svaśastraṃ te na hi pravrajitādṛte || k39.84 ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā jananī tamabhāṣata |
āyāsitāhaṃ bhavatā garbhabhāreṇa kevalam || k39.85 ||
saṃgharṣamarṣaśūnyena dainyātsarvapraṇāminā |
dharṣaṇānirvimarṣeṇa kriyate puruṣeṇa kim || k39.86 ||
loke sakalaratnānāṃ tejasaiva mahārghatā |
ko hyarthaḥ puruṣaprāṇaistejojīvanavarjitaiḥ || k39.87 ||
mithyā tadgranthalābḥāya pravrajyā gṛhyate na kim |
mūrdhni kṛtteṣu jāyante kiṃ keśeṣu kuśaḥ punaḥ || k39.88 ||
iti māturgirā tasya manaḥ kaluṣatāṃ yayau |
sahasā kālavātālīrajoruddhamivāmbaram || k39.89 ||
tataḥ sa kūṭapraśamapraṇayī bhikṣukānanam |
gatvā gṛhītvā pravrajyāṃ śāstraṃ saugatamāptavān || k39.90 ||
kālena dharmakathakaḥ sa vidvān guṇagauravāt |
siṃhāsanaṃ samāruhya vidadhe dharmadeśanām || k39.91 ||
jananyā preritastasyāṃ deśanāyāṃ krameṇa saḥ |
bhikṣudharmaviruddhārthamavktuṃ samupacakrame || k39.92 ||
dharmaprahāravyathitairbhikṣubhiḥ sa pade pade |
nivāryamāṇastānūce kṛtvā vikṛtamānanam || k39.93 ||
ajñātvā darpamukharairayathā bahuvādibhiḥ |
bhavadbhiḥ sthūladantoṣṭhairvyākhyā mama visūditā || k39.94 ||
yūyaṃ gardabhamarkaṭoṣṭravadanā dvīpyāsyapaśvānanā
mārjāraiṇavarāhakukkuramukhā durdarśavakrkraḥ param |
sahyā maunajuṣo'pi naiva vikaṭāṭopaṃ raṭantaḥ kimu
bhrūbhaṅgairiti bhikṣusaṃghamasakṛnnirbhartsayan so'bhyadhāt || k39.95 ||
tasya vākyaśaraistīkṣṇairvikṛttā iva bhikṣavaḥ |
anuktvaiva prativacastyaktvā taṃ yayuranyataḥ || k39.96 ||
tena vākpātakenātha paścāttāpamupāgataḥ |
tatyāja jananīmeva pravrajyāṃ na tu tāṃ dvijaḥ || k39.97 ||
śramaṇairme hṛtaḥ putra iti sā vipralāpinī |
unmādinī tanuṃ tyaktvā prapede narakasthitim || k39.98 ||
tataḥ kālena kapilaḥ svayaṃ kalitakilbiṣaḥ |
dehānte vākyapāruṣyādimāṃ makaratāṃ gataḥ || k39.99 ||
tānyetāni mukhānyasya yānyūce bhikṣubhartsane |
phalaṃ sadṛśarūpaṃ hi karmabījātprajāyate || k39.100 ||
ityuktvā tatra bhagavān dharmamādiśya śāśvatam |
janasyānugrahaṃ cakre nānābodhividhāyakam || k39.101 ||
tataḥ prayāte svapadaṃ jine tanmayamānasaḥ |
kamaraḥ projjhitāhārastyaktvā dehaṃ divaṃ yayau || k39.102 ||
cāturmahārājikeṣu deveṣu viśadadyutiḥ |
śrīmān sa jātaḥ sugate kṣaṇaṃ cittaprasādanāt || k39.103 ||
tataḥ pūrṇenduvadanaḥ sragvī rucirakuṇḍalah |
sa sākāra ivānandaḥ sugataṃ draṣṭumāyayau || k39.104 ||
prakīrṇadivyakusumaḥ kirīṭaspṛṣṭabhūtalaḥ |
prabhāpūritadikcakrastaṃ bhaktyā praṇanāma saḥ || k39.105 ||
cakre tasyopaviṣṭasya bhagavān dharmadeśanām |
yayā srotaḥphalaṃ prāpya satyadarśī jagāma saḥ || k39.106 ||
tṛṇamiva gurukāyo'pyuddhṛtaḥ pāpapaṅkād
iti sa jananikāyaḥ so'pi duḥkhājjinena |
vyasananipatitānāṃ līlayā puṇyaśīlā
nikhilamatulamūlaṃ kleśamunmūlayanti || k39.107 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ kapilavadānaṃ nāma ekonacatvāriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


40. udrāyaṇāvadānam |

tulyameva puruṣeṇa bhujyate kāyabhājanagataṃ śubhāśubham |
dehināṃ vividhakarmajaṃ phalaṃ na hyabhuktamupayāti saṃkṣayam || k40.1 ||
bhagavān sugataḥ pūrvaṃ pure rājagṛhābhidhe |
kalandakanivāsākhye vijahāra vanāntare || k40.2 ||
bimbisāraḥ kṣitipatistatrābhūdviśrutaḥ śriyā |
ākaraḥ saravratnānāṃ ratnākaramivāparaḥ || k40.3 ||
babhūva samaye tasmin raurukākhye pure nṛpaḥ |
śrīmānudrāyaṇo nāma yaśaścandramahodadhiḥ || k40.4 ||
tasya candraprabhā nāma patnī candrānanābhavat |
śikhaṇḍī yuvarājaśca sūnurvikramakarkaśaḥ || k40.5 ||
hiruko bhirukaśceti tasyāmātyau babhūvatuḥ |
gaṇanīyau na vinaye yayuḥ śukrabṛhaspatī || k40.6 ||
jātā bhūmibhujaḥ prītirlekhakhatviṣastayoḥ |
raverdūrasthitasyāpi kamalākarayoriva || k40.7 ||
apūrvaratnanicayapreṣaṇairbahuśastayoḥ |
paripūrṇaṃ paraṃ prāpa sakhyaṃ prema vidhānataḥ || k40.8 ||
dūrasthāpi paraṃ prītiḥ satāṃ kīrtirivākṣayā |
saṃsaktāpi khalaprītistṛṇajvāleva na sthirā || k40.9 ||
kadāciddivyaratnāṅkaṃ kavacaṃ kāñcanojjvalam |
prāhiṇodbimbisārāya sāramudrāyaṇo nṛpaḥ || k40.10 ||
viṣahsastrāgnirakṣārhaṃ vicitraratnakaṃ ca tat |
suhṛtpreṣitamādāya provāca saciavānnṛpaḥ || k40.11 ||
idaṃ me prahitaṃ tena saujanyamiva bhūbhujā |
sarva rakṣākṣamaṃ varma gāḍhapremanivedakam || k40.12 ||
na paśyāmyasya sadṛśaṃ pratideyaṃ tathādhikam |
alpapratikriyā śalyamupakārāpakārayoḥ || k40.13 ||
ucitaṃ cintyatāṃ kiṃcitpreṣaṇīyamato'dhikam |
sarvairbhadbhirityuktvā nṝpaścintākulo'bhavat || k40.14 ||
atha dhīmānmahāmātyaściraṃ dhyātvā tamabravīt |
varṣakārābhidho vipraḥ sarvavidyāsu pāragaḥ || k40.15 ||
ato bahuguṇaṃ rāgannekamevāstyupāyanam |
tasya saṃpreṣane yatnaḥ kriyatāṃ yadi śakyate || k40.16 ||
ya eṣa bhagavān buddhaḥ sthitastvadviṣayāntike |
devānāmādarasthānaṃ paṭastatpratimānvitaḥ || k40.17 ||
aśeṣalokakalyāṇakalikākalpapādapaḥ |
citre svapne'tha saṃkalpe pṛthupuṇyaiḥ sa dṛśyate || k40.18 ||
iti mantrivacaḥ śrutvā tathetyuktvā mahīpatiḥ |
gatvā bhagavate namrastamevārthaṃ nyavedayat || k40.19 ||
anujñātastatastena nṛpaścitrakarān varān |
ādideśāśu bhagavatpratimollekhakarmaṇi || k40.20 ||
jinasyālokayantaste mūrti rūpavśīkṛtāḥ |
yayuḥ pramāṇagrahaṇe pragalbhā apyaśaktatām || k40.21 ||
saṃkrāntāṃ nimalapaṭe chāyāṃ bhagavatastataḥ |
suvarṇabhāvanābhikhyāṃ te śanaiḥ samapūrayan || k40.22 ||
prāḥiṇodatha bhūpālastaṃ buddhapratimāpaṭam |
jagannayanapuṇyānāṃ mūrtānāmiva saṃcayam || k40.23 ||
bimbisārasya hastāṅkalekhāmudrāyaṇo nṛpaḥ |
paṭasya purataḥ prāptāṃ hṛṣṭaḥ svayamavācayat || k40.24 ||
sugatacaraṇapadmanyāsapuṇyopakaṇṭhāt
tridaśapuraviśeṣānmāgadhodāraradeśāt |
kuśalakalitamūrtirbhūpatirbimbisāraḥ
kṣititalatilakaṃ tvāṃ dharmabandhurbravīti || k40.25 ||
etatte prahitaṃ hitaṃ bhavamahāmohāmaye bheṣajaṃ
rājadveṣaviṣāpahaṃ bhagavato bimbaṃ śaśāṅkatviṣaḥ |
tṛṣṇāpacchamanaṃ prasannamadhuraṃ tvannetrapātrārpitaṃ
dhanyaḥ puṇyarasāyanaṃ piba haṭhādākaṇṭhamutkaṇṭhitaḥ || k40.26 ||
sanmārge viniyojanaṃ sguṇagaṇādhāne sadādhyāpanaṃ
durvyāpāranivāraṇaṃ thirasukhaprāptau paripreraṇam |
nirvyājopakṛtau nirantaratayā sarvātmanā vartanaṃ
kartavyaṃ kimataḥ paraṃ priyahitaṃ kalyāṇamitraiḥ satām || k40.27 ||
iti lekhārthamāsvādya suhṛtpremāmṛtocitam |
rājā gajādhirūḍhasya paṭasya prayayau puraḥ || k40.28 ||
abhinandya tamānandātsāmātyaḥ sapurohitaḥ |
hemasiṃhāsanotsaṅge sa prasārya nyaveśayat || k40.29 ||
lāvaṇyapuṇyanilayaṃ dṛṣṭvā tatsaugataṃ vapuḥ |
namo namahprabuddhāyetyavadajagatījanaḥ || k40.30 ||
buddhābhidhānaṃ śrutvaiva pulakālaṃkṛtākṛtiḥ |
puṣpavarṣe surairmukte vismito'bhūnmahīpati || k40.31 ||
puṇyaṃ bhagavataḥ śrutvā sa tatra caritāmṛtam |
payodānādasotkaṇṭhanīlakaṇṭhatulāṃ yayau || k40.32 ||
dvādaśāṅgaṃ paṭasyādha sānulomaviparyayam |
pratītyasamutpādaṃ ca dṛṣṭvā mohaṃ mumoca saḥ || k40.33 ||
srotaḥprāptiphalenaivaṃ dṛṣṭasatyo'tha bhūpatiḥ |
dideśa pratisaṃdeśaṃ sakhyurbhikṣurvisarjanaiḥ || k40.34 ||
bimbisārastatastasmai kṛtvā bhagavato'rthanām |
kātyāyanaṃ ca vyasṛjatśailākhyāṃ cāpi bhikṣuṇīm || k40.35 ||
udrāyaṇasya nṝpaterāryaḥ kātyāyano'tha saḥ |
pūjāvidhāyinastatra vidadhe dharmadeśanām || k40.36 ||
dharmadeśanayā tasya saṃgataḥ sumahān janaḥ |
srotaḥsakṛdanāgāmiphalārhatpadamāptavān || k40.37 ||
tasmin gṛhapatī khyātau tiṣyapuṣyābhidhau pure |
śāntyai pravrajya tasyāgre parinirvṛtimāpatuḥ || k40.38 ||
kālena vihitai stūpau dehānte jñātibhistayoḥ |
tannāmacihnāvadyāpi vandante caityavandakāḥ || k40.39 ||
devyāścandraprabhāyāśca śaolākhyā sāpi bhikṣuṇī |
krameṇāntaḥpure cakre satataṃ dharmadeśanām || k40.40 ||
kadācidatha bhūpālahkrīḍagāragatāṃ priyām |
tāmajñāsīnnimittajñaḥ saptāhāvadhijīvitām || k40.41 |
jñātasaṃsāracaritastatastāṃ vaśudhādhipaḥ |
anujajñe śubhapadaṃ prātuṃ pravrajyayā puraḥ || k40.42 ||
svākhyāte dharmavinmaye bhikṣukyā nṛpatergirā |
devī pravrajitā dehaṃ dine tatyāja saptame || k40.43 ||
cāturmahārājikeṣu deveṣu sahasaiva sā |
prayātā devakanyātvaṃ jagāma jinakānanam || k40.44 ||
tatra pūrṇenduvadanā divyābharaṇabhūṣitā |
dṛṣṭvā śākyamuniṃ hṛṣṭā sā papātāsya pādayoḥ || k40.45 ||
prakīrṇadivyapuṣpāyāstasyāścakre tathāgataḥ |
dharmopapādanaṃ yena dṛṣṭasatyā jagāma sā || k40.46 ||
sā gatvā nagaraṃ patyurvyomnā mūrtirivaindavī |
vibodhya niśi suptasya cakre bodhiprakāśanam || k40.47 ||
yātāyāṃ svapadaṃ tasyāṃ prabhāte vasudhādhipaḥ |
pravrajyābhimukhaḥ putramabhiṣicya śikhaṇḍinam || k40.48 ||
prajātrāṇāya nikṣipya taṃ mahāmātyayostataḥ |
nṛpaterbimbisārasya suhṛdaḥ sa yayau puram || k40.49 ||
bimbisārastamāyātaṃ vigatacchatracāmaram |
nṛpopacāraiḥ praṇataḥ prītipūtairapūjayat || k40.50 ||
viśrāntamāsanāsīnaṃ sa tamūce savismayaḥ |
hṛṣṭhaḥ saṃdarśanenāsya śrīviyogena duḥkhitaḥ || k40.51 ||
rājannanantasāmantamauliviśrāntaśāsanaḥ |
pākaśāsanatulyastvaṃ kathamevamupāgataḥ || k40.52 ||
abhinnavakrkraprakṛterguptamantrasya dhīmataḥ |
pareṇa rājyaharaṇaṃ vīra saṃbhāvyate na te || k40.53 ||
iti pṛṣṭaḥ sa suhṛdā sasmitastamabhāṣata |
rājan vṛddhivibhūtirme na priyā sarvagāminī || k40.54 ||
viṣayāsvādavaimukhyādvitṛṣṇena mayā svayam |
utsṛṣṭamiva saṃtyaktamaiśvaryaṃ bhogabhājanam || k40.55 ||
tvayā kalyāṇamitreṇa sugatapratimāpaṭaḥ |
yo'sau hitāya prahitaḥ savairāgyagururmamaḥ || k40.56 ||
adhunā tvatprasādena gatvā bhagavato'ntike |
icchāmyavāptuṃ pravrajyāmagārādanagārikaḥ || k40.57 ||
iti sakhyurvacaḥ śrutvā tattatheti vicintya ca |
sādarastadvivekena bimbisārastamabravīt || k40.58 ||
dhanyo bahumataścāsi satāṃ tvaṃ pṛthivīpate |
kathaṃ saṃsāravimukhī jātā te matirīdṛśī || k40.59 ||
saṃtoṣavibhavo bhogasubhagaḥ śobhase param |
lakṣaṇaṃ śuddhasattvānāṃ vairājyābharaṇaṃ manah || k40.60 ||
kiṃ sāmrājyamahaujasā sarajasā duḥsādhanaiḥ sādhanaiḥ
kiṃ bhogaiḥ kṣaṇabhojanaiḥ kulaṣadaiḥ kiṃ sattvaduḥkhaiḥ sukhaiḥ |
saṃsāroparamāya cetasi satāṃ janmāntaropārjitaṃ
vairāgyaṃ kurute padaṃ yadi mahāmohaprarohāpaham || k40.61 ||
yena prāṇamanaḥpriyā vasumatī saṃtyajyate līlayā
trailokyābhimate'pi yaḥ smarasukhe vaimukhyadīkṣāguruḥ |
saṃmohavysanāturaṃ jagadidaṃ yenānukampāspadaṃ
puṇyaiḥ ko'pi sa jāyate matimatāṃ saṃsāravāmaḥ śamah || k40.62 ||
ityuktvā bimbisārastaṃ nītvā veṇuvanāśramam |
sapraṇāmaṃ bhagavate tadvṛttāntaṃ nyavedayat || k40.63 ||
udrāyaṇo'pi sugatākāraṃ suciracintitam |
vilokya harṣādātmānaṃ kṛtakṛtyamamanyata || k40.64 ||
parṇāmavyagraśirasā saṃsāracchedini tanau |
papāta bhagavaddṛṣṭistasya pravrajyayā saha || k40.65 ||
bhikṣubhāvamathāsādya pātrapāṇiḥ sa cīvarī |
nagare piṇḍapātārthī cakāra janavismayam || k40.66 ||
prajāḥ śikhaṇḍī dharmeṇa pālayitvā prasannadhīḥ |
yātaḥ kālena kāluṣyamadharmābhirato'bahvat || k40.67 ||
kaluṣaṃ kāñcanarucivyaktavidyudvilāsinī |
na kasya kurute lakṣmīrmeghamāleva mānasam || k40.68 ||
anāyattaṃ mahāmātyau hiruko bhirukaśca tam |
adharmakarmanirataṃ kruddhau tatyajatuḥ prabhum || k40.69 ||
tatpade vihitau rājñā sacivau daṇḍamugdarau |
cittānuvṛttikuśalau svairaṃ saktaṃ tamūcatuḥ || k40.70 ||
prajārañjanasaṃsaktā rājadaurjanyavādinaḥ |
svayaśaḥkhyāpanāyaiva jāyante dhūrtamantriṇaḥ || k40.71 ||
na dharmaṃ na sukhaṃ nārthaṃ na kīrti na ca jīvitam |
gaṇayanti prabhorarthe te bhṛtyā bhavyabhaktayaḥ || k40.72 ||
nākhaṇḍitā nākṣayitā nātaptā nāpyapīḍitāḥ |
kurvantyarthakriyā rājñastilatulyāḥ kila prajāḥ || k40.73 ||
iti bruvāṇau tau rājñā niyuktau rājyacintane |
lobhātpravṛttau durnītau hantuṃ niḥśaraṇāḥ prajāḥ || k40.74 ||
nirvicāre durācāre kumatau pṛthivīpatau |
luptasatye mahāmātye prajānāṃ jīvitaṃ kutaḥ || k40.75 ||
udrāyaṇo'thācireṇa kadācidvaṇijaṃpathi |
nijadeśāgataṃ vārtāṃ papraccha nṛparāṣṭrayoḥ || k40.76 ||
so'vadaddeva kuśalī sutastava mahīpatiḥ |
kiṃ tu sanmantrirahitaḥ kumantrivaśamāgataḥ || k40.77 ||
tatra prajānāṃ vitatopatāpaḥ ko'pi pravṛttaḥ prabhuśāsanena |
yenādya tatkutsitadeśajanma divāniśaṃ śocati pauralokaḥ || k40.78 ||
yatra dhvāntaṃ sṛjati taraṇiryatra candro'gnivarṣī
yatrodeti prakaṭamamṛtādutkaṭaḥ kālakūṭaḥ |
yatra trātā harati nṛpatirjīvavṛttiṃ prajānāṃ
tatrākrandaṃ prasṝtavipulopaplavaṃ kaḥ śṛṇoti || k40.79 ||
ityāsaktanṛpāyāsakhinnasyārtimayaṃ vacaḥ |
duḥsahaṃ vaṇijaḥ śrutvā sa tamūce kṛpānidhiḥ || k40.80 ||
dadgirā tvamito gatvā tūrṇamāśvāsaya prajāḥ |
sthāpayiṣyāmyahaṃ dharme svayametya śikhaṇḍinam || k40.81 ||
ityuktastena sānandaḥ svadeśaṃ śanakairvaṇik |
gatvā prajānāṃ vidadhe svairamāśvāsanaṃ puraḥ || k40.82 ||
pravāde prasṛte tasminnamātyau daṇḍamudgarau |
atītabhūpāgamanatrastau bhūpatimūcatuḥ || k40.83 ||
sarvatra śrūyate deva pravādaḥ sādhuninditaḥ |
vṛddhaḥ pravrajito rājā rājyārthī yatnavāniti || k40.84 ||
tīvravrataparikliṣṭaḥ saṃbhogābhimukhādaraḥ |
lajjāṃ pravrajyayā sārdhaṃ tyaktvā sa punareṣyati || k40.85 ||
rājannapakkavairāgyāstyajanti sahasaiva yat |
tatpūrvābhyadhikaṃ teṣāṃ prayāti priyatāṃ punaḥ || k40.86 ||
lokasthitiviruddheṣu viṣayeṣu viśeṣataḥ |
spṛhā saṃjāyate jantorapathyeṣviva rogiṇaḥ || k40.87 ||
sukhotsekātparityaktaṃ prahastamupāgatam |
prāyaḥ sarvaṃ bhavatyeva jaḍasyāmramiva priyam || k40.88 ||
tasmādbhavantamutsārya pratāpanidhimāsanāt |
kṣīṇaḥ śaśīva sthaviraḥ sa rājyaṃ bhoktumicchati || k40.89 ||
cīvarodvignagātrasya varavastrābhilāṣiṇaḥ |
jātāsya muṇḍite mūrdhni ratnāṅkamukuṭaspṛhā || k40.90 ||
ratnaharmyeṣu navatāsaṃbhogavibhavodbhavam |
tyaktvā vilāsamāyāsaṃ vanavāsaṃ saheta kaḥ || k40.91 ||
mṛduśayanasukhārhā ye kathaṃ śerate te
hariṇakharakhurodyatkatkaṇṭakāsu sthalīṣu |
madhu vidhukaraśītaṃ yairnipītaṃ kathaṃ te
vanajagajamadoṣṇaṃ tiktamambhaḥ pibanti || k40.92 ||
adhunaiva tavāsannapraveśaviṣamasthiteḥ |
ādyaṃmataṃ nyāyavidāṃ rājaputra nipātanam || k40.93 ||
tasmādanāgato rājā pūrvaṃ vadhyastava prabho |
dīpaṃ hanti pataṅgo hi na dagdhaścetsamāpatan || k40.94 ||
tayoriti girā kṣipramabhūdbhūpatirākūlaḥ |
khalameghaiḥ kaluṣatāṃ nītaṃ kasya na mānasam || k40.95 ||
sa tau babhāṣe sāśaṅkaḥ krakacakrūratāṃ gataḥ |
bādhaḥ sādhāraṇaścāyaṃ yuvayormama cāgrataḥ || k40.96 ||
bhavadbhyāmeva vinayopāyaviśrāntayā dhiyā |
vicārya kāryatātparyaṃ yadyuktaṃ tadvidhīyatām || k40.97 ||
iti rājñā kṛtotsāhau tau visṛjyāśu ghātakān |
udrāyaṇasyāgrapathaṃ vadhāyaiva babandhatuḥ || k40.98 ||
so'piprajñāparitrāṇe niyoktuṃ putramudyataḥ |
bhagavantaṃ samabhyetya vrajāmīti vyajijñapat || k40.99 ||
sarvajñenābhyanujñātaḥ svakṛtaṃ bhujyatāmiti |
karmapāśasamākṛṣṭaḥ sa yayau rorukaṃ puram || k40.100 ||
tasmādvrajantaṃ nirvyājamācāramiva durjanāḥ |
duṣṭāmātyaprayuktāste jagnurvartmani ghātakāḥ || k40.101 ||
tasya cāīvarapātrādīn gṛhītvā nihatasya te |
vyadevayan kṛtaṃ prītyau rājākāryamamātyayoḥ || k40.102 ||
tataḥ pāpaprahṛṣṭābhyāṃ nṛpastābhyāṃ pradarśitam |
dṛṣṭvā mumoha sahasā raktāktaṃ cīvaraṃ pituḥ || k40.103 ||
sa labdhasaṃjñaḥ śanakaiḥ śuśoca na tathā gurum |
yathā patitamātmānaṃ ghore narakagahvare || k40.104 ||
so'vadadvatsa saṃprāptaṃ phalaṃ khalajanānmayā |
aiśvaryamadhulubdhena pāpapātamapaśyatā || k40.105 ||
aho bata nirālambe ghore narakasaṃkaṭe |
unnatārohiṇāṃ sadyaḥ pātakaṃ khalasaṃgatam || k40.106 ||
kṛtametanmahatpāpaṃ duṣṭāmātyadhiyā mayā |
patitasya mamedānīṃ pāvako'pi na pāvakah || k40.107 ||
tulyaṃ pituścārhatasya vadhe kā mama niṣkṛtiḥ |
pītaṃ yatra mayaikasmin pātre sadahanaṃviṣam || k40.108 ||
vṛddhe pitari niḥsaṅke śamaṃ pravrajyayāśrite |
svacitta niśitaṃ śastraṃ lobhādvyāpāritaṃ mayā || k40.109 ||
yatsaṃcintitameva kampajanakaṃ śrotuṃ nayatśakyate
dṛṣṭaṃ yacca karoti śokakalanāṃ niścetanānāmapi |
yatra krairyamapi prayāti mṛdutāṃ tīvrānutāpāgninā
tatrāpi prasaranti nirghṛṇadhiyāṃ nistriṃśatīkṣṇāḥ kriyāḥ || k40.110 ||
ityuktvā duḥkahsaṃtaptaḥ pralāpamukharānanaḥ |
nyavārayattayoḥ kopātpraveśaṃ duṣṭamantriṇoḥ || k40.111 ||
guṇāntaraṃ parijñāya bhirukaṃ hirukaṃ ca saḥ |
ānināya prasādyāśu purāṇau sacivau pituḥ || k40.112 ||
tataścintākṛśe rājñi śokātpāṇḍuratāṃ gate |
svairaṃ tajjananīmetya duṣṭāmātyāvavocatām || k40.113 ||
devi tvatanayaḥ śrīmān svabhāvasaralāśayaḥ |
rājyarakṣāṃ na jānāti svajanocchedakarkaśām || k40.114 ||
pitā pravrajito'pyasya rājyaṃ hartumupāgataḥ |
āvābhyāṃ praśamaṃ nītastatra kā nāma vācyatā || k40.115 ||
nīcatantropapannaścetkramo'yamaśubhakramaḥ |
rājyābhilāṣiṇo bhikṣostasyāpi sa kathaṃ kramaḥ || k40.116 ||
āvāṃ pitṛvadhakridhādvāritau bhūbhujā padāt |
svayamadyāpi śokena kiṃ mithyā pariśuṣyate || k40.117 ||
sukṛtaṃ kṛtamāvābhyāṃ prabhorduḥkhakṛśāṅgatā |
bhavanti sarvabhāveṣu bhṛjyā evāparādhinaḥ || k40.118 ||
gataṃ śocati kiṃ rājā yatkṛtaṃ kṛtameva tat |
upekṣyate tvayā devi kasmāccintākṛśaḥ sutaḥ || k40.119 ||
tābhyāmityuditaṃ śrutvā sā rājajananī śanaiḥ |
ūce taralikā nāma tadvākyavihitādarā || k40.120 ||
ānantaryamidaṃ karma dvayornarakapātakam |
yuṣmanmatādupanataṃ rājñaḥ pūrvakṛtena vā || k40.121 ||
ahaṃ tu vārayāmyasya śokaṃ pitṛvadhodbhavam |
arhadvadhodbhavaṃ duḥkhaṃ bhavadbhyāmapi vāryatām || k40.122 ||
iti tau svairamādiśya sā gatvā pārthivāntikam |
tamuvāca śucākrāntaṃ parikṣīṇamivoḍupam || k40.123 ||
dharmādharmamayaṃ putra rājyaṃ rājñāṃ bahucchalam |
pāpānāṃ śaṅkayā tasmin kiṃ śucā pariśuṣyasi || k40.124 ||
piturvadhātpratapto'si yadi nāma gurupriyaḥ |
tatrocyate samutsṛjya lajjāṃ tvadduḥkhasaṃkaṭe || k40.125 ||
svairaṃ jātastvamanyena na sa taddharmataḥ pitā |
svecchāhārasukhāḥ putra striyo hi nirapatrpāḥ || k40.126 ||
ityapriyamapi śrutvā rājā tadvacanaṃ rahaḥ |
pitṛvaiśasapāpograduḥkhasaṃtāpamatyajat || k40.127 ||
prakurvantyastādrerudayagiriṇā kleśakalanāṃ
kṣaṇātkṣoṇīkṣmābhṛdvighaṭanavonodaṃ vidadhati |
sṛjantyetā vahniṃ sapadi salilāttacca dahanād
aśādhyaṃ nārīṇāṃ na hi bhavati kiṃcitrtribhuvane || k40.128 ||
atha so'rhadvadhenaiva śalyatulyena pīḍitaḥ |
nṛpaḥ papraccha dharmajñānniṣkṛtiṃ tasya karmaṇaḥ || k40.129 ||
tatastau duṣṭasacivau tiṣyapuṣyākhyacaityayoḥ |
mārjārapotau dhṛtvāntaḥsaktāvāmiṣaśikṣayā || k40.130 ||
niṣiddhāvapi dhāṣṭaryena praviśya nṛpateḥ sabhām |
tamūcatustīvratāpasaṃtāpapraśamārthinam || k40.131 ||
deva mithyaiva bhavatā cittamāyāsyate bhṛśam |
sarvakalyāṇā loke'sminnārhantaḥ santi te matāḥ || k40.132 ||
yadi satyaṃ bhaveyuste nabhaso rājahaṃsavat |
ṛddhimantaḥ kathaṃ teṣāmanyena vadhasaṃbhavaḥ || k40.133 ||
na santi tasmādarhantaḥ kutastadvadhapātakam |
sīmāvivādaḥ kastatryatra grāmo na vidyate || k40.134 ||
tiṣyapuṣyau gṛhapatī yāvarhatpadamāpatuḥ |
mārjārāvantare jātau tāvevādyasvacaityayoḥ || k40.135 ||
prakaṭau tau ca dṛśyete pratyakṣaṃ kasya saṃśayaḥ |
pratyayo yadi nāstyeva svayaṃ kiṃ na nirīkṣyate || k40.136 ||
ityuktvā bhūpateḥ kṛtvā khalau dolākulaṃ manaḥ |
jagmatuḥ sahitau tena caityasaṃdarśanāya tau || k40.137 ||
apūrvakautukāveśāttatra saṃghaṭite jane |
vilokanodyate rājño sāmātye duṣṭamantriṇau || k40.138 ||
āmiṣābhyāsasaṃbaddhatiṣyapuṣyābhidhānayoḥ |
dhūrtau cakraturāhvānaṃ śanairbālabiḍālayoḥ || k40.139 ||
tau tiṣyapuṣyāvarhantau mārjārau stho yuvāṃ yadi |
pradakṣīṇaṃ vā kriyatāṃ tena satyena caityayoḥ || k40.140 ||
māṃsadānakṣaṇe tābhyāmiti vācamudīritau |
tūrṇaṃ nirgatya mārjārau cakratustau pradakṣiṇam || k40.141 ||
taddṛṣṭvā sahasāvāptapratyaye sānuge nṛpe |
yāte durjanamāyaiva jagajjayamahīṃ yayau || k40.142 ||
muṣṭau vāyuṃ dṛṣadi kamalaṃ citramākāśadeśe
jihvāgre ca pracuraracanāsṛṣṭisaṃhāralīlāḥ |
kiṃ vā nānyatpaśuśiśudhiyāṃ mohanāyendrajālaṃ
mūrtaṃ dhurtāḥ kṣaṇaparicitapratyayaṃ darśayanti || k40.143 ||
niṣpratyayaparo rājā tataḥ saugatadarśane |
āryakātyāyanasyāgre śraddhāpūjāmavārayat || k40.144 ||
rājadhānyāṃ niṣiddho'tha bahireva sasānugaḥ |
vineyakṛpayā tatra tasthau śailā ca bhikṣuṇī || k40.145 ||
tataḥ kadācidāyāntaṃ dṛṣṭvā kātyāyanaḥ puraḥ |
nṛpatiṃ janasaṃpātādavamānabhayādyayau || k40.146 ||
preṣitaṃ pūrvamantribhyāṃ vrajantamavalokya tam |
duṣṭāmātyau narapatiṃ dīrghavairāvavocatām || k40.147 ||
rājannamaṅgalanidhirmuṇḍo'yaṃ viśiraḥ pathi |
dṛṣṭo'dya bhikṣurasmābhirna vidmaḥ kiṃ bhaviṣyati || k40.148 ||
na paśyāmi mukhaṃ rājñaḥ pāpasyeti bhaṇatyasau |
tathā hi kṣaṇamekānte gatvā dūramitaḥ sthitaḥ || k40.149 ||
śrutvaitaddurjanāmarṣāduvācānucarānnṛpaḥ |
eṣa dūrasthitaḥ pāṃśumuṣṭibhiḥ pūryatāmiti || k40.150 ||
pūryamāṇaḥ sa taiḥ pāṃśumuṣṭibhirduṣṭaceṭakaiḥ |
divyāṃ kuṭīṃ praveśena parihārāya nirmame || k40.151 ||
amarṣakopitāḥ sarpāḥ vyāghrā vā pītalohitāḥ |
śānterāyānti mṛdutāṃ natu bhūpaticeṭakāḥ || k40.152 ||
tataḥ prayāte nṛpatau pāṃśurāśiśatāvṛtam |
duḥkhādūcaturabhyetya hiruko bhirukaśca tam || k40.153 ||
ārya kṛcchramavāpto'si rājñā krūreṇa duṣkṛtaiḥ |
locanāni dhigasmākaṃ yairidaṃ dṛśyate pureḥ || k40.154 ||
mohāndhaḥ pātakaśvabhre durjanaiḥ pātito nṛpaḥ |
karmaṇo vayamapyasya darśanātpāpabhāginaḥ || k40.155 ||
bhūriyaṃ bhūripāpārtā tyājyā prājyamatestava |
duḥsahaḥ khalasaṃvāsaḥ tyāgaḥ kasya na saṃmataḥ || k40.156 ||
prayāti na śamaḥ śamaṃ kṣayamupaiti naiva kṣamā
bhavanti na ca buddhayaḥ paruṣaroṣadoṣaspṛśaḥ |
vasanti na vimānanā manasi śalyatulyāḥ satāṃ
na duṣṭajanavarjanādaparamāsti loke sukham || k40.157 ||
aiśvaryaṃ guṇināmadhonipatanāyāsaprayāsapradaṃ
gāmbhīryaṃ timirākāraṃ praviśatāṃ prāṇāpahaṃ prāṇinām |
naṣṭā sāpi nikṛṣṭduṣṭkuṭilavyālairupādeyatā
kūpasyeva khalasya nāsti tadaho doṣāliyuktaṃ yataḥ || k40.158 ||
tayoriti vacaḥ śrutvā mahākātyāyano'vadat |
na nikāre'pi me kopaḥ karmaṇo gatirīdṛśī || k40.159 ||
etāvadeva me duḥkhaṃ yanmūḍhasya mahīpateḥ |
khalasaṃgamadoṣeṇa bhayaṃ mahadupasthitam || k40.160 ||
prathame hi mahāvāyuḥ pureasya nipatiṣyati |
dvitīye puṣpavṛṣṭiśca vastravṛṣṭistataḥ pare || k40.161 ||
rūpyavṛṣṭiścaturthe ca hemavṛṣṭiśca pañcame |
ratnavṛṣṭistataḥ ṣaṣṭhe pāṃśuvṛṣṭiśca saptame || k40.162 ||
tayā sabandhurāṣṭro'sayna bhaviṣyati bhūpatiḥ |
tasmādbhavadbhyāṃ gantavyaṃ ratnānyādāya bhūyase || k40.163 ||
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā viniścitya tatheti tau |
hirukaḥ śyāmakaṃ putraṃ tasyopasthāpakaṃ vyadhāt || k40.164 ||
bhirukaśca sutāṃ śyāmāvatīmādāya pāṇinā |
abhyetya bhikṣukīṃ śailāṃ praṇayādidamabravīt || k40.165 ||
ārye bhavatyā me kanyā ghoṣilasya gṛhaprabhoḥ |
gṛhe samarpaṇīyeyamāsannapratipannayā || k40.166 ||
evamuktvārpayitvā tāvamātyau jagmaturgṛham |
śailāpi kanyāmādāya prayayau ghoṣilālayam || k40.167 ||
tataḥ krameṇa tadabhūdyathoktaṃ bhikṣuṇā pure |
jñānadīpavatī prajñā yathātattvaṃ hi paśyati || k40.168 ||
ṣaṣṭhe'hni ratnavarṣe'tha patite ratnapūritām |
yayaturnāvamādāya tāvamātyāvalakṣitau || k40.169 ||
tau dakṣiṇāṃ diśaṃ gatvā cakraturnagaradvayam |
hiruko hirukākhyānaṃ bhirukākhyaṃ tathāparaḥ || k40.170 ||
pare'hni pāṃśuvarṣeṇamahatā patatā nṛpaḥ |
sabandhurāṣṭraḥ pralayṃ prayayau narakātithiḥ || k40.171 ||
sadaṇḍimudgare rājñi yāte kilbiṣaśeṣatām |
taṃ mantriputramādāya vyomnā kātyāyano yayau || k40.172 ||
tamevānugatā prītyā nabhasā puradevatā |
tadājñayā khavacanīkarvaṭe vidadhe sthitim || k40.173 ||
bhikṣupuṇyānubhāvena bhāgyairmantrisutasya ca |
adhiṣṭhānena devyāśca śrīmattadabahvatpuram || k40.174 ||
tatrātha devatā cakre caityaṃ kātyāyanasya sā |
suravatyāṃ yadadyāpi vandante caityavandakāḥ || k40.175 ||
mantrisūnumathādāya lagnaṃ cīvarakarṇike |
lambanaṃ sa yayau vyomnā deśaṃ kātyāyanaḥ param || k40.176 ||
lambate lambate ko'yamityukte vismayājjanaiḥ |
babhūvuste janāstatra lambakā iti viśrutāḥ || k40.177 ||
atrāntare divaṃ yāte tatrāputre mahīpatau |
sa kṛtaḥ śyāmako rājā lakṣaṇajñistadājñayā || k40.178 ||
gatvā bhokkānakaṃ nāma diśā kātyāyanastataḥ |
jananyāstatra saṃśuddhāṃ vidasdhe dharmadeśanām || k40.179 ||
sā dṛṣṭasatyā putrasya yaṣṭīmādāya sādaram |
vandyamādyapi mahatī yaṣṭicaityamakārayat || k40.180 ||
śrāvastīmatha sotkaṇṭhaḥ prāpya kātyāyanaḥ śanaiḥ |
jinaṃ vilokya sānandaścakre tatpādavandanam || k40.181 ||
udrāyaṇasutakathāṃ tatra tena niveditām |
ākarṇya bhikṣubhiḥ pṛṣṭaḥ sarvajñastānabhāṣata || k40.182 ||
lubdhakahkālapāśākhyaḥ karvaṭopāntakānane |
sakūṭāṃ mṛgabandhāya nidadhe vāgurāṃ puraḥ || k40.183 ||
yantraṃ pāśāvṛtaṃ datvā yāte tasmin yadṛśchayā |
pratyekabuddhastaṃ deśaṃ prāpya viśrāntimāptavān || k40.184 ||
tasya puṇyānubhāvena bandhaṃ na viviśurmṛgāḥ |
na hi śuddhātmanāmagre prāpnotyakuśalaṃ janah || k40.185 ||
lubdhako'pi tato'bhyetya pāśānālokya nirmṛgān |
pratyekabuddhaṃ krodhāndho viṣadigdheṣuṇāvadhīṭ || k40.186 ||
tasya sāyakaviddhasya jvalajjvalanatejsaḥ |
prabhāvamadbhutaṃ dṛṣṭvā pādayornipapāta saḥ || k40.187 ||
akāryakaraṇodvegasaṃtāpādatha lubdhakaḥ |
nininda śocannātmānaṃ saṃtyajya śaravāgurāḥ || k40.188 ||
parinirvāṇamāptasya tasyāsthīni nidhāya saḥ |
chatradhvajādisaṃbhāraiḥ stūpaṃ cakre sadārcitam || k40.189 ||
lubdhakastena puṇyena babhūvodrāyaṇo nṛpaḥ |
vadhātpratyekabuddhasya bahuśo vadhamāptavān || k40.190 ||
nandanāmno gṛhapatermadalekhābhidhā sutā |
babhūva dhanadhanyādisphūtiḥ karvaṭavāsinaḥ || k40.191 ||
sā kadācinmadotsiktāḥ gṛhamārjanareṇubhiḥ |
pratyekabuddhamāyāntaṃ pathi mohādavākirat || k40.192 ||
tasminneva dine tasyāściracintābhirarthitaḥ |
varaḥ stanabharārtāyā varaṇārthī samāyayau || k40.193 ||
mūdhni pratyekabuddhasya pāṃśumuṣṭiunipātanāt |
pratyāsannavivāhāhamiti bhrātaramāha sā || k40.194 ||
tatastasyāhpravādena cikṣipurvaraṇāptaye |
mūrdhni pratyekabuddhasya rajāṃsi prauḍhakanyakāḥ || k40.195 ||
guṇākārapravṛttena prayayena vimohitāḥ |
nirvicārya pravartante viruddheṣvapi vastuṣu || k40.196 ||
pravṛttapātakācāre tasmin buddhabudhābhidhau |
nivāraṇaṃ gṛhapatī karmaṇastasya cakratuḥ || k40.197 ||
saiva kanyā narapatiḥ śikhaṇḍī pāpabhāgabhūt |
pravādakartā tadbhrātā bhikṣuḥ katyāyano'pyayam || k40.198 ||
jātau gṛhapatī rūḍhaduṣṭācāranivāraṇāt |
puripatāpānnirmuktau hiruko bhirukaśca tau || k40.199 ||
iti bhagavataḥ śrutvā vākyaṃ vicārya ca bhikṣavaḥ
phalapariṇatiṃ jñātvā citrāṃ śubhāśubhakarmaṇām |
khalajanavacastulyaṃ śatruṃ vicārasamaṃ guruṃ
sukṛtasadṛśaṃ bandhuṃ loke na kiṃcana menire || k40.200 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāmudrāyaṇāvadānaṃ catvāriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


41. kapilāvadānam |

yadbhūpālaviśāladānabibhavaprodbhūtapuṇyādhikaṃ
dānasyātikṛśasya satphalabharamapnotyalaṃ durgataḥ |
śuddhasyaiva vivṛddhadharmadhavalaśraddhāsamṛdhyānvitaṃ
niḥsaṃsāravijṛmbhitaṃ taducitaṃ cittasya vittasya ca || k41.1 ||
jine jetavanārāmavihāriṇi mahādhanah |
dhīrābhidhānaḥ śrāvastyāmabhūdgṛhapatiḥ purāḥ || k41.2 ||
tasya paṇḍitanāmabhūtputraḥ sukṛtapaṇḍitaḥ |
akhaṇḍitayaśaḥpuṇyadānālaṃkāramaṇḍitaḥ || k41.3 ||
sa bāla eva bhikṣūṇāṃ rājārhairvastrabhojanaiḥ |
śāriputrapradhānānāṃ cakārātithisatkriyām || k41.4 ||
tataḥ kadācidakṣīṇādurbhikṣakṣayite jane |
yācyayācakatulyatve piṇḍicchedo'thināmabhūt || k41.5 ||
bhikṣūṇāṃ saṃkaṭe tasmin kāle paramadāruṇe |
paṇḍitaḥ sugatāhūtaḥ pratasthe jetakānanam || k41.6 ||
taṃ vrajantaṃ turaṅgeṇa kāñcanādāmaśobhinam |
ūcurviṭāḥ samabhyetya guṇotsāhāsahiṣṇavaḥ || k41.7 ||
arthisārthārthanākalpavṛkṣastvaṃ dikṣu viśrutaḥ |
śatāni pañca saṃprāptāstvāmuddiśyāśayā vayam || k41.8 ||
alaṃkārāṃśukayugaṃ pratyekaṃ naḥ samīhitam |
adhunaivāvilambena dīyatāṃ yadi śakyate || k41.9 ||
ityuktastaiḥ sadācāraḥ so'vatīrya turaṅgamāt |
sādhu pūjāṃ vidhāyaiṣāṃ dhīmān kṣaṇamacintayat || k41.10 ||
bhagavantamadṛṣṭvaiva gacchāmi svagṛhaṃ yadi |
āsannāmṛtapānasya taṃ vighnaṃ kathamutaśe || k41.11 ||
adatvā priyamarthibhyo vrajāmi yadi nistrapah |
kathaṃ karomi dānasya tāṃ svayaṃ vratakhaṇḍanām || k41.12 ||
iti cintayatastasya bhittvā bhūmiṃ samudgataḥ |
nāgarājaḥ svayaṃ śeṣaḥ prādādarthisamīhitam || k41.13 ||
dattāni nāgarājena vastrāṇyābharaṇāni ca |
sa tebhyaḥ pratipādyāśu yayau niḥśalyatāmiva || k41.14 ||
te'pi dṛṣṭvā tadāścaryaṃ puṇyāṃ sugatabhāvanām |
sarvārthasaṃpatsiddhīnāṃ jananīmeva menire || k41.15 ||
jātacittaprasādāste tenaiva sahitāstataḥ |
bhagavantaṃ yayurdraṣṭuṃ vinaṣṭadveṣakalmaṣāḥ || k41.16 ||
bhagavantamathālokya kumāraḥ praṇatānanaḥ |
tatpādapadmarajasā dhanyaścakre lalāṭikām || k41.17 ||
hāraṃ punaścaraṇayoḥ śāstuḥ śaśikarojjvalam |
vinyasya praṇatānagre sa tānasmai nyavedayat || k41.18 ||
dharmadeśanayā teṣāṃ bhagavān jñānavajrabhṛt |
bhittvā satkāyadṛṣṭyadriṃ srotaḥprāptiphalaṃ vyadhāt || k41.19 ||
dṛṣṭasatyeṣu yāteṣu tatasteṣu praṇamya tam |
kumāraṃpaṇḍitaṃ prītyā bhagavān svayamabhyadhāt || k41.20 ||
vatsa puṇyairavāpto'si paryāptiṃ sukṛtaśriyām |
durbhikṣesvapi bhikṣūṇāṃ kuru bhojyādhivāsanām || k41.21 ||
parigraho me bhikṣūṇāṃ śatānyarhatrayodaśa |
anye cānviṣya kṛcchrārtāḥ saṃvibhajyāstvayā pure || k41.22 ||
iti śrutvā bhagavataḥ paṇḍitaḥ pramadākulaḥ |
bhaktyā saṃghasya vidadhe yāvajjīvaṃ nimantraṇam || k41.23 ||
tataḥ svagṛhamabhyetya rājārhaibhikṣusaṃmataiḥ |
saṃbuddhapramukhaṃ saṃghaṃ sadā bhojyairapūjayat || k41.24 ||
daridrānadaridrāṃśca yācyānapi ca yācakān |
anukampyān sa vidadhe dānenānyānukampinah || k41.25 ||
śeṣān kṛpaṇasaṃghātān so'nviṣya karuṇāmbudhiḥ |
ratnarāśiṃ dadau tebhyo daurgatyatimirāpaham || k41.26 ||
sa ratnanikarasteṣāṃ jagāmāṅgārarāśitām |
nṛṇāṃ bhāgyāni ratnāni maṇayaḥ prasthajātayaḥ || k41.27 ||
te tamūcuḥ samabhyetya svapnadṛṣṭadhanā iva |
ratnanāmnā tvayāsmākaṃ sa datto'ṅgārasaṃcayaḥ || k41.28 ||
dhanalābhena mahatā sadyaḥ prāptonnatirjanah |
tatsaṃkṣayātkṣaṇenaiva paribhraṣṭo na jīvati || k41.29 ||
iti teṣāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā paṇḍitaḥ karuṇānidhiḥ |
tānūce puṇyadīnānāṃ ratnānyāyāntyaratnatām || k41.30 ||
yuṣmābhirna kṛtaḥ pūrvaṃ mohātsukṛtasaṃcayaḥ |
tenāyaṃ ratnarāśirvaḥ prayāto'ṅgārasāratām || k41.31 ||
ratnāni yatnanihitānyapi yānti dūraṃ puṇyakṣayādupanayanti ca bhāgyayogāt |
vittārjanaṃ patitaśokanimittameva vittaṃ hi cittamucitaṃ sukṛtapravṛttam || k41.32 ||
tasmādbhavadbhirbhojyāya bhikṣusaṃgho'dhivāsyatām |
bhogasaṃbhārasaṃpattimahaṃ saṃpādayāmi vaḥ || k41.33 ||
ityuktāstena taddattavittabhojanasaṃpadā |
te buddhapramukhaṃ saṃghaṃ dinamekamapūjayan || k41.34 ||
saṃghaṃ yathāvadabhyarcya praṇīdhānamakāri taiḥ |
mākadācana dāridyraṃ syādasmākamiti kṣaṇam || k41.35 ||
tataste paṇḍitagirā gatvā dadṝśuragrataḥ |
tamevāṅgāranikaraṃ prayātaṃ ratnarāśitām || k41.36 ||
bhavane paṇḍitasyātha kumārasya prabhavataḥ |
vivṛtānāṃ nidhānānāṃ nirvighnaṃ śatamudyayau || k41.37 ||
sa prasenajite rājñe dharmajñaḥ sthitirakṣaṇāt |
dadau nidhānaṣaḍbhāgaṃ sa cāsyāṅgāratāmagāt || k41.38 ||
kumārasyaiva sukṛtairbhogyo'yaṃ nidhisaṃcayaḥ |
ityantarīkṣādvacanaṃ tataḥ śuśrāva bhūpatiḥ || k41.39 ||
kumārasyaiva vacasā tānnidhīnnirdhitāṃ punaḥ |
prāptāṃ vilokya sāścaryaḥ prahiṇottadgṛhaṃ nṛpaḥ || k41.40 ||
tatastadakhilaṃ vittaṃ vitīrya vipulāśayaḥ |
kumāraḥ saṃpadāṃ cakre sthitiṃ durgataveśmasu || k41.41 ||
atha niḥsārasaṃsāravicāravirataspṛhaḥ |
anityatāṃ sa saṃcintyaḥ dīraḥ pitarabravīt || k41.42 ||
anujānīhi māṃ tāvatgantuṃ tāta patovanam |
imā janmaśatocchiṣṭāḥ kliṣṭā mama vibhūtayaḥ || k41.43 ||
trailokyasaṃpatsaṃprāptiryasmin vrajati bhogyatām |
tadidaṃ sarvabhūtānāmāyurbhājanamalpakam || k41.44 ||
śīte yasya karomi saṃtatamṛdusparśāṃśukaurgūhanaṃ
saṃtāpe racayāmi yasya śiśiraśrīkhaṇḍacarcārcanam |
yasyārthe viṣaśastravahnibhujagavrātātparaṃ me bhayaṃ
prāptaḥ so'pyamapāyataḥ parihṛte'pyāyāti kāyaḥ kṣayam || k41.45 ||
bhogādviraktaḥ pravrajyāmādāya dayitāṃ vane |
viharāmi haran cintāṃ cintātaptasya cetasaḥ || k41.46 ||
ityuktvā sa parityajya viṣayasnehabandhanam |
kṛtābhyuopagamaḥ pitrā śāriputrāśamaṃ yayau || k41.47 ||
tatra pravrajitastena pātrapāṇiḥ sacīvaraḥ |
tasyaivānucaro bhūtvā vicacāra yatavfataḥ || k41.48 ||
sa dṛṣṭvā karṣakairdhārāṃ kṣetrātkṣetrapravartitām |
nirdiṣṭena pathā yāntīṃ vismayādityacintayat || k41.49 ||
aho vihitamārgeṇa gacchatāmapyacetasām |
jalānāṃ karmasaṃsiddhardṛśyate natu dehinām || k41.50 ||
saṃcintyeti vrajannagre dṛṣṭvā yaṣṭīkṛtaṃ śaram |
prataptamiṣukāreṇa pradadhyau dhīmatāṃ varaḥ || k41.51 ||
tāpātpraguṇatāmete yānti niścetanāḥ śarāḥ |
na tu saṃsārasaṃtaptā api vakrāḥ śarīriṇaḥ || k41.52 ||
iti dhyāyan vilokyāgre takṣṇā śakaṭacakratām |
nītāni dṛḍharūpāṇi punaścintāṃ samāyayau || k41.53 ||
aho nu ghaṭanāyogādyānti karmaṇyatāṃ kṣaṇāt |
niścetanānidārūṇi na cittāni śarīriṇām || k41.54 ||
itcaṃcintya saṃyātaḥ sudharmaniyamādaraḥ |
vatsalaṃ pitaraṃ putra ivācāryamuvāca saḥ || k41.55 ||
ārya eva prayātvadya piṇḍapātāya matkṛte |
ahaṃ tu bhavatādiṣṭaṃ cintayāmi nijavratam || k41.56 ||
ityupādhyāyamabhyarthya bhaktakṛtyāya paṇḍitaḥ |
tasmin yāte tadādiṣṭaṃ vihārāgāramāviśat || k41.57 ||
tatra yaṣṭīkṛtatanuḥ kṛtvā pratimukhīṃ smṛtim |
sa pradadhyau nijaṃ dharmaṃ baddhaparyaṅkaniścalaḥ || k41.58 ||
tasmin samādhisaṃnaddhe vasudhā sadharādharā |
vicacālākhilāmbhodhijalaloladukūlinī || k41.59 ||
śakrastaṃ dhyānanirataṃ jñātvā nirvighnasiddhaye |
dideśa dikṣu rakṣāyai dikpālān sendubhāskarān || k41.60 ||
bhagavānatha sarvajñastasya siddhimupasthitām |
pākātkuśalamūlānāṃ jñātvā kṣaṇamacintayat || k41.61 ||
āsannārhatpadasyāsya śāriputraḥ sametya cet |
dvāramuddhāṭayenmadhye vighna eṣa na saṃśayaḥ || k41.62 ||
tasmādāgacchatastasya gatvā svayamahaṃ puraḥ |
karomi kālahārāya nānāpraśnāśrayāḥ kathāḥ || k41.63 ||
iti saṃcintya bhagavān svayaṃ taddiśamāgataḥ |
bhikṣorāgacchatasyasya vjlambaṃ kathayākarot || k41.64 ||
suraprabhāvānniḥśabde nabhogatavihaṃgame |
loke nirvātadīpasya tulyatāṃ prāpa paṇḍitaḥ || k41.65 ||
srotaḥ prāptiphalādūrdhvaṃ sakṛdāgāmyavāpya saḥ |
anāgāmiphalaṃ prāpya tato'rhatphalamāptavān || k41.66 ||
tataḥ kathānte sugate prayāte nijamāśramam |
śāriputraḥ praviśyarkamiva śiṣyaṃ vyalokayat || k41.67 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā sahasottīrṇaṃ viśīrṇabhavabandhanam |
siddhiṃ yugaśataprāpyāṃ tasya tāṃ praśaśaṃsa saḥ || k41.68 ||
tāṃ tasyārhatpadaprāptiṃ śrutvā jagati viśrutām |
bhikṣubhirbhagavāṇpṛṣṭastatkathāmabravījjinah || k41.69 ||
bhagavān kāśyapaḥ pūrvaṃ vārāṇasyāṃ tathāgataḥ |
saha bhikṣūsahasrāṇāṃ viṃśatyā puravāsibhiḥ || k41.70 ||
śraddhāpraṇītaiḥ śucibhiḥ sarvabhogyairmanonugaiḥ |
uvāsa pūjitahkaṃcitkālaṃ sattvahitodyataḥ || k41.71 ||
bhikṣupūjāpare tatra vartamāne gṛhe gṛhe |
acintyayadviniḥśvasya durgato nāma durgataḥ || k41.72 ||
dhiṅmāmatīva dāridyrātnīcaṃ niṣkuśalakriyam |
naiko'pi mandabhāgyena yena bhikṣurnimantritaḥ || k41.73 ||
tyājyā janasya sakalavyavahārabāhyāḥ vākyapramāṇapadasaṃdhiṣu naiva yogyāḥ |
naṣṭakriyā vigatakārakatarkahīnāḥ śabdā ivārtharahitāḥ puruṣā bhavanti || k41.74 ||
iti cintānalākrāntaṃ ninditaṃ dhanahīnataḥ |
taṃ samāhūya ko'pyetya sukṛtaprerako'bhyadhāt || k41.75 ||
kṣīṇārthenāpi bhavatā janmāntaraśubhāptaye |
yathākathaṃcideko'pi bhikṣuḥ kiṃ na nimantritaḥ || k41.76 ||
ityuktastena saṃsaktaśalyaḥ punarivāhataḥ |
bhiokṣubhojanavaikalyātsa bhṛdhaṃ vyathito'bhavat || k41.77 ||
kathaṃcitkṣḥutparikṣāmaḥ sa gatvā śreṣṭhimandiram |
yatnena prāpa mūlyāṃśaṃ dārupāṭanakarmaṇā || k41.78 ||
kṛtvā tatraiva tadbhāryā śuddhataṇḍulakhaṇḍanam |
tadaṃśabhṛtimūlyāptaṃ bhaktyā bhartre nyavedayat || k41.79 ||
samudyatasya tasyātha bhikṣubhojanasiddhaye |
śuddhaye śuddhasattvasya śakro'bhūdanusādhakah || k41.80 ||
divyavarṇarasāmode bhojye śakreṇa sādhite |
prītyā pracchannarūpeṇa bhikṣuṃ lekhe na durgataḥ || k41.81 ||
vibhūtimohitairgūḍhaiḥ pūrvaṃ puranivāsibhiḥ |
saṃghe nimantrite duḥkhātdurgato martumudyayau || k41.82 ||
kṛpayā tasya bhagavān svayamabhyetya kāśyapaḥ |
śuddhisiddhiṃ parijñāya cakre bhojyapratigraham || k41.83 ||
aho'haṃ bhavato bhojyaṃ prayacchāmīti bhūbhujā |
prayatnātprārtito'pyarthaṃ naivāmanyata durgataḥ || k41.84 ||
guṇadraviṇasaṃpūrṇaḥ syāṃ daridraprasādanaḥ |
bhagavantamathābhyarcya praṇīdhānaṃ cakāra saḥ || k41.85 ||
svāśramaṃ kāśyape yāte surendre ca divaṃ gate |
durgatasya gṛhaṃ sarvaṃ divyaratnaipūrayat || k41.86 ||
viśvakarmā tatastasya vidadhe śakraśāsanāt |
bhavanaṃ rucirodyānaṃ ratnastambhavibhūṣitam || k41.87 ||
saṃprāptavimalaiśvaryaḥ sahitaṃ sarvabhikṣubhiḥ |
saptāhaṃ vibhavairbhogaiḥ sa kāśyapamapūjayat || k41.88 ||
kṣutkṣāmāṅganamarthibhiḥ parihṛtadvāraṃ rudaddārakaṃ
gehaṃ niścalakajjalānyapi sthalīkoṇasvananmakṣikam (?) |
cullīsuptabiḍālabālamaparaṃ yasyābhavadrauravaṃ
śrīstasyaiva nṛpaspṛhāspadatayāścaryaṃ na kasya svayam || k41.89 ||
tena dānaprabhāveṇa sudhāśuddhena durgataḥ |
janmāntare paṇḍitatāmavāpyārhattvamāgataḥ || k41.90 ||
iti paṇḍitapūrvajanmavṛttaṃ kathitaṃ sarvavidā guṇādareṇa |
avadhārya viśuddhadānapuṇyaṃ kuśalārhaṃ praśaśaṃsa bhikṣusaṃghaḥ || k41.91 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ kapilāvadānaṃ nāmaikacatvāriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||

42. kanakavarṇāvadānam |

sattvena sūryarucayastamasi sphuranti dharmeṇa ratnanicayā nabhasaḥ tapanti |
dhairyeṇa sarvavipadaḥ praśamaṃ vrajanti dānena bhogasubhagāḥ kakubho bhavanti || k42.1 ||
bhagavān sugataḥ pūrvaṃ śrāvastyāṃ jetakānane |
kuśalānāṃ prapānnānāṃ vidadhe dharmadeśanām || k42.2 ||
pūrvakalpāntarajane vatsarāṣṭāyutāyuṣi |
śrīmān kanakavarṇākhyo babhūva pṛthivīpatiḥ || k42.3 ||
kanakākhyā purī tasya śakrasyevāmarāvatī |
sarvalokeśvarasyāpi vasatirvallabhābhavat || k42.4 ||
nāyakārhaṃ yaśaḥśubhraṃ cāruvṛttaguṇocitam |
hṝdaye yaḥ prajākāryaṃ muktāhāramivāvahat || k42.5 ||
prajākarmavipākena pure paramadāruṇā |
avṛṣṭirabhavattatra sarvabhūtabhayapradā || k42.6 ||
sā dhairyahāriṇī sarvalokasaṃtāpakāriṇī |
avṛṣṭiḥ prayayau bhūbhṛnmānasāyāsahetutām || k42.7 ||
kuṇṭhasarvapratīkāraḥ sa cintāstimitaṃ puraḥ |
uvāca suciraṃ dhyātvā pradhānāmātyamaṇḍalam || k42.8 ||
avarṣopanipāto'yaṃ prajānāṃ niṣpratikriyaḥ |
karoti me yatnakṛtaṃ niṣphalaṃ paripālanam || k42.9 ||
nivṛttavarṣāḥ kakubho bhavantyabhrāśca svacchakāḥ |
pravṛttabāṣpavarṣāśca prajāḥ pāpena bhūbhujām || k42.10 ||
trāṇaṃ mahābhayādrājā prajānāṃ na karoti yaḥ |
tasya spaṣṭaṃ naṭasyeva kirīṭamukuṭagrahaḥ || k42.11 ||
tadā kṝtayugaṃ loke yadā rājā prajāhitaḥ || k42.12 ||
durbhikṣakṣayitāḥ pṛthutarakleśāvalīvihvalāḥ |
hāhākāraviśṛṅkhalāḥ khalatarairatyarditā vallabhaiḥ
śocantyaḥ pralayaṃ prayāntaśaraṇāḥ pāpairnṛpāṇāṃ prajāḥ || k42.13 ||
tasmātsamastakoṣeṇa rakṣaṇīyā mayā prajāḥ |
rājñāṃ prajāparitrāṇapuṇyaṃ ratnamayo nidhiḥ || k42.14 ||
ityuktvā sarvalokasya saṃcintya koṣṭhakoṣayoḥ |
sa nināya nijaṃ sarvaṃ sadā bhogyopabhogyatām || k42.15 ||
tataḥ kālena tasyogradurbhikṣeṇānnasaṃcayaḥ |
yayau mahāvyayādekapuruṣāśanaśeṣatām || k42.16 ||
tasminnavasare vyomnā samabhyetya raviprabhaḥ |
pratyekabuddhastasyātha vidadhe bhojanārthanām || k42.17 ||
niyame saṃśaye tasminnātmanaḥ prāṇadhāraṇe |
nirvikalpya sa tatasrvaṃ dadau tasmai prasannadhīḥ || k42.18 ||
svaprāṇavṛttiṃ tenāsau kṛtvātithyaprasādinā |
prayayau nabahsā tasya prasaṃśan sattvaśīaltām || k42.19 ||
athodyayau vyaomamahādvipasya nīlālimāleva sadambulekhā |
meghāvalī paścimadikpralambā kapolakālāgurumañjarīva || k42.20 ||
tataḥ samastaṃ gaganāntarālamutphullanīlotpalakānanābham |
ācchādyamānaṃ sarasairbabhāse bhṛṅgapravandhauriva meghasaṃghaiḥ || k42.21 ||
tataḥ papātākhilabhojyavṛṣṭiriṣṭā prajānāṃ bhuvi sapta rātrīḥ |
dhānyādivṛṣṭistadanantaraṃ ca ratnadivṛṣṭiśca tataḥ krameṇa || k42.22 ||
iti sa kanakavarṇaḥ kṣmāpatirbhūpatīnāṃ mukuṭamaṇirivoccairbhrājamānaḥ prajānām |
akṛta sukṛtasaṃpatprīṇitahprāṇarakṣāṃ prabhavati hi parārthe sajjanānāṃ prabhāvaḥ || k42.23 ||
bhūpatiḥ kanakavarṇa eṣa yaḥ so'hameva vapuṣātmanādhunā |
ityudīrya bhagavān jinaḥ satāṃ dhīmatāṃ vyadhita dharmadeśanām || k42.24 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ kanakavarṇāvadānaṃ nāma dvicatvāriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


43. hiraṇyapāṇyavadānam |

sarvopakārapraṇayī prabhāvaḥ sarvopajīvyā mahatī vibhūtiḥ |
puṇyāṅkurārhasya phalaṃ viśālapalārhametatprathamaṃ hi puṣpam || k43.1 ||
jine jetavanārāmavihārābhigate purā |
śrāvastyāṃ devasenākhyaḥ śrīmānāsīdgṛhādhipaḥ || k43.2 ||
hiraṇṃyapāṇistasyābhūtputraḥ satputramāninaḥ |
yasya hemamayaṃ jātaṃ pāṇidvitayamadbhutam || k43.3 ||
rūpyalakṣadvayaṃ tasya prātaḥ prātaḥ karadvaye |
prādurāsītsa tenābhūdarthināṃ kalpapādapaḥ || k43.4 ||
tasya vyaktavivekena paripākena bhūyasā |
kāle kuśalamūlānāṃ jine bhaktirajāyata || k43.5 ||
atha jetavana gatvā bahgavantaṃ tathāgatam |
sa dṛṣṭvā vidadhe tasya sānandaḥ pādavandanam || k43.6 ||
bhagavānapi saṃṣāratāpapraśamacandikām |
sudhāsakhīṃ dideśāsmai dṛśaṃ kuśalavṛtikām || k43.7 ||
sa śāsturdarśanenaiva saṃmohatimitojjhita |
babhāse suryakiraṇaprabuddhakamalopamaḥ || k43.8 ||
bhagavān vidadhe tasya tataḥ saddharmadeśanām |
