yayā dharmamayaṃ cakṣurakṣuṇṇālokamudyayau || k43.9 ||
prākpuṇyapariṇāmena jātavairāgyavāsanaḥ |
praṇamya vimalaprajñaḥ sa sarvajñamabhāṣataḥ || k43.10 ||
śaraṇya śaraṇāptasya bhagavan bhavahāriṇī |
aśeṣakleśanāśāya pravrajyā me vidhīyatām || k43.11 ||
capalaṃ prāṇināmāyuṣtato'pi navayauvanam |
vidyudvilāsacapalāstato'pyetā vibhūtayaḥ || k43.12 ||
iti tasya bruvāṇasya sugatānugrahoditā |
papāta vitarajasaḥ pravrajyā vapuṣi svayam || k43.13 ||
raktacīvarasuvyaktāṃ bibhrāṇaḥ sa viraktatām |
pātragraheṇa tatyāja punaḥ saṃsārapātratām || k43.14 ||
tasya tāmadbhutāṃ siddhiṃ pratyakṣaṃ vīkṣyaṃ bhikṣavaḥ |
tatpūrvavṛttaṃ papracchurbhagavantaṃ sa cābravīt || k43.15 ||
vārāṇasyāṃ purā rājā kṛkirnāma tathāgate |
kāśyapākhye bhagavati prayāti parinirvṛtim || k43.16 ||
śarīramasya samkṛtya stūpaṃ ratnamayaṃ vyadhāt |
svargāvagāhanaprauḍhaṃ mūrtaṃ puṇyamivonnatam || k43.17 ||
tasminnāropyamāṇāyāṃ yaṣṭayāṃ pūjāparigrahe |
kitavaḥ kandalo nāma nidadhe rūpakadvayam || k43.18 ||
cittaprasādaśuddhena tena puṇyena bhūyasā |
hiraṇyapāṇiḥ prāpto'dya mahatāṃ spṝhaṇīyatām || k43.19 ||
bhavati vibhavastyāgodāraḥ samagraguṇo bhuvi
prasaratiḥ yaśaḥ śuklaṃ loke sudhāṃśusahodaram |
pariṇatipade puṇyaṃ dhatte yadalpamanalpatāṃ
vimalamanasaḥ śraddhāśuddhaṃ tadeva vijṛmbhitam || k43.20 ||
iti prabhāvaṃ kathitaṃ jinena puṇyānubhāvasya hiraṇyapāṇeḥ |
śrutvaiva harṣādaravismayānāṃ sa bhikṣusaṃghaḥ praṇayī babhūva || k43.21 ||

iti kṣemandraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ hiraṇyapāṇyavadānaṃ tricatvāriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


44. ajātaśatrupitṛdrohāvadānam |

durjanaduḥsahaviṣadharabhīṣaṇataratimirapatitānām |
ālambanajananaṃ bhavabhayaharaṇaṃ jinasmaraṇam || k44.1 ||
pure rājagṛhābhikhye bhagavān bhūbhṛtaḥ purā |
kaṭake gṛdhrakūṭasya vijahāra tathāgataḥ || k44.2 ||
tasminnavasare rājā bimbisāraḥ sutapriyaḥ |
ajātaśatruṇā tatra putreṇa krūrakāriṇā || k44.3 ||
suhṛdaḥ pāvakasyeva devadattasya saṃmatam |
ghorāndhabandhanāgāraṃ niḥsaṃcāraṃ praveśitaḥ || k44.4 ||
patnyā praveśitaṃ tasya bandhane gūḍhabhojanam |
jñātvā sa tatkṣayākāṅkṣī kṣutkṣāmasya nyavārayat || k44.5 ||
rūkṣaḥ kṛśo'timalinaḥ so'bhavatpṛthivīpatiḥ |
akālakālameghārtaḥ kṛṣṇapakṣa ivoḍupaḥ || k44.6 ||
saṃkīrṇavāsasaṃtāpātprāyaḥ peśalacetasām |
karotyāliṅganaṃ prauḍhā gāḍhapraṇayinī vipat || k44.7 ||
sa samuddhiśya śokārtaḥ sugatādhyuṣitāṃ diśam |
kṛtāñjalirnataśirāḥ kṣāmasvaramabhāṣata || k44.8 ||
nāmastubhyaṃ bhagavate mahārhāya mahārhate |
dīnoddharaṇasaṃnaddhasamyaksaṃbodhibodhicetase || k44.9 ||
namaste ghorasaṃsāramakarākarasetave |
jināya janatājanmakleśapraśamahetave || k44.10 ||
namo nityaprabuddhāya sarvasattvaikabandhave |
viśuddhadhānme buddhāya karuṇāmṛtasindhave || k44.11 ||
iti bhaktisudhāṃ śiktvā sugataśravaṇocitām |
puṇyapuṣpaprasavinīṃ sa cakre stutimañjarīm || k44.12 ||
sarvajñastasya vijñāya kāyakleśaśamayīṃ daśām |
bandhanāgāravivarālokairāpyāyanaṃ vyadhāt || k44.13 ||
ajātaśatrustadvṛttaṃ jñātvā śaṅkākulaḥ pituḥ |
nyavārayadbandhagṛhe susūkṣmavivarāṇyapi || k44.14 ||
tatastasya tadādeśātcakrurbandhanarakṣiṇaḥ |
kṣureṇa gāḍhabaddhasya pādayostadvikartanam || k44.15 ||
sa tīvravaiśasakleśavyathitahpārthivaḥ param |
namo buddhāya buddhāyetyārtasaṃkrandanaṃ vyadhāt || k44.16 ||
bhagavānatha sarvajñaḥ puraḥ pratyakṣatāṃ gataḥ |
śakradattāsanāsīnaḥ kāruṇyāttamabhāṣataḥ || k44.17 ||
rājan kiṃ kriyate krūrakarmaṇāṃ gatirīdṛśī |
śubhāśubhasamudbhūtaṃ na bhuktaṃ kṣiyate phalam || k44.18 ||
rāgadveṣaviṣāsakte nānāvyasanaduḥsahe |
evaṃvidhaiva niḥsāre saṃsāre duḥkhasāratā || k44.19 ||
saṃkleśakalile kāle vipatsaṃpadvisaṃkaṭe |
dhairyameva paritrāṇaṃ vairāgyaṃ ca nirākulam || k44.20 ||
saṃsāraghoragahanāntaravardhamānaiḥ duḥkhānalavyatikaraprasṛtairasiktāḥ |
dhūmodgamairiva punaḥ sukṛtocitānāṃ bāṣpāmbubindukalilā na dṛśo bhavanti || k44.21 ||
bhajasva dhairyaṃ duḥkhe'smin bhogāśāṃ tyaja bhūpate |
pariṇāmavirodhinyaḥ sarvāḥ saṃsāravṛttayaḥ || k44.22 ||
adhunaiva tavāsannā dehātte kuśalasthitiḥ |
ityuktvā taṃ samāśvasya bhagavān svapadaṃ yayau || k44.23 ||
bimbisāro'pi dehānte tasminneva kṣaṇe divi |
abhūjjinarṣabho nāma śrīmān vaiśravaṇātmajaḥ || k44.24 ||
ajātaśatrurjanakaṃ jñātvā vigatajīvitam |
śarīramasya satkṛtya nininda nijaduṣkṛtam || k44.25 ||
tasyātitīvrapāpārtaṃ cittaṃ durvṛttadūṣitam |
paścāttāpāgnipatanaṃ prāyaścittamivākarot || k44.26 ||
so'vadadbata saṃmohādaiśvaryamadalubdhadhīḥ |
durvṛttapātakaśvabhre patito'hamadhomukhaḥ || k44.27 ||
śrutaprajñādaridrāṇāṃ nijanidrāsukhāpahā |
cintā dahati gātrāṇi khalamantrānuvartinām || k44.28 ||
patitasyāvasannasya pāpapaṅke pramādinah |
anālambasya saṃtrāṇaṃ jinasaṃsmaraṇaṃ mama || k44.29 ||
iti saṃcintya suciraṃ sa gatvā sugatāntikam |
jugupsamānaḥ kukṛtātparaṃ saṃkocamāyayau || k44.30 ||
tatrāpavitramātmānaṃ manyamānaḥ sapatrapaḥ |
praṇanāma jinaṃ dūrātpāpasparśabhayādiva || k44.31 ||
sāśrunetraḥ paritrāṇaṃ sa sarvajñaṃ vyajijñapat |
sakampaḥ kāyasaṃsaktaṃ vidhunvanniva duṣkṛtam || k44.32 ||
bhagavan kṛtapāyo'hamāsannanarakānalaḥ |
uttaptaḥ karuṇāsindhuṃ tvāmeva śaraṇaṃ gataḥ || k44.33 ||
māmiyaṃ śoṇaparyantā dṛṣṭiste puṣkaraprabhā || k44.34 ||
khalamantrapravṛttena durvṛttena pramādinā |
mayā vibhavalubdhena pāpena nihataḥ pitā || k44.35 ||
iti pralāpinastasya vacaḥ śrutvā tathāgataḥ |
sasarha tatpāparajaḥśuddhyai puṇyasarasvatīm || k44.36 ||
rājanna cintitaḥ pāpaḥ khaleneva svakarmaṇā |
preritastvaṃ pitṛvadhe patitaḥ pāpasaṃkaṭe || k44.37 ||
duḥkhaṃ tattena bhoktavyaṃ prāptavyaṃ kilbiṣaṃ tvayā |
tava tasya ca bhūpāla tulyaiṣā bhavitavyatā || k44.38 ||
nijakaṇṭhasamutkīrṇāṃ lalāṭapaṭavartinī |
śilāśakalalekheva niścalā niyatirnṛṇām || k44.39 ||
kurvatā kaluṣaṃ karma khalapreraṇayā tvayā |
pratyāsannāmṛtaśreyaḥ svahastena tiraskṛtam || k44.40 ||
adyāpi yadi te pāpaṃ hantuṃ prāptuṃ ca saṃpadam |
vāñchāsti tatkuru matiṃ puṇye pāpaśamātmani || k44.41 ||
dīpavṛttyā sukhaṃ sūte jīvayatyujjvalaṃ yaśah |
amṛtasya prakāro'yaṃ suvṛttaḥ satsamāgamaḥ || k44.42 ||
paścāttāpāgnupātena sādhunā saṃgamena ca |
saṃkīrtanena dānena pāpaṃ naśyati dehinām || k44.43 ||
pātraṃ pavitrayati naiva guṇān kṣiṇoti snehaṃ na saṃharati naiva malaṃ prasūte |
doṣāvasānaruciraścalatāṃ na dhatte satsaṃgamaḥ sukṛtasadmani ko'pi dīpaḥ || k44.44 ||
guṇigaṇavipaddīkṣādakṣaḥ kṣapākṣaṇasaṃnibhaḥ
sakalanayanavyāpārāṇāṃ janeṣu nirodhakaḥ |
asamaviṣamāyāsāvāsaḥ prakāśaparikṣayāt
sṛjati hi mahāmohāgdāḍhaṃ tamaḥ khalasaṃgamaḥ || k44.45 ||
pratyekabuddhastvaṃ rājan kālena kṣīṇakilbiṣaḥ |
bhaviṣyasi vivekena kṛtālekaḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ || k44.46 ||
iti tasya dayāśvāsaṃ cakāra bahgavān jinah |
patiteṣvadhikaṃ santaḥ karuṇāsnigdhalocanāḥ || k44.47 ||
tataḥ praṇamya sugataṃ prayātaḥ svapadaṃ nṛpaḥ |
mahataḥ pāpabhārasya viveda laghutāmiva || k44.48 ||
tasmin prayāte sarvajñaḥ pṛṣṭastatkarma kautukāt |
bhikṣubhiḥ kṣitipālasya pūrvavṛttamabhāṣata || k44.49 ||
vārāṇasyāṃ nirāyāsavilāsavyavasāyinaḥ |
catvāraḥ śreṣṭhitanayā babhūvuḥ śrīviśṛṅkhalāḥ || k44.50 ||
te kadācitsukhakṣībā mithaḥ kalikathāsthitāḥ |
pratyekabuddhamāyāntaṃ dadṛśuryauvanoddhatāḥ || k44.51 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā jātavidveṣāḥ śamasaṃyamanindakāḥ |
jyeṣṭhaḥ sundarako nāma bhrātṝn provāca sasmitaḥ || k44.52 ||
ayaṃ cīvarapātrāṅkaḥ pānena gatajīvitaḥ |
kṣibo vidhīyate bhikṣurityayaṃ me manorathaḥ || k44.53 ||
ityukte cāpalāttena dvitīyaḥ kundarābhidhaḥ |
uvāca bhikṣuṃ kṣiptvemaṃ hantumicchāmyahaṃ jale || k44.54 ||
tatastṛtīyo'pyavadatpāpaḥ sundarakābhidhaḥ (?) |
eṣa bhikṣurvaraṃ tasyāṃ vīthyāṃ nikṣipyate javāt || k44.55 ||
caturtho'pyavadatkrūramatiḥ kandarakābhidhaḥ |
bhikṣoḥ kṣureṇa kriyate niścarma caraṇadvayam || k44.56 ||
iti teṣāṃ bruvāṇānāṃ kaluṣo'bhūnmanorathaḥ |
yena janmāntare prāpuste svecchāsadṛśaṃ phalam || k44.57 ||
dhanaṃ paśyati lobhāndhaḥ kridhāndhaḥ śatrumeva ca |
kāmāndhahkāminīmeva darpāndhastu na kiṃcana || k44.58 ||
dhanidbhūtavikārāṇāṃ prayātyaniyatātmanām |
madamandavicārāṇāmānandaḥ kleśabandhatām || k44.59 ||
krudhyantyakāraṇamakāraṇamutpatanti snihyantyakāraṇamakāraṇamāmananti |
mohāhatāḥ khalu hitāhitanirvicārāḥ tṛptāḥ paraṃ nṛpaśavaḥ samadā bhavanti || k44.60 ||
jyeṣṭhaḥ śreṣṭhisutaḥ pāpātsa evāparajanmani |
śāriryānābhidhaḥ śākyaḥ pītvā madyaṃ vyapadyata || k44.61 ||
dvitīyo'pi mahānnāma śākyastoye kṣayaṃ gataḥ |
tṛtīyaśca svaputreṇa vyasto rājā prasenajit || k44.62 ||
bimbisāraścaturtho'sau dhṛtaḥ putreṇa bandhane |
prayuktaṃ dhanavatkarma bhujyate hi savṛddhikam || k44.63 ||
mohāhatairiha hi sadbhirasadbhireṣāṃ niḥśarma karma sahasaiva viḍambyate yat |
bāṣpāmbupūrṇanayanairanayopanītaḥmastokaśokavivaśairanubhūyate'tra || k44.64 || k44.||
sugatakathitametatpūrvajanmapravṛttaṃ viṣaviṣamavipākaṃ bimbisārasya vṛttam |
vibudhasadasi bhikṣuḥ spaṣṭamākarṇya mene vyasanaśatanimittaṃ dūṣitaṃ cittameva || k44.65 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāmajātaśatrupitṛdrodāvadānaṃ nāma catuścatvāriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


45. kṛtajñāvadānam |

andhīkṛto'pi svadṛśā tamasā khalena lakṣmīvihāravirahe vinipātito'pi |
kaṣṭāṃ daśāmiva niśāmativāhya padmaḥ svāmeva saṃpadamupaiti punarguṇāḍhyaḥ || k45.1 ||
śrāvastyāṃ sugate jetavanodyānavihāriṇi |
devadattaḥ paridveṣavyādhivyāpto vyacintayat || k45.2 ||
tulyaḥ samāno me bhrātā manuṣyaḥ śākyavaṃśajaḥ |
prāptaḥ puṇyaprabhāveṇa trijagatpūjyatāṃ jinaḥ || k45.3 ||
jīvitodvṛttaye tasmāttaysya yatnaṃ karomyaham |
na hyanastaṃgate bhānau paratejaḥ prakāśate || k45.4 ||
vijñānenānubhāvena vidyayā tapasā śriyā |
paraprakarṣaṃ sahate na hi mānonnataṃ manah || k45.5 ||
viṣaṃ nijanakhāgreṣu dhṛtvā tasya praṇāmakṛt |
saṃcārayāmi vapuṣi nediṣṭhaḥ pādapīḍaṇaiḥ || k45.6 ||
iti saṃcintya kaluṣaṃ vidveṣavivaśaḥ khalah |
sa tiṣyapramukhānetya bāndhavānidamabhyadhāt || k45.7 ||
krūraḥ kṛtāpakāro'haṃ sugatasyādya pādayoḥ |
saralasya prasādāya ptāmi gurupātakah || k45.8 ||
iti bruvāṇastaiḥ sarvaiḥ sudattānumataiḥ saha |
jinaṃ jetavanāsīnaṃ draṣṭuṃ duṣṭamatiryayau || k45.9 ||
bhagavantaṃ vilokyābhūttatra yāvatsa sarvaśaḥ |
tāvaddagdho'hamityuccairutkṣiptacaraṇo'vadat || k45.10 ||
huiṃsāsaṃkalpapāpena vrajreṇeva samāhataḥ |
saśarīraṃ kṣaṇe tasminnarakāgnau papāta saḥ || k45.11 ||
sarvajñaḥ sahasā dṛṣṭvā taṃ ghoranarake cyutam |
uvāca śrutatadvṛttavismitāṃ bhikṣusaṃsadam || k45.12 ||
eṣa kilbiṣadoṣeṇa patitaḥ kleśasaṃkaṭe |
tīvraṃ hi timira sūte sarvathā malinaṃ manaḥ || k45.13 ||
nagaryāmatighoṣāyāṃ ratisomasya bhūpateḥ |
kṛtajñaścākṛtajñaśca purā putrau babhūvatuḥ || k45.14 ||
kṛtajñaḥ kṛpayārthibhyaḥ kalpavṛkṣaḥ ivāniśam |
nijaṃ vimucya pradadau ratnābharaṇasaṃcayam || k45.15 ||
avibhaktaṃ piturdravyaṃ sarvaṃ sādhāraṇaṃ tayoḥ |
vadannityakṛtajñpo'pi tenadattaḥ jahattat || k45.16 ||
tataḥ ślādhyāya vacasā matighoṣābhodho nṛpaḥ |
janakalyānikāṃ nāma kṛtajñāya sutāṃ dadau || k45.17 ||
svayamevārjitaṃ vittaṃ dātuṃ jātamanorathaḥ |
ārurohaṃ pravahaṇaṃ kṛtajño'tha mahodadhau || k45.18 ||
ratnārjanodyataṃ yāntaṃ taṃ dveṣaspardhitādaraḥ |
tamevānuyayau lobhādakṛtajño'pi durjanaḥ || k45.19 ||
saṃpūrṇaṃ vaṇijāṃ sārthaiḥ tataḥ pravahaṇaṃ śanaiḥ |
ānukūlyena matutāmavāpa dvīpamīpsitam || k45.20 ||
tasmin pratinivṛtte'tha svadeśaṃ gantumudyate |
ratnarāśibhirāpūrṇasaṃklape svadeśaṃ gantumudyate || k45.21 ||
kṛtajñaḥ pṛthivīmūlyaṃ ratnānāṃ śatapaḥñcakam |
ādāya granthipaṭṭena babandhāṃśukapallave || k45.22 ||
ratnabhārapariśrāntaṃ tataḥ pravahaṇaṃ mahat |
abhajyata mahāvātairaiśvaryamiva durnayaiḥ || k45.23 ||
tataḥ phalakavāhastaṃ kṛtajñaḥ prāptajīvitaḥ |
akṛtajñaṃ nimajjantaṃ pṛṣṭhena samatārayat || k45.24 ||
tāritaḥ kṛpayā bhrātrā sa ghoramakarākarāt |
apaśyadañcale tasya ruciraṃ ratnasaṃcayam || k45.25 ||
sa tasya ratnalobhena dveṣena ca vaśīkṛtaḥ |
samudratīre śrāntasya bhrāturdrohamacintayat || k45.26 ||
tasya nidrānilīnasya śastreṇotpāṭya locanam |
gṛhītvā ratnanicayaṃ kṛtaghnaḥ sa yayau javāt || k45.27 ||
krūreṇāṅgīkṛtastena rāhuṇeva divākara |
lokopakāravihato duḥkhitaḥ so'pyacintayat || k45.28 ||
adhunārthipradāne'rthe vyarthībhūte manorathe |
kiṃ mamāndhasya vandhyena jīvitena prayojanam || k45.29 ||
aprāptaviṣayāḥ prāṇā na prayānti yadi kṣayam |
tadasaṃgatayo yogāḥ kleśaynti kṣyakṣamāḥ || k45.30 ||
kṣaṇe dhane jane dveṣamānavaikalyavihvale |
pūjye puṃsāṃ samenaiva śeṣasya ca yaśovyayaḥ || k45.31 ||
iti saṃcintya sa śanairvrajansārthena tāritaḥ |
avāpa nagaropāntaṃ matighoṣasya bhūpateḥ || k45.32 ||
gopālabhavane tatra sa kaṃcitkālamāsthitaḥ |
udyānayātrāgatayā rājaputryā vilokitah || k45.33 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvāndhamapi vyaktarājalakśaṇalakṣitam |
prāgjanmapremabandhena sābhilāṣā babhūva sā || k45.34 ||
tataḥ svayaṃvaravidhiṃ sā kṛtvā śāsanātpituḥ |
rājñāṃ madhye ca mānyānāṃ vavre vigatalocanam || k45.35 ||
bhūmipālān parityajya vṛto'ndhaḥ pāpayā tvayā |
uktveti pitrā kopena nirastā śuśubhe na sā || k45.36 ||
udyāne sā nidhāyāndhaṃ yatnenāhṛtya bhojanam |
sadā tasmai dadau premapraṇayopacitādarā || k45.37 ||
kadācittāṃ cirāyātāmāhārāvasare gate |
uvāca rājatanayaḥ paraṃ mlānānanaḥ kṣudhā || k45.38 ||
asamīkṣitakāriṇā tvayā kevalacāpalāt |
vṛto'hamandhaḥ saṃtyajya nṛpān vipulalocanān || k45.39 ||
paścāttāpena nūnaṃ tvaṃ mayi paryuṣitādarā |
adhunā tāṇḍavaṃ premṇahpradarśayitumudyatā || k45.40 ||
andhasaṃdarśanodvignā surūpālokanonmukhī |
āhārakāle'tikrānte cireṇeha tvamāgatā || k45.41 ||
ityuktvā paruṣaṃ tena kampamānā lateva sā |
uvāca guñjanmadhupaśreṇīmadhuravādinī || k45.42 ||
nātha mithyaiva me śankāṃ na kopātkartumarhasi |
vāgbāṇapātaṃ sahate na cetaḥ prītipeśalam || k45.43 ||
tvāmeca devatāṃ jāne yadyahaṃ śuddhamānasā |
tena satyena sālokamekaṃ nayanamastu te || k45.44 ||
ityukte sattvaśālinyā tayā tasyāśu locanam |
utphullakamalākāramekaṃ vimalatāṃ yayau || k45.45 ||
tasyāhsatyaprabhāveṇa saṃjātapṛthuvismayah |
satyapratyayasotsāhaṃ kṛtajñastāmabhāṣata || k45.46 ||
bhrātrā tenākṛtajñna pāṭite locanadvaye |
tasmin vikāro vairaṃ vā na nikāro'pyabhūnmama || k45.47 ||
svacchaṃ tenāstu satyena dvitīyamapi locanam |
ityukte takṣaṇenāsya spaṣṭaṃ cakṣuralakṣyata || k45.48 ||
ataḥ kathitavṛttāntaṃ kṛtajñamucitaṃ patim |
prahṛṣṭā janakalyāṇī gatvā pitre nyavedayat || k45.49 ||
pūjitaḥ śvaśureṇātha sa ratnagajavājibhiḥ |
śriyeva kāntayā sārdhaṃ jagāma nagaraṃ pituḥ || k45.50 ||
sa tatra pitrā hṛṣṭena caraṇālīnaśekharaḥ |
janānurāgasubhage yaivarājye pade dhṛtaḥ || k45.51 ||
akṛtajño'pi nirlajjastaṃ prasādayituṃ śaṭhaḥ |
vicintya pādapatane tasya drohaṃ samāyayau || k45.52 ||
unmanā hantumāyātaḥ sa taṃ kuṭulaceṣṭitaḥ |
hāhā dagdho'smi dagdho'smītyuktvaiva narake'patat || k45.53 ||
sa eva devadatto'sau kṛtajño'pyahameva ca |
janmāntarānubandhena dveṣo'sya na nivartate || k45.54 ||
sarvajñabhāṣitamiti pracuropakāraṃ taddevadattacaritaṃ paritāpakāri |
janmāntaropacitapātakasaṃnibaddhaṃ śrutvā babhūva vimanā iva bhikṣusaṃghaḥ || k45.55 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ kṛtajñāvadānaṃ pañcacatvāriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


46. śālistambāvadānam |

dānaikatānamanasāṃ pṛthusattvabhājāmutsāhamānaguṇabhogavibhūṭipūtaḥ |
prākuṇyasaṃcayamayaḥ kuśalabhidhānaḥ kāle phalatyavikalaḥ kila kalpavṛkṣaḥ || k46.1 ||
kosalendrasya bhūbhartuḥ śrāvastyāṃ bhagavān purā |
vijahāra va rodyāne saha bhikṣagaṇairjinaḥ || k46.2 ||
ādimadhyāṇtakalyāṇaṃ bahvābhibhavasādhakam |
saṃdideśa sa saddharmaṃ trailokyakuśalodyataḥ || k46.3 ||
atrāṇtare nāgarājaputrāḥ sāgaravāsinah |
catvāraḥ sugatodīrṇaṃ saddharmaṃ paramāmṛtam || k46.4 ||
abhiratyākhyayā svasrā preritāḥ śrotumāgatāḥ |
te balātibala-śvāsa-mahāśvāsābhidhāḥ samam || k46.5 ||
krakucchandasya sudhiyaḥ kāle bhagavataḥ pura |
kanakākhyasya ca muneḥ kāśyapasya ca yatnataḥ || k46.6 ||
ājagmuḥ śrīsukhāsaktāḥ śrotumaprārthitā api |
tatpuṇyapariṇāmena prāptāḥ śākyamuneḥ purā || k46.7 ||
teṣu praṇamya śāstāraṃ caraṇālīnamauliṣu |
vidhāya mānuṣaṃ rūpamupaviṣṭeṣu parṣadi || k46.8 ||
saddharmamāyayau śrotuṃ kosalendraḥ prasenajit |
lakṣmīmandasmitacchāyaṃ nivārya cchatracāmaram || k46.9 ||
śāstuḥ pādapraṇāmāya viśatastasya saṃsadi |
avakāśaṃ natāścakruḥ sarve nṛpatigauravāt || k46.10 ||
tasyābhinandyamānasya varṣāśramagurornṛbhiḥ |
nāgarājasutāścakrurnāvakāśaṃ na satkṛtam || k46.11 ||
tasyābhinandyamānasya varṇāśramagurornṛbhiḥ |
nāgarājasutāścakrurnāvakāśaṃ na satkṛtam || k46.12 ||
sa saṃjñayā samādiśya nijaṃ parijanaṃ puraḥ |
gamane nigrahaṃ teṣāṃ nirvikāra ivābhavat || k46.13 ||
bhagavānapi sarvajñastasya jñātvā ca niścayam |
dharmopadeśaparyante provāca racitasmitaḥ || k46.14 ||
na vidveṣarajaḥpūrṇamanomalinadarpaṇe |
bhāti dharmopadeśasya pratibimbapratigrahaḥ || k46.15 ||
avihitasamatānāṃ kopamohāhatānāṃ kṛśamapi kuśalāṃśaṃ nopadeśaḥ karoti |
na hi vahutaradoṣe śuddhihīne śarīre vrajati hatamatīnāṃ bheṣajaṃ bheṣajatvama || k46.16 ||
iti yuktaṃ bhagavatā hitamuktaṃ mahīpatiḥ |
śrutvāpi na ca tatyāja nāgeṣu vimanaskatām || k46.17 ||
bhagavantaṃ praṇamyātha prayāte svapadaṃ nṛpe |
nāgāstatsainikābaddhamārge vyomapathā yayuḥ || k46.18 ||
te vicintya svabhavane kṣmāsaṃkṣayakṛtakṣaṇāḥ |
ghoranirghātameghaughagrastalokāḥ samāyayuḥ || k46.19 ||
teṣāṃ vyavasitaṃ jñātvā sarvajñaḥ pakṣapātinām |
rakṣḥākṣamaṃ kṣitipatermaudgalyāyanamādiśat || k46.20 ||
atha nāgagaṇotsṛṣṭā vajravṛṣṭirmahīpatau |
bhūbhartustatprabhāveṇa prayayau puṣpavṛṣṭitām || k46.21 ||
śastrāstravṛṣṭirnibiḍakṣiptā tairatha duḥsahā |
maudgalyāyanasaṃkalpādyayau rājārhabhojyatām || k46.22 ||
tatprabhāvātprayāteṣu bhognotsāheṣu bhogiṣu |
gatvā vavande sugataṃ nṛpatirvītaviplavaḥ || k46.23 ||
sa maudgalyāyanasyārghyamucitaṃ bhogasaṃpadā |
bhaktisaṃskārasubhagaṃ vidadhe jianśāsanāt || k46.24 ||
tataḥ svargocitāṃ bhikṣurvibhūtiṃ vīkṣya bhūpateḥ |
papraccha kautukavaśātsarvajñaṃ caritāñjaliḥ || k46.25 ||
bhagavan kasya puṇyasya prabhāveṇa prasenajit |
sarvairvirājitaṃ bhogaiḥ prājyaṃ rājyamavāptavān || k46.26 ||
ikṣustambavadetasya śālistambaśca jāyate |
divyapānānnasaṃpattiḥ phalaṃ tatkasya karmaṇaḥ || k46.27 ||
iti pṛṣṭaḥ praṇayinā bhikṣuṇā bhagavān jinaḥ |
uvāca śrūyatāṃ rājñaḥ kāraṇaṃ bhogasaṃpadām || k46.28 ||
kosale'smin janapade khaṇḍākhyaguḍakarṣakaḥ |
dadau pratyekabuddhāya pūrvamikṣurasaudanam || k46.29 ||
bhuktenekṣurasānnena tena vātagadārditaḥ |
pratyekabuddhastatpuṇyaiḥ prasannaḥ susthatāṃ yayau || k46.30 ||
rājā prasenajitso'yaṃ puṇyavān guḍakarṣakah |
tena puṇyaprabhāveṇa bhogabhāgī virājate || k46.31 ||
upakāraḥ kṛtajñānāṃ nikāraḥ krūracetasām |
sukṛtāṃśaśca śādhūnāmaplo'pyāyātyanalpatām || k46.32 ||
sarvajñeneti kathite pūrvapuṇye mahīpateḥ |
babhūva sukṛtotkarṣe bhikṣurāścaryaniścalah || k46.33 ||
atha bhaktyā bhagavataḥ kṛtvā rājādhivāsanām |
upaninye svayaṃ tāṃ tāṃ surārhāṃ bhogasaṃpadam || k46.34 ||
paropacārai rucirairarcite kāñcanāsane |
sukhopaviṣṭaṃ provāca naranāthastathāgatam || k46.35 ||
bhagavanme bhavadbhaktivibhaktasukṛtaśriyaḥ |
cayahkuśalamūlānāmanirmuktyai bhaviṣyati || k46.36 ||
vinayātpārthiveneti pūrṇapuṇyābhimāninā |
pṛṣṭaḥ smitasitālokaṃ jagāda sugataḥ sṛjan || k46.37 ||
rājan saṃsāramārgo'yamanādinidhanodbhavaḥ |
helālaṅghyaḥ kathaṃ puṃsāmaprāpya kleśasaṃkṣayam || k46.38 ||
ciraparicitaiścakrāvartairasaktagatāgatiḥ
prakṛtigahanaḥ saṃsāro'yaṃ sukhena na laṅghyate |
asati hi vinā yogābhyāsaṃ kṣaye kila karmaṇāṃ
sphuṭaphalatatirdharmo'pyasminnibandhanatāṃ gataḥ || k46.39 ||
sarvato vinivṛttasya dānābhyāsena bhūyasā |
mamāpi dharmasaṃsāro babhūva bhūrijanmakṛt || k46.40 ||
dhaniko nāma dhanavān vārāṇasyāmabhūtpurā |
tāpāpahaḥ phalasphīṭaśchāyāvṛkṣa ivārthinām || k46.41 ||
durbhikṣakṣapite loke viṣamakleśavihvale |
bhojyaṃ pratyekabuddhānāṃ so'rthitaḥ pañcabhiḥ śataiḥ || k46.42 ||
sa teṣāṃ parabhogārhaṃ durbhikṣāvadhi bhojanam |
akalpayadanalpaśrīḥ koṣṭhāgārī gatasmayaḥ || k46.43 ||
śatapañcakasaṃghātairbhoktuṃ tasya gṛhaṃ tataḥ |
kramātpratyekabuddhānāṃ sahasradvayamāyayau || k46.44 ||
tasya tatpuṇyavāsena jāto labdhaphalaśriyā |
durbhikṣadānajanito ratnakośastadākṣayaḥ || k46.45 ||
sukhaṃ sanātanaṃ puṇyabhogyatvaṃ praṇidhānataḥ |
śāstustataḥ pareṇāyaṃ samyaksaṃbodhimāpitaḥ || k46.46 ||
puṇyena pāpena ca veṣṭiteyaṃ saṃsāriṇāṃ karmaphalapravṛttiḥ |
sitāsitā bandhanarajjureṣā tatsaṃkṣaye mokṣapathaṃ vadanti || k46.47 ||
iti kṣitīśaḥ kathitaṃ jinena mohavyapāyena niśamya mokṣam |
kleśakṣayārhaṃ śamameva matvā puṇyābhimānaṃ śithilīvakāra || k46.48 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ śālistambāvadānaṃ nāma ṣaṭcatvāriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


47. sarvārthasiddhāvadānam |

svārthapravṛttau vigataspṛhāṇāṃ paropakāre satatodyatānām |
kleśeṣvabhītā vyasanairanītā vighnairapīḍākarameti siddhiḥ || k47.1 ||
śrāvastyāṃ bhagavān pūrvaṃ jino jetavanasthitaḥ |
dharmākhyānaprasaṅgena bhikṣusaṃghamabhāṣata || k47.2 ||
āsīdakhilabhūpālamaulilālitaśāsanah |
siddhārtho nāma sukṛtī sārvabhaumo mahīpatiḥ || k47.3 ||
sāgarākhyasya nāgasya sūnurjaladhivāsinah |
sarvārthasiddhaḥ putratvaṃ prayayau tasya bhūpateḥ || k47.4 ||
sa bhādrakalpiko bodhisattvaḥ sattvojjvalaprabhaḥ |
jātamātraḥ kṣititalaṃ cakre pūrṇaṃ svaṛddhibhiḥ || k47.5 ||
tasya pravardhamānasya dharmasyeva samudyayau |
samastabhuvanavyāpi vibudhābhyarcitaṃ yaśaḥ || k47.6 ||
sa kadācidvarodyāne syandanena yuvā vrajan |
dadarśa vṛddhapuruṣaṃ devatānirmitaṃ puraḥ || k47.7 ||
taṃ vilokya jarājīrṇaṃ jātavairāgyavāsanah |
saṃsāramiva niḥsāraṃ sa śarīvamamanyata || k47.8 ||
udyānayātrāvirataḥ śanaiḥ pratinivṛtya saḥ |
dāridyravidrutacchāyānadrākṣītkṛpaṇān pathi || k47.9 ||
dṛṣṭvā tānasukhakleṣṭān karuṇākṛṣṭamānasaḥ |
acintayadaho duḥkhaṃ sahante bhuvi durgatāḥ || k47.10 ||
adānaprabhavaṃ duḥkhaṃ vadantīti visaṃgatāḥ |
pṛthivyāṃ ratnapūrṇāyāṃ parapiṇḍopajīvinaḥ || k47.11 ||
idamevāvisaṃvādi cihnaṃ kaluṣakarmaṇām |
dīnāṃ yadete yācante puruṣaṃ puruṣāḥ param || k47.12 ||
aho duṣkṛtameteṣāmavadhūtāḥ pade pade |
yadete mārgaṇodvignā bhikṣitvāpi bubhukṣitāḥ || k47.13 ||
iti saṃcintya suciraṃ viścakleśakṣayodyataḥ |
yadaridraṃ jagatkartuṃ ratnārthī jaladhiṃ yayau || k47.14 ||
kathacidiva saṃsaktaḥ sa pitrā dṛḍhaniścayaḥ |
smāruhya pravahaṇaṃ ratnadvīpamavāptavān || k47.15 ||
tatra pravahaṇārūḍhān vaṇijaḥ sahayāyinaḥ |
so'bravītkriyatāṃ kāmaṃ yuṣmābhirmaṇisaṃgraham || k47.16 ||
etaiḥ sāmānyaratnaistu mama nāsti prayojanam |
kośe mahānti bhāsvanti santi ratnottamāni naḥ || k47.17 ||
kiṃ tu cintāmaṇiprāptyai vipulo'yaṃ mamodyamaḥ |
tena vidrutadāridyrāṃ kartumicchāmi modenīm || k47.18 ||
śrutaṃ mayā nāgarājaḥ sāgarākhyo mahodadhau |
vasatyasti gṛhe tasya cintitārthaprado maṇiḥ || k47.19 ||
vilaṅghya viṣamaṃ mārgaṃ tamādātuṃ vrajāmyaham |
nāsti dhairyasahāyānāṃ durgamaṃ vyavasāyinām || k47.20 ||
na ca madvirahe kiṃcidvyasanaṃ vo bhaviṣyati |
satyameva parārtho'yaṃ yadi me sukṛtodyamaḥ || k47.21 ||
ityuktvā tān samāmantrya pratasthe sthiraniścayaḥ |
mahatīṃ dhṛtimālambya sattvavān pārthivātmajaḥ || k47.22 ||
gulphamātreṇa saptāhaṃ gatvā gaṅgamavartmanā |
jānudaghnena saptāhaṃ saptāhaṃ pauruṣeṇa ca || k47.23 ||
catvāri saptarātrāṇi tataḥ puṣkariṇījalaiḥ |
gatvā dṛṣṭiviṣān ghorān dadarśa phaṇinaḥ puraḥ || k47.24 ||
maitrīyuktena manasā kṛtvā tānatha nirviṣān |
krūrakopairvṛtaṃ yakṣairyakṣadvīpamavāpa saḥ || k47.25 ||
tatra maitreṇa manasā vītakrodhān vidhāya tān |
śuśrāva tairabhihitaṃ vipulotsāhavismitaiḥ || k47.26 ||
kumāra sphīṭasattvena tathā vīryeṇacāmunā |
nāgarājasya bhavanaṃ samāhitamavāpya tam || k47.27 ||
kālena samyaksaṃbuddhaḥ sarvajñastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi |
śrāvakāśca bhaviṣyāmo vayaṃ tvadanuyāyinaḥ || k47.28 ||
prasannairiti tairuktamabhinandya nṛpātmajaḥ |
rakṣevarāvṛtaṃ prāpa rākṣasadvīpamutkaṭam || k47.29 ||
tathaiva vigatakrūravikāraistaiḥ sa pūjitaḥ |
bhūjotkṣepeṇa nikṣiptaḥ kṣaṇānnāgendrasadmāni || k47.30 ||
sa tatra dīptavibhave divyotsāsasukhocitaḥ |
aśṛṇoddīrghaduḥkhārtisūcakaṃ rodanadhvanim || k47.31 ||
sa tamākarṇya sodvegaḥ prakṛtyaiva dayārdradhīḥ |
kimetaditi papraccha dṛṣṭvāgre nāgakanyakām || k47.32 ||
sā taṃ babhāṣe saṃsaktaśokoṣmapiśunairmuhuḥ |
mlānayantīṃ svaniśvāsaurbimbādharadalatviṣam || k47.33 ||
guṇāvānnāgarājasya putraḥ kamalalocanaḥ |
jyoṣṭhaḥ sarvārthasiddhākhyaḥ priyaḥ pañcatvamāgataḥ || k47.34 ||
tataḥ pratigatānande vinivṛttasukhotsave |
dhanena rodanenāsminna bhavedbhavane sthitiḥ || k47.35 ||
iti tasyā vacaḥ śrutvā so'ntaḥ paricitāṃ vahan |
svadeśadarśanaprāpto nāgarājāntikaṃ yayau || k47.36 ||
nāgarājastamāyāntaṃ parijñāya priyāsakhaḥ |
ehyehi putreti vadan babhūvānandavihvalaḥ || k47.37 ||
martyajanmakathāṃ tena svaṃ cāgamanakāraṇam |
śrutvā niveditaṃ nāgaḥ pariṣvajya jagāda tam || k47.38 ||
cintāmaṇirayaṃ putra mama maulivibhūṣaṇam |
gṛhyatāṃ tava saṃkalpaṃ na vandhyaṃ kartumutsahe || k47.39 ||
deyaḥ kṛtajagatkṛtyo mamaivāyaṃ punastvayā |
ityuktvāsmai dadau divyacūḍaṃ ratna vimucya saḥ || k47.40 ||
hṛṣṭaḥ praṇamya nāgendraṃ yayau pravahaṇāntikam || k47.41 ||
samudradevatā tatra taṃ dṛṣṭvā śrutatatkathā |
uvāca kīdṛśaḥ sādho prāptaścintāmaṇīstvayā || k47.42 ||
* * * * | * * * * || k47.43 ||
* * * * | * * * * || k47.44 ||
samudre patitaṃ dṛṣṭvā ratnaṃ kṛcchratarārjitam |
sa jagāda dṛḍhodyogavaiphalyodveganiścalaḥ || k47.45 ||
aho guṇocitākārā praṇayānmṛduvādinī |
vidveṣakaluṣaṃ karma kṛtvā tvaṃ na vilajjase || k47.46 ||
parotkarṣeṣu saṃgharṣaśokakleśamupaiti yaḥ |
śītalā api tasyaitā jvālāvalayitā diśaḥ || k47.47 ||
paritsāhaḥ priyo yasya tasya sattvamahodadheḥ |
karpūradhavalaṃ dhatte trilokītilakaṃ yaśaḥ || k47.48 ||
devi prayaccha me ratnamasmādvirama pātakāt |
apavādalatāṃ karma na sādhoradhirohhati || k47.49 ||
lobhātpramādāddveṣādvā ratnaṃ cenna prayacchasi |
śoṣayāmyeṣa jaladhiṃ tadimaṃ te samāśrayam || k47.50 ||
ityuktvā pyasakṛttena sā ratne na dadau yadā |
sa tadā svaprabhāveṇa śoṣāyābdheḥ samudyayau || k47.51 ||
dhyātamātraṃ sahasrākṣavacasā viśvakarmaṇā |
nirmitaṃ sahasā tasya patramāvirabhutkare || k47.52 ||
sa tenāgastyaculukākāreṇāmbhaḥ payonidheḥ |
antarīkṣe samutkṣipya cikṣepa kṣamaṇodyataḥ || k47.53 ||
kṛte bhūbhāgaśeṣe'bdhau tenātyadbhutakāriṇā |
suranirbhartsitā bhītā devatāsmai maṇīṃ dadau || k47.54 ||
nirvyājaṃ sāhasaṃ dīptiṃ ratnānāmiva tattvataḥ |
prabhāvaṃ vetti mahatāṃ mantrāṇāṃ tapasāṃ ca kaḥ || k47.55 ||
sphārastāvadapāravārivirasavyāpārahelābalāt
kallolāvaliyantritāmbaratayā ratnākaraḥ śrūyate |
gambhīraḥ punaraprameyamahimā ko'pi prabhāvaḥ satāṃ
yasmin vismayadhāmni cintanavidhāvante plavante dhiyaḥ || k47.56 ||
tataścintāmaṇiṃ buddhvā nijasārthena saṃgataḥ |
rājasūnuḥ svanagaraṃ prāpa pūrṇamanorathaḥ || k47.57 ||
kṛtakṛtyaḥ prahṛṣṭenaḥ sa pitrā tatra pūjitaḥ |
dhvajāgre ratnamādhāya jagāda janasaṃsadi || k47.58 ||
parārtha eva yatno'yaṃ nātmārtho yadi me kkacit |
tena satyena loko'ya sarvaṃ yātvadaridratām || k47.59 ||
ityukte sattvanidhinā tena dīnadayālunā |
ratnavṛṣṭiraparyantā nipapāta mahītale || k47.60 ||
tena ratnasamūhena dikṣu sarvāsu bhāsvatā |
yayau janasya dāridyramayaṃ niḥśeṣatāṃ tamaḥ || k47.61 ||
āśāpāśavatāṃ balāptraviśatāṃ bāhyāṅgaṇaṃ śrīmatāṃ
dvāḥsthāghātavatāṃ muhurvicalatāṃ dvārodare sīdatām |
dīrghairniḥśvasitaiḥ śucā nipatitāṃ dehakṣayaṃ kāṅkṣatāṃ
dīnānāṃ maṇirāśiraśmiśabalaḥ śrīsaṃgamaḥ ko'pyabhūt || k47.62 ||
tacchāsanāduraganāyakameva yāte cintāmaṇau vigatadainyajane ca loke |
sarvatra dānarasikasya janasya cetaḥ sarvārthisārthaviratākulitaṃ babhūva || k47.63 ||
sarvārthasiddhaḥ kṣitipālasūnuḥ yo'bhūtsa evāhamihānyadehaḥ |
śrutvetivṛttaṃ kathitaṃ jinena te bhikṣavastanmayatāmavāpuḥ || k47.64 ||

iti kṣemendraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ sarvārthasiddhāvadānaṃ satpacatvāriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||


48. hastakāvadānam |

mattebhakumbhoccakucābhirāmāḥ karpūrahārāṃśuvilāsahāsāḥ |
prītipradāḥ puṇyavatāṃ bhavanti prauḍhā yuvatyaśca vibhūtayaśca || k48.1 ||
tathāgate bhagavati śrāvastyāṃ vanacāriṇi |
abhavatsuprabuddhākhyaḥ śrīmān gṛhapatiḥ purā || k48.2 ||
babhūva hastako nāma tasyātidayitaḥ sutaḥ |
pūrvārjitānāṃ puṇyānāṃ sākāra iva saṃcayaḥ || k48.3 ||
tasya janmadine jātaścāmīkaramayo mahān |
ekībhūta ivāścaryavrajaḥ pravarakuñcaraḥ || k48.4 ||
sa gajendraḥ kumāraśca tatpituśca manorathaḥ |
lokakautukakośāśca pūrṇatāṃ tyulyamāyayuḥ || k48.5 ||
sa śaśīva śiśuḥ kāle kalānilayatāṃ gataḥ |
ruruce rucimān sarvalokalocanabāndhavaḥ || k48.6 ||
sa śanaiḥ pūritābhogabhujastambhavibhūṣaṇam |
lebhe manobhavārambhabhavanaṃ navayauvanam || k48.7 ||
kadācidatha bhūbhartuḥ sa prasenajitaḥ sutām |
tanucīvaracihnena sahajena virājitām || k48.8 ||
kanyāṃ cīvarakanyākhyāṃ lāvaṇyalalitānanām |
udyānadarśanāyātāṃ dadarśāyatalocanām || k48.9 ||
akliṣṭarūpāmālokya tāmaparyuṣitadyutim |
vismayasmarayostulyamāyayau sahasā vaśam || k48.10 ||
so'cintayadaho kāntamidamatyadbhutaṃ vapuḥ |
yasmin bhāti mukhavyājādadoṣaviśadaḥ śaśī || k48.11 ||
lāvaṇyamapratimameva bibhati tanvī bandhūkabandhuradharo madhurasvabhāvaḥ |
drohodyataḥ sarasavidrumapallavānāṃ bimbaprabhāprasabhabhramavandhyakāraḥ || k48.12 ||
vakrkraṃ na kṣamate madaṃ śaśamṛtaḥ kleśnāti kāntiḥ sudhām
utphullotpalakānanasya kurute dṛṣṭiḥ prabhābhrtsanam |
manye manmathasaṃgamocitatanoḥ sāptnyabhītipradā
līlāyāsyāḥ sahasā vilāsalaharīśoṣaṃ vidhatte rateḥ || k48.13 ||
udvṛtte kaṭhine parodharayuge naśyadviveke ciraṃ
yasyā doṣamaye'pyaho guṇavatā hāreṇa baddhā sthitiḥ |
yaccāsminnavalambate'mbujadhiyā rolambarekhā mukhe
lolākṣyāḥ kimapi praśāntanayane līnaṃ munīnāṃ manaḥ || k48.14 ||
iti cintayatastasya vapuḥ puṣpaśaropamam |
vilokya bhūpatisutā babhūvāścaryaniścalā || k48.15 ||
hṛte lajjāṃśuke tasyāḥ smareṇa smitakāriṇā |
pratyagrapulakākīrṇaṃ vapuḥ spaṣṭamadṛśyata || k48.16 ||
ruddhā navābhilāṣeṇa vailakṣyeṇa nivartitā |
manastatraiva nikṣipya sā śūnyeva śanairyayau || k48.17 ||
rājadhānīṃ samāsādya lajjāvismayamanmathaiḥ |
mīliteva nilīneva proṣiteva babhūva sā || k48.18 ||
kumāro'pi svabhavane samudbhūtamanobhāvḥ |
tāmevendumukhīmagre saṃkalpairalikhanmuhuḥ || k48.19 ||
sa tāṃ mānasasarvasvaṃ smaravidyāmiva smaran |
pradadhyau kurlabhāṃ matvā tanayāṃ cakravartinaḥ || k48.20 ||
janmāntare tanuryasya tapaḥparicitā ciram |
dhanyaḥ sa tāmavāpnoti latāṃ sukṛtaśākhinaḥ || k48.21 ||
ramyapradānapuṇyena taddarśanamavāpyate |
na jāne tāni puṇyāni yeṣāṃ tatsaṃgamaḥ phalam || k48.22 ||
tadvakrkraśītakiraṇasmaraṇotsavena
tasyāśca durlabhatayā virahoṣmaṇā me |
no vedmi kiṃ dhṛtiriyaṃ kimayaṃ vimohaḥ
kiṃ jīvitaṃ kimasubhiḥ sahaḥ viprayogaḥ || k48.23 ||
tadvakrkrabjajitaḥ prasahra bhajate kṣaiṇyaṃ kṣapāvallabhaḥ
tadbhūvibhramalajjitaṃ ca vinatiṃ dhate dhanurmānmatham |
tasyāhpallavapeśaladyutimuṣā śoṣādhareṇārditaṃ
nūnaṃ prāpya parājayaṃ vanamahīṃ bimba samālambate || k48.24 ||
iti pūrṇenduvadanāvadanadhyānaniścalah |
niśāṃ nināya saṃtyaktaḥ serṣyayeva sanidrayā || k48.25 ||
kanyādarśanavṛttāntaṃ tatastena niveditam |
śrutvā pitāsya saṃkrantacintāparicito'bhavat || k48.26 ||
sa tamūce vayaṃ putra rājño'sya puravāsinah |
sa kathaṃ te duhitaraṃ cakravartī pradāsyati || k48.27 ||
aśakyaṃ naiva kurvanti samīhante na durlabham |
asaṃbhāvyaṃ na bhāṣante mānakāmā manīṣiṇaḥ || k48.28 ||
cūtacampakavallīṣu svādhīnāsu nirādaraḥ |
cintayan pārijātasya latāṃ śuṣyati ṣaṭpadaḥ || k48.29 ||
tava tasyāśca saṃbandhaḥ prājganmavihito yadi |
tadavaśyaṃ bhavatyeva niṣprayatnaphalodayah || k48.30 ||
āśāpāśairanākṛṣṭaṃ vicārairakadarthitam |
prayatnabhārairaśrāntaṃ vidhatte bhavitavyatā || k48.31 ||
ityākarṇya piturvākyaṃ tattatheti vicintayan |
na cetaḥ kanyakānītaṃ samānetuṃ śaśāka saḥ || k48.32 ||
sa gatvā dantayugalaṃ yayāce hemakuñcaram |
navasaṃdarśane rājñaḥ prītiyogyamupāyanam || k48.33 ||
puṇyabandhena kariṇā dattaṃ dantayugaṃ tataḥ |
sa hemamayamādāya draṣṭuṃ bhūmipatiṃ yayau || k48.34 ||
sa ratnaruciraṃ prāpya bhavanaṃ pṛthivīpateḥ |
praviśyaḥ praṇataḥ prītyai hemadantadvayaṃ dadau || k48.35 ||
bhūbhujā viśrutaguṇaḥ prasādenābhinanditaḥ |
varaṃ gṛhāṇetyuktaśca sa na jagrāha kiṃcana || k48.36 ||
tasyādīnadyuteścakre mānamabhyadhikaṃ nṛpaḥ |
aucityacārucaritaḥ priyaḥ kasya na niḥspṛhaḥ || k48.37 ||
sa sadā darśane rājñaḥ kāñcanāṅgāni dantinaḥ |
punarjātanavāṅgena dattāṇi prīyate dadau || k48.38 ||
tamuvāca mahīpālaḥ sevāpraṇayayantritaḥ |
dūtīṃ manaḥprasādaya vadanadyutimudvahan || k48.39 ||
prabhūtahemasaṃbhārāṃ gurvīṃ sovāmimāmaham |
na sahe prauravārgo hi bhavaṇīyo mahībhṛtām || k48.40 ||
saṃvibhajya janānītaiḥ kā prītirmama kāñcanaiḥ |
tavānarghaguṇā mūrtiriyameva mama priyā || k48.41 ||
lobhaḥ puruṣaratneṣu bhūṣaṇārheṣu śobhate |
rājñāṃ kośeṣu sīdanti hemaratnāśmasaṃcayāḥ || k48.42 ||
samīhitatamaṃ tubhyaṃ kiṃ prayacchāmi kathyatām |
niḥśeṣakośadānena na nāmānuśayo'stu me || k48.43 ||
rājñṃ dṛkpātapātreṇa prāpyante yadi na śriyaḥ |
tadanarthaṃ gatārthinyā ko'thaḥ pārthivasevayā || k48.44 ||
ityuktaḥ kṣitipālena kumāraḥ kalitāñjaliḥ |
tamabhāṣata bhūpāla dātumarhati ko'paraḥ || k48.45 ||
anarthitena ratnāni vibudhebhyaḥ prayacchatā |
tadunnidraṃ samudrasya mudritaṃ bhavatā yaśaḥ || k48.46 ||
mahatāpi prayatnena pūryate na mahāśayaḥ |
alpakānāṃ tadairśvaryaṃ dāridyaṃ tanmahīyasām || k48.47 ||
kiṃ tu tvadbhujaguptānāṃ dharmamārgeṇa jīvatām |
janānāṃ nāsti dāridyaṃ draviṇaṃ yena mṛgyate || k48.48 ||
dhanārthino na tu vayaṃ na ca sevādhikāriṇaḥ |
dhanaṃ dhanaṃ dhanadhiyāṃ māna eva manasvinām || k48.49 ||
mīladguṇena parameśvarasevanena nurmūlatāṃ sumanasāṃ sahasā gatānām |
dainyātpunaḥ kṛpaṇapaṇyapathe cyutānāṃ na sparśamātramapi sādhujanaḥ karoti || k48.50 ||
arthitvānmaraṇaṃ varaṃ tanubhṛtāṃ dainyāvasannātmanāṃ
arthī sarvajanāvamānavasatiḥ satkārayogyaḥ śavaḥ |
kumbhastāvadadhahprayāti guṇavān kūpāvatāre paraṃ
yāvanmohatamaḥpraveśavivaśaḥ prāpto'rthitā lambate || k48.51 ||
sāmānyā dhanasaṃpadaḥ krayakṛṣiprāpyā sadā dhīmatāṃ
saṃtoṣo yadi nāsti tatkimaparā bhūmirnidhānāvṛtā |
santyevātiśayaprasādaniratāstā hemaratnakriyāḥ
kasyeṣṭaḥ priyasaṃgamāya vapuṣāṃ sevāmayo vikrayaḥ || k48.52 ||
ityapāradhiyastasya vacaḥ śrutvā mahīpatiḥ |
gṝhyatāmaparaṃ kiṃcidityabhāṣata sādaraḥ || k48.53 ||
aucityacaturālāpaḥ karkaśo'pi nṛpāṃ priyaḥ |
kṛpaṇaścāṭukāro'pi karṇaśūlāya kevalaḥ || k48.54 ||
aurdāryaparituṣṭena sa rājñabhyarthitaḥ param |
tamūce yadu tuṣṭo'si sutā mahyaṃ pradīyatām || k48.55 ||
ityukte tena nṛpatiḥ saṃdehāndolitāśayaḥ |
prātarvaktāhamityuktvā kṣaṇaṃ kṣmātalamaikṣata || k48.56 ||
sa kumāraṃ visṛjyātha pradhānāmātyamabravīt |
prasādarabhasenaiva kṛtaṃ vākcāpalaṃ mayā || k48.57 ||
cakravartikulotpannā kanyā puṇyapaṇocitā |
kathaṃ sāmānyapaurāya guṇamātreṇa dīyate || k48.58 ||
dadāmīti pratiśrutya paścādanuśayākulaḥ |
kathaṃ sarādhano bhūtvā bhaviṣyāmyarthiniṣphalaḥ || k48.59 ||
kathaṃ prāptasya tasyāhaṃ prātardraṣṭmukhaṃ puraḥ |
priyo'pyapriyatāṃ yātaḥ sa me durlabhayecchayā || k48.60 ||
nūnaṃ guṇopapanno'pi prakṛtyaiva śarīriṇām |
vakti yāvanna dehīti tāvadgavati vallabhah || k48.61 ||
iti bhūmipateḥ śrutvāḥ vaco dolāvalambinaḥ |
tamuvāca mahāmātyaḥ saṃcintyāvasarocitam || k48.62 ||
anālocitaparyantāḥ pratyagrasarasādarāḥ |
svabhāvarabhasā eva bhavanti prabhubuddhayaḥ || k48.63 ||
aśakyārthanayā tena lubdheneva guṇodayaḥ |
rājasevāpravṛttena hemahastī vināśitaḥ || k48.64 ||
vācyo'sau bhavatā svairaṃ kanyārthī punarāgataḥ |
hemahastinamāruhya prāptaḥ prāpsyasi me sutām || k48.65 ||
tenotkṛttaḥ svahastena kutastasya sa kuñjaraḥ |
na cāsau tadvirahitaḥ punarāyāti lajjayā || k48.66 ||
ityamātyasya vacasā nṛpatiryuktimāśritaḥ |
prāptaṃ kumāramanyedyustadevābhimukho'vadat || k48.67 ||
kumāro'pi gṝhaṃ gatvā vivāhocitamaṅgalaiḥ |
haumadviradamāruhya svajanena sajāyayau || k48.68 ||
svarṇavāraṇasaṃrūḍhaṃ tamāyāntaṃ mahīpatiḥ |
vilokyāścaryavibhavaṃ mene puṇyavatāṃ varam || k48.69 ||
kautukādatha bhūpālastaṃ gajaṃ hemavigraham |
āruroha mahotsāhaḥ sumerumiva vajrabhṛt || k48.70 ||
ārūḍhe pṛthivīpāle na cacāla sa kuñjaraḥ |
prasarsarpa kumāreṇa punaścālaṃkṛtāsanaḥ || k48.71 ||
taṃ jñātvā nṛpatirdevaṃ tatprabhāveṇa vismitaḥ |
dhanyo'smīti vadan kanyāṃ dadau tasmai smaraśriyam || k48.72 ||
abhyarcya kanyāratnena nṛpatiḥ puruṣottamam |
harṣotsavasamuddhūtaḥ sudhāsindhurivābabhau || k48.73 ||
tataḥ kumāre dayitāmādāya svagṛhaṃ gate |
saphalo'bhūdanaṅgasya kārmukākarṣaṇaśramaḥ || k48.74 ||
nave vayasi bhogārhe navakāntāsamāgame |
tasyābhūdvibhavodāraḥ sadā navanavotsavaḥ || k48.75 ||
tataḥ kadācidbhūpālaḥ kṛtakṛtyaḥ prasenajit |
puṇyaprabhāvaṃ jāmātuḥ kalayan samacintayat || k48.76 ||
aho divyaḥ prabhāvo'sau kumārasya pradṛśyate |
na hi sāmānyapuṇyānāṃ pāko bhavati tadvidhaḥ || k48.77 ||
kulaṃ lakṣmīharmyaṃ hṛtaśaśimadā rūpalaharī
vayaḥ saṃbhogārhaṃ guṇaparicayo bhūṣaṇacayaḥ |
yaśaḥ puṇyodyānaprasṛtakusumollāsaruciraṃ
na vidmaḥ kasyāyaṃ kuśalapariṇāmasya vibhavaḥ || k48.78 ||
iti saṃcintya suciraṃ sa saṃjātakutūhalaḥ |
sarvajñadarśanāvaddhamāruroha manoratham || k48.79 ||
sa jātātaramāhūya sutāṃ ca sacivaiḥ saha |
bhagavantaṃ yayau draṣṭuṃ manasā prathamaṃ gataḥ || k48.80 ||
yāte dṛṣṭipathaṃ jetavane saṃtyajya vāhanam |
upasṛtyaḥ nṛpaḥ padbhyāṃ bhagavantaṃ vyalokayat || k48.81 ||
sa taṃ praṇamya tatpādapadmabhūtiśikhāmaṇiḥ |
sutāṃ jāmātaraṃ cāsmai namro nāmnā nyavedayat || k48.82 ||
upaviṣṭeṣu sarveṣu praṇāmānatamauliṣu |
papraccha rājā sarvajñaṃ bhagavantaṃ kṛtāñjaliḥ || k48.83 ||
ayaṃ guṇagaopetaḥ kumāraḥ śrīmatāṃ varaḥ |
haimeva dantināyāto bhagavan kena karmaṇā || k48.84 ||
iyaṃ cīvarakanyā ca matsutāsya navā vadhūḥ |
kena puṇyaprabhāveṇa jīvitādadhivallabhā || k48.85 ||
iti pṛṣṭaḥ kṣitīśena sarvavidbhagavān jinaḥ |
tamūce bhūpate puṃsāṃ puṇyodbhūtā vibhūtayaḥ || k48.86 ||
yadudāro yaducito yadbhrājiṃṣṇu yadadbhutam |
spṛhaṇīyaṃ ca yalloke tattatpuṇyasamudbahvam || k48.87 ||
vipaśvī bhagavān pūrvaṃ sugataḥ saha bhikṣubhiḥ |
cacāra lokakṛpayā rājño bandhumataḥ pure || k48.88 ||
tasminnavasare tatra kumāryā saha dārakaḥ |
vikrīḍāvartmani puraḥ kṛtvā dārumayaṃ gajam || k48.89 ||
tau vilokya samāyāntaṃ dhmātajambūnadadyutim |
phullapadmadalākārakaruṇāsnigdhalocanam || k48.90 ||
bhagavantaṃ samudbhūtatadbhaktisarasonmukhau |
krīḍāgajaṃ nivedyāsmai praṇatau tasthatuḥ puraḥ || k48.91 ||
bhagavānapi sarvajñastayorjñātvā manoratham |
dayayā caraṇasparśaṃ vidadhe dārudantinaḥ || k48.92 ||
samyakcittaprasādena dṛṣṭau bhagavatātha tau |
praṇīdhānaṃ vivāhāya cakraturdārakau mithaḥ || k48.93 ||
kulaprabhāvavibhavairbhūyājjanma mamocitam |
vāhanaṃ hemadantī ca kumārasyetyabhūnmatiḥ || k48.94 ||
dṛṣṭvā bhagavataḥ kanyā saṃsaktre cārucīvare |
janmacīvarayuktāṃ syāmahametacintayat || k48.95 ||
sa eṣa praṇidhānena jātasteneha hastakaḥ |
iyaṃ cīvarakanyā ca tanucīvaralakṣaṇa || k48.96 ||
iti kṣrutvā kṣitipatistadvṛttaṃ sugato'ditam |
mukuṭaspṛṣṭatatpādapadmaḥ svabhavanaṃ yayau || k48.97 ||
yāte savismayaṃ rājñi kumāraḥ saha jāyayā |
kathyamānaṃ bhagavatā dharmaṃ śuśrāva śuddhadhīḥ || k48.98 ||
tatastau jātavairāgyau kṣīṇasaṃsāravāsanau |
pravrajyayā jitakleśau śuddhāṃ bodhimavāpatuḥ || k48.99 ||
vitatasukṛtapuṇyābhyāsayogena puṃsāṃ bhavati kuśalabhājāṃ dharmakāmārthasaṃpat |
abhimatamatha bhuktvā tatphalaṃ sadārāste vighanagaganakāntiṃ śāntimante bhajante || k48.100 ||

iti kṣemandraviracitāyāṃ bodhisattvāvadānakalpalatāyāṃ hastakāvadānamaṣṭacatvāriṃśaḥ pallavaḥ ||
